Other key points highlighted by the teacher:

如果这些功能失常，会导致线粒体损伤积聚，引发持续性炎症和心律失常等严重后果。 If these functions are compromised, it will cause accumulation of damaged mitochondria, persistent inflammation, and severe consequences such as arrhythmia. 5

心脏巨噬细胞失调还可能导致局部组织结构异常，比如过多胶原沉积致心肌僵硬、血管顺应性下降，从而加重心脏疾病风险。 Cardiac macrophage dysfunction can also result in abnormal tissue structure such as excessive collagen deposition and reduced vascular compliance, increasing the risk of cardiac diseases.

问1. 心脏中最重要的免疫细胞是什么？
问What are the most important immune cells in heart?
答

心脏中最重要的免疫细胞是组织驻留巨噬细胞（resident macrophages）。 The most important immune cells in the heart are resident macrophages.

这些巨噬细胞不是游走而来，而是长期驻留在心脏组织中。 These macrophages are not recruited but are long-term residents in the heart tissue. 5

问2. 这些细胞的三大主要功能是什么？
问What are their 3 main functions?
答

（1）清除细胞碎片，维持线粒体完整性。 (1) Clear cellular debris and maintain mitochondrial integrity.

巨噬细胞通过识别和清除受损线粒体或凋亡细胞碎片，保障心肌细胞正常功能。 Macrophages identify and remove damaged mitochondria or apoptotic debris, ensuring proper cardiomyocyte function. 53

（2）调节局部炎症，维持组织稳态。 (2) Modulate local inflammation and maintain tissue homeostasis.

通过感知如心磷脂等线粒体损伤信号，调节炎症反应，避免损伤。 By sensing mitochondrial damage signals such as cardiolipin, they regulate inflammatory responses to prevent further injury. 3

（3）与心肌细胞形成缝隙连接，促进电信号传导。 (3) Form gap junctions with cardiomyocytes to promote electrical conduction.

巨噬细胞与心肌细胞形成“缝隙连接”，帮助离子和信号的交流，维持心脏收缩节律。 Macrophages form gap junctions with cardiomyocytes, facilitating ion and signal exchange to maintain rhythmic contraction of the heart. 3

问3. 新生儿心脏T细胞有何特殊之处？
问What renders cardiac neonates T cells special?
答

新生鼠的心脏T细胞不产生干扰素γ，因此炎症反应较低，心脏具备更强再生能力。 Neonatal cardiac T cells do not produce interferon-γ, which lowers inflammation and grants the heart a stronger regenerative capacity. 48

问4. 心脏调节性T细胞表达哪些标志物？
问What markers are expressed by cardiac T regs?
答

心脏调节性T细胞表达CD69等表面标志物。 Cardiac regulatory T cells express surface markers such as CD69. 8

这些标志物有助于鉴定和研究调节性T细胞对心脏免疫的贡献。 These markers help identify and study the contribution of regulatory T cells to cardiac immunity.

问5. 他们如何控制心梗后的损伤修复？
问How do they control post-infarction injuries remodelling?
答

调节性T细胞通过调控炎症、促进修复因子的产生，有助于抑制过度炎症反应，促进损伤心肌组织的修复和重塑。 Regulatory T cells control post-infarction remodelling by modulating inflammation and promoting repair factors, thus suppressing excessive inflammation and aiding in the repair and remodelling of injured myocardial tissue. 8

问1. 肺免疫学属于哪类免疫学？
问What Kind of Immunology does Lung Immunology refer to?
答

肺免疫学主要研究发生在黏膜系统的免疫应答。 Lung immunology mainly studies immune responses occurring at mucosal membranes.

黏膜包括：

呼吸道（lungs/respiratory tract）

消化系统（digestive system）

泌尿道（urinary tract）

唾液腺（salivary glands）

哺乳期乳腺（lactating breast）

泪腺（lachrymal glands）

扁桃体和腺样体（tonsils & adenoids）

阑尾（appendix） Mucosa includes:

Respiratory tract

Digestive system

Urinary tract

Salivary glands

Lactating breast

Lachrymal glands

Tonsils & adenoids

Appendix

黏膜表面持续接触微生物、食物和吸入性抗原。 The mucous membranes are in constant contact with microorganisms, food, and inhaled antigens.

问2. 黏液的作用是什么？
问What is the function of mucus?
答

黏液是一种由水、盐和保护性免疫细胞构成的透明液体，能帮助器官保持湿润和润滑。 Mucus is a thin, clear liquid consisting of water, salts, and protective immune cells that helps our organs stay moist and lubricated.

黏液还有助于防止感染和刺激物的侵害。 Mucus also helps protect from infections and irritants.

当病原体或刺激物进入呼吸道时，黏液能将其黏住并清除。 When a germ or irritant enters the airways, the material gets trapped by sticky mucus and removed.

总结 Summary

本页强调肺免疫学属于黏膜免疫，关注呼吸道等黏膜部位的免疫反应。黏液为维持器官润滑、防止感染及清除刺激物发挥重要作用。

This page highlights that lung immunology is a branch of mucosal immunology, focusing on immune responses at mucosal sites like the airways. Mucus plays a key role in keeping organs lubricated, preventing infection, and clearing irritants.

Mucosal Tissues of the Human Body 人体黏膜组织

Lung-immunology Take home-message 肺免疫学要点总结

1
人体黏膜组织类型与特点（中英对照）

Types and Characteristics of Mucosal Tissues in the Human Body

Type I型黏膜：主要分布于消化道、呼吸道和女性上生殖道。 Type I mucosal surfaces: mainly found in the alimentary tract, respiratory tract, and female upper reproductive system.

Type I型黏膜表面由单层柱状上皮细胞组成，主要抗体为IgA。 Type I mucosal surfaces have a simple columnar epithelial layer, where IgA is the major immunoglobulin (Ig) isotype.

Type II型黏膜：存在于视觉系统、口腔、消化道和女性下生殖道。 Type II mucosal surfaces: found in the visual system, mouth, alimentary tract, and female lower reproductive system.

Type II型黏膜表面为分层鳞状上皮，主要抗体为IgG。 Type II mucosal surfaces have a stratified squamous epithelial layer, and the major Ig isotype is IgG.

2
肺部单层柱状上皮的细胞类型与功能（中英对照）

Cell Types and Functions in the Simple Columnar Epithelium of the Lung

肺气道上皮由多种细胞类型组成，这些细胞提供物理和化学屏障，对抗感染。 The epithelium of the conducting airways is made of different cell types that produce physical and chemical barriers against infection.

纤毛细胞：清除黏液。 Ciliated cells: clearance of mucus.

杯状细胞：分泌黏液。 Goblet cells: secretion of mucus.

Club(Clara)细胞：分泌保护性糖胺聚糖。 Club (Clara) cells: secretion of protective glycosaminoglycans.

基底细胞：起始细胞，能再生其他类型细胞。 Basal cells: progenitor cells.

其他（如纤毛束细胞、离子细胞、肺内分泌细胞PNEC等）：各有特殊功能。 Others (tuft cells, ionocytes, pulmonary neuroendocrine cells (PNECs)): specific functions.

在肺泡层面，I型肺泡细胞(AT1)促进气体交换，II型肺泡细胞(AT2)分泌表面活性物质。 At the alveolar level, alveolar type 1 (AT1) pneumocytes facilitate gas exchange, while AT2 pneumocytes secrete pulmonary surfactant.

Club细胞是表达pIgR（聚合免疫球蛋白受体）的主要细胞，维持分泌型IgA免疫屏障起关键作用。 Club cells are the dominant cell type responsible for pIgR expression and play a key role in maintaining the secretory IgA immunobarrier.

3
课堂补充说明（老师录音要点归纳，中英对照）

Class Supplementary Notes (Summary from the Teacher’s Lecture)

老师强调，所有黏膜表面都会和微生物、食物以及吸入抗原持续接触，需要稳定而强有力的屏障。 The teacher emphasized that all mucosal surfaces are in constant contact with microbes, food, and inhaled antigens, requiring a stable and robust barrier 10.

其中，Type I型黏膜分布的器官内IgA最为重要，对抗外界抗原和感染提供最前线保护。 Among them, IgA in type I mucosal organs offers frontline protection against external antigens and infection 7.

呼吸道的分泌型IgA主要来源于Club细胞，对维持局部免疫至关重要。 Secretory IgA in the respiratory tract mainly originates from club cells, which are vital for maintaining local immunity 7.

Type II型黏膜因其角化和多层结构，IgG在这些部位更占主导。 In type II mucosa, due to keratinization and stratified structure, IgG is more predominant 7.

简要总结 Summary

类型不同的黏膜决定了抗体分布与防御策略。 Different types of mucosa determine antibody distribution and defense strategies.

肺内单层柱状上皮的多种细胞协同建立局部免疫屏障。 Various cells in the lung simple columnar epithelium form a local immune barrier collaboratively.

1
肠道黏膜屏障的基本结构

The basic structure of the gut mucosal barrier

肠道黏膜屏障属于典型的黏膜屏障，是肠道防御外界抗原与微生物的第一道防线。 The gut mucosal barrier is a typical mucosal barrier and is the first line of defense against external antigens and microorganisms in the intestine.

屏障由上皮细胞层和下层免疫细胞群组成，具有物理和免疫双重防御功能。 The barrier consists of an epithelial cell layer and underlying immune cell populations, providing both physical and immunological defense.

2
肠道上皮细胞的主要类型与功能

Main types and functions of intestinal epithelial cells

杯状细胞分泌黏液，为肠腔形成润滑和物理屏障。 Goblet cells secrete mucus, forming a lubricating and physical barrier in the intestinal lumen.

潘氏细胞位于肠隐窝底部，分泌抗菌肽以调控或杀伤肠道微生物。 Paneth cells are located at the base of the crypts and secrete antimicrobial peptides to regulate or kill intestinal microorganisms.

M细胞位于与淋巴组织相关的上皮区（如派尔氏结表面），能够转运抗原，是“诱导位点”免疫应答启动的关键。 M cells are located in epithelium associated with lymphoid tissues (e.g., over Peyer’s patches), responsible for antigen transcytosis and immune response initiation at the "inductive site".

3
屏障功能分区：诱导位点与效应位点

Functional compartmentalization: inductive site and effector site

诱导位点是指肠道黏膜相关淋巴组织（MALT），如派尔氏结，是抗原初始识别和免疫反应启动的地方。 The inductive site refers to gut-associated lymphoid tissues (MALT), like Peyer’s patches, which are sites of initial antigen recognition and immune response initiation.

效应位点是活化后的免疫细胞迁移并发挥免疫效应的区域。 The effector site is where activated immune cells migrate to execute their immune functions.

4
课堂要点与补充（老师讲解内容）

Key points from the lecture (teacher's comments)

肠道黏膜作为Type II型黏膜屏障，结构复杂多样，既有分泌黏液的细胞，也有产生抗菌肽和运输抗原的专门细胞。 The gut mucosa, as a type II mucosal barrier, has complex structures including mucus-secreting cells, antimicrobial peptide-producing cells, and antigen-transporting specialized cells14.

派尔氏结等淋巴相关组织为抗原进入后的首个免疫应答启动区域，由M细胞将抗原递递送入淋巴组织，激活特异免疫应答。 Lymphoid-associated structures like Peyer’s patches are the first area for immune response initiation after antigen entry; M cells deliver antigens to lymphoid tissue and activate specific immune responses14.

诱导位点与效应位点的划分，有助于理解肠黏膜免疫的时空调控。 The distinction between inductive and effector sites helps understand the spatiotemporal regulation of gut mucosal immunity14.

5
小结

Summary

肠道黏膜屏障依赖多种上皮细胞和免疫细胞的协作，整体功能分为抗原感知和效应反应两个环节，保证了机体对肠道外来抗原和微生物的有效防御。 The gut mucosal barrier relies on the cooperation of various epithelial and immune cells, with overall function separated into antigen recognition and effector response, ensuring effective defense against luminal antigens and microbes.

肠道黏膜屏障的结构 Structure of Gut Mucosal Barrier

1
肠道黏膜屏障是由分泌粘蛋白的上皮细胞形成的，粘蛋白将肠道菌群与上皮细胞分隔开。

The gut mucosal barrier is formed by epithelial cells that secrete mucin glycoproteins, which separate the gut microbiota from epithelial cells.

2
粘蛋白形成一种粘稠的凝胶状双层结构，可延伸至距离上皮表面150μm。

Mucins form a viscous gel-like bilayer that extends up to 150 μm from the intestinal epithelial cell surface. 16

3
屏障分为两层结构：

The barrier consists of two layers:

紧密的内层（内层粘液层, 保护区域）： Firm inner layer (inner mucus layer, protected zone):

这一层含有防御因子，如防御素、C型凝集素（潘氏细胞分泌），能抵抗微生物侵袭。 This layer contains protective components against microbes, such as defensins and C-type lectins (secreted by Paneth cells).

IgA和抗菌蛋白存在于此，有效阻止细菌穿透上皮。 IgA and antimicrobial proteins here impede bacterial penetration into the epithelium. 16

疏松的外层（外层粘液层）： Loose outer layer (outer mucus layer):

外层存在大量细菌。 This outer layer contains huge numbers of bacteria.

与内层相比，外层被细菌大量定植，但与上皮细胞仍有物理隔离。 Compared to the inner layer, the outer layer is heavily colonized with bacteria, while still maintaining a physical separation from epithelial cells. 1613

4
教师补充要点（录音内容）：

Additional key points from the lecture:

老师强调，肠道粘膜屏障为Type II型粘膜屏障，具有高度分区和动态流动性。 The teacher emphasized that the gut mucosa is a type II mucosal barrier, characterized by strong compartmentalization and dynamic flow. 16

粘液的内外两层保证了细菌不直接接触上皮细胞，内层保护性最强。 The two mucus layers ensure bacteria do not directly contact epithelial cells, with the inner layer providing the greatest protection. 16

潘氏细胞产生的防御素协同IgA共同阻止细菌穿越内层进入上皮。 Defensins secreted by Paneth cells and IgA work together to prevent bacteria from crossing the inner mucus and invading the epithelium. 16

外层为细菌大量定植区，是与肠道微生物群落直接相互作用的“缓冲带”。 The outer layer is the main site for dense bacterial colonization and serves as a “buffer zone” for direct interactions with the gut microbiota. 1613

5
小结 Summary：

肠道黏膜屏障通过双层粘液结构，实现了对微生物的物理和免疫隔离，核心保护依赖于紧密内层的IgA和抗菌成分。

The gut mucosal barrier forms a double mucus layer for physical and immune separation from microbes, with essential protection provided by the firm inner layer’s IgA and antimicrobial components.

抗菌肽的释放与作用机制——Paneth细胞

Antimicrobial Peptide Release by Paneth Cells

1
Paneth细胞是肠道隐窝底部特化的上皮细胞，主要功能是分泌多种抗菌肽，如防御素。

Paneth cells are specialized epithelial cells located at the crypt base of the intestine, and their main function is to secrete various antimicrobial peptides, such as defensins.

2
抗菌肽（主要包括防御素）通过分泌到肠腔，直接杀伤或抑制细菌等微生物，维护肠道内环境稳定。

Antimicrobial peptides (mainly defensins) are secreted into the intestinal lumen, directly killing or inhibiting bacteria and other microbes, thus maintaining intestinal homeostasis.

3
具体释放和杀菌机制如下：

The detailed release and bactericidal mechanism are as follows:

（1）Paneth细胞在受到微生物刺激时释放抗菌肽进入肠腔。 (1) Upon microbial stimulation, Paneth cells release antimicrobial peptides into the intestinal lumen.

（2）抗菌肽（如防御素）带有正电荷，与细菌膜表面的负电荷发生静电吸引。 (2) The antimicrobial peptides (such as defensins) are positively charged and are attracted to the negatively charged surface of bacterial membranes by electrostatic forces.

（3）在电场和静电作用下，抗菌肽嵌入细菌脂质双层膜内。 (3) Driven by the electric field and electrostatic attraction, antimicrobial peptides insert into the bacterial lipid bilayer.

（4）多分子抗菌肽聚集形成跨膜孔道，破坏细胞膜完整性。 (4) Clusters of antimicrobial peptides form transmembrane pores, disrupting the integrity of the cell membrane.

（5）细菌膜通透性异常，导致细胞内容物泄漏和细胞死亡。 (5) This abnormal membrane permeability results in leakage of cellular contents and eventual cell death.

4
Paneth细胞介导的抗菌肽释放是肠道固有免疫屏障的重要组成部分，能有效防止有害细菌入侵并调控肠道菌群。

Paneth cell-mediated antimicrobial peptide release is a key part of the innate gut immune barrier, effectively preventing harmful bacterial invasion and regulating the microbiota.

简要总结：

Paneth细胞通过分泌抗菌肽（如防御素），利用静电作用嵌入细菌膜并打孔，快速杀伤肠道有害菌，维持肠道微生态平衡。

Paneth cells secrete antimicrobial peptides (such as defensins) that use electrostatic attraction to insert into bacterial membranes, create pores, and rapidly kill harmful intestinal bacteria, maintaining gut microbial balance.

Microfold cells (M Cells)与肠上皮内CD8+淋巴细胞杀伤功能 Killing of infected epithelia by intraepithelial CD8+ lymphocytes (IELs)

PPT 1：Microfold cells (M Cells)

Microfold细胞（M细胞）

1
M细胞是专门的上皮细胞，能转运肠腔抗原（转胞作用transcytosis）。 M cells are specialized epithelial cells capable of performing transcytosis of luminal antigens.
2
分布于：Peyer氏结、鼻咽相关淋巴组织NALT、孤立淋巴滤泡和阑尾等部位。 Located in Peyer's patches (PPs), nasopharynx-associated lymphoid tissue (NALT), isolated lymphoid follicles, and the appendix.
3
它们是黏膜免疫系统的“守门人”，有屏障和筛选作用。 They play a critical role as gatekeepers in the mucosal immune system.
4
含有淋巴细胞和抗原递呈细胞（APCs）的对接位点，促进黏膜特异性免疫应答。 They contain docking sites for lymphocytes and antigen presenting cells (APCs), which is important for stimulating specific mucosal immune responses.
5
M细胞过程图解（左至右顺序）： Diagram explanation for M cell function (left to right):

T细胞经血管进入Peyer氏结，由趋化受体CCR7和L-selectin引导。 T cells enter Peyer's patches from blood vessels, directed by homing receptors CCR7 and L-selectin.

M细胞通过胞吞和吞噬摄取抗原。 M cells take up antigens by endocytosis and phagocytosis.

抗原经M细胞运输，以囊泡形式释放至基底侧表面。 Antigens are transported across the M cell in vesicles and released at the basal surface.

T细胞在Peyer氏结内被树突状细胞激活。 T cells in the Peyer's patch encounter antigen and become activated by dendritic cells.

PPT 2：Killing of infected epithelia by intraepithelial CD8+ lymphocytes (IELs)

肠上皮内CD8+淋巴细胞杀伤感染上皮细胞的机制

1
病毒感染肠上皮细胞，病毒进入上皮细胞。 Virus infects mucosal epithelial cell and enters the epithelial cell.
2
感染的上皮细胞将病毒肽呈递给CD8 IEL（通过MHC I）。 Infected epithelial cell displays viral peptide to CD8 IEL via MHC class I.
3
CD8 IEL被激活后，通过分泌穿孔素/颗粒酶以及Fas途径杀死感染细胞。 Activated IEL kills infected epithelial cell by perforin/granzyme and Fas-dependent pathways.
4
穿孔素和颗粒酶介导细胞凋亡，Fas配体-Fas也可触发凋亡通路。 Perforin and granzyme induce apoptosis of infected cells; the Fas ligand–Fas interaction can also trigger apoptosis.

简要总结

M细胞负责捕获并递送肠腔抗原，激活局部免疫反应，是肠道免疫防线的第一步。 M cells capture and deliver luminal antigens, activating local immune responses—the first step of intestinal immune defense.

肠上皮内CD8+淋巴细胞负责清除被感染的上皮细胞，保障肠道屏障的完整性。 Intraepithelial CD8+ lymphocytes kill infected epithelial cells to maintain intestinal barrier integrity.

共生菌与致病菌的免疫应答Immune Response to commensal vs. Pathogenic Bacteria

1
共生菌存在时，PGE₂、TGF-β和TSLP的产生抑制树突状细胞成熟。 In the presence of commensal bacteria, production of PGE₂, TGF-β, and TSLP inhibits dendritic cell maturation.
2
未成熟的树突状细胞发出较弱的共刺激信号，使CD4 T细胞分化为Treg。 Immature dendritic cells give weak co-stimulatory signals and induce CD4 T cells to differentiate into Treg cells.
3
Treg细胞产生IL-10等抑制性细胞因子，维持免疫耐受和黏膜稳态，防止炎症。 Treg cells produce inhibitory cytokines such as IL-10, maintaining immune tolerance and mucosal homeostasis to prevent inflammation.
4
入侵性致病菌可穿透上皮，激活树突状细胞。 Invasive pathogenic microorganisms penetrate the epithelium and activate dendritic cells.
5
活化的树突状细胞表达高水平的共刺激分子（如CD80、CD28等）和促炎细胞因子（如IL-12），诱导CD4 T细胞分化为效应Th1和Th17细胞。 Activated dendritic cells express high levels of co-stimulatory ligands (e.g., CD80, CD28) and pro-inflammatory cytokines (e.g., IL-12), inducing CD4 T cells to differentiate into effector Th1 and Th17 cells.
6
Th1和Th17细胞促进炎症反应，有助于清除病原体，同时也可能导致组织损伤。 Th1 and Th17 cells enhance inflammatory responses, helping to clear pathogens but also potentially causing tissue damage.
7
免疫稳态时（a图左侧），共生菌与免疫调节因素处于平衡，炎症水平低；失衡时（a图右侧），致病菌增多，调节失效，炎症↑。 During immunological equilibrium (left side of diagram a), commensals and regulatory factors are balanced, resulting in low inflammation; in disequilibrium (right side of diagram a), pathogens increase, regulation fails, and inflammation rises.
8
健康的微生物组包含多种优势菌，促进免疫调节，维持稳态；微生态失调时，益生菌减少、致病菌↑，易导致非特异性炎症和疾病。 A healthy microbiota contains various dominant symbionts that promote immune regulation and maintain homeostasis; dysbiosis leads to a reduction in beneficial bacteria, an increase in pathogens, and predisposes to non-specific inflammation and disease【@ref_19】【@ref_20】。

无菌小鼠肠道免疫缺陷 Intestinal immunological defect in germ-free mice

1
无菌小鼠小肠结构和细胞数量减少，如Peyer氏结、固有层、胚中心等均少且小。 Germ-free mice have reduced size and cellularity in the small intestine, including Peyer's patches, lamina propria, and germinal centers.
2
肠道CD8+ T细胞数量减少，细胞毒性功能↓。 Intestinal epithelial CD8+ T cells are fewer and their cytotoxicity is decreased.
3
固有层CD4+ T细胞数量减少，小肠Th17细胞↓，结肠Th17细胞↑。 Lamina propria CD4+ T cells are reduced, with fewer Th17 cells in the small intestine but more in the colon.
4
FOXP3表达↓调节性CD4+CD25+ T细胞功能受损，免疫调节能力下降。 Decreased FOXP3 expression impairs regulatory CD4+CD25+ T cell function and reduces immune regulation.
5
Paneth细胞分泌的抗菌分子（如Angiogenin 4、REG3γ）下降。 Expression of antimicrobial molecules from Paneth cells such as Angiogenin 4 and REG3γ is decreased.
6
B细胞分泌的IgA减少，肠道免疫屏障功能↓。 B cell production of secretory IgA is reduced, weakening the gut immune barrier.
7
肠道上皮细胞MHC II分子表达↓，TLR9和IL-25等分子表达也↓。 Expression of MHC class II, TLR9, and IL-25 in intestinal epithelial cells is reduced.
8
整体肠道免疫环境受损，易感病原体侵袭和炎症。 Overall, the intestinal immune environment is impaired, making mice more susceptible to pathogen invasion and inflammation【@ref_19】。

T细胞非依赖性产生微生物反应性IgA Generation of microbiota-reactive IgA through T cell-independent

1
T细胞非依赖性IgA主要在Peyer氏结外、无T细胞情况下产生。 T cell-independent IgA is generated primarily outside Peyer's patches and in the absence of T cells.
2
树突状细胞分泌视黄酸（RA）、一氧化氮（NO）、TGF-β、BAFF和APRIL等因子，为IgA产生提供微环境。 Dendritic cells secrete retinoic acid (RA), nitric oxide (NO), TGF-β, BAFF and APRIL, creating an IgA-promoting microenvironment.
3
BAFF和APRIL通过B细胞表面TACI受体，促进B细胞向IgA分化。 BAFF and APRIL bind to the TACI receptor on B cells to promote class switching to IgA.
4
这种方式产生的IgA多为多反应性，对微生物特异性低，对菌群组成影响小。 The resulting IgA is polyreactive, has low specificity for microbes, and tends to have a minimal effect on microbiota composition【@ref_19】。

黏膜IgA的结构 Structure of Mucosal IgA

1
与血清型IgA不同，分泌型IgA（SIgA）为二聚体，由J链连接两个单体。 Unlike serum IgA, secretory IgA (SIgA) is a dimer, with two monomers joined by a J chain.
2
人类IgA1主要分布于小肠和血清，蛋白酶抗性↓；IgA2主要见于结肠，蛋白酶抗性↑。 In humans, IgA1 is mainly found in the small intestine and serum and is less protease resistant, while IgA2 is abundant in the colon and more protease resistant.
3
二聚体IgA借助聚合免疫球蛋白受体（pIgR）分泌进入肠腔，部分pIgR与IgA共价结合，形成具蛋白酶抗性的分泌成分。 Dimeric IgA is secreted into the lumen via the polymeric immunoglobulin receptor (pIgR), with part of the receptor remaining covalently linked to IgA to form a protease-resistant secretory component【@ref_19】。

sIgA在维持肠道稳态中的作用

Role of sIgA in maintaining gut homeostasis

1
分泌型免疫球蛋白A（sIgA）是主要的黏膜抗体。 Secretory immunoglobulin A (SIgA) is the major mucosal antibody.
2
sIgA有助于限制肠道内细菌，防止其系统性扩散，但其对肠道定植的影响尚不完全确定。 SIgA is important in containing bacteria within the gut and preventing their systemic spread; however, the effect of IgA on intestinal colonization itself is less clear.
3
当sIgA针对入侵病原体产生时，可以通过经典的凝集作用和链式生长，形成免疫排斥作用。 When SIgA is induced against an invading pathogen, interactions result in immune exclusion through a combination of classic agglutination and enchained growth.
4
IgA通过以下机制调节肠道菌群平衡： IgA regulates the gut microbiome through the following mechanisms:

（1）细菌代谢产物调节免疫反应。 Bacterial metabolites regulate immune response.

（2）浆细胞分泌IgA至肠腔。 Plasma cells release IgA into the gut lumen.

（3）IgA维持肠道菌群的共生性和多样性。 IgA keeps microbiota symbiotic and diverse.

（4）IgA帮助肠道菌群恢复稳态。 IgA helps the gut microbiota return to homeostasis.

5
凝集作用：抗体介导的细菌聚集。多价抗体同时结合多个细菌，发生于高细菌密度。 Agglutination: Antibody-mediated aggregation of target bacteria, due to simultaneous binding of multiple bacterial cells by multivalent antibody. Occurs at high bacterial densities.
6
链式生长：抗体在细菌无性繁殖时介导母细胞与子细胞的连接，发生于细菌密度较低时。 Enchained growth: Antibody-mediated linkage of a bacterium to its own daughter cells during asexual reproduction. Occurs at low bacterial densities.

>

IgA acts in a context-dependent manner to shape the colonization and function of the intestinal microbiota

IgA在不同情境下调控肠道菌群的定植与功能

Role 1: Prevent colonization

作用1：抑制定植

IgA prevents bacterial colonization through the aggregation of fast-growing bacteria and the shedding of these aggregates in luminal flow, and through the immune exclusion of invasive pathogens and epithelial-proximal commensals. IgA通过聚集快速生长的细菌并使其随肠道腔内流动排出，以及对入侵性致病菌和靠近上皮的共生菌的免疫排斥，来阻止细菌定植。

Role 2: Promote colonization

作用2：促进定植

IgA promotes bacterial colonization through the facilitation of mucosal adhesion and biofilm formation in slow-growing bacteria, which are thus less susceptible to being shed in luminal flow; IgA may also promote metabolic interactions between bacteria. IgA通过促进慢生长菌群在黏膜表面的黏附和生物膜形成，使其不易被肠腔流体冲刷排出；IgA还可能促进细菌之间的代谢互作，从而帮助细菌定植。

Role 3: Alter gene expression and functionality

作用3：调节基因表达和功能

IgA influences bacterial gene expression and functionality, including the downregulation of bacterial flagellar genes and motility, and the regulation of glycan metabolism genes (PULs, polysaccharide utilization loci). IgA调控细菌的基因表达和功能，包括下调鞭毛基因和运动能力，以及调控多糖代谢相关基因（如多糖利用位点PULs）等。

简明听课笔记中英对照一一对应｜Concise Bilingual Notes

1
IgA聚集快速生长细菌，阻止其定植与侵袭。 IgA aggregates fast-growing bacteria, preventing their colonization and invasion.
2
IgA帮助慢生长细菌附着黏膜，促进其定植与稳定。 IgA facilitates mucosal adhesion of slow-growing bacteria, promoting their colonization and persistence.
3
IgA能够调节细菌的基因表达，例如降低鞭毛相关基因表达和多糖利用位点的激活，影响细菌功能。 IgA regulates bacterial gene expression, such as downregulating flagellin genes and modulating polysaccharide utilization loci, thereby affecting bacterial functions.
4
不同情境下，IgA既能抑制有害菌群（作用1），也能促进有益共生菌稳定定植（作用2），并参与细菌群落结构和功能的精细调控（作用3）。 In different contexts, IgA not only inhibits harmful bacteria (Role 1), but also supports beneficial commensals (Role 2), and finely tunes gut microbiota structure and function (Role 3).

营养与IgA-微生物群互作 Nutrition and IgA–Microbiota Interactions

1
营养和IgA、肠道微生物功能之间存在双向调控关系。 There is a bidirectional relationship between nutrition, IgA, and gut microbiota function.
2
健康饮食中的糖类、脂肪酸、谷氨酰胺、视黄酸等可调节宿主的IgA免疫反应及代谢–免疫平衡。 Dietary carbohydrates, fatty acids, glutamine, and retinoic acid in a healthy diet regulate host IgA responses and the balance between metabolism and immunity.
3
肠道细菌代谢产物（如短链脂肪酸和ATP）也能促进或抑制IgA的产生。 Bacterial metabolites in the gut (such as short-chain fatty acids and ATP) can also promote or inhibit the production of IgA.
4
IgA结合微生物后可影响细菌的代谢过程，比如调节多糖利用位点的表达。 IgA binding to microbes can affect their metabolism, such as by regulating the expression of polysaccharide utilization loci.
5
营养不良时，整体IgA包被增加，致病菌包被量增多，而某些共生菌（如乳酸杆菌）IgA包被减少。 During undernutrition, overall IgA coating increases, coating of pathobionts increases, while IgA coating of some commensals (e.g., Lactobacillus) decreases.
6
营养不良驱动某些共生菌如乳酸杆菌表面抗原改变，适应性增强，逃避免疫排除。 Undernutrition drives commensal bacteria like Lactobacillus to change surface antigens, increasing adaptation and evading immune exclusion.
7
营养过剩情况下（如高脂饮食），Clostridiales等重要共生菌的IgA包被减少，而乳酸杆菌的包被增加。 In overnutrition situations (e.g., high-fat diet), IgA coating of Clostridiales (key commensals) decreases, while coating of Lactobacillus increases.
8
IgA识别功能紊乱与高脂饮食共同加重脂代谢、葡萄糖代谢失衡，产生正反馈恶性循环。 Dysregulated IgA targeting, together with high-fat diet, aggravates lipid and glucose metabolic disorders, forming a positive-feedback vicious cycle.
9
不论营养不良还是营养过剩，都会破坏IgA–微生物群的正常互作，影响宿主代谢及免疫稳态。 Both undernutrition and overnutrition disrupt normal IgA–microbiota interactions, impairing host metabolic and immune homeostasis.

肠道共生菌对炎症性损伤的保护作用示例 Example of anti-inflammatory protection by commensal bacteria over pathogenic bacteria

重点内容总结（严格中英对照一一对应）
1
Bacteroides fragilis产生多糖A（PSA），能够诱导保护性免疫反应。 Bacteroides fragilis produces polysaccharide A (PSA), which induces a protective immune response.
2.PSA被肠道树突状细胞　摄取，并递呈到CD4+ T细胞，主要在肠系膜淋巴结内进行抗原提呈。 PSA is taken up by intestinal dendritic cells (DCs), which present it to CD4+ T cells, mainly in mesenteric lymph nodes.
答案 CD
3
PSA活化CD4+ T辅助细胞（THH​细胞），促进其分化为具有抗炎/调节功能的Tregreg​细胞。 Recognition of PSA by CD4+ T helper (THH​) cells promotes their differentiation into anti-inflammatory/regulatory Tregreg​ cells.
4
Tregreg​细胞分泌白介素10（IL-10），抑制炎症性细胞因子（如IL-17, IL-23和TNF）的产生。 Tregreg​ cells secrete interleukin-10 (IL-10), which suppresses the production of pro-inflammatory cytokines (such as IL-17, IL-23, and TNF).
5
通常情况下，由致病菌Helicobacter hepaticus等引发的炎症，依赖TH17H17​细胞及其炎性因子。 Normally, inflammation induced by pathogenic bacteria like Helicobacter hepaticus depends on TH17H17​ cells and their inflammatory cytokines.
6
Tregreg​细胞/IL-10可抑制TH17H17​介导的炎症反应，保护肠道免受炎症性损伤。 Tregreg​ cells / IL-10 inhibit TH17H17​-mediated inflammatory responses, protecting the gut from inflammatory damage.
7
THH​细胞与Tregreg​细胞间的动态平衡决定肠道炎症反应是被激活还是被抑制。 The dynamic balance between THH​ and Tregreg​ cells determines whether intestinal inflammation is activated or suppressed.
8
共生菌B. fragilis通过诱导Tregreg​细胞和IL-10产生，有效对抗致病菌（如H. hepaticus）引发的肠道炎症。 The commensal B. fragilis, by inducing Tregreg​ cells and IL-10, effectively counteracts intestinal inflammation triggered by pathogens such as H. hepaticus.

结论和精要（图解内容）：

共生菌（如B. fragilis）通过多糖A诱导免疫调节反应，促进Tregreg​/IL-10产生，抑制炎症；而致病菌（如H. hepaticus）诱导TH17H17​及炎症反应。二者平衡决定炎症水平。

Commensals (like B. fragilis) induce regulatory immune responses via PSA, promoting Tregreg​/IL-10 production and suppressing inflammation; while pathogens (like H. hepaticus) stimulate TH17H17​ and inflammatory responses. The balance between these determines the level of inflammation.

炎症性肠病中的免疫应答 Immune Response in Inflammatory Bowel Disease

1
概念与疾病类型

Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD)是一组慢性炎症性疾病，主要影响消化道，常见类型包括克罗恩病（Crohn's disease）和溃疡性结肠炎（ulcerative colitis）。

Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD) is a group of chronic inflammatory diseases mainly affecting the digestive tract, commonly including Crohn's disease and ulcerative colitis.

2
不同病理表现

正常结肠粘膜结构完整，无溃疡或明显增厚。 Normal colon shows a complete mucosal structure, without ulceration or obvious thickening.

溃疡性结肠炎
炎症主要局限于结肠粘膜和粘膜下层，常为连续性分布，表面有溃疡。 Ulcerative colitis: Inflammation mainly limited to the colon mucosa and submucosa, usually distributed continuously, with ulcerative surfaces.
克罗恩病
可累及全消化道，常见于回肠和结肠，炎症呈节段性/斑片状，全层受累，肠壁变厚，粘膜呈鹅卵石样改变，可形成瘘管。 Crohn’s disease: Can affect the entire digestive tract, often ileum and colon, with patchy/segmental, transmural inflammation, wall thickening, cobblestone mucosa, and possible fistula formation.
3
免疫应答机制的对比

正常肠道：

有完整的黏液层，阻隔细菌接触上皮； A complete mucus layer blocks bacterial contact with epithelium.

居民免疫细胞维持平衡，主要有调节因子TGF-β、IL-10抑制炎症。 Resident immune cells maintain balance, with regulatory factors like TGF-β and IL-10 suppressing inflammation.

IBD中（溃疡性结肠炎/克罗恩病）：

黏液层损伤，上皮屏障通透性↑，细菌易进入固有层。 Mucus layer is damaged, epithelial permeability increases, bacteria easily enter the lamina propria.

免疫应答失调，促炎因子（TNFα、IL-12、IL-23）↑，调节型T细胞功能↓。 Immune response is dysregulated, pro-inflammatory cytokines (TNFα, IL-12, IL-23) increase, regulatory T cell function decreases.

各类免疫细胞（如中性粒细胞、巨噬细胞）大量募集，启动持续性炎症循环，导致更多的趋化因子和炎症因子释放，形成炎症放大、损伤加重。 Various immune cells (such as neutrophils and macrophages) are massively recruited, initiating a persistent inflammatory cycle, with more chemokines and cytokines released, amplifying inflammation and worsening tissue damage.

4
小结

溃疡性结肠炎以连续性粘膜浅层炎症和溃疡为主，克罗恩病以斑片、全层、深部结构破坏为主。 Ulcerative colitis is characterized by continuous, superficial mucosal inflammation/ulceration; Crohn’s disease by patchy, transmural deep tissue injury.

两者均有屏障缺损、过度炎症细胞募集及促炎因子上调等免疫失衡特征。 Both share barrier defects, excessive inflammatory cell recruitment, and upregulation of pro-inflammatory cytokines as immune imbalance features.

保护性细菌在炎症性肠病中的作用 Protective bacteria in IBD

1
某些肠道细菌菌株可通过多种机制抑制炎症性肠病（IBD）的发生和发展。

Some gut bacterial strains can suppress the occurrence and development of inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) through various mechanisms.

2
Bacteroidetes门和乳酸杆菌属（Lactobacillus）为主的细菌具有抗炎作用↑，而厚壁菌门（Firmicutes）则倾向于促进炎症↑。

Bacteroidetes and Lactobacillus (genus) bacteria are mainly anti-inflammatory (↑), while Firmicutes tend to be pro-inflammatory (↑).

3
常见的保护性肠道菌株及其机制如下：

Common protective gut bacterial strains and their mechanisms are as follows:

VSLA3（多种乳酸杆菌和双歧杆菌混合菌）能促进IL-10和TGFβ表达T细胞的诱导，抑制炎症。 VSLA3 (mixture of Lactobacillus and Bifidobacterium) induces IL-10 and TGFβ-expressing T cells, suppressing inflammation.

Bifidobacteria lactis、Bifidobacteria infantis等双歧杆菌能降低结肠TNF和iNOS表达，促进Treg细胞生成，抑制NF-κB通路。 Bifidobacteria lactis, Bifidobacteria infantis, etc. decrease colonic TNF and iNOS, promote Treg cells, and inhibit NF-κB pathway.

Escherichia coli Nissle 1917可通过TLR2和TLR4途径，诱导Treg细胞减少结肠黏膜炎症。 Escherichia coli Nissle 1917 induces Treg cells via TLR2/TLR4 pathways, reducing colonic mucosal inflammation.

Lactobacillus rhamnosus GG等乳酸杆菌能诱导Treg细胞，降低炎症。 Lactobacillus rhamnosus GG and other Lactobacilli induce Treg cells and lower inflammation.

Lactobacillus salivarius、L. reuteri、L. plantarum等有助于降低促炎细胞因子（如IL-8、TNF）的表达，减轻肠道炎症。 Lactobacillus salivarius, L. reuteri, L. plantarum, etc. help decrease pro-inflammatory cytokines (such as IL-8, TNF), reducing gut inflammation.

Bacteroides fragilis通过诱导CD4+ T细胞来源IL-10提升抗炎能力。 Bacteroides fragilis enhances anti-inflammatory capacity by inducing CD4+ T cell-derived IL-10.

4
保护性细菌通过上调抗炎因子（如IL-10、TGFβ）和调节性T细胞（Treg）来实现其作用。

Protective bacteria exert their effects mainly by upregulating anti-inflammatory factors (such as IL-10, TGFβ) and regulatory T cells (Treg).

5
但厚壁菌门（Firmicutes）成员多促进促炎反应，在IBD中常见其数量上升并伴随疾病进展。

However, members of the Firmicutes phylum mostly promote pro-inflammatory responses, with their abundance often increasing and accompanying disease progression in IBD.

老师讲解补充：

Teacher’s explanation supplement:

老师强调，“Bacteroidetes + Lactobacillus = 抗炎，Firmicutes = 促炎”。这种平衡决定肠道的炎症状态。 The teacher emphasized: “Bacteroidetes + Lactobacillus = anti-inflammatory, Firmicutes = pro-inflammatory.” This balance determines the inflammatory state of the gut.

保护性细菌主要通过调节免疫环境、诱导Treg与抗炎因子，抑制IBD，改善肠道微生态。 Protective bacteria mainly modulate the immune environment, induce Treg and anti-inflammatory cytokines, suppress IBD, and improve gut microecology.

炎症性肠病的免疫治疗 Immunotherapy for IBD disorder

1
炎症性肠病（IBD）患者肠道屏障功能障碍，包括微生物贴壁增加、抗原捕获、黏液层减少、Paneth细胞功能异常以及紧密连接紊乱。 Intestinal barrier dysfunction in IBD patients includes increased microbial adherence, antigen capture, reduced mucus layer, dysfunctional Paneth cells, and dysregulation of tight junctions.
2.这些障碍导致抗原更容易进入固有层，激活树突状细胞　，DC迁移至PeYer氏结，进而激活巨噬细胞。 These defects allow antigens to enter the lamina propria more easily, activating dendritic cells (DCs), which migrate to Peyer’s patches and then activate macrophages.
答案 CD
3
激活的巨噬细胞产生促炎因子（TNF, IL-23, IL-12, IL-6），进而诱导Th1、Th17细胞分化。 Activated macrophages produce pro-inflammatory cytokines (TNF, IL-23, IL-12, IL-6), promoting Th1 and Th17 cell differentiation.
4
Th1、Th17细胞在肠系膜淋巴结中进一步被激活，并释放致炎性细胞因子（IFN-γ, IL-17A, IL-17F, IL-22），参与炎症放大。 Th1 and Th17 cells are further activated in mesenteric lymph nodes and secrete inflammatory cytokines (IFN-γ, IL-17A, IL-17F, IL-22), amplifying inflammation.
5
ILC1、ILC3等固有淋巴样细胞也可被激活，参与炎症反应。 ILC1 and ILC3 innate lymphoid cells can also be activated, participating in the inflammatory response.
6
Treg细胞则发挥抑制作用，分泌抗炎因子，维持免疫耐受，限制炎症进程。 Treg cells exert inhibitory effects by secreting anti-inflammatory factors, maintaining immune tolerance and limiting inflammation.
治疗方面
Therapeutic approaches include:

抗TNF、PDE4抑制剂、TLR9激动剂、JAK抑制剂，阻断巨噬细胞促炎因子产生与信号传导。 Anti-TNF, PDE4 inhibitors, TLR9 agonists, and JAK inhibitors block pro-inflammatory cytokine production and signaling from macrophages.

抗IL-12p40和抗IL-23p19抗体，抑制Th1/Th17细胞分化与活化。 Anti-IL-12p40 and anti-IL-23p19 antibodies inhibit Th1/Th17 cell differentiation and activation.

S1PR调节剂（S1PR modulators），防止致炎性T细胞迁移进入肠道。 S1PR modulators prevent inflammatory T cell migration into the intestine.

α4β7、αEβ7、β7、MAdCAM-1单抗，阻止T细胞从循环中向肠道黏膜迁移。 α4β7, αEβ7, β7, and MAdCAM-1 antibodies prevent T cells from trafficking from the circulation to the intestinal mucosa.

8
免疫治疗的核心是多环节干预炎症信号，恢复免疫平衡，减轻肠道损伤。 The core of immunotherapy is multi-step intervention in inflammatory signaling, restoring immune balance and alleviating intestinal damage.
老师补充讲解
Teacher’s explanation supplement:

老师强调上述药物可针对不同的免疫环节精确阻断，有的解除了上游巨噬细胞炎症，有的直接防止淋巴细胞进入肠道，“是精准化综合治疗的体现”。 The teacher emphasized that these drugs can precisely block different immune nodes: some dampen upstream macrophage inflammation, while others directly prevent lymphocyte gut entry, representing precision and comprehensive therapy.

只有综合考虑免疫细胞激活、促炎因子释放与细胞迁移，才能在临床取得理想治疗效果。 Only by comprehensively addressing immune cell activation, pro-inflammatory cytokine release, and cell migration can optimal clinical outcomes in IBD be achieved.

JAK/STAT信号通路调控多种细胞因子受体下游信号传导及其与免疫应答和人类疾病突变的关系

JAK/STAT controls multiple cytokine receptor downstream signaling, immune response, and mutation-related human diseases

1
JAK/STAT信号通路的激活及负调控机制

1.1 经典激活过程

细胞因子与受体结合，诱导受体构象变化，募集相关JAKs。 Cytokines bind to their receptors, inducing conformational change and recruitment of JAKs.

JAKs进行自身磷酸化及受体酪氨酸残基磷酸化，形成STATs结合位点。 JAKs undergo autophosphorylation and phosphorylate tyrosine residues on receptors, creating docking sites for STATs.

STAT蛋白被磷酸化后解离，作为二聚体进入细胞核，结合靶基因调控细胞因子相关基因转录。 STATs are phosphorylated, then dissociate as dimers, enter the nucleus, and regulate cytokine-related gene transcription2.

1.2 负向调控机制

CIS/SOCS家族、PIAS家族和PTP家族对信号通路发挥负调节作用。 The CIS/SOCS, PIAS, and PTP families negatively regulate the pathway.

CIS/SOCS直接结合JAK或受体，抑制酶活并阻断STAT结合，或促进JAKs/STATs降解。 CIS/SOCS bind JAKs or their receptors to inhibit kinase activity, intercept STAT-receptor binding, or promote JAK/STAT degradation.

PIAS家族通过阻断STAT二聚体与DNA结合，抑制JAK-STAT信号转导。 The PIAS family blocks STAT dimers binding to DNA and inhibits signal transduction.

PTP家族去磷酸化受体、JAKs和STATs，实现下调调控。 The PTP family dephosphorylates receptors, JAKs, and STATs for downregulation2.

2
JAK/STAT与免疫细胞分化及效应

2.1 信号通路作用于免疫细胞分化

IFN和IL-12促进Th1细胞分化，分别经STAT1及STAT4驱动T-bet表达。 IFN and IL-12 promote Th1 differentiation via STAT1 and STAT4 to drive T-bet gene expression.

IL-4上调GATA3，STAT6介导Th2细胞分化。 IL-4 induces GATA3 via STAT6 for Th2 cell differentiation.

IL-6和TGFβ是Th17分化必需，STAT3上调RORγt表达。 IL-6 and TGFβ are essential for Th17 differentiation, and STAT3 upregulates RORγt.

IL-6、IL-12通过STAT3调控Tfh细胞的Bcl-6表达，促进Tfh分化。 IL-6 and IL-12 use STAT3 to upregulate Bcl-6 for T follicular helper cell differentiation.

IL-2通过STAT5A/B与Foxp3基因联合诱导Treg细胞分化。 IL-2 induces Treg differentiation via STAT5A/B and Foxp32.

2.2 录音补充讲解

JAK-STAT通路是许多细胞因子的共同信号平台，决定免疫应答类型与强度，通过调节STAT家族成员让T细胞分化出不同亚型（如Th1, Th2, Th17, Tfh, Treg），各分化环节有专门配对的STAT分子2。 The JAK-STAT pathway is a common signal cascade for many cytokines, determines immune response type and intensity, and uses different STATs to drive subtype differentiation (Th1/Th2/Th17/Tfh/Treg), with specific STATs at different steps2.

3
JAK/STAT突变与人类疾病

3.1 相关疾病类型及分子靶点

JAK1/JAK2/JAK3/TYK等多种突变可导致造血、免疫缺陷、自身免疫、炎症等病理，包括MPNs、AML、HCC、淋巴瘤、白血病等。 Mutations in JAK1, JAK2, JAK3, TYK lead to hematopoiesis, immunodeficiency, autoimmunity, inflammation, including MPNs, AML, HCC, lymphoma, leukemia, etc.

关键病因性突变主要位于SH2结构域（JAKs/STATs），影响其对磷酸酪氨酸的结合、信号转导与蛋白互作。 Disease-causing mutations frequently occur in the SH2 domain of both JAKs and STATs, altering phosphotyrosine binding and signal transmission2.

3.2 SH2结构域功能

SH2结构域负责识别磷酸酪氨酸，调控蛋白互作，是信号级联的模块化“对接”中心。 SH2 domains recognize phosphotyrosine, mediate protein-protein interactions, and are modular docking centers for signal cascades.

乳生生成的免疫学 Immunology of lactogenesis

1
乳腺上皮内淋巴细胞（mIELs）在妊娠期定植于乳腺。 Mammary intraepithelial lymphocytes (mIELs) populate mammary glands during pregnancy.
2
mIELs与小肠CD8αα⁺上皮内淋巴细胞（IELs）来源于相同的胸腺前体。 mIELs originate from the same thymic precursors as intestinal CD8αα⁺ IELs.
3
mIELs有助于最佳乳生生成和新生儿生长。 mIELs contribute to optimal lactogenesis and neonatal growth.
4
人母乳中含有具有mIEL特征的T细胞。 Human breast milk contains T cells with a mIEL-like signature.
5
mIELs依赖上皮来源的IL-15进行分化和积累，这与小肠中的机制类似。 mIELs depend on epithelial-derived IL-15 for their differentiation and accumulation, like in the small intestine.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s supplementary explanation

老师强调，mIELs的发育与胸腺和上皮的相互作用密切相关，IL-15和T-bet信号在此过程中关键。 The teacher emphasized that the development of mIELs is closely related to thymic and epithelial interactions, with IL-15 and T-bet signals being critical.

mIELs不仅通过调节乳腺上皮细胞促进乳汁合成，还能影响肌上皮细胞，促进乳汁排出和新生儿生长。 mIELs not only promote milk synthesis by regulating mammary epithelial cells, but also influence myoepithelial cells to enhance milk ejection and neonatal growth.

肝脏组织结构与功能 Structure of Liver Histology and function

1
人类肝脏通常被认为是“非免疫器官”，主要参与代谢、营养储存和解毒等过程。 The human liver is often viewed as a "non-immunological organ" primarily involved in metabolism, nutrient storage, and detoxification processes.
2
然而，健康的肝脏其实是一个复杂的免疫活性场所，由多种免疫细胞和非造血细胞共同参与。 Nevertheless, a healthy liver serves as a site of intricate immunological activity, facilitated by various immune cell types alongside non-hematopoietic cells.
3
肝脏在生理上连接肠道和体循环介导的分子，是体内重要的分子枢纽。 It acts as a physiological link between molecules derived from the gut and systemic circulation.
4
肝门静脉系统为肝脏提供80%的血流量，这是一套低压系统，携带了大量肠道细菌产物和其他外来物质，为免疫应答提供刺激。 The portal venous system supplies 80% of the liver’s blood flow, which carries significant amounts of bacterial products and other foreign substances introduced to the immune system.
5
肝实质细胞（肝细胞）除代谢外，还承担重要免疫学功能，包括： Hepatocytes perform important immunological roles in addition to metabolic functions, including:

合成分泌血浆蛋白，如凝血因子、补体及抗微生物蛋白。 Production of plasma proteins such as clotting factors, complement, and anti-microbial proteins.

局部或全身感染时，产生急性反应蛋白。 Production of acute phase proteins upon local and systemic infection.

在肝内进行抗原递呈，促进T细胞的激活。 Antigen presentation to T cells within the liver.

6
【老师补充】 [Teacher’s Supplement]

老师讲解指出，虽然肝脏长期被认为是代谢和解毒器官，但研究已证实肝细胞与多种免疫细胞（如库普弗细胞、树突状细胞、NK细胞等）协同，实现耐受和防御的双重功能，是免疫耐受与炎症调控的关键枢纽。 The teacher emphasized that although the liver was historically seen as primarily metabolic and detoxifying, research has shown that hepatocytes collaborate with immune cells (Kupffer cells, dendritic cells, NK cells, etc.) to achieve both tolerance and defense, making the liver a central hub for immune tolerance and inflammation regulation20.

肝脏对从肠道输入的细菌产物和抗原进行识别，并在局部决定耐受或激发免疫反应，关系到全身免疫稳态。 The liver recognizes bacterial products and antigens from the gut, and locally decides whether to induce tolerance or immunological activation, thus maintaining systemic immune homeostasis20.

结构与功能
免疫性肝脏 Structure of Immunological Liver
1
肝脏含丰富免疫细胞 Extensive repertoire of immune cells

肝脏嵌有丰富的免疫细胞，分布于肝窦的网状结构中，肝窦是静脉血与富氧动脉血混合流经的地方。

The liver harbors a rich repertoire of immune cells embedded in a meshwork of liver sinusoids, where hepatic venous blood mixes with oxygen-rich arterial blood.

2
肝窦周围环境与分子交换 Sinusoidal microenvironment & molecular exchange

肝窦周围有特殊的肝窦内皮细胞（LSECs），其表面有大量窗孔，有助于分子向邻近肝细胞的高效扩散，这对肝脏的代谢功能至关重要。

Specialized liver sinusoidal endothelial cells (LSECs) with numerous fenestrations surround the sinusoids, promoting efficient diffusion of molecules into hepatocytes, crucial for metabolic processing.

这种结构安排促进了血源分子的交换与代谢过程，同时便于肝脏局部与全身免疫细胞的高效互作。

This arrangement enables efficient exchange of blood-derived molecules and metabolic processing, and allows significant interactions with both local and systemic immune cells.

3
免疫细胞的定位与Dissé间隙 Immune cell localization and Space of Dissé

肝内免疫细胞主要位于肝窦内及窦下区，被称为Dissé间隙，位于肝细胞与LSEC之间。

Liver immune cells are found within sinusoids and the subendothelial area (space of Dissé), situated between hepatocytes and LSECs.

4
Kupffer细胞（KCs）与髓系特异细胞 Kupffer cells & liver-specific myeloid cells

Kupffer细胞（肝巨噬细胞）占全部固有组织巨噬细胞的80-90%，也是肝脏非实质细胞的近三分之一，是肝内最重要的免疫细胞群体。

Kupffer cells (KCs) constitute 80-90% of all fixed tissue macrophages and nearly one-third of the liver's nonparenchymal cells, making them essential.

肝脏特有髓系细胞能在血流中识别和清除入侵微生物。

Myeloid cells specific to the liver identify pathogens in the bloodstream and eliminate invading microorganisms.

5
Kupffer细胞的功能 Function of Kupffer cells

Kupffer细胞与巨噬细胞类似，具有吞噬活性。其不同于其他髓系细胞，既能分泌促炎又能分泌抗炎因子，兼有诱导耐受和炎症反应的双重作用。

KCs have phagocytic activity like macrophages, but unlike other myeloid cells, they secrete both proinflammatory and anti-inflammatory substances, underlying their dual function in mediating tolerogenic and inflammatory responses.

6
肝星状细胞的作用 Role of hepatic stellate cells

肝星状细胞参与肝脏发育、再生、免疫调节、血管生成及维生素A（视黄醇）储存等。

Hepatic stellate cells play a role in liver development, regeneration, immune functions, angiogenesis, and storage of vitamin A (retinol).

7
重点总结 Key points from teacher

老师强调，肝脏并非单纯代谢器官，其丰富的免疫细胞网络支持了肝脏在全身免疫稳态、耐受诱导及抗感染中的独特作用。

The teacher emphasizes that the liver is not just a metabolic organ; its rich immune cell network supports unique roles in immune homeostasis, tolerance induction, and anti-infection defense.

调控全身稳态的肝脏

Regulation of systemic homeostasis by the Liver

1
正常生理状态下肝脏的功能

The function of the liver under normal physiology

肝细胞合成和分泌脂蛋白、白蛋白、凝血因子和补体蛋白，调节体内代谢和免疫稳态。 Hepatocytes synthesize and secrete lipoproteins, albumin, clotting factors, and complement proteins, thereby regulating metabolic and immune homeostasis.

2
急性期反应中的肝脏角色

Liver response during acute phase reaction

感染发生时，炎症因子（如IL-6）促使肝细胞产生急性期蛋白，包括补体、抗菌蛋白、铁结合蛋白、凝血因子和炎症相关蛋白。 Upon infection, inflammatory cytokines (like IL-6) stimulate hepatocytes to produce acute phase proteins including complement, antimicrobial proteins, iron-binding proteins, clotting factors, and inflammatory proteins.

急性期蛋白通过增强吞噬作用、杀菌、维持铁稳态、促进凝血等方式参与机体免疫防御。 Acute phase proteins participate in immune defense by enhancing phagocytosis, bacterial killing, maintaining iron homeostasis, and promoting coagulation.

肝脏调控白细胞增多反应、体温变化等全身性应激反应。 The liver also regulates systemic responses such as leukocytosis and temperature change.

3
急性损伤与慢性损伤的肝脏应答机制

Liver response to acute and chronic injury

急性损伤、毒素或病原体可触发初步的炎症反应，激活肝星状细胞（HSCs）和肝窦内皮细胞（LSECs），破坏肝脏稳态。 Acute injury, toxins, or pathogens can initiate an inflammatory response, activating hepatic stellate cells (HSCs) and liver sinusoidal endothelial cells (LSECs), thereby disrupting liver homeostasis.

微生物相关分子模式（MAMPs）和损伤相关分子模式（DAMPs）信号激活炎症通路，促进白细胞募集至损伤部位。 Microbe-associated molecular patterns (MAMPs) and damage-associated molecular patterns (DAMPs) activate inflammatory pathways and promote leukocyte recruitment to the site of injury.

4
纤维化和修复过程

Fibrosis and repair process

长期持续炎症下，肝星状细胞分化为肌成纤维细胞，分泌大量细胞外基质（ECM），导致肝纤维化甚至肝硬化。 With persistent inflammation, hepatic stellate cells differentiate into myofibroblasts, secreting large amounts of extracellular matrix (ECM), resulting in liver fibrosis and potentially cirrhosis.

肝组织在炎症与纤维化间动态调节，组织修复和纤维降解也在持续进行。 The liver dynamically balances tissue remodeling and fibrosis, where repair and matrix degradation occur simultaneously.

5
急性期蛋白功能一览

Summary of acute phase proteins functions

类别代表免疫功能
补体C3, C4, C9促进吞噬、趋化、细胞裂解
铁结合蛋白血影蛋白等降低游离铁，抗菌作用
抗菌蛋白肝源抗菌肽等抑菌、杀菌
凝血因子纤维蛋白原等促进凝血
炎症蛋白IL-6等促进炎症信号
其他C反应蛋白、抑制剂等补体激活、抗炎控制

这些蛋白共同调控免疫炎症反应，影响全身及局部稳态。 These proteins coordinately regulate immune and inflammatory responses, affecting both systemic and local homeostasis.

6
老师补充与要点强调

Teacher’s Key Points

老师强调，肝脏不仅仅是代谢器官，更是调控全身免疫稳态的重要枢纽，通过产生多种急性期蛋白、调节免疫细胞迁移及组织修复实现其防御与耐受功能。 The teacher emphasized that the liver is more than a metabolic organ; it is a key regulator of systemic immune homeostasis, achieving defense and tolerance by producing acute phase proteins, guiding immune cell migration, and mediating tissue repair.

慢性炎症过程如进展不被控制，将导致肝纤维化甚至功能衰竭。 If chronic inflammation is uncontrolled, it leads to liver fibrosis and eventual organ failure.

系统稳态失调导致的肝脏疾病 = NASH（非酒精性脂肪性肝炎）

Dysregulation of systemic homeostasis of the Liver = NASH (Non-alcoholic Steatohepatitis)

听课笔记 Lecture Notes

1
NASH发病机制与免疫异常 Pathogenic Mechanism and Immune Dysregulation in NASH

NASH发病机制涉及多种免疫细胞类型的相互作用。 The pathogenic immune mechanisms of NASH involve interactions among various immune cell types.

脂肪酸升高和脂毒性↑会导致肝细胞损伤，并促使损伤相关分子模式（DAMPs）释放，加剧炎症反应。 Elevated fatty acids and lipotoxicity↑ directly damage hepatocytes and trigger the release of damage-associated molecular patterns (DAMPs), amplifying the inflammatory response.

损伤的肝细胞释放DAMPs，激活库普弗细胞（Kupffer cell），随后分泌大量促炎因子（如TNFα、IL-1β、CCL2）。 Damaged hepatocytes release DAMPs, which activate Kupffer cells that then secrete large amounts of proinflammatory cytokines (e.g., TNFα, IL-1β, CCL2) 19.

单核细胞与中性粒细胞在Kupffer细胞激活下招募到肝脏，产生ROS和促纤维化信号，损伤进一步加重。 Monocytes and neutrophils are recruited to the liver under activation by Kupffer cells, producing ROS and profibrotic signals, which worsen the injury 1920.

树突状细胞　和自然杀伤细胞（NK细胞）也被激活，参与进一步炎症级联反应和肝星状细胞活化，导致肝纤维化。 Dendritic cells (DCs) and natural killer (NK) cells are also activated, participating in further inflammatory cascades and hepatic stellate cell activation, resulting in fibrosis.
答案 CD

细胞毒性T细胞（如CD8⁺T细胞）释放IFNγ和TNFα，延续和加剧炎症，进一步刺激肝细胞和星状细胞。 Cytotoxic T cells (such as CD8⁺ T cells) release IFNγ and TNFα, perpetuating and aggravating inflammation, further stimulating hepatocytes and stellate cells 20.

结果是慢性炎症-损伤-修复的恶性循环，推动脂肪肝进展为NASH，最后发展为肝纤维化。 This results in a vicious cycle of chronic inflammation-damage-repair, driving progression from steatosis to NASH, eventually advancing to liver fibrosis 1920.

2
免疫治疗策略（在研） Immunotherapeutic Strategies (Under Investigation)

口服抗CD3单抗可诱导CD4⁺延迟相关肽（LAP）阳性调节性T细胞（Treg），并↑TGF-β水平，实现免疫调控。 Oral anti-CD3 monoclonal antibody induces CD4⁺ latency-associated peptide (LAP)-positive regulatory T cells (Tregs) and increases TGF-β, achieving immune modulation.

牛初乳（IMM-124E）用于NAFLD，其含有针对细菌LPS的IgG抗体，阻断TLR4信号通路，减少炎症因子释放。 Bovine colostrum (IMM-124E) for NAFLD contains IgG antibody against bacterial LPS to block TLR4 signaling and inhibit cytokine release.

Cenicriviroc是一种阻断C-C趋化因子受体2/5的抑制剂，用于NASH及肝纤维化，通过减少炎症细胞募集减轻疾病进展。 Cenicriviroc, a blocker of C-C chemokine receptors type 2 and 5, targets NASH and liver fibrosis by reducing recruitment of inflammatory cells.

低温体外灌注技术应用于肝移植的缺血再灌注损伤，通过滤除炎症因子（TNFα、IL-6、IL-8、内皮素1）来降低免疫损害。 Hypothermic ex vivo machine perfusion, used in ischemia-reperfusion injury during liver transplantation, reduces immune damage by filtering proinflammatory cytokines (TNFα, IL-6, IL-8, endothelin-1).

3
课堂强调要点 Highlights from Lecture
老师强调
NASH是多细胞、多信号通路交互导致的免疫稳态失调与慢性炎症性疾病，免疫细胞活化、炎症因子升高、DAMPs释放和纤维化是进展关键。 Teacher’s highlight: NASH results from multicellular, multifactorial immune dysregulation and chronic inflammation; immune cell activation, inflammatory cytokine upregulation, DAMPs release, and fibrogenesis are the key processes 1920.

研究型免疫疗法多靶向阻断炎症放大环节、诱导免疫耐受、干预纤维化信号通路。 Experimental immunotherapies primarily target inhibition of inflammatory amplification, induction of immune tolerance, and blocking profibrotic signaling.

I- 肠-肝互作
屏障与解剖连接 I- Cross-talk Gut-Liver: Barriers and Anatomical connections
I- 肠-肝互作
免疫细胞、肝脏和肠道菌群的功能交互 I- Cross-talk Gut-Liver: Functional interactions between immune cells, liver and gut microbiota
I- 肠-肝互作
核心总结 I- Cross-talk Gut-Liver: Take home message
1
肠-肝屏障与解剖连接 Gut-liver axis barriers and anatomical connections

肠道微生物通过肠道上皮屏障与全身循环隔离。 The intestinal microbiota is separated from the systemic circulation by the intestinal epithelial barrier.

近端小肠通过pH值、酶和胆汁抑制细菌定植。 In the proximal small intestine, pH, host enzymes, and bile suppress microbial colonization.

分泌型IgA及抗菌肽（AMPs）为化学屏障，抑制细菌生长。 Secretory IgA (sIgA) and antimicrobial peptides (AMPs) act as chemical barriers to limit bacterial growth.

粘液层（由杯状细胞产生）是首要物理屏障，隔开微生物与上皮细胞。 The mucus layer (produced by goblet cells) is the initial physical barrier separating microbes and epithelial cells.

单层柱状上皮细胞通过紧密连接进一步防止微生物入侵。 A single columnar epithelial cell layer joined by tight junctions blocks further microbial invasion.

上皮下丰富的单核吞噬细胞（如巨噬细胞、树突状细胞）清除入侵微生物。 Below the epithelium, abundant mononuclear phagocytes (macrophages and dendritic cells) remove invading microorganisms.

肠道内皮细胞形成血管屏障，阻止大分子与细胞进入循环。 Endothelial cells form a gut-vascular barrier, preventing large molecules and cells from entering systemic circulation.

肠道引流淋巴液流入肠系膜淋巴结，加强防卫，限制细菌扩散。 Gut-derived lymph flows to mesenteric lymph nodes, adding an extra firewall against microbial spread.

肠道血液最终入肝，窦内Kupffer细胞与LSEC进一步免疫过滤，限制肠源物质进入系统循环。 Intestinal blood enters the liver, where Kupffer cells and liver sinusoidal endothelial cells (LSEC) serve as immune barriers, restricting entry of gut-derived material into the systemic circulation.

2
免疫细胞、肝脏和肠道菌群的功能交互 Functional interactions between immune cells, liver and gut microbiota

sIgA通过结合肠道细菌调节微生态平衡。 sIgA maintains microbial balance by binding and neutralizing gut bacteria.

胆汁酸、抗菌肽、短链脂肪酸等微生物及膳食产物调控肠道屏障和免疫。 Bile acids, antimicrobial peptides, short-chain fatty acids, and other microbial or dietary products modulate gut barrier and immunity.

微生物代谢产物和微生物相关分子模式（PAMPs）增强上皮屏障，促进上皮增殖、粘液生成和AMP分泌。 Microbial metabolites and pathogen-associated molecular patterns (PAMPs) strengthen epithelial barriers, foster proliferation, mucus, and AMP secretion.

微生物代谢物可提高肠道巨噬细胞和DC的耐受性，促进调节性T细胞（Treg）和Th17细胞平衡。 Microbial metabolites enhance the tolerogenic properties of gut macrophages and dendritic cells, promoting the FOXP3⁺ Treg/Th17 cell balance.

IL-22等细胞因子维持上皮屏障和调控免疫稳态。 Cytokines such as IL-22 help maintain the epithelial barrier and regulate immune homeostasis.

肠道DC能摄取抗原，经淋巴管递送至肠系膜淋巴结，诱导Treg定向分化。 Gut dendritic cells can capture antigens and transport them via lymphatics to mesenteric lymph nodes to induce gut-homing Tregs.

肠源代谢物和PAMPs可通过门静脉影响肝脏，改变Kupffer细胞和T细胞的表型和功能。 Gut metabolites and PAMPs reach the liver through the portal vein, affecting the phenotype and function of hepatic Kupffer cells and T cells.

肝脏NKT细胞、T细胞、Kupffer细胞、LSEC等参与调节来自肠道的信号，维持全身免疫稳态。 Hepatic NKT cells, T cells, Kupffer cells, and LSECs respond to gut-derived signals and maintain systemic immune homeostasis.

3
总结要点 Take home message

肠肝轴依靠物理、化学和免疫性屏障，限制微生物及成分向全身扩散。 The gut-liver axis relies on physical, chemical, and immune barriers to limit the systemic dissemination of microbes and their components.

肝脏通过血液途径主动限制肠源物质扩散，肠道引流淋巴结则限制淋巴源性传播并诱导免疫耐受。 The liver limits dissemination of gut material via blood, while gut-draining lymph nodes restrict lymphogenic spread and induce immune tolerance.

微生物及饮食代谢物和免疫系统共同调控肠肝轴的屏障功能，健康状态下维持稳态平衡。 Microbial and dietary metabolites, together with the immune system, fine-tune the gut-liver barriers to maintain homeostatic balance in the healthy gut and liver.

I-心脏-肠道互作 I- Cross-talk Heart-Gut

1
心脏与肠道的双向交流机制（Cross-talk mechanisms between heart and gut）

心脏和肠道之间存在复杂的双向交流，调控机理尚未完全揭示。 There is a complex bidirectional communication between the heart and the gut, but the precise regulatory mechanisms remain unclear.

肠道微生物、它们产生的代谢物（LPS/内毒素、胆汁酸、短链脂肪酸SCFAs、三甲胺TMA）可通过免疫细胞和分子信号影响心脏健康。 Gut microbiota and their metabolites (LPS/endotoxin, bile acids, short-chain fatty acids, trimethylamine) can affect cardiac health via immune cells and molecular signaling.

包括嗜酸性粒细胞、中性粒细胞、单核细胞、嗜碱性粒细胞等免疫细胞可能参与该过程。 Immune cells such as eosinophils, neutrophils, monocytes, and basophils are likely involved in this cross-talk.

信号通过免疫检查点（如CTLA-4、CD28）等分子传递，调节免疫反应。 Immune checkpoints (such as CTLA-4, CD28) mediate signaling and regulate immune responses.

2
肠道共生菌来源多肽可模拟心脏抗原（Peptides derived from commensal bacteria can mimic cardiac antigen）

SPF环境下的TCRM小鼠自发发生自身免疫性心肌炎，并约50%进展为致死性心肌病。 All TCRM mice under SPF (specific pathogen-free) conditions spontaneously developed autoimmune myocarditis, and about 50% progressed to lethal cardiomyopathy.

相反，无菌（GF）环境去除共生菌显著减轻了疾病严重度，消除了致死后果、心脏扩张和纤维化。 In contrast, removal of commensal microbes in germ-free (GF) conditions substantially reduced disease severity, abolished lethal outcomes, cardiac dilatation, and cardiac fibrosis.

微生物群被动转移（如同居）会恢复上述心脏损伤等表型，进一步证明微生物群的关键作用。 Passive transfer of the microbiome (e.g., co-housing) restored the cardiac injury phenotypes described above, confirming the key role of the microbiota.

数据表明肠道共生菌产生的抗原肽可诱导外周自身反应，模拟心脏特异性抗原，引起心脏自身免疫炎症和功能障碍。 The data suggest that antigenic peptides derived from commensal microbes can trigger peripheral autoimmunity by mimicking heart-specific antigens, leading to cardiac autoimmune inflammation and dysfunction.

宏分子机制
分子模拟导致心脏特异性Th17免疫应答及心肌病（Molecular mechanism: molecular mimicry leads to cardiac-specific Th17 response and cardiomyopathy）

MYH-6特异性CD4⁺T细胞表现出交叉反应，可被共生菌Bacteroides的β-半乳糖苷酶等抗原激活。 MYH-6-specific CD4⁺ T cells show cross-reactivity and can be activated by antigens such as β-galactosidase from commensal Bacteroides.

无菌TCRM小鼠单菌定植Bacteroides（缺失或表达β-半乳糖苷酶）证实该细菌酶参与心肌自身反应性T细胞激活。 Colonizing germ-free TCRM mice with Bacteroides (either lacking or expressing β-galactosidase) confirmed that this bacterial enzyme is involved in activating self-reactive cardiac T cells.

肠道定植改变免疫表型，增加心脏、结肠内IFNγ⁺/IL-17⁺细胞比例及IgA响应。 Gut colonization altered the immune phenotype, increasing the proportion of IFNγ⁺/IL-17⁺ cells and IgA responses in heart and colon.

4
分子模拟机制印记抗心肌Th17反应，导致扩张型心肌病（Molecular mimicry imprints anti-myosin Th17 response, leading to dilated cardiomyopathy）

分子模拟机制使肠道起源的Th17反应针对心肌抗原（如肌球蛋白链6），促进心脏炎症和组织纤维化，最终导致扩张性心肌病（DCM）。 Molecular mimicry allows gut-derived Th17 response to target cardiac antigens (such as myosin heavy chain 6), promoting cardiac inflammation, fibrosis, and leading to dilated cardiomyopathy (DCM).

这一机制强调了肠道微生物和心脏疾病间的重要免疫学联系。 This mechanism highlights the important immunological connection between the gut microbiota and cardiac disease.

022025-10-14

Cardiomyocyte心肌细胞 | Cardiac muscle cell心脏肌肉细胞

1
心肌细胞是心脏的主要功能细胞。 Cardiomyocytes are the main functional cells of the heart.
2
心肌细胞约占全部心脏细胞的30%。 Cardiomyocytes account for about 30% of all heart cells.
形态学特征包括
分支结构，直径约10-15微米，长度可达100微米。 Morphological features include: branching structure, a diameter of approximately 10-15 μm, and a length up to 100 μm.
4
心肌细胞之间通过闰盘（intercalated discs）相连，保证了细胞间的联系与协作。 Cardiomyocytes are connected by intercalated discs, which ensure communication and cooperation between cells.
5
闰盘中包含间隙连接（gap junctions）和桥粒（desmosomes），分别负责电信号传递和细胞结构稳固。 Intercalated discs contain gap junctions and desmosomes, which are responsible for electrical signal transmission and structural stability, respectively.
6
心肌细胞呈现高度有序的超微结构，适合完成心脏收缩功能。 Cardiomyocytes have a highly organized ultrastructure, which suits them for cardiac contraction.
7
心肌细胞的特化结构——分支与闰盘——对维持心脏整体同步收缩十分关键。 The specialized features of cardiomyocytes—branching and intercalated discs—are critical for synchronizing the heart’s overall contraction.
老师补充
心肌细胞体积较大，是分支状细胞，通过闰盘形成心脏收缩的“功能合胞体”，便于信号和能量的快速传递。 Additional from teacher: Cardiomyocytes are relatively large, branched cells that, through intercalated discs, form a functional syncytium for the heart’s contraction, allowing rapid signal and energy transmission.
9
细胞内高度有序的结构和致密排列决定了心肌细胞对物质运输有特殊要求，例如能量分子的扩散。 The highly ordered and densely packed intracellular structure of cardiomyocytes requires specialized mechanisms for the transport of substances, such as the diffusion of energy molecules.

Cellular architecture and contraction细胞结构与收缩 | Cellular architecture and contraction

心肌细胞具有高度有序的细胞内结构。 Cardiac muscle cells have highly organized intracellular architecture. 21

1
肌原纤维占据心肌细胞体积的50%。 Myofilaments occupy 50% of the cardiac muscle cell volume. 21
2
线粒体占心肌细胞体积的30-40%。 Mitochondria account for 30-40% of the cardiac muscle cell volume. 21
3
游离胞质只占心肌细胞体积的不到5%。 Free cytosol constitutes less than 5% of the cell volume in cardiac muscle cells. 21
4
这种高度有序的结构有利于心脏强有力且同步的收缩。 This highly organized structure enables the heart to contract powerfully and synchronously.
5
超微结构下可以看到肌原纤维与线粒体规则排列，保证收缩和能量供应的高效衔接。 Electron micrographs show regular arrangements of myofilaments and mitochondria, ensuring efficient coupling between contraction and energy supply.
6
（老师补充）由于肌原纤维和线粒体紧密排列，心肌细胞内部游离空间极少，这对能量分子如ATP的扩散提出更高要求。 (Teacher’s addition) As myofilaments and mitochondria are densely packed, the free intracellular space is minimal, which creates a significant demand for efficient diffusion of energy molecules such as ATP.
7
（老师补充）高度组织的结构同时要求特殊的物质运输机制，以满足能量和信号传递的需要。 (Teacher’s addition) This highly organized architecture also requires special transport mechanisms for substances to meet the needs for energy and signal transmission.

收缩活动与能量消耗Contractile activity and energy consumption

心肌细胞的主要能量消耗者包括
肌球蛋白ATP酶、SERCA和膜转运体。 The main energy consumers in the cardiomyocyte are: myosin ATPase, SERCA, and membrane transporters.
2
肌球蛋白ATP酶负责心肌细胞收缩过程中的能量消耗。 Myosin ATPase is responsible for the energy consumption during the contraction of cardiomyocytes.
3
SERCA（肌浆网Ca²⁺-ATP酶）将钙离子主动泵回肌浆网，维持钙离子循环，也消耗大量ATP。 SERCA (sarcoplasmic/endoplasmic reticulum Ca²⁺-ATPase) actively pumps calcium ions back into the sarcoplasmic reticulum to maintain the calcium cycle, which also consumes a large amount of ATP.
4
各类膜转运体如钠钾泵、钠钙交换器等维持离子梯度，这同样需要消耗能量。 Various membrane transporters, such as the Na⁺/K⁺ pump and Na⁺/Ca²⁺ exchanger, maintain ion gradients and also require energy.
5
心脏有极高的能量需求（约1mM ATP/秒）。 The heart has a very high energy demand (about 1 mM ATP per second).
6
心脏中能量储备有限（ATP含量约8mM，磷酸肌酸含量约15mM）。 Energy reserves in the heart are limited (ATP content about 8 mM, phosphocreatine about 15 mM).
7
因为能量储备低，心肌细胞必须持续不断地生成ATP以保证正常的收缩功能。 Due to the low energy reserves, cardiomyocytes must continuously generate ATP to maintain normal contraction.
8
（老师补充）每次心肌细胞收缩和舒张都要消耗ATP，尤其是在钙离子进入、激活收缩装置和随后泵回肌浆网的过程中。 (Teacher’s addition) Each cycle of contraction and relaxation in cardiomyocytes consumes ATP, especially during calcium entry, activation of the contractile apparatus, and subsequent reuptake by the sarcoplasmic reticulum.
9
（老师补充）如果能量供应受阻，心脏的泵血功能会立刻受影响，表现为心肌收缩力降低。 (Teacher’s addition) If energy supply is impaired, the pumping function of the heart is immediately affected, resulting in reduced contractility of the myocardium.
10
（老师补充）所以，心脏对能量供应的依赖极高，能量代谢异常是许多心脏病重要的发病基础。 (Teacher’s addition) Thus, the heart is highly dependent on energy supply, and abnormalities in energy metabolism are fundamental to many cardiac diseases.

能量底物与心肌细胞代谢 Energy substrates and metabolism in the cardiomyocyte

1
心肌可以利用多种底物作为能量来源。 The myocardium can use many kinds of substrates for energy.
2
在正常条件下，主要能量底物是脂肪酸。 Under normal conditions, fatty acids are the primary energy substrate.
3
糖类（如葡萄糖）通过糖酵解生成丙酮酸；乳酸也可被利用。 Glucose undergoes glycolysis to produce pyruvate; lactate can also be used.
4
脂肪酸通过β-氧化生成乙酰辅酶A（Acetyl-CoA）。 Fatty acids undergo β-oxidation to form acetyl-CoA.
5
乙酰辅酶A进入三羧酸（TCA）循环进一步代谢，产生活性氢载体（FADH₂、NADH）。 Acetyl-CoA enters the tricarboxylic acid (TCA) cycle for further metabolism, generating reduced carriers (FADH₂, NADH).
6
TCA循环产生的FADH₂和NADH在线粒体呼吸链中用于合成ATP。 FADH₂ and NADH produced by the TCA cycle are used in the mitochondrial respiratory chain to synthesize ATP.
7
线粒体负责心肌细胞90-95%的能量产生。 Mitochondria are responsible for producing 90-95% of the cell’s energy.
8
ADP被磷酸化生成ATP，满足心肌的高能量需求。 ADP is phosphorylated to ATP, meeting the high energy demand of the myocardium.

糖酵解Glycolysis | Energy production in the cardiomyocyte

1
糖酵解是心肌细胞的一种主要能量生成途径，尤其在缺氧情况下更为重要。 Glycolysis is a major pathway for energy production in cardiomyocytes, especially under hypoxic conditions.
2
糖酵解过程发生在细胞质中，将一分子葡萄糖分解为两分子丙酮酸。 Glycolysis takes place in the cytosol, breaking down one molecule of glucose into two molecules of pyruvate.
3
此过程中净生成2分子ATP，同时产生2分子NADH。 This process produces a net gain of 2 ATP molecules and 2 NADH molecules.
总反应式为
The overall reaction is: Glucose+2 ADP+2 Pi+2 NAD+→2 Pyruvate+2 ATP+2 NADH+2 H++2 H2OGlucose+2 ADP+2 Pi+2 NAD+→2 Pyruvate+2 ATP+2 NADH+2 H++2 H2​O
5
上述过程无需氧气即可进行，称为无氧糖酵解。 This reaction can proceed without oxygen, which is termed anaerobic glycolysis.
6
糖酵解的主要产物为2分子丙酮酸、2分子NADH和2分子ATP。 The main products of glycolysis are 2 molecules of pyruvate, 2 NADH, and 2 ATP.
课件中标明的无氧糖酵解产物为
2分子丙酮酸、2分子NADH和2分子ATP。 The slide highlights that anaerobic glycolysis yields: 2 pyruvate, 2 NADH, and 2 ATP.
8
（老师补充）虽然糖酵解不是心脏的主要能量来源，但在缺氧和某些疾病条件下变得非常关键。 (Teacher’s addition) Although glycolysis is not the primary energy source for the heart, it becomes crucial during hypoxia and under certain pathological conditions.

线粒体在心脏能量代谢中的核心作用 Mitochondria: at the heart of cardiac energetics

1
线粒体是心肌细胞能量产生的核心胞器。 Mitochondria are the central organelle for energy production in cardiomyocytes.
线粒体由四个主要结构组成
外膜、膜间隙、带嵴的内膜和基质。 The mitochondrion consists of four main structures: outer membrane, intermembrane space, inner membrane with cristae, and matrix.
3
外膜包裹整个线粒体，内膜形成多褶皱（嵴），增加能量合成效率。 The outer membrane encloses the mitochondrion, while the inner membrane forms folds (cristae) to enhance energy synthesis efficiency.
4
基质区域是三羧酸（TCA）循环等重要代谢反应的发生场所。 The matrix is where key metabolic processes like the tricarboxylic acid (TCA) cycle occur.
5
“线粒体”一词来源于希腊语，“mitos”意为丝状，“chondros”意为颗粒状的种子，反映出线粒体形态上的多样性。 The term “mitochondrion” comes from Greek, with “mitos” meaning filament and “chondros” meaning seed, highlighting the diverse morphology of mitochondria.
6
线粒体形态是动态变化的，可以呈现高融合长丝状或较短、碎片化的状态，这主要受融合（fusion）和分裂（fission）过程调控。 Mitochondria are morphologically dynamic, with forms ranging from hyperfused filaments to short, fragmented pieces, regulated by fusion and fission processes.
7
融合有助于细胞在高能需求时提高能量效率，分裂则帮助细胞清除受损线粒体，适应应激状态。 Fusion helps optimize energy production during high demand, while fission facilitates removal of damaged mitochondria and adaptation to stress.
8
线粒体在健康心肌中约占细胞体积的30%-40%，为高能消耗的心脏持续提供所需ATP。 In healthy cardiomyocytes, mitochondria occupy about 30%-40% of cell volume and constantly supply the large amounts of ATP required by the heart.

线粒体功能Mitochondrial functions

1
线粒体不仅负责细胞的能量产生，还具有多种重要功能。 Mitochondria are not only responsible for cellular energy production, but also have various other essential functions.
2
能量产生是最核心的功能之一，线粒体合成ATP和磷酸肌酸（PCr），为心肌收缩等生命活动提供能量。 Energy production is one of the core functions; mitochondria synthesize ATP and phosphocreatine (PCr) to fuel vital processes such as cardiac contraction.
3
线粒体调控细胞凋亡过程，相关因子包括Bax、BCL2和细胞色素C。 Mitochondria regulate apoptosis, with key factors including Bax, BCL2, and cytochrome C.
4
线粒体在脂质和碳水化合物稳态中起到调节作用。 Mitochondria play a role in lipid and carbohydrate homeostasis.
5
线粒体维持离子平衡，特别是钾离子（K⁺）和钙离子（Ca²⁺）的稳定。 Mitochondria help maintain ionic homeostasis, especially the balance of potassium (K⁺) and calcium (Ca²⁺).
6
线粒体参与体温生成，通过UCP（一类解偶联蛋白）介导调节产热。 Mitochondria contribute to thermogenesis through uncoupling proteins (UCP).
7
线粒体调节细胞的pH值，维持细胞内环境的稳定。 Mitochondria regulate cellular pH, helping maintain intracellular stability.
8
线粒体是甾体激素合成的重要场所。 Mitochondria are key sites for steroid hormone synthesis.
9
线粒体既可产生活性氧（ROS），如过氧化氢（H₂O₂）、超氧阴离子（O₂⁻），又能通过解毒途径清除这些有害物质。 Mitochondria are involved in both the production and detoxification of reactive oxygen species (ROS), such as hydrogen peroxide (H₂O₂) and superoxide anion (O₂⁻).

成年心肌细胞中的线粒体Mitochondria in adult cardiomyocytes

1
成年心肌细胞中每个细胞含有5000到10000个线粒体。 There are 5,000 to 10,000 mitochondria per cell in adult cardiomyocytes.
2
单个线粒体的平均体积约为1微米。 The mean volume of a single mitochondrion is about 1 μm.
3
线粒体占心肌细胞体积的30%到40%。 Mitochondria occupy 30–40% of the total cell volume.
4
老师补充指出，心肌细胞的线粒体数量远高于许多其他类型细胞，是所有细胞中线粒体最丰富的类型之一，这是因为心脏对能量的需求极高。 The instructor added that the mitochondrial content in cardiomyocytes is much higher than in many other cell types, making them one of the most mitochondria-rich cells because of the heart’s extremely high energy demand.
5
线粒体之间通过细胞骨架（尤其是特殊的支架蛋白）排列得非常精确，使得线粒体与肌丝及其他细胞器之间可以形成亚结构域，实现高效的能量供应和分子交换。 Mitochondria are precisely arranged through the cytoskeleton (notably specific scaffold proteins), forming microdomains with myofilaments and other organelles, which ensures efficient energy supply and molecular exchange.
6
图像展示了通过高分辨率成像技术获得的单个心肌细胞三维重建，清晰显示了线粒体在细胞内的大量分布和精确定位。 The image shows a 3D reconstruction of a single cardiomyocyte acquired by advanced imaging, clearly illustrating the abundance and precise localization of mitochondria within the cell.
7
由于心肌细胞结构致密，化学物质的扩散较慢，因此这种亚结构域的形成尤其重要，使能量（如ATP）能够迅速有效地从线粒体传递到耗能部位（如肌浆球蛋白ATP酶）。 Because cardiomyocytes have a dense structure and slow diffusion of molecules, the formation of these microdomains is especially important—allowing rapid and efficient transfer of energy (such as ATP) from mitochondria to energy-consuming sites (e.g., myosin ATPase).

线粒体与心肌细胞结构Mitochondria and cardiomyocyte cytoarchitecture

1
心肌细胞内的成分排列极其精确。 The arrangement of components within cardiomyocytes is extremely precise.
2
这种精确排列依赖于高度专化的细胞骨架，包括微管、肌动蛋白以及中间丝蛋白（去甲肌凝蛋白）。 This precise organization relies on a highly specialized cytoskeleton, including microtubules, actin, and intermediate filaments (desmin).
3
细胞骨架不仅支撑细胞结构，还决定线粒体与肌丝和其他细胞器的相对定位。 The cytoskeleton not only supports cellular structure but also determines the spatial relationship of mitochondria to myofilaments and other organelles.
4
线粒体与肌丝、肌浆网之间可以形成微结构域（microdomain），实现高效的能量供应和分子交换。 Microdomains can form between mitochondria and myofilaments, as well as between mitochondria and the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR), enabling efficient energy supply and molecular exchange.
老师特别补充
这种微结构域的形成和紧密排列，可以让ATP等分子直接、迅速地从线粒体传递至耗能位点（如肌球蛋白ATP酶），弥补心肌细胞结构致密、扩散速度慢的缺陷。 The instructor emphasized that forming these microdomains and their tight arrangement allows molecules like ATP to be directly and rapidly delivered from mitochondria to energy-consuming sites (e.g., myosin ATPase), compensating for the dense structure and slow diffusion within cardiomyocytes 244.
6
右侧图片显示了心肌细胞内线粒体与肌丝交错排列的超微结构。 The right image shows the ultrastructural interweaving of mitochondria and myofilaments within cardiomyocytes.
7
线粒体与肌丝的这种空间耦合对于心脏高效持续地产生和传递能量至关重要。 This spatial coupling of mitochondria and myofilaments is crucial for the efficient and continuous production and transfer of energy in the heart.

Krebs循环Krebs cycle

1
Krebs循环在线粒体基质中进行。 The Krebs cycle takes place in the mitochondrial matrix.
2.Krebs循环又称柠檬酸循环　或三羧酸循环（TAC）。 It is also known as the citric acid cycle (CAC) or tricarboxylic acid cycle (TAC).
答案 AC
3
该循环是由乙酰辅酶A（acetyl-CoA）参与的一系列化学反应组成。 The cycle consists of a series of chemical reactions involving acetyl-CoA.
4
主要作用是释放还原型辅酶（NADH与FADH₂），它们是后续线粒体呼吸链电子传递的关键分子。 Its main function is to release reducing agents (NADH and FADH₂), which are essential for the mitochondrial electron transport chain (ETC).
5
Krebs循环每经过一轮可产生1分子ATP、3分子NADH和1分子FADH₂。 Each turn of the Krebs cycle produces 1 ATP, 3 NADH, and 1 FADH₂.
6
这些生成的NADH和FADH₂将被送往线粒体电子传递链以驱动大量ATP的合成。 The produced NADH and FADH₂ are then sent to the mitochondrial electron transport chain to drive massive ATP synthesis.
7
老师强调，这个循环是心肌细胞能量产生的中心环节，也是有氧代谢的关键步骤，几乎所有可代谢底物（如葡萄糖、脂肪酸、氨基酸）都会以乙酰辅酶A的形式进入此循环。 The instructor emphasized that this cycle is the central step of energy production in cardiomyocytes and a critical process of aerobic metabolism; nearly all metabolizable substrates (e.g., glucose, fatty acids, amino acids) enter this cycle as acetyl-CoA2425.
8
Krebs循环的发现者为Hans Krebs博士，他因该发现获得1953年诺贝尔奖。 The Krebs cycle was described by Dr. Hans Krebs, who was awarded the Nobel Prize in 1953 for this discovery25.

糖酵解Glycolysis

1
糖酵解是心肌细胞能量产生的主要途径之一。 Glycolysis is one of the main pathways for energy production in cardiomyocytes.
2
糖酵解在细胞质中进行，将葡萄糖分解为丙酮酸，过程中产生ATP和NADH。 Glycolysis takes place in the cytosol, breaking down glucose to pyruvate and producing ATP and NADH during the process220.
3
一个葡萄糖分子通过糖酵解最终生成两分子丙酮酸、两分子ATP（净产）和两分子NADH。 One glucose molecule, through glycolysis, yields two molecules of pyruvate, a net gain of two ATP, and two NADH2.
4
丙酮酸随后进入线粒体，在丙酮酸脱氢酶（PDH）的作用下转化为乙酰辅酶A。 Pyruvate then enters the mitochondrion and, under the action of pyruvate dehydrogenase (PDH), is converted to acetyl-CoA214.
5
乙酰辅酶A进入Krebs循环（柠檬酸循环），进一步进行有氧代谢产生更多能量。 Acetyl-CoA enters the Krebs cycle (citric acid cycle), where further aerobic metabolism occurs to produce more energy214.
老师补充说明
虽然心肌细胞主要利用脂肪酸产生能量，但葡萄糖的糖酵解在心脏缺氧或病理状态下仍十分重要。 The instructor added: Although cardiomyocytes mainly use fatty acids for energy, glycolysis of glucose plays a critical role during hypoxia or pathological conditions in the heart82.
7
糖酵解过程中产生的丙酮酸不仅用于氧化代谢，也可在缺氧时转化为乳酸。 The pyruvate produced from glycolysis can be used for oxidative metabolism or converted to lactate in anaerobic conditions2.
8
在能量需求极高、且ATP储备低的心肌细胞内，糖酵解为心脏提供了快速但有限的能量来源。 In cardiomyocytes, which have very high energy demand and low ATP reserves, glycolysis provides a rapid but limited source of energy

心肌细胞能量产生Energy production in the cardiomyocyte

1
线粒体是心肌细胞的能量工厂（the powerhouse），主导能量（ATP）的生成。 The mitochondrion is known as the powerhouse of the cardiac muscle cell, dominating the generation of cellular energy (ATP) 827.
2
该页PPT展示了脂肪酸β-氧化的重要性。脂肪酸在线粒体基质中分解为乙酰辅酶A，并释放还原当量（NADH和FADH₂）。 This slide highlights the importance of fatty acid β-oxidation. Fatty acids are broken down in the mitochondrial matrix into acetyl-CoA, releasing reducing equivalents (NADH and FADH₂) 827.
3
心肌细胞主要以脂肪酸为能量底物，约90-95%的ATP由脂肪酸氧化产生。 Cardiomyocytes mainly use fatty acids as energy substrates, with about 90-95% of ATP produced from fatty acid oxidation 8.
4
脂肪酸β-氧化经过多步酶催化（如acyl-CoA脱氢酶、enoyl-CoA水合酶等），最终生成乙酰辅酶A。 Fatty acid β-oxidation involves several enzyme-catalyzed steps (such as acyl-CoA dehydrogenase, enoyl-CoA hydratase, etc.) and ultimately generates acetyl-CoA 827.
5
乙酰辅酶A随后进入Krebs循环（柠檬酸循环），进一步被彻底氧化，生成更多NADH和FADH₂。 The acetyl-CoA generated then enters the Krebs cycle (citric acid cycle) for further complete oxidation, yielding more NADH and FADH₂ 827.
6
生成的NADH和FADH₂会进入电子传递链，用于驱动大量ATP的合成。 The resulting NADH and FADH₂ are delivered into the electron transport chain to drive the synthesis of large amounts of ATP 827.
老师强调
线粒体双膜结构为物质运输和化学反应提供了分区，使能量代谢高效且有序。 The instructor emphasized: The double-membrane structure of mitochondria enables compartmentalization of transport and biochemical reactions, making energy metabolism efficient and organized 28.
8
心脏能量代谢的中心角色决定了线粒体功能损伤会严重影响心肌收缩、舒张等生命活动。 The central role of mitochondrial metabolism in the heart means that mitochondrial dysfunction can severely affect vital activities such as cardiomyocyte contraction and relaxation 8.

ETC及氧化磷酸化ETC and oxidative phosphorylation

1
电子传递链（ETC）发生在线粒体内膜，是心肌细胞能量转换的最后阶段。 The electron transport chain (ETC) occurs in the mitochondrial inner membrane and is the final stage of energy conversion in cardiomyocytes27.
2
NADH和FADH₂（来自Krebs循环和β-氧化）将高能电子供给电子传递链。 NADH and FADH₂, generated from the Krebs cycle and β-oxidation, donate high-energy electrons to the ETC278.
3
电子沿着复合物I至IV转移，每转移一步带动质子（H⁺）从线粒体基质泵入膜间隙。 Electrons are transferred through complexes I to IV, and at each step, protons (H⁺) are pumped from the mitochondrial matrix into the intermembrane space2719.
4
这导致膜间隙中的H⁺浓度增加，形成质子浓度梯度和电化学梯度。 This causes an accumulation of H⁺ in the intermembrane space, forming a proton concentration gradient and an electrochemical gradient2719.
5
质子浓度梯度表现为膜间隙内H⁺浓度比基质高（约相差0.5个pH单位），并形成电势差（约-150mV）。 The proton concentration gradient means a higher H⁺ concentration in the intermembrane space (about 0.5 pH units higher), and an electrical potential difference of around -150 mV is established2719.
6
这种电化学梯度即为质子驱动力（proton motive force），驱使质子经ATP合酶（复合物V）回流至基质，合成ATP。 This electrochemical gradient, called the proton motive force, drives protons back into the matrix via ATP synthase (complex V), resulting in the synthesis of ATP2719.
老师强调
能量转化高度依赖于电子传递链与氧气的有序耦合。氧气作为末端电子受体，吸收电子和H⁺生成水。 The lecturer emphasized that energy conversion heavily relies on the ordered coupling of the ETC with oxygen as the terminal electron acceptor, combining with electrons and protons to form water2719.
8
ETC功能障碍会迅速导致心脏能量供应不足，影响心肌收缩/舒张等基本生命活动。 Dysfunction of the ETC rapidly leads to insufficient energy supply to the heart, compromising essential activities such as myocardial contraction and relaxation。

ATP合酶ATP synthase

1
ATP合酶是心肌细胞能量产生的关键分子，位于线粒体内膜上。 ATP synthase is a key molecule for energy production in cardiomyocytes, located on the inner mitochondrial membrane10.
2
质子通过ATP合酶的通道流动，驱动蛋白的机械旋转运动。 Protons flow through a channel in ATP synthase, which drives the mechanical rotation of the protein10.
3
这种旋转带动ATP合酶的转子部分，如同纳米马达（涡轮）一样工作。 This rotation causes the rotor part of ATP synthase to act like a nanomotor (turbine)10.
4
机械能被转化为化学能，促使无机磷酸根（Pi）与ADP结合，生成ATP。 The mechanical energy is converted into chemical energy, facilitating the binding of inorganic phosphate (Pi) to ADP and producing ATP10.
老师特别强调
ATP合酶的结构和功能极为精巧，通过旋转运动高效合成ATP，是生物能量转化的经典范例。 The lecturer emphasized that ATP synthase’s structure and function are extremely sophisticated; its rotary mechanism allows highly efficient ATP synthesis and is a classic example of bioenergetic conversion10.
6
质子梯度的建立（膜间隙高、基质低）为ATP合酶提供能量，是氧化磷酸化顺利进行的基础。 The proton gradient (high in intermembrane space, low in the matrix) provides the driving force for ATP synthase and is the basis for efficient oxidative phosphorylation10.

能量产生的调控 Regulation of energy production

1
心肌细胞能量产生受钙离子和ADP双重调控。 Energy production in cardiomyocytes is regulated by both calcium ions and ADP6.
2
工作负荷增加时，胞质内钙离子浓度升高。 When workload increases, the cytosolic calcium concentration rises6.
3
更多的钙进入线粒体基质，促进线粒体内脱氢酶和ATP合酶的激活。 More calcium enters the mitochondrial matrix, stimulating mitochondrial dehydrogenases and ATP synthase6.
4
这增加了NADH和ATP的生成。 This leads to higher production of NADH and ATP6.
5
老师强调，钙信号是心肌线粒体能量代谢与肌肉收缩需求精确协调的关键因子。 The lecturer emphasized that calcium signaling is the key factor coordinating mitochondrial energy metabolism with contractile demand in the heart6.
6
此外，胞质内ADP浓度升高也能刺激ATP合酶，促进ATP合成。 Moreover, an increase in cytosolic ADP stimulates ATP synthase, thus further promoting ATP synthesis6.
7
这种对钙和ADP变化的敏感性，使心肌细胞可以根据能量消耗的变化迅速调节能量供应。 This sensitivity to calcium and ADP allows cardiomyocytes to rapidly adjust energy supply according to varying energy consumption6.

从能量生产者到能量利用者From energy producers to energy consumers

1
心肌细胞具有高密度的肌丝和线粒体。 Cardiomyocytes have a high density of myofilaments and mitochondria2731。
2
由于细胞内结构极为致密，能量分子的扩散效率很低。 Because of the extremely dense intracellular architecture, the diffusion efficiency of energy molecules is very low2731。
3
老师强调，线粒体和肌丝之间通过特殊的细胞骨架有序排列，形成微结构域，保证能量能够在产能部位与需能部位之间精确转移。 The lecturer emphasized that mitochondria and myofilaments are precisely arranged by a specialized cytoskeleton, forming microdomains to ensure accurate transfer of energy between production and consumption sites2731。
4
由于扩散受限，心肌细胞需要特定的能量转移系统来实现高效的能量供应。 Because diffusion is restricted, cardiomyocytes require specific energy transfer systems to ensure efficient energy supply2731。
课上讲到
线粒体通过这些微结构域与肌丝紧密相连，允许ATP等能量分子无需长距离扩散即可直接被肌肉收缩机制利用。 In class, it was mentioned that mitochondria are tightly connected to myofilaments through these microdomains, allowing ATP and other energy molecules to be used directly by the contractile machinery without long-distance diffusion2731。
6
这对心脏这样高工作量、低能量储备的器官至关重要，保证了心肌细胞在高频率跳动时始终获得充足能量。 This is crucial for the heart, an organ with high workload and low energy reserves, ensuring cardiomyocytes always get enough energy during frequent contractions2731。
备注
本页重点突出心肌细胞特殊的能量结构安排，课上补充了细胞骨架和微结构域对于能量高效传递的决定性作用。
Note
This slide highlights the cardiomyocyte’s unique arrangement for energy transfer, with additional classroom emphasis on the decisive role of the cytoskeleton and microdomains in efficient energy delivery.

能量转运系统Energy transfer systems

心肌细胞中存在两大主要能量转运系统
磷酸肌酸激酶穿梭系统和腺嘌呤核苷酸直接通道系统。 There are two main energy transfer systems in cardiomyocytes: the creatine kinase (CK) shuttle and the direct adenine nucleotide channeling (DANC) system2933。
2
磷酸肌酸激酶穿梭系统通过磷酸肌酸（PCr）将线粒体产生的高能磷酸基团高效运输到肌丝和离子泵等能量消耗位点。 The CK shuttle transports high-energy phosphate groups generated in mitochondria via phosphocreatine (PCr) efficiently to energy-consuming sites such as myofilaments and ion pumps2933。
3
在线粒体，ATP和肌酸在肌酸激酶（CK）作用下生成PCr和ADP，PCr在细胞质中扩散到消耗区后再被CK利用，将磷酸基团转移给ADP生成ATP，供收缩和泵浦活动使用。 In mitochondria, ATP and creatine are converted to PCr and ADP by CK; PCr then diffuses in cytosol to consumption sites, where CK transfers the phosphate group to ADP, forming ATP for contraction and pump function2933。
4
这种“能量穿梭”模式克服了心肌细胞结构致密导致的分子扩散受限问题，使能量在产需两端高效衔接。 This “shuttle” mode overcomes the limitation of slow molecular diffusion due to dense cellular structure, allowing efficient coupling of energy production and consumption2932。
5
直接腺嘌呤核苷酸通道则通过线粒体腺嘌呤核苷酸转运体（ANT）和下游ATP酶形成微结构域，实现ATP和ADP的直接交换与定位供应。 The DANC system forms microdomains through mitochondrial adenine nucleotide translocase (ANT) and downstream ATPases, enabling direct exchange and localized supply of ATP and ADP2933。
6
课上老师强调，这两种方式在心脏高工作负荷与能量需求快速波动时可以协同保障能量精确及时地送达各功能区，维持高效收缩。 In class, the lecturer emphasized that these two systems work together to ensure precise and timely energy delivery to functional domains during high cardiac workload and rapid energy demand fluctuation, sustaining efficient contraction2921。
最下方公式展示了CK催化的可逆反应
The equation at the bottom shows the reversible reaction catalyzed by CK: 肌酸 + ATP ↔ 磷酸肌酸 + ADP creatine + ATP ↔ phosphocreatine + ADP

线粒体生命周期 Mitochondrial life cycle

1
线粒体在细胞内经历持续的生长、分裂、融合及降解等动态变化。 Mitochondria in the cell undergo continuous dynamic processes including growth, fission, fusion, and degradation22。
2
线粒体生物发生（Mitochondrial biogenesis）指新线粒体的产生，这一过程涉及核DNA和线粒体DNA的转录、蛋白质组装以及线粒体数量的增加。 Mitochondrial biogenesis refers to the production of new mitochondria, involving transcription from nuclear and mitochondrial DNA, protein assembly, and increase in mitochondrial numbers22。
3
线粒体动力学（Mitochondrial dynamics）包括线粒体的融合（fusion）和分裂（fission），调节线粒体的形态、数量及功能状态。 Mitochondrial dynamics, including fusion and fission, regulate mitochondrial morphology, quantity, and functional state22。
4
老师强调，融合有助于缓冲线粒体损伤、均一其内含物，而分裂则有助于去除受损部分、适应细胞能量需求。 The lecturer emphasized that fusion buffers mitochondrial damage and homogenizes contents, while fission helps remove damaged parts and adapt to changes in energy demand2210。
5
功能健康的线粒体能为细胞供能，维持正常生理活动。 Functional, healthy mitochondria supply energy for the cell and maintain normal physiological activities22。
6
线粒体功能障碍（Dysfunction）时，受损的线粒体会被选择性降解。 When dysfunction occurs, damaged mitochondria are selectively degraded22。
7
线粒体自噬（Mitophagy）是一种特异性的自噬过程，通过相关蛋白（如PINK1/Parkin）识别并清除受损线粒体，以维持细胞能量稳态及健康。 Mitophagy is a specific type of autophagy, using proteins such as PINK1/Parkin to identify and remove damaged mitochondria, maintaining cellular energy homeostasis and health1028。
课上补充
线粒体生命周期的调控贯穿心肌细胞能量生产全程，保证线粒体数量与质量始终适应心脏高耗能的需求。 Class addition: The regulation of the mitochondrial life cycle runs through the whole energy production process in cardiomyocytes, ensuring mitochondrial quantity and quality can always meet the heart’s high energy requirements

线粒体基因Mitochondrial genes

1
