线粒体DNA是闭环结构，共有16,569个核苷酸对。 Mitochondrial DNA is circular and consists of 16,569 nucleotide pairs33。
2
线粒体DNA编码13种电子传递链（ETC）蛋白。 Mitochondrial DNA encodes 13 proteins of the electron transport chain (ETC)。
3
线粒体DNA还编码22种转运RNA（tRNA）和2种核糖体RNA（rRNA）。 It also encodes 22 transfer RNAs (tRNAs) and 2 ribosomal RNAs (rRNAs)。
4
绝大多数线粒体蛋白（约1000种）由细胞核DNA编码，并在细胞质合成后导入线粒体。 The vast majority of mitochondrial proteins (about 1000 types) are encoded by nuclear DNA and imported into mitochondria after synthesis in the cytoplasm33。
课上老师补充
线粒体蛋白的双重基因来源（核基因和线粒体基因）对线粒体功能调控、基因表达依赖协调极为关键。 The lecturer added: The dual genetic origin (nuclear and mitochondrial genes) of mitochondrial proteins is critical for the regulation of mitochondrial function and requires highly coordinated gene expression33。
6
图示右侧为线粒体基因组结构示意，展示了各个重要功能基因的位置，包括编码ATP合酶、细胞色素b及多个电子传递链复合物的基因分布。 The diagram on the right illustrates the structure of the mitochondrial genome, showing the location of major functional genes, including those encoding ATP synthase, cytochrome b, and various electron transport chain complexes33。

线粒体基因与电子传递链 Mitochondrial genes and ETC

1
线粒体DNA只编码少数电子传递链蛋白，大部分亚单位由核基因编码。 Mitochondrial DNA encodes only a few electron transport chain proteins; most subunits are encoded by nuclear genes21。
2
电子传递链由五大复合物组成，分别为： The electron transport chain consists of five main complexes:
复合物I
NADH 辅酶Q氧化还原酶 Complex I: NADH coQ oxydoreductase
复合物II
琥珀酸脱氢酶 Complex II: Succinate dehydrogenase
复合物III
细胞色素c还原酶 Complex III: Cytochrome c reductase
复合物IV
细胞色素氧化酶 Complex IV: Cytochrome oxidase (COX)
复合物V
ATP合酶 Complex V: ATP synthase
3
其中，mtDNA编码复合物I的7个亚单位，复合物III的1个亚单位，复合物IV的3个亚单位，复合物V的2个亚单位，而其他亚单位均由核DNA编码。 Among them, mtDNA encodes 7 subunits of Complex I, 1 of Complex III, 3 of Complex IV, and 2 of Complex V, while the other subunits are all encoded by nuclear DNA2135。
4
并且，复合物II的所有亚单位都由核DNA编码。 Furthermore, all subunits of Complex II are encoded by nuclear DNA2135。
课上老师强调
线粒体功能依赖于核基因和线粒体基因的高度协同，只有在两者紧密配合时，电子传递链的组装和ATP合成才能顺利进行。 The lecturer emphasized in class: Mitochondrial function relies on highly coordinated nuclear and mitochondrial gene expression, and only when both cooperate closely can the ETC be properly assembled and ATP synthesis proceed efficiently21。
6
图中展示了TCA循环与电子传递链之间的联系，通过生成NADH/FADH₂供电子传递链使用，同时推动质子的跨膜转运形成电化学梯度，为ATP合成提供驱动力。 The figure shows the link between the TCA cycle and the ETC: NADH/FADH₂ generated by the TCA cycle fuels the electron transport chain, promotes proton translocation across the membrane, and creates an electrochemical gradient to drive ATP synthesis

线粒体生物合成中的转录调控 Regulation of transcription in mitochondrial biogenesis

1
线粒体生物合成需要核基因和线粒体基因的协调表达。 Mitochondrial biogenesis requires coordinated expression of nuclear and mitochondrial genes317。
2
绝大部分线粒体蛋白由细胞核基因编码，通过PGC-1α等协同调控转录后导入线粒体行使功能。 Most mitochondrial proteins are encoded by nuclear genes, with PGC-1α and related cofactors regulating their transcription and subsequent import into mitochondria317。
3
PGC-1α是转录调控的核心调节因子，通过作用于多类下游转录因子（如NRFs、ERRs、PPARs等）促进线粒体蛋白的表达及多种代谢通路相关蛋白的合成。 PGC-1α is the central regulator of transcription, acting on several downstream transcription factors (such as NRFs, ERRs, PPARs, etc.) to promote mitochondrial protein expression and the synthesis of proteins involved in various metabolic pathways317。
具体调控作用包括
TRβ1和NRFs驱动线粒体蛋白及生物合成相关基因表达，ERRs促进葡萄糖利用、血管生成和磷脂合成，PPARα调控脂肪酸氧化与转运，PPARβ/δ参与抗氧化和解毒系统的调控。 Specifically, TRβ1 and NRFs drive expression of mitochondrial proteins and biogenesis-related genes; ERRs promote glucose utilization, angiogenesis, and phospholipid synthesis; PPARα regulates fatty acid oxidation and transport; PPARβ/δ is involved in the regulation of antioxidant and detoxification systems17。
5
Tfam（线粒体转录因子A）由核基因编码，经转录后运输到线粒体，是mtDNA复制和转录的关键因子，保证线粒体自身基因的表达。 Tfam (mitochondrial transcription factor A), encoded by nuclear genes and imported post-transcriptionally into mitochondria, is a key factor for mtDNA replication and transcription, ensuring expression of mitochondrial genome317。
教师补充
线粒体生物合成的调控机制高度复杂，PGC-1α不仅决定线粒体数量，也影响心肌细胞能量代谢方式和适应力，是心脏对环境变化及疾病压力应答的重要分子基础。 Teacher's addition: The regulation of mitochondrial biogenesis is highly complex—PGC-1α not only determines mitochondrial quantity but also affects the metabolic mode and adaptability of cardiomyocytes, being an important molecular basis for the heart’s adaptation to environmental changes and pathological stress3。

线粒体生物发生与生命周期

Mitochondrial biogenesis and life cycle

1
线粒体生物发生的基本过程
1
Major steps in mitochondrial biogenesis

线粒体通过分裂进行增殖。 Mitochondria proliferate via division.

包括线粒体DNA复制。 Includes mitochondrial DNA replication.

核DNA和线粒体DNA的转录。 Transcription of nuclear DNA and mitochondrial DNA.

细胞质合成的前体物质被运送至线粒体胞内。 Import of precursors produced in cytosol to mitochondrial compartments.

蛋白复合体的组装。 Complex assembly.

脂质和磷脂的合成。 Lipid and phospholipid synthesis.

线粒体动力学（融合与分裂过程）。 Mitochondrial dynamics (fusion and fission processes).

2
线粒体生命周期概览
2
Overview of mitochondrial life cycle

线粒体生命周期包括生物合成、动态变化（分裂与融合）、功能保持、失能（功能障碍）以及自噬过程。 The mitochondrial life cycle includes biogenesis, dynamic changes (fission and fusion), function maintenance, dysfunction, and mitophagy.

线粒体动力学指的是线粒体之间反复的融合与分裂，有助于维持结构和功能的完整性。 Mitochondrial dynamics refer to repetitive fusion and fission processes between mitochondria, helping to maintain structural and functional integrity.

当线粒体发生不可逆损伤或功能障碍时，可以通过自噬（mitophagy）机制清除异常线粒体，保障细胞质量控制。 When mitochondria suffer irreversible damage or dysfunction, they can be removed by mitophagy to ensure cellular quality control.

3
听课补充（老师强调）
3
Supplement from class (as emphasized by the teacher)

线粒体功能维持高度依赖于其生物合成及动力学的调控，异常会导致能量供应不足，影响心脏等高能耗器官。 The maintenance of mitochondrial function highly depends on the regulation of biogenesis and dynamics. Dysfunction leads to insufficient energy supply, affecting high-energy organs like the heart.

教师特别提到，核基因和线粒体基因的转录需精准协调，才能保证新线粒体的正常生成和运作。 The teacher specifically mentioned that the transcription of nuclear and mitochondrial genes must be precisely coordinated to ensure proper generation and function of new mitochondria.

整理要点

Summary points

1
线粒体生物合成包括DNA复制、基因转录、前体物运输、复合体组装、脂质合成和动力学调控。 Mitochondrial biogenesis involves DNA replication, gene transcription, precursor import, complex assembly, lipid synthesis, and dynamics regulation.
2
生命周期涵盖生物合成—功能—失能—自噬的循环，通过融合和分裂保持动态平衡。 The life cycle covers a loop of biogenesis-function-dysfunction-mitophagy, and dynamic balance is maintained via fusion and fission.
3
上述每一环节异常都可直接影响能量代谢和细胞功能，尤其在心肌细胞等高需求细胞中尤为重要。 Any abnormalities in these steps can directly affect energy metabolism and cell function, especially critical in high-demand cells like cardiomyocytes.

线粒体动力学的主要调控因子Main actors in Mitochondrial Dynamics

一、裂变（Fission）

裂变是指一个线粒体分裂成为多个子线粒体的过程。 Fission refers to the division of a mitochondrion into several daughter mitochondria.

裂变的主要调控因子为DRP1和Fis1。 The main regulators of fission are DRP1 and Fis1.

裂变过程中，DRP1在胞质中激活并招募至线粒体表面，与Fis1结合，形成收缩环并最终实现线粒体的分裂。 During fission, DRP1 is activated in the cytosol and recruited to the mitochondrial surface, binding Fis1 to form a constriction ring and eventually drive mitochondrial division.

二、融合（Fusion）

融合是两个原本独立的线粒体的外膜和内膜发生物理融合的过程。 Fusion refers to the physical merging of the outer and inner membranes of two originally distinct mitochondria.

融合的主要调控因子为Mitofusins（Mfn1与Mfn2）和OPA1。 The main regulators of fusion are Mitofusins (Mfn1 and Mfn2) and OPA1.

融合过程分为两个阶段
首先由Mfn1和Mfn2介导外膜的融合；随后由OPA1介导内膜的融合。 The fusion process occurs in two steps: first, Mfn1 and Mfn2 mediate fusion of the outer membrane; then OPA1 mediates inner membrane fusion.

三、老师补充讲解（录音内容）

老师强调，线粒体的动态平衡依赖于裂变与融合的精确调控，这对维持线粒体功能特别重要。 The teacher stressed that mitochondrial dynamism relies on precise regulation of fission and fusion, which is especially important for maintaining mitochondrial function.

裂变有助于更新损伤或功能异常的线粒体，防止异常线粒体积累。 Fission helps to renew damaged or dysfunctional mitochondria, preventing the accumulation of impaired organelles.

融合有助于线粒体内部物质和基因的交换，有利于功能的恢复和均一化。 Fusion allows exchange of mitochondrial contents and genetic materials, contributing to functional recovery and homogenization.

这些过程失衡时，会导致能量代谢障碍，心肌细胞等高能耗细胞尤其受影响。 When these processes are imbalanced, it leads to energy metabolism disorder, particularly affecting high-energy cells like cardiomyocytes.

四、图示要点简释

裂变图解显示DRP1与Fis1在线粒体表面形成聚集环，实现收缩与断裂。 The fission diagram shows DRP1 and Fis1 assembling on mitochondria to form a constriction ring, leading to scission.

融合图展示Mfn蛋白位于外膜、OPA1位于内膜，通过两个阶段完成膜的融合。 The fusion diagram shows Mfn proteins at the outer membrane and OPA1 at the inner membrane, coordinating to fuse both membranes in two steps.

小结

动态过程的主调控因子
裂变→DRP1, Fis1；融合→Mfn1, Mfn2, OPA1。 Main regulators: Fission→DRP1, Fis1; Fusion→Mfn1, Mfn2, OPA1.

两者保持动态平衡，保证线粒体数量、质量和功能。 The balance of both ensures proper mitochondrial quantity, quality, and function.

线粒体网络Mitochondrial network

1
线粒体网络的形态（融合或碎片化）
1
Mitochondrial network morphology (fused or fragmented)

线粒体网络可以是融合状态，也可以是碎片化状态。 The mitochondrial network can be in a fused or fragmented state.

2
线粒体网络的状态取决于以下因素：
2
The state of the mitochondrial network depends on:

细胞类型。 Cell type.

细胞环境（如能量代谢状况、活性氧等）。 Cellular environment (such as energetics, reactive oxygen species, etc.).

细胞周期（包括有丝分裂和凋亡）。 Cell cycle life (including mitosis and apoptosis).

3
线粒体网络的调控：
3
Regulation of the mitochondrial network:

通过融合和分裂（profusion 和 profission）蛋白表达的平衡来调控。 Regulated by the balance of fusion and fission protein expression.

相关动力蛋白（如dynamin家族）的活性调控（包括翻译后修饰）。 Dynamin activity modulation (including post-translational modifications).

4
网络状态对细胞的影响：
4
Impact of network state on cells:

影响细胞对死亡信号的抵抗能力。 Affects the cell’s resistance to death signals.

影响线粒体的生物能效能。 Influences bioenergetic efficiency.

5
课堂录音核心补充
5
Key teacher’s explanation from lecture recording

老师强调，线粒体的形态不是静止不变的，可以因细胞类型、环境压力或周期改变而融合或断裂。 The teacher emphasized that mitochondrial morphology is not static—it can fuse or fragment depending on cell type, environmental stress, or cell cycle.

上述动态调控直接影响细胞对能量和氧化应激的适应能力。 This dynamic regulation directly affects the cell’s adaptation to energy demands and oxidative stress.

动态平衡失调会影响心脏等器官对损伤与能量需求的应对。 Imbalance in this dynamic can influence the response of organs like the heart to injury and energy demand.

简要总结

Summary:

线粒体网络通过动态变化适应细胞需求，依赖于融合/分裂蛋白的调控和平衡，与细胞功能、能量代谢和生存密切相关。 The mitochondrial network adapts to cellular needs through dynamic changes, regulated by the balance of fusion/fission proteins, closely linked to cell function, metabolic efficiency, and survival.

线粒体动力学在成年心肌细胞中的作用

Mitochondrial dynamics in adult cardiomyocyte

1
OPA1基因对线粒体形态的影响
1
The effect of OPA1 gene on mitochondrial morphology

OPA1是在线粒体内膜调控融合的关键蛋白。 OPA1 is a key protein regulating inner mitochondrial membrane fusion.

正常表达OPA1（OPA1+/+）时，线粒体结构较为规则，形态均一。 With normal OPA1 expression (OPA1+/+), mitochondria are regular in structure and uniform in morphology.

OPA1表达减少（OPA1+/-）时，线粒体出现形态异常，呈现为体积增大、异质性增强等现象。 When OPA1 expression is reduced (OPA1+/-), mitochondria display morphological abnormalities, such as increased size and enhanced heterogeneity10。

2
线粒体体积分析
2
Mitochondrial volume analysis

采用三维重建和体积测量显示，OPA1+/-小鼠心肌细胞中的线粒体体积分布发生显著变化。 3D reconstruction and volume measurement show significant alterations in mitochondrial volume distribution in OPA1+/- mouse cardiomyocytes.

与OPA1+/+相比，OPA1+/-心肌细胞中小体积线粒体比例减少，大体积线粒体比例升高。 Compared to OPA1+/+, the proportion of small-volume mitochondria decreases and large-volume mitochondria increases in OPA1+/- cardiomyocytes10。

3
统计分析结果
3
Statistical analysis results

图中柱状图显示，OPA1+/-组大体积线粒体的比例显著升高，而小体积线粒体比例明显下降。 The bar graphs show a significant increase in the proportion of large mitochondria and a decrease in small mitochondria in the OPA1+/- group.

OPA1+/-组的线粒体总体积增加，同时每单位体积内线粒体数量减少。 The total mitochondrial volume increases while the number of mitochondria per unit volume decreases in OPA1+/- mice.

4
课堂录音补充内容
4
Key explanations from lecture recording

老师强调，OPA1不足使线粒体融合障碍，导致较大的线粒体聚集，进而影响线粒体的功能和细胞能量代谢。 The teacher emphasized that OPA1 deficiency impairs mitochondrial fusion, leading to aggregation of larger mitochondria, further affecting mitochondrial function and cellular energy metabolism10。

实验结果证明，线粒体动力学异常（如OPA1缺陷）会导致线粒体分布和数量发生变化，这在心脏等高能耗组织中会产生重要的生理病理影响。 The experimental data demonstrate that abnormal mitochondrial dynamics (such as OPA1 deficiency) cause changes in mitochondrial distribution and number, which have significant physiological and pathological impacts in high-energy organs like the heart10。

小结

Summary

OPA1调控线粒体融合，维持线粒体正常形态和分布，其下降导致心肌细胞中线粒体体积增大、数量减少和形态异质性。 OPA1 regulates mitochondrial fusion and maintains normal mitochondrial shape and distribution; its reduction leads to increased mitochondrial size, decreased number, and morphological heterogeneity in cardiomyocytes.

心肌细胞能量代谢和生理功能会因线粒体动力学失衡而受损。 Energy metabolism and physiological function of cardiomyocytes are impaired due to imbalanced mitochondrial dynamics

线粒体生命周期 Mitochondrial life cycle

1
线粒体生命周期包括线粒体发生、动力学（融合与分裂）以及线粒体自噬。 The mitochondrial life cycle includes mitochondrial biogenesis, dynamics (fusion and fission), and mitophagy26。
2
线粒体发生指新线粒体的生成，这确保细胞不断获得新的、功能完整的线粒体。 Mitochondrial biogenesis refers to the formation of new mitochondria, which ensures the cell continuously obtains new and functional mitochondria26。
3
线粒体动力学包括融合（Fusion）和分裂（Fission），这两个过程交替进行，维持线粒体数量和形态的动态平衡。 Mitochondrial dynamics include fusion and fission, which alternate to maintain the dynamic balance of mitochondrial number and morphology26。
4
融合有助于缓解损伤、均化线粒体成分，而分裂能够剔除受损部分，促进线粒体更新。 Fusion helps mitigate damage and homogenize mitochondrial contents, while fission removes damaged parts and promotes mitochondrial renewal1526。
5
正常的线粒体经过生命周期后，能够有效地维持细胞功能。 Mitochondria that complete the life cycle normally are able to effectively support cell function1526。
6
出现结构或功能异常的线粒体会经历选择性清除过程，即线粒体自噬。 Mitochondria with structural or functional abnormalities undergo selective elimination called mitophagy26。
7
线粒体自噬是细胞通过特定机制（如PINK1/Parkin通路）定向清除损伤或失效线粒体，维持线粒体质量和细胞健康的关键步骤。 Mitophagy is a key step for cellular health, in which damaged mitochondria are selectively degraded via mechanisms such as the PINK1/Parkin pathway to maintain mitochondrial quality2629。
8
老师补充说明，线粒体生命周期中的每一步都对细胞能量代谢和适应压力刺激至关重要，如果任一环节障碍，都会影响细胞生存，尤其是在高能量需求的心脏等组织中。 The teacher supplemented that every step in the mitochondrial life cycle is crucial for cellular energy metabolism and adaptation to stress; dysfunction at any step would compromise cell survival, especially in high energy-demanding organs like the heart1526。

简要总结 Summary:

线粒体生命周期是动态循环，包括发生、动力学调控和自噬清除，共同保证线粒体数量、形态和功能的稳定，维护细胞能量代谢和整体健康。

The mitochondrial life cycle is a dynamic process involving biogenesis, dynamic regulation, and autophagic clearance, ensuring stability in mitochondrial number, shape, and function, thus supporting cellular energy metabolism and overall health。

线粒体自噬的分子机制 Molecular mechanism of Mitophagy

1
线粒体自噬是线粒体的选择性自噬，依赖特定蛋白质机器的参与。 Mitophagy is a selective autophagy of mitochondria, involving a specific protein machinery8。
2
损伤/功能异常的线粒体首先在其外膜上积累PINK1蛋白。 When mitochondria are damaged or dysfunctional, PINK1 protein accumulates on the outer mitochondrial membrane98。
3
PINK1的积累促进了E3泛素连接酶Parkin的募集和激活（见图），Parkin随后被招募在线粒体外膜。 Accumulation of PINK1 promotes the recruitment and activation of E3 ubiquitin ligase Parkin (as shown in the diagram), which is then recruited onto the outer mitochondrial membrane98。
4
Parkin对线粒体外膜表面的蛋白进行泛素化修饰，形成泛素链信号。 Parkin ubiquitinates proteins on the mitochondrial surface, forming ubiquitin chain signals98。
5
泛素信号被P62等自噬受体识别。P62进一步与自噬体膜上的LC3B相互作用。 The ubiquitin chain is recognized by autophagic receptors such as P62, which further interact with LC3B on the autophagosome membrane98。
6
受损的线粒体被包裹到自噬体（Autophagosome）中，最终与溶酶体融合，实现线粒体降解和清除。 The damaged mitochondria are engulfed by the autophagosome, which will ultimately fuse with the lysosome, leading to mitochondrial degradation and clearance98。
老师特别强调
这个过程是细胞维护线粒体质量、清除功能障碍线粒体的关键，特别是在心脏等高能量需求组织中至关重要。 The lecturer highlighted that this process is essential for maintaining mitochondrial quality and removing dysfunctional mitochondria, especially in energy-demanding organs like the heart98。

简要总结 Summary:

线粒体自噬依赖PINK1-Parkin通路，通过泛素化、P62识别、LC3介导的自噬体包裹和清除机制，有效移除损伤线粒体，维持心肌细胞等高能耗细胞的能量代谢和健康。

Mitophagy relies on the PINK1-Parkin pathway. Through ubiquitination, P62 recognition, LC3-mediated autophagosome engulfment, and clearance mechanisms, damaged mitochondria are efficiently removed, maintaining energy metabolism and health in high-energy cells like cardiomyocytes98。

（结合前面部分整合后的链式关键事件总结：）

1
线粒体生命周期包含线粒体发生、动力学调控（融合&分裂）与自噬清除。 The mitochondrial life cycle includes biogenesis, dynamic regulation (fusion & fission), and autophagic clearance2634。
2
动力学调控保证形态和数量稳态，OPA1等调控融合，Mitofusins/DRP1等调控分裂。 Dynamics maintain homeostasis in shape and number, with OPA1 regulating fusion and Mitofusins/DRP1 regulating fission3628。
3
发生异常、功能失常的线粒体通过PINK1-Parkin途径启动自噬，完成最后的选择性清除。 Abnormal or dysfunctional mitochondria activate mitophagy via the PINK1-Parkin pathway, achieving selective removal as the final step89。

心脏中的线粒体自噬 Mitophagy in the heart

2010年代初，人们首次在受压应激的心脏中识别到了线粒体自噬。 Identification of mitophagy in stressed heart in the early 2010’s33。

2
通过电镜观察，可以在心脏组织中看到包裹线粒体的自噬体结构，这为心脏中存在线粒体自噬提供了直接证据。 Electron microscopy allowed observation of autophagosomes engulfing mitochondria within cardiac tissue, providing direct evidence for the presence of mitophagy in the heart33。
3
右侧柱状图显示，在脓毒症所致应激后，线粒体自噬对心脏功能的恢复至关重要。 The bar graph on the right shows that after stress induced by sepsis, mitophagy is essential for recovery of cardiac function33。
4
心脏正常野生型小鼠（WT）在经历脓毒症后，其心输出量能够部分恢复，而缺失线粒体自噬关键分子的敲除小鼠（KO）恢复明显受限。 In wild-type (WT) mice, cardiac output partially recovers after sepsis, while knockout (KO) mice lacking key mitophagy components display severely impaired recovery33。
5
实验结果提示，线粒体自噬缺失会导致心脏在应激后功能恢复受阻，强调了线粒体自噬在心肌耐受压力和维持功能中的关键作用。 These results indicate that loss of mitophagy leads to poor recovery of cardiac function after stress, underscoring the critical role of mitophagy in cardiac stress adaptation and function maintenance33。

简要总结 Summary：

线粒体自噬不仅在心脏压力应激时可被直接观察，还对脓毒症等急性损伤后心脏功能的恢复起着决定性作用，是心肌适应损伤和维持能量稳态必不可少的机制。

Mitophagy can be directly observed in the stressed heart and is crucial for cardiac functional recovery following acute injuries such as sepsis, making it an essential mechanism for cardiomyocyte adaptation and energy homeostasis33。

心力衰竭及其能量代谢改变IHeart failure and bioenergetics modulations

1
心力衰竭定义为心脏泵血功能不足，无法满足机体的需求。 Heart failure is defined as the inability of the heart to provide adequate blood flow to meet the needs of the organism.
2
心力衰竭常常与心脏能量代谢异常密切相关。 Heart failure is often closely associated with alterations in cardiac energy metabolism.

Heart failure and energetics 心力衰竭与能量代谢

3
在心力衰竭时，线粒体能量生成能力显著下降。 In heart failure, the mitochondrial energy production capacity decreases significantly.
4
氧化代谢能力（VO₂）以及柠檬酸合酶活性在HF（心力衰竭）组中明显降低。 Oxidative capacities (VO₂) and citrate synthase activity are significantly decreased in the HF (heart failure) group39.
5
心脏能量转移系统同样受损，特别是线粒体肌酸激酶（mi-CK）和肌浆型肌酸激酶（MM-CK）、肌型肌酸激酶（MB-CK）活性下降。 The cardiac energy transfer system is also impaired, with activities of mitochondrial creatine kinase (mi-CK), myofibrillar (MM-CK), and muscle-type (MB-CK) creatine kinases being reduced37.
6
电子显微镜下观察发现，心力衰竭心脏结构发生改变，线粒体与肌丝间排列紊乱。 Electron microscopy reveals structural alterations in failing hearts, with disorganization between mitochondria and myofibrils37.
7
图示比较显示，心力衰竭时能量流动及ATP转运效率降低，关键的能量转移结构受损。 Illustrations compare how, in heart failure, energy flow and ATP transfer efficiency decrease due to the disruption of key energy-transfer structures37.

简要总结 Summary

8
心力衰竭本质上涉及能量生成和转运的双重障碍，与线粒体功能障碍密切相关。 Heart failure fundamentally involves both impaired energy generation and transfer, which is closely linked to mitochondrial dysfunction3937.
9
能量代谢异常不仅是心力衰竭的标志，也是疾病进展和心脏功能恶化的重要原因。 Disturbed energy metabolism is not only a hallmark but also a major contributor to disease progression and worsening of cardiac function

心脏衰竭与能量代谢 Heart failure and energetics

线粒体氧化能力的评估 Assessment of mitochondrial oxidative capacities

1
该页介绍了如何评估线粒体的氧化能力，这是检测心肌能量代谢状况的重要实验方法。 This slide introduces how to assess mitochondrial oxidative capacities, which is a key method to evaluate cardiac energy metabolism.
左上为实验系统图
将心肌细胞线粒体纤维置入密闭的反应腔，腔体接有氧电极（可监测溶氧量）。 Top left is the diagram of the experimental system: isolated cardiac mitochondrial fibers are placed in a sealed reaction chamber, which is connected to an oxygen electrode to monitor oxygen concentration.
3
中间仪器和右侧电脑用于实时采集和显示溶氧变化数据。 The center device and computer on the right are used for real-time collection and display of oxygen concentration data.
4
下方左图为典型的实验数据曲线，显示在不同底物添加时氧气消耗速度的变化： The lower left shows a typical data curve, indicating oxygen consumption rates in response to different substrates:

首先添加谷氨酰胺/苹果酸（Glutamate/malate）启动NADH生成和线粒体呼吸链。 First, glutamate/malate is added to initiate NADH production and mitochondrial respiratory chain activity.

随后加入ADP，激活氧化磷酸化（促进氧气消耗）。 ADP is then added, triggering oxidative phosphorylation and accelerating oxygen consumption.

最后加入琥珀酸（succinate），进一步刺激电子传递链复合物II活动，使氧耗速率继续上升。 Finally, succinate is added to further stimulate complex II, leading to an additional increase in oxygen consumption rate.

右下图为电子传递链示意
NADH和FADH₂将电子传递给线粒体呼吸链，引发质子从基质泵入膜间隙，形成质子梯度，驱动ATP合酶合成ATP。 The diagram on the lower right shows the electron transport chain: NADH and FADH₂ donate electrons to the respiratory chain, protons are pumped from the matrix into the intermembrane space, creating a proton gradient which drives ATP synthase to produce ATP.
6
该实验流程可以定量评估线粒体氧化功能，是判断心脏能量状态和心力衰竭时代谢异常的重要检测手段。 This experimental approach quantitatively evaluates mitochondrial oxidative capacity and is a critical assay to assess cardiac energetic status or metabolic dysfunction in heart failure.

简要总结 Summary:

通过溶氧电极实验，分别加入不同底物和ADP，可定量显示线粒体氧化呼吸能力及功能异常，有利于分析心力衰竭等病理状态下线粒体能量生成的变化。

By using an oxygen electrode setup and sequential substrate/ADP addition, mitochondrial oxidative respiration capacity and dysfunction can be quantitatively measured, facilitating the study of energy generation alterations in pathologies such as heart failure

心力衰竭中的生物能量调控变化 Bioenergetics modulations in heart failure

1
线粒体能量生成障碍 Mitochondrial energy production impairment

心力衰竭（HF）时，心肌线粒体氧化能力明显下降，表现为VO₂消耗速率（μmol O₂/min/mg dw）和柠檬酸合酶活性（IU/mg protein）均显著↓。 In heart failure (HF), cardiac mitochondrial oxidative capacity declines significantly, shown by decreased VO₂ consumption rate (μmol O₂/min/mg dw) and citrate synthase activity (IU/mg protein) .

这一改变说明心力衰竭患者心脏能量生成基础障碍，已从分子和酶活层面得到证实。 This indicates a fundamental impairment of energy production in the failing heart, as confirmed at the molecular and enzymatic levels .

课上强调，氧化能力和柠檬酸合酶活性的降低，是心力衰竭时线粒体数量或功能均受损的反映。 The professor emphasized in class that reduced oxidative capacity and citrate synthase activity reflect both mitochondrial quantity and functional impairment during heart failure .

2
能量转运系统受损 Energy transfer impairment

心力衰竭心肌中，线粒体肌酸激酶（mi-CK）活性严重↓，同时肌浆型（MM-CK）和肌型（MB-CK）也受抑，能量转运效率大幅下降。 In the failing myocardium, mitochondrial creatine kinase (mi-CK) activity dramatically decreases, and both myofibrillar (MM-CK) and muscle-type (MB-CK) CK are suppressed, leading to markedly reduced energy transfer efficiency 37 .

电子显微镜下，心力衰竭时线粒体与肌丝间的排列紊乱，能量流及直接ATP转运通路变差。 Electron microscopy shows disorganization between mitochondria and myofibrils in heart failure, compromising both energy flux and direct ATP translocation routes 37 .

课堂补充指出，能量生成与能量转运障碍是心力衰竭发生和加重的基础。 The professor added that defects in both energy production and transfer are fundamental to the onset and worsening of heart failure 37 .

3
线粒体生物发生受损 Mitochondrial biogenesis impairment

各组织（心室VG、比目鱼肌Sol、腓肠肌Gas）心衰小鼠柠檬酸合酶活性、氧化能力均↓，提示外围组织也随心力衰竭受累。 Citrate synthase activity and oxidative capacity are decreased in multiple tissues (ventricle, soleus, gastrocnemius) in HF mice, indicating extra-cardiac involvement during heart failure 38 .

心力衰竭时，PGC-1α、NRF-2和Tfam等线粒体生物生成主控因子的表达水平显著↓。 In heart failure, the expression of key mitochondrial biogenesis regulators such as PGC-1α, NRF-2, and Tfam is significantly reduced .

课堂强调，PGC-1α下调→NRF-2、Tfam下调→mtDNA转录/复制障碍→OXPHOS蛋白减少，最终能量生成能力减弱。 The professor highlighted: Downregulation of PGC-1α leads to reduced NRF-2 and Tfam, causing impaired mtDNA transcription/replication, decreased OXPHOS proteins, and ultimately weaker energy production .

4
线粒体生物发生通路解读 Mitochondrial biogenesis pathway summary

PGC-1α 通过上调NRFs，促进调控基因的表达，并激活mtTFA（即Tfam）促进mtDNA复制和转录，产生OXPHOS蛋白和复合体。 PGC-1α upregulates NRFs, which activate regulatory genes, and in turn stimulates mtTFA (Tfam) to drive mtDNA replication and transcription, resulting in the production of OXPHOS proteins and complexes 39 .

该通路心力衰竭时受抑导致线粒体能量产生能力全面下降。 This pathway is suppressed in heart failure, leading to an overall decline in mitochondrial energy production capacity 39 .

归纳 Summary

心力衰竭时，线粒体能量生成、能量转运与线粒体新生均受损，分子机制包括PGC-1α、NRF-2、Tfam等主控因子水平下调。 In heart failure, mitochondrial energy production, energy transfer, and mitochondrial biogenesis are all impaired, with key molecular regulators (PGC-1α, NRF-2, Tfam) being downregulated 373839 .

结构和功能障碍协同导致心脏和外周组织能量供应不足，是心力衰竭的重要基础。 Such coordinated structural and functional disturbances result in insufficient energy supply to the heart and other tissues, forming the pathophysiological basis of heart failure 373839 .

PGC-1α主调控因子的失调↘ Dysregulation of the master regulator PGC-1α↘

1
PGC-1α是心脏能量代谢的核心调控因子，能与多种转录因子协作调节能量生成和代谢通路。 PGC-1α is the central regulator of cardiac energy metabolism and coordinates with various transcription factors to control energy production and metabolic pathways.
2
课上强调，PGC-1α下调是心力衰竭时最重要的分子事件之一，会影响多个分支代谢网络。 In class, the teacher highlighted that the downregulation of PGC-1α is one of the most important molecular events in heart failure and impacts multiple metabolic networks1.
3
PGC-1α与TRβ1、NRFs协作，促进线粒体生成与线粒体蛋白的表达。 PGC-1α partners with TRβ1 and NRFs to promote mitochondrial biogenesis and mitochondrial protein expression1.
4
PGC-1α通过ERRs调控葡萄糖利用、血管生成与磷脂合成，这对心脏适应和功能恢复非常关键。 PGC-1α acts via ERRs to regulate glucose utilization, angiogenesis, and phospholipid biosynthesis, which are critical for cardiac adaptation and recovery1.
5
PGC-1α联合PPARα调节脂肪酸氧化、脂肪酸转运及β-氧化，是心脏主要能量来源的基础。 PGC-1α works with PPARα to modulate fatty acid oxidation, fatty acid transporters, and β-oxidation, forming the basis of cardiac energy supply1.
6
与PPARβ/δ合作的PGC-1α负责调控抗氧化与解毒系统，维持心脏细胞的抗逆与代谢平衡。 PGC-1α, together with PPARβ/δ, controls antioxidant and detoxification systems to maintain cardiomyocyte defense and metabolic balance1.
7
课堂讲解补充，PGC-1α不仅调控一个方向，而是通过多个靶点控制心脏能量代谢全局，因此其表达下降将导致多重代谢障碍。 The professor further explained that PGC-1α does not act on a single target, but orchestrates cardiac energy metabolism globally through multiple pathways, so decreased expression leads to diverse metabolic disorders1.
8
在心力衰竭时，PGC-1α及各协同因子的下调，导致线粒体生成障碍、能量底物利用下降和抗氧化能力减弱，为心脏功能受损的重要机制。 In heart failure, downregulation of PGC-1α and its co-factors impairs mitochondrial biogenesis, reduces substrate utilization, and weakens antioxidant defense, constituting a key mechanism of cardiac dysfunction1.
注：课堂内容强调PGC-1α及其调控网络整体下调，是导致心力衰竭能量代谢障碍的核心分子基础。
Note
The lecture stressed that the overall downregulation of PGC-1α and its regulatory network is the molecular foundation for metabolic impairment in heart failure

脂质双层的通透性及物质跨膜运输原理 Lipid Bilayer Permeability and Principles of Transmembrane Transport

1
脂质双层的通透性

Lipid Bilayer Permeability

只有气体（如CO₂, O₂, N₂）能很容易穿过细胞膜。 Only gases (such as CO₂, O₂, N₂) can easily cross the cell membrane.

小的非带电极性分子（如乙醇、尿素、类固醇、甘油）也可以通过，但效率低于气体。 Small uncharged polar molecules (such as ethanol, urea, steroids, glycerol) can pass, but less efficiently than gases.

水（H₂O）可以部分通过脂质双层，但其通透性相对较低。 Water (H₂O) can partially cross the lipid bilayer, but its permeability is relatively low.

离子（如Na⁺, K⁺, Ca²⁺, Mg²⁺, Cl⁻, HCO₃⁻, HPO₄²⁻）和大多数极性及带电分子无法自由通过，需要借助通道或转运蛋白。 Ions (such as Na⁺, K⁺, Ca²⁺, Mg²⁺, Cl⁻, HCO₃⁻, HPO₄²⁻) and most polar or charged molecules cannot freely pass and require channels or transporters.

大的非带电极性分子（如葡萄糖、果糖）及带电极性大分子（如氨基酸、ATP、蛋白质、核酸）几乎无法透过双层。 Large uncharged polar molecules (like glucose, fructose) and large charged polar molecules (like amino acids, ATP, proteins, nucleic acids) hardly permeate the bilayer.

上述内容在老师讲解时特别强调了气体和小分子（乙醇等）可以直接通过，而水和离子等则基本不能，需蛋白帮助。 The lecturer emphasized that gases and small molecules (ethanol, etc.) can pass directly, while water and ions cannot and need protein assistance. 1

2
跨膜转运类型

Types of Transmembrane Transport

渠道/孔
通过扩散运输特定分子，多见离子通道。 Channels/Pores: Facilitate diffusion of specific molecules, commonly ion channels.
转运体
依赖浓度梯度被动转运分子。 Transporters: Mediate passive transport depending on the concentration gradient.
ATP酶泵/依赖ATP的转运体
利用ATP主动将分子从低浓度一侧运送到高浓度侧。 ATPase pumps/ATP-dependent transporters: Use ATP to actively transport molecules from low to high concentration.

协同转运（Symporters）：一次同时转运两种/多种分子（比如同向运输A和B）。 Symporters: Simultaneously transport two or more molecules in the same direction (e.g., A and B).

反向转运（Antiporters）：两种分子反方向运输（如A进C出）。 Antiporters: Transport two molecules in opposite directions (e.g., A in, C out).

区分被动运输（如通道和被动转运器），和主动运输（原发性如ATP泵，继发性如协同和反向转运）。 Distinguish passive transport (channels, passive transporters) from active transport (primary like ATP pumps, secondary like symporters/antiporters). 1

3
菲克扩散定律

Fick's Laws of Diffusion

菲克定律可以描述无电荷分子跨膜扩散的速率。 Fick's law describes the diffusion rate of uncharged molecules across the membrane.

公式：Jd = (dn / dt) = -D · (dCp / dx)

Formula
Jd=dndt=−D⋅dCpdxJd​=dtdn​=−D⋅dxdCp​​
其中
Where:
Jd​
扩散通量 Jd​: diffusion flux
D
扩散系数 D: diffusion coefficient
Cp​
分子浓度 Cp​: molecule concentration
x
距离 x: distance

对于非带电分子，扩散方向总是由高浓度向低浓度。 For uncharged molecules, diffusion is always from high to low concentration.

当两侧浓度相等时，达到扩散平衡。 When concentrations on both sides are equal, diffusion equilibrium is reached. 1

4
平衡电位（反转电位）

Reversal (Equilibrium) Potential

当膜两侧某离子浓度相等时，没有电位差。 When the concentration of a particular ion is equal on both sides of the membrane, there is no electric potential difference.

当一侧含较多离子时，离子会沿浓度梯度移动，带来正电荷流动。 When one side has higher ion concentration, ions move along the concentration gradient, leading to positive charge transfer.

这会在膜两侧产生电势梯度（即电场），与浓度梯度方向相反。 This creates an electric gradient (i.e., electric field) across the membrane opposing the concentration gradient.

当电势梯度和浓度梯度大小相等且方向相反时，离子通量停止，达到平衡电位。 When the electric and concentration gradients are equal in magnitude but opposite in direction, ion flux stops — this is the equilibrium/reversal potential.

平衡电位决定了该离子在膜上进出的驱动力。 The equilibrium potential determines the driving force for that ion across the membrane.

老师提到
以K⁺为例，细胞内高浓度K⁺向外流动时，内负外正形成电势抵消，使K⁺流动最终停止。 The lecturer explained: taking K⁺ as an example, as K⁺ flows outward, inside becomes negative and outside positive, creating a potential that counteracts further movement until the K⁺ flux stops. 1
重点总结 Key Summary
1
细胞膜选择性通透，主要允许气体和小分子自由通过，大分子与离子则需特殊蛋白辅助。 The cell membrane is selectively permeable — mainly allowing gases and small molecules to cross freely, while large molecules and ions require specific protein assistance.
2
跨膜运输分为被动和主动两类，主动需要能量消耗（如ATP）；被动依靠浓度梯度。 Transmembrane transport is divided into passive and active types; active transport requires energy (e.g., ATP), passive relies on concentration gradients.
3
无电荷分子的扩散遵循菲克定律，最终达到浓度平衡。 The diffusion of uncharged molecules follows Fick's law and finally reaches equilibrium.
4
离子的跨膜运动会涉及电势变化，平衡电位由浓度梯度和电场共同决定。 Ion movement across the membrane involves changes in membrane potential; the equilibrium/reversal potential is jointly determined by the concentration gradient and the electric field.

反转电位与Na/K ATP酶泵 Reversal Potential and Na/K ATPase Pump

1
反转电位基本概念

Reversal potential basic concept

反转电位是指对于某种离子来说，当膜两侧达到特定电位时，该离子不再有净跨膜流动。 The reversal potential for an ion is the membrane potential at which there is no net ion flux.

每种离子的反转电位由其在细胞内外的浓度决定。 The reversal potential of each ion is determined by its concentration difference between the inside and outside of the cell.

2
内外离子浓度分布

Distribution of intra- and extracellular ion concentrations

钾离子（K⁺）：细胞内高（140 mM），细胞外低（5 mM）。 Potassium (K⁺): high inside the cell (140 mM), low outside (5 mM).

钠离子（Na⁺）：细胞内低（10 mM），细胞外高（140 mM）。 Sodium (Na⁺): low inside (10 mM), high outside (140 mM).

钙离子（Ca²⁺）：细胞内极低（＜1 μM），细胞外高（2 mM）。 Calcium (Ca²⁺): extremely low inside (＜1 μM), high outside (2 mM).

氯离子（Cl⁻）：细胞内中等（20 mM），细胞外高（140 mM）。 Chloride (Cl⁻): moderate inside (20 mM), high outside (140 mM).

3
尼恩斯特方程与反转电位的计算

Nernst equation and calculation of reversal potential

反转电位可以用尼恩斯特方程计算
The reversal potential can be calculated using the Nernst equation: 公式见👈
RR
理想气体常数（8.314 J·K⁻¹·mol⁻¹） RR: Ideal gas constant (8.314 J·K⁻¹·mol⁻¹)
TT
温度（单位：开尔文K） TT: Temperature (in Kelvin)
zz
离子的价数 zz: Valence of the ion
FF
法拉第常数（96485 C·mol⁻¹） FF: Faraday constant (96485 C·mol⁻¹)
cece​/cici​
细胞外/内离子浓度 cece​/cici​: extracellular/intracellular ion concentration
4
主要离子的典型反转电位

Typical reversal potentials for major ions

钠离子 ENa=+60ENa​=+60 mV Sodium ENa=+60ENa​=+60 mV

钾离子 EK=−100EK​=−100 mV Potassium EK=−100EK​=−100 mV

钙离子 ECa=+100ECa​=+100 mV Calcium ECa=+100ECa​=+100 mV

氯离子 ECl=−50ECl​=−50 mV Chloride ECl=−50ECl​=−50 mV

老师强调，反转电位是电势梯度与浓度梯度共同作用的结果。 The lecturer emphasized that the reversal potential is determined by the balance between the electrical and concentration gradients.

5
Na/K ATP酶泵的作用

Role of Na/K ATPase pump

Na/K ATP酶泵通过主动转运每次将3个Na⁺泵出细胞，2个K⁺泵入细胞。 The Na/K ATPase pump actively transports 3 Na⁺ out of the cell and 2 K⁺ into the cell per cycle.

该过程消耗1个ATP分子，维持了细胞内外Na⁺和K⁺的浓度梯度。 This process consumes one ATP molecule and maintains the concentration gradients of Na⁺ and K⁺ across the membrane.

维持电化学梯度对于激发性细胞的电活动至关重要。 Maintenance of the electrochemical gradient is essential for the electrical activity of excitable cells.

老师讲到，缺乏这个梯度细胞就无法发生膜电位变化，也就无法兴奋和传导信号。 The lecturer pointed out that without this gradient, cells would not be able to generate membrane potential changes and could not be excitable or conduct signals.

重点总结 Key Summaries

反转电位反映了特定离子在膜上达到“平衡”无净流动时的膜电位。 The reversal potential reflects the membrane potential at which a particular ion is at equilibrium with no net flow.

Na/K ATP酶泵通过消耗ATP建立并维持Na⁺和K⁺的浓度梯度，是细胞电兴奋性的基础。 The Na/K ATPase pump uses ATP to establish and maintain Na⁺ and K⁺ gradients, forming the basis for cellular excitability.

膜电位Membrane potential

1
膜电位定义 Membrane potential definition

膜电位是细胞内外之间的电位差。 Membrane potential is the potential difference between the intracellular and extracellular compartments.

2
离子跨膜分布与方向 Transmembrane distribution and movement directions of ions

钠离子（Na+）主要在细胞外，倾向进入细胞。 Sodium ions (Na+) are mainly outside the cell and tend to enter the cell.

氯离子（Cl-）主要在细胞外，倾向进入细胞。 Chloride ions (Cl-) are mostly outside the cell and tend to enter the cell.

钾离子（K+）主要在细胞内，倾向移出细胞。 Potassium ions (K+) are mostly inside the cell and tend to leave the cell.

钙离子（Ca2+）主要在细胞外，倾向进入细胞。 Calcium ions (Ca2+) are mainly outside the cell and tend to enter the cell.

3
膜电位形成机制 Mechanism of membrane potential formation

离子的分布不均及其选择性通透性导致膜两侧带不同电荷。 The uneven distribution of ions and their selective permeability make the two sides of the membrane carry different charges.

膜内相对带负电，膜外相对带正电。 The intracellular side is relatively negative, and the extracellular side is relatively positive.

4
Goldman-Hodgkin-Katz（GHK）方程 Goldman-Hodgkin-Katz (GHK) voltage equation

GHK方程用于计算多种单价离子共同参与时的膜电位。 The GHK equation is used to calculate membrane potential when multiple monovalent ions are involved.

公式如下（纯文字表达）： Formula (plain text representation):

Vm = (RT/F) * ln { PNa*[Na+]out + PK*[K+]out + PCl*[Cl-]in } / { PNa*[Na+]in + PK*[K+]in + PCl*[Cl-]out }

其中，Vm为膜电位，PNa、PK、PCl分别是钠、钾、氯离子的通透性，[Na+]out/in、[K+]out/in、[Cl-]out/in是内外浓度，R为气体常数，T为温度，F为法拉第常数。 Here, Vm is membrane potential; PNa, PK, and PCl are permeabilities for Na+, K+, and Cl-; [Na+]out/in, [K+]out/in, and [Cl-]out/in are inner/outer concentrations; R is the gas constant; T is temperature; F is the Faraday constant.

5
欧姆定律与电导率表达式 Ohm’s law and conductance equation
欧姆定律
U = R · I。 Ohm’s law: U = R · I.

电导（G）为电阻的倒数：G = 1 / R。 Conductance (G) is the reciprocal of resistance: G = 1 / R.

1
多离子条件下的膜电位公式 Membrane potential formula under multiple ion conditions

可利用各离子的电导和反转电位算出总膜电位。 Total membrane potential can be calculated by conductance and reversal potentials of different ions.

公式如下（纯文字表达）： Formula (plain text representation):

Vm = (GKEK + GNaENa + GClECl + GCaECa) / (GK + GNa + GCl + GCa)

其中，GK、GNa、GCl、GCa为各离子的电导，EK、ENa、ECl、ECa为各离子的反转电位。 Here, GK, GNa, GCl, and GCa are the conductances of K+, Na+, Cl-, and Ca2+; EK, ENa, ECl, and ECa are their corresponding reversal potentials.

2
课堂讲解补充 Additional points from lecture

老师特别指出，离子的流动依赖其浓度梯度和电位梯度。 The lecturer emphasized that ion flux depends on both concentration and electrical gradients.

每种离子的反转电位由内外浓度和电荷数共同决定。 The reversal potential for each ion is determined by intra- and extracellular concentrations and their charge.

多种离子同时作用时，膜电位是各离子的影响加权平均结果。 When multiple ions are involved, membrane potential is the weighted average of each ion’s effect.

驱动力Driving Force

1
反转电位的公式 Formula for reversal potential
课件给出的Nernst方程为
The Nernst equation as given in the slide is:   E_ion = (RT / zF) * ln([ion]_e / [ion]_i)

其中， Here, R：气体常数 R: Gas constant T：温度（开尔文） T: Temperature (Kelvin) z：离子的电荷数 z: Ion's valence F：法拉第常数 F: Faraday constant [ion]_e：[离子]的胞外浓度 [ion]_e: Extracellular ion concentration [ion]_i：[离子]的胞内浓度 [ion]_i: Intracellular ion concentration

老师在录音中提示，他之前写的公式有错误，但修正后应为公式如上，分子是胞外浓度，分母是胞内浓度。 The teacher mentioned in the recording that there was a mistake in the previous formula, but the corrected formula should be as above, with the numerator as extracellular concentration and denominator as intracellular concentration5。

2
驱动力的定义 Definition of driving force
驱动力的计算方法为
The driving force is calculated as:   Vm - E_ion Vm - E_ion   其中Vm是实际膜电位，E_ion是该离子的反转电位。 Where Vm is the actual membrane potential, E_ion is the reversal potential for the ion4。
3
驱动力的生理意义与结果 Physiological significance and outcomes of driving force

当 Vm - E_ion > 0 mV 时，阳离子外流或阴离子内流，导致去极化。 When Vm - E_ion > 0 mV, cations exit or anions enter, causing depolarization.

当 Vm - E_ion < 0 mV 时，阳离子内流或阴离子外流，导致复极化或过度极化。 When Vm - E_ion < 0 mV, cations enter or anions exit, causing repolarization or hyperpolarization.

当 Vm - E_ion = 0 mV 时，离子净通量为零，不引起膜电位变化。 When Vm - E_ion = 0 mV, net ion flux is zero, and no modification of membrane potential occurs4。

4
课堂补充说明 Classroom additional notes

老师特别指出，对于每个离子，反转电位就是该离子达平衡时的膜电位，即净流动为零。 The teacher especially emphasized that the reversal potential for each ion is the membrane potential where that ion reaches equilibrium, i.e., net flux is zero5。

对于Nernst方程，老师课上纠正了公式的写法，并说明注意分子分母顺序，否则结果正负号会相反。 For the Nernst equation, the teacher corrected the formula in class and highlighted the importance of the numerator and denominator order, otherwise the sign of the result would be opposite

膜电位记录 Membrane potential recording

1
膜电位的测量方法 Method for measuring membrane potential

使用微电极将其插入细胞内部，通过与外部的参考电极连接，记录内外电位差。 A microelectrode is inserted into the cell, paired with an external reference electrode, to record the potential difference between the inside and outside of the cell.

2
仪器组成与原理 Instrument components and principle

主要装置包括电压-电流转换器（V/A转换器）、微电极和参考电极。 The main setup includes a voltage-to-current converter (V/A), a microelectrode, and a reference electrode.

微电极探测细胞内部（Int），参考电极放在外液（Ext）中。 The microelectrode detects the intracellular region (Int), while the reference electrode is placed in the extracellular solution (Ext).

系统检测并显示两极之间的膜电位。 The system measures and displays the membrane potential between the two electrodes.

3
膜电位记录的意义 Significance of membrane potential recording

可以真实反映细胞静息电位及其激活动作电位的全过程。 This method accurately represents the resting potential and the full process of action potential activation in a cell.

4
课上讲解补充 Additional classroom explanation

老师强调，只有通过穿刺细胞膜的微电极，才能获得真实的细胞内膜电位，而非表面贴附的电极。 The teacher emphasized that only a microelectrode penetrating the cell membrane can measure the true intracellular membrane potential, rather than electrodes attached to the surface.

课件图中还展示了动作电位的一个典型记录，包括去极化（1）、平台期（2）、复极化（3）以及静息期（4）。 The figure also shows a typical action potential record, including depolarization (1), plateau (2), repolarization (3), and resting phase (4).

这种记录方法广泛用于心肌细胞和神经细胞的电生理研究。 This recording method is widely used in electrophysiological studies of cardiomyocytes and neurons.

心脏中的血流与传导系统及心电图 Blood Flow, Conduction System and ECG in the Heart

1
心脏中的血流路径 Blood flow through the heart

全身静脉血首先经上下腔静脉流入右心房。 Systemic venous blood first enters the right atrium via the superior and inferior vena cava.

经三尖瓣进入右心室，再通过肺动脉瓣流向肺动脉，运送到肺部进行气体交换。 Blood passes through the tricuspid valve into the right ventricle, then exits via the pulmonary valve into the pulmonary artery and goes to the lungs for gas exchange.

富氧血经肺静脉流回左心房，穿过二尖瓣进入左心室。 Oxygenated blood returns via the pulmonary vein to the left atrium, passes through the mitral valve into the left ventricle.

最后经主动脉瓣被射入主动脉，泵送到全身。 Finally, blood is ejected through the aortic valve into the aorta and distributed throughout the body.

正常成年人的心率为每分钟60-100次。 The normal adult heart rate is 60-100 cycles per minute.

2
心脏传导系统 Cardiac conduction system

窦房结处于右心房上部，是主要的起搏点。 The sinoatrial (SA) node is located in the upper right atrium and acts as the primary pacemaker.

电活动自窦房结发起，经右心房传导至房室结（AV结）。 Electrical activity starts at the SA node, spreads through the right atrium to the atrioventricular (AV) node.

然后信号通过希氏束（His bundle）、左右束支，最终传至浦肯野纤维，促使心室同步收缩。 The signal travels via the bundle of His and left/right bundle branches, finally reaching Purkinje fibers to ensure coordinated ventricular contraction.

该系统保证心脏按序激活并高效泵血。 This system ensures orderly activation and efficient pumping of the heart.

3
心电图基础及波形含义 Electrocardiogram (ECG) basics and wave interpretation

P波代表心房去极化，即心房收缩。 The P wave represents atrial depolarization, i.e., atrial contraction.

PQ间期反映冲动从窦房结到房室结的传播。 The PQ interval reflects propagation of the impulse from the SA node to the AV node.

QRS波群代表心室去极化，其中： The QRS complex represents ventricular depolarization. -- Q波：室间隔去极化。 Q wave: Interventricular septum depolarization. -- R波：心室主要部分的去极化。 R wave: Main mass of ventricular depolarization. -- S波：心室去极化的最后阶段（心底）。 S wave: Last phase of ventricular depolarization (base).

QRS波群期间也发生心房复极化，但波形被掩盖。 Atrial repolarization also occurs during the QRS but is masked.

ST段对应心室动作电位平台期，代表心室肌收缩期。 The ST segment corresponds to the plateau phase of the ventricular action potential and represents ventricular contraction.

T波表示心室复极化。 The T wave indicates ventricular repolarization.

4
课堂补充与核心理解 Key additions and clarifications from lecture

老师强调，心脏的血液流动与电信号传导密切协同，是实现有效泵血的基础。 The instructor emphasized that cardiac blood flow and electrical conduction are closely synchronized, forming the basis for effective pumping.

心脏起搏点顺序激活能保证心房-心室收缩的先后及血液单向流动。 Sequential activation of pacemaking points ensures proper timing of atrial and ventricular contraction and unidirectional blood flow.

心电图的每一波形均对应心脏内特定部位的电激活，其形态和时间关系对于判断心律及疾病有重要意义。 Each waveform in the ECG corresponds to distinct electrical events in the heart, and their shape/timing are vital for assessing rhythm and detecting pathology.

心脏动作电位 Cardiac Action Potentials

1
不同部位心脏细胞的动作电位具有不同形态 The action potentials of cardiac cells from different regions have distinct shapes

窦房结（Sinus node）、心房肌（Atrial muscle）、房室结（A-V node）、希氏束（Common bundle）、束支（Bundle branches）、浦肯野纤维（Purkinje fibers）和心室肌（Ventricular muscle）动作电位曲线各异。 The action potential curves are different among the sinus node, atrial muscle, A-V node, common bundle, bundle branches, Purkinje fibers, and ventricular muscle.

2
窦房结动作电位最先产生，代表心脏自律性 The sinus node generates the earliest action potential, representing the heart's automaticity

窦房结（SA node）动作电位上升较缓，无真正的静息期，因其特殊的慢去极化特性赋予了心脏起搏功能。 The SA node action potential rises slowly and lacks a true resting phase, due to unique slow depolarization that endows pacemaker function.

3
房室结动作电位速度慢，起到延迟传导作用 The A-V node action potential is slower, providing a conduction delay

房室结动作电位上升速度慢，有助于确保心房收缩完成后心室再收缩。 Slow upstroke in the A-V node action potential helps ensure that the ventricles contract only after atria finish contracting.

4
心肌细胞（包括心室、浦肯野纤维等）动作电位特征明显 Myocardial (ventricular, Purkinje) cells have characteristic action potentials

心室肌、浦肯野纤维的动作电位上升迅速，平台期明显，便于有效泵血。 Ventricular and Purkinje fiber action potentials have rapid upstroke and distinct plateau phases, facilitating effective pumping.

5
动作电位传导顺序与心脏结构一致，推动血液有序流动 The propagation order of action potentials matches cardiac structure, driving orderly blood flow

动作电位依次出现在窦房结、心房、房室结、希氏束、束支、浦肯野纤维、心室肌，确保从心房到心室的收缩传递。 Action potentials appear sequentially in the SA node, atria, AV node, His bundle, bundle branches, Purkinje fibers, and ventricular muscle to ensure contraction propagates from atria to ventricles.

6
不同部位动作电位的电生理意义 Electrophysiological significance of action potentials in different regions

起搏细胞（如窦房结、浦肯野纤维）能自发产生动作电位，心肌细胞则以收缩为主。 Pacemaker cells (SA node, Purkinje fibers) can spontaneously generate action potentials, while myocardial cells are mainly responsible for contraction.

7
动作电位的形态差异与心电图（ECG）的各波段密切相关 The shape variation of action potentials in each region closely relates to ECG waveforms

不同心脏部位的动作电位时程和幅度，决定了P波、QRS波群、T波等在心电图中的形成和特性。 The duration and amplitude of action potentials in different parts determine the formation and characteristics of the P wave, QRS complex, and T wave in ECG1525。

8
课堂补充说明 Additional classroom explanation

老师强调，浦肯野纤维动作电位平台期特别长，这对心室同步收缩非常关键。 The instructor emphasized that the Purkinje fiber action potential has an especially long plateau, which is crucial for synchronous ventricular contraction.

不同细胞动作电位的不同，有助于保证心脏电活动的高度协调和高效泵血。 The diversity of action potentials among cell types ensures highly coordinated electrical activity and efficient pumping of blood.

心室细胞的性质与兴奋-收缩耦联 Ventricular Cell Properties and Excitation-Contraction Coupling

1
心室细胞的形态与结构 Morphology and Structure of Ventricular Cells

呈棒状，具分支。 Stick shape with ramifications.

宽约25微米，长约100微米，厚度小于20微米。 Width is about 25 μm, length about 100 μm, thickness less than 20 μm.

有明显横纹。 Striated.

多为单一中央细胞核，有时为双核。 Single central nucleus, sometimes two nuclei.

具备三个基本功能
兴奋性、收缩性、传导性。 Possess three fundamental properties: excitability, contractility, and conductivity.
2
心室细胞的三大基本功能 Three Major Functions of Ventricular Cells
兴奋性
对电信号能做出反应并产生动作电位。 Excitability: Respond to electrical stimuli and generate action potentials.
收缩性
能将电信号转化为机械收缩，实现力量输出。 Contractility: Convert electrical signal into mechanical contraction to generate force.
传导性
能够将电信号沿细胞或细胞间快速有效传递。 Conductivity: Transmit electrical signals rapidly and efficiently within or between cells.
3
兴奋-收缩耦联的定义及过程 Definition and Process of Excitation-Contraction Coupling

兴奋-收缩耦联是指电信号（动作电位）诱发机械收缩的机制。 Excitation-contraction coupling is the mechanism by which an electrical signal (action potential) induces mechanical contraction.

动作电位引发膜去极化，进而打开膜上的L型Ca2+Ca2+通道，Ca2+Ca2+流入细胞。 The action potential causes membrane depolarization, opening the L-type Ca2+Ca2+ channels and leading to Ca2+Ca2+ influx.

4
分子机制与离子流变化 Molecular Mechanism and Ionic Movements

膜去极化后，Ca2+Ca2+穿过L型Ca2+Ca2+通道进入细胞，引发肌质网（SR）上的RyR受体介导的大量Ca2+Ca2+释放。 After depolarization, Ca2+Ca2+ enters via L-type channels and triggers the RyR receptors on the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR) to release more Ca2+Ca2+.

Ca2+Ca2+与肌丝结合，引发肌丝滑行，实现肌肉收缩。 Ca2+Ca2+ binds to myofilaments, triggering filament sliding and muscle contraction.

随后Ca2+Ca2+通过SR的Ca-ATP酶泵、胞膜的Na++-Ca2+2+交换体（NCX）和线粒体等被清除，细胞回到静息状态。 Then, Ca2+Ca2+ is removed via the SR Ca-ATPase pump, Na++-Ca2+2+ exchanger (NCX) on the sarcolemma, and mitochondria, returning the cell to rest.

5
兴奋收缩耦联的关键媒介物——Ca2+Ca2+ Key Mediator of Excitation-Contraction Coupling: Ca2+Ca2+

Ca2+Ca2+作为信号分子，把动作电位转换成机械收缩，是细胞级别信号传递的核心。 Ca2+Ca2+ serves as the signaling molecule transitioning the action potential into mechanical contraction, the core of cell-level signal transduction.

6
示意图与老师补充 Illustration and Instructor’s Additional Notes

图示展示了Ca2+Ca2+从膜、T管到肌质网及线粒体的动态流动路径，并有AP、[Ca2+][Ca2+]与收缩三者的时间关系曲线。 The diagram shows the Ca2+Ca2+ movement pathway across the membrane, T-tubule, SR, and mitochondria, along with a time-course of AP, [Ca2+][Ca2+], and contraction.

老师强调，兴奋-收缩耦联的高效性来源于Ca2+Ca2+的放大作用和SR储存释放能力，是心肌同步强有力收缩的物质基础。 The instructor emphasized that the efficiency of excitation-contraction coupling derives from the amplifying action of Ca2+Ca2+ and the storage/release capacity of the SR, underpinning strong and synchronous cardiac contraction3.

膜片钳技术 Patch-Clamp Technique

1
膜片钳定义与基本原理 Definition and Basic Principle of Patch-Clamp

膜片钳技术是一种通过玻璃微电极将细胞膜局部或整个细胞电隔离的方法，可向其施加电流（电流钳）或电压（电压钳），并记录细胞的电生理反应。 The patch-clamp technique allows for the electrical isolation of a membrane patch or whole cell using a glass microelectrode, enabling the application of either current (current clamp) or voltage (voltage clamp) and recording of the electrophysiological response3。

2
发展历史与重要贡献 Development History and Key Contributions

该技术由Neher和Sakmann于1978年首次开发，并在1981年进一步完善。 This technique was first developed by Neher and Sakmann in 1978 and further improved in 19813。

二者因膜片钳技术开创性工作获得1991年诺贝尔生理学或医学奖。 Neher and Sakmann were awarded the Nobel Prize in Medicine in 1991 for their pioneering work on patch-clamp.

3
技术要点——高阻抗密封 Technical Key Point: High-Resistance Seal

膜片钳中，玻璃吸管与细胞膜之间封接电阻极高（通常为GΩ级别），以保证信号准确性和单通道电流的精确测量。 In patch-clamp, the seal resistance between the glass pipette and the cell membrane is extremely high (usually in the GigaOhm range), ensuring signal accuracy and enabling precise measurement of single-channel currents3。

4
膜片钳的多种模式 Various Modes of Patch-Clamp
主要操作模式包括
细胞贴附（cell-attached）、全细胞（whole-cell）、膜内外（inside-out、outside-out）。 The major operational modes include: cell-attached, whole-cell, inside-out, and outside-out3。
操作流程
用玻璃微管贴附于膜表，形成高阻密封后，可以吸出局部膜片或进入全细胞模式。 Operating procedure: a glass micropipette is attached to the membrane to form a high-resistance seal, after which a patch of membrane can be excised or whole-cell configuration achieved.
5
装置组成与基本仪器连线 Setup Components and Basic Instrumentation

常规设置包含计算机、模拟-数字转换器、放大器、示波器、前置放大器、玻璃电极和灌流池。 The standard setup includes a computer, analog-to-digital converter, amplifier, oscilloscope, pre-amplifier, glass pipette, and perfusion chamber.

参考电极置于灌流池中，用于记录膜电位差。 Reference electrode is placed in the perfusion chamber to record membrane potential differences3。

6
全细胞模式的电路模型与基本参数 Circuit Model and Basic Parameters in Whole-Cell Configuration
系统可等效为
The system can be modeled as: Vimp：外加电压（imposedpotential）Vimp​：外加电压（imposedpotential）Vm：膜电位（membranepotential）Vm​：膜电位（membranepotential）Rs：串联电阻（seriesresistance）Rs​：串联电阻（seriesresistance）Rm：膜电阻（主要由离子通道决定，membraneresistance）Rm​：膜电阻（主要由离子通道决定，membraneresistance）Cm：膜电容（主要由脂质双层决定，membranecapacitance）Cm​：膜电容（主要由脂质双层决定，membranecapacitance）Vm=Rm×IVm​=Rm​×I

电压钳（voltage-clamp）：对膜施加指定电压，记录跨膜电流，常用于研究离子通道电流，总电流公式为 I=N⋅Po⋅iI=N⋅Po​⋅i （NN为通道数，PoPo​为开放概率，ii为单通道电流）。 Voltage clamp: a fixed potential is imposed on the membrane, and the transmembrane current is recorded, often to study ion channel currents. The total current is I=N⋅Po⋅iI=N⋅Po​⋅i (NN is the number of channels, PoPo​ is open probability, ii is single channel current)3。

电流钳（current-clamp）：对膜施加电流，记录膜电位变化。 Current clamp: a fixed current is imposed, and the variation of membrane potential is recorded.

7
课堂补充与讲解细节 Instructor’s Clarifications and Details

老师强调膜片钳的突破在于能区分并精确测量单一离子通道的电流活动，对于膜电生理研究具有革命性意义。 The instructor emphasized that the breakthrough of patch-clamp lies in distinguishing and precisely measuring single ion channel activity, which is revolutionary for membrane electrophysiology3。

补充说明膜片钳操作过程中，通道开放概率和单通道电流幅度可反映通道的生理调控状态。 The open probability and single-channel current amplitude measured during patch-clamp reflect the physiological regulation status of ion channels.

电压门控通道与心室动作电位Voltage-gated Channels and Ventricular Action Potential

1
动作电位的产生依赖于三类主要的电压门控离子通道：Na⁺、K⁺和Ca²⁺通道。 The generation of action potential depends on three main types of voltage-gated ion channels: Na⁺, K⁺, and Ca²⁺ channels.
2
电压门控钠通道负责快速去极化。 Voltage-gated sodium channels are responsible for rapid depolarization.

当膜电位阈值被触发时，Na⁺通道开放，使Na⁺迅速内流，导致膜电位迅速变正，实现“0期”去极化。 When the membrane potential hits the threshold, Na⁺ channels open, allowing rapid Na⁺ influx, causing the membrane potential to become rapidly positive, known as phase 0 depolarization17。

3
电压门控钾通道介导复极化。 Voltage-gated potassium channels mediate repolarization.

在动作电位“3期”，K⁺通道开放，K⁺外流，使膜电位回到负值，完成复极化。 During phase 3 of the action potential, K⁺ channels open, K⁺ exits the cell, returning the membrane potential to negative values, completing repolarization17。

4
电压门控钙通道参与维持平台期和辅助去极化。 Voltage-gated calcium channels contribute to the plateau phase and assist depolarization.

在“2期”，L型Ca²⁺通道开放，Ca²⁺缓慢内流，导致动作电位出现平台，维持较长的正电位，有利于心肌细胞收缩。 During phase 2, L-type Ca²⁺ channels open, and a slow Ca²⁺ influx creates the plateau, maintaining a prolonged depolarized state, which facilitates cardiomyocyte contraction17。

5
动作电位的五个时相（以人心室肌为例）： The action potential has five phases (taking human ventricular myocytes as an example):
4期
静息期，膜电位维持在接近K⁺的平衡电位（约-90 mV）； Phase 4: Resting phase, membrane potential remains near K⁺ equilibrium potential (about -90 mV);
0期
钠通道开放，快速去极化； Phase 0: Sodium channels open, rapid depolarization;
1期
部分钾通道（瞬时外向电流）开放，初始小复极化； Phase 1: Some potassium channels (transient outward current) open, initial brief repolarization;
2期
钙通道开放，平台期，钙内流与钾外流平衡； Phase 2: Calcium channels open, plateau phase, balance of Ca²⁺ influx and K⁺ efflux;
3期
钾通道大量开放，快速复极化，膜电位恢复至静息水平。 Phase 3: Potassium channels fully open, rapid repolarization, membrane potential returns to resting level17。

心室肌细胞动作电位的物种差异Difference of Ventricular Cardiomyocytes Action Potential Between Species

6
人与小鼠心室肌细胞动作电位在形态和离子流分布上有明显差异。 There are distinct differences in shape and ionic current distribution of ventricular action potentials between humans and mice.
7
人类心室肌动作电位平台期明显，持续时间更长。 Human ventricular action potentials have a prominent and prolonged plateau phase.

小鼠心室肌动作电位更短，平台期不明显，复极速度更快。 Mouse ventricular myocyte action potentials are shorter, with less obvious plateau, and faster repolarization17。

主要参与电流及其特征
Main contributing currents and their features:
INaINa​
钠内流，负责0期去极化（人和鼠均显著）； INaINa​: Sodium influx, responsible for phase 0 depolarization (prominent in both human and mouse);

ICa,LICa,L​：L型钙内流，维持平台期（人>鼠）； ICa,LICa,L​: L-type calcium influx, maintains plateau (more prominent in human);

INCXINCX​
Na⁺/Ca²⁺交换电流，也辅助2期； INCXINCX​: Na⁺/Ca²⁺ exchange current, also assists phase 2;

Ito,fIto,f​, Ito,sIto,s​：瞬时外向钾流，1期初期复极（鼠占主导，人中等）； Ito,fIto,f​, Ito,sIto,s​: Transient outward K⁺ current, phase 1 repolarization (dominant in mouse, moderate in human);

IKsIKs​, IKrIKr​：缓慢和快速延迟整流钾流，参与3期复极（对人更重要）； IKsIKs​, IKrIKr​: Slow and rapid delayed rectifier K⁺ current, mediate phase 3 (more important in human);

其它如IK,slowIK,slow​、ISSISS​、IK1IK1​、IKATPIKATP​等调控复极的细致特征。 Others such as IK,slowIK,slow​, ISSISS​, IK1IK1​, IKATPIKATP​ contribute to fine modulation of repolarization17。

9
教师强调，平台期的存在对于心肌收缩持续和有效泵血至关重要，人比鼠更为显著。 The instructor emphasized that the plateau phase is vital for sustained contraction and effective pumping of the heart, being more evident in humans.
10
物种间离子通道表达差异导致动作电位时程和心率等生理特性差异。 Differences in ion channel expression between species result in varied action potential duration and physiological features such as heart rate

、

膜静息电位
I_K1 Membrane resting potential: I_K1
1
内向整流钾通道决定细胞膜的静息电位。 Inward rectifier K⁺ channels are responsible for the membrane resting potential.
2
I_K1通道由Kir2.1和Kir2.2亚基构成的异源四聚体组成。 I_K1 channels are heterotetramers composed of Kir2.1 and Kir2.2 subunits.
3
I_K1电流主要维持细胞膜电位处于-80 mV左右，确保心室肌细胞在静息状态下稳定。 The I_K1 current maintains the membrane potential at approximately -80 mV, ensuring ventricular myocytes are stably at rest11.
4
I_K1通道在静息和整个动作电位阶段几乎始终开放，特别是在静息期（第4期）有最大通透性。 I_K1 channels are almost always open during the resting phase (phase 4) and throughout the action potential, with maximal permeability during rest11.
5
该通道产生的电流是维持静息高K⁺通透性、稳定负膜电位的主要来源。 The current through these channels is the main source for maintaining high K⁺ permeability and thus the stable negative membrane potential11.
6
I_K1通道对铯离子和钡离子敏感，这两者可有效阻断I_K1电流。 The I_K1 channel is sensitive to cesium and barium ions, which can effectively block I_K1 current.
讲解补充
老师强调I_K1的生理学意义。 Additional explanation: The instructor emphasized the physiological importance of I_K1.

当细胞膜电位接近E_K（K⁺平衡电位，-90 mV左右），I_K1通道处于最大开放状态。 When the membrane potential is near E_K (the K⁺ equilibrium potential, about -90 mV), I_K1 channels are maximally open11。

此时K⁺外流主导，帮助细胞维持静息-极化状态。 At this point, K⁺ efflux dominates, helping the cell maintain its hyperpolarized, resting state11。

在去极化时（动作电位高电位），I_K1通道部分关闭，减少K⁺外流，有助于心肌细胞动作电位的形成和持续。 During depolarization (high potential during action potential), the I_K1 channel partially closes, reducing K⁺ efflux, which assists in forming and sustaining the action potential11。

老师还强调I_K1通道在窦房结细胞等自律细胞中几乎不存在，这是这些细胞能自主产生活动电位而不被极化钳制的原因。 The instructor also highlighted that I_K1 channels are absent in pacemaker cells (e.g., SA node), allowing these cells to spontaneously generate action potentials without being clamped at a hyperpolarized state11。

8
图中所示I_K1的电流-电压关系曲线反映了其在负电位下的强内向电流特性（曲线在-80 mV附近为内向）。 The current-voltage curve shown reflects the strong inward current characteristic at negative potentials (the curve is inward around -80 mV)。

电压门控钠通道Voltage-gated sodium channels

电压门控钾通道Voltage-gated potassium channels

一、电压门控钠通道Voltage-gated sodium channels

1
电压门控钠通道主要参与动作电位的第0期（快速去极化）。 Voltage-gated sodium channels are primarily responsible for phase 0 (rapid depolarization) of the action potential.
2
当膜电位达到阈值（约-70~-60 mV）时，钠通道迅速开放，导致Na⁺快速内流，使膜电位迅速升高。 When the membrane potential reaches threshold (around -70~-60 mV), sodium channels open rapidly, resulting in fast Na⁺ influx and a sudden increase in membrane potential11。
3
钠通道的开放是瞬时的，并且会迅速失活，导致电流快速衰减。 The opening of sodium channels is transient, and they inactivate quickly, causing the sodium current to decay rapidly11。
4
钠通道开启导致的电流INaINa​是方向依赖的，其电流-电压关系如右图所示： The sodium current INaINa​ is voltage-dependent, as shown by the current-voltage plot on the right:

在负电位下（低于-40 mV），通道基本关闭，电流接近于零。 At negative potentials (below -40 mV), channels are mostly closed and current is close to zero.

随着去极化，INaINa​迅速增加，当电位接近0 mV时达到峰值，然后随着电位进一步升高，电流逐渐减小。 With depolarization, INaINa​ increases rapidly, peaks near 0 mV, then decreases as the potential rises further11。

当膜电位接近Na⁺反转电位（约+60 mV）时，钠流变为外流，电流变为正值。 As the membrane approaches the Na⁺ reversal potential (about +60 mV), the sodium current reverses direction and becomes outward (positive current).

5
老师强调，钠通道失活是“快失活”，保证一次动作电位只产生一个激活，防止高频兴奋。 The instructor emphasized that sodium channel inactivation is “fast inactivation,” ensuring each action potential generates only one activation and prevents high-frequency excitability11。
6
钠通道可被河豚毒素（TTX）特异性阻断，是鉴定Na⁺流的经典方法。 Sodium channels can be specifically blocked by tetrodotoxin (TTX), which is a classical method to identify Na⁺ currents17。

二、电压门控钾通道Voltage-gated potassium channels

1
电压门控钾通道主导动作电位的复极化，即第1~3期，维持细胞的电稳定性。 Voltage-gated potassium channels govern repolarization phases (phases 1-3) of the action potential, maintaining cell electrical stability17。
2
钾通道由4个α亚基组成，每个亚基有6个跨膜区(S4为电压传感器)。 Potassium channels are composed of 4 α-subunits, each with 6 transmembrane domains; S4 acts as the voltage sensor.
3
主要亚基表示为Kvx.xKvx.x​，如Kv1.2Kv​1.2等。 Main subunits are denoted as Kvx.xKvx.x​, such as Kv1.2Kv​1.2.
4
钾通道家族极为多样，已知有40多种，按氨基酸序列分为12个主要组（Kv1Kv1​–Kv12Kv12​）。 The potassium channel family is highly diverse, with more than 40 types divided into 12 groups based on amino acid sequence (Kv1Kv1​–Kv12Kv12​)。
5
动作电位期间，钾通道的逐步开放决定了不同阶段的复极速度。 During the action potential, sequential opening of different potassium channels determines the pace of repolarization.
6
动作电位的第一期（phase 1）是由瞬时外向钾流(ItoIto​)介导的初始复极； Phase 1 is mediated by transient outward K⁺ current (ItoIto​), causing initial repolarization17。 第二期（phase 2）平台期，钾外流与钙内流平衡； Phase 2 (plateau) reflects a balance between K⁺ efflux and Ca²⁺ influx； 第三期（phase 3）快速复极，主要由延迟整流钾通道（IKrIKr​, IKsIKs​）完成。 Phase 3 (rapid repolarization) is mainly governed by delayed rectifier potassium channels (IKrIKr​, IKsIKs​)。
7
钾通道的开启速度较钠通道慢，确保动作电位短暂后的及时复极，防止持续去极化。 Potassium channels open more slowly than sodium channels, ensuring prompt repolarization after a brief depolarization and preventing prolonged excitability17。
8
钾通道的功能异常会导致动作电位时程异常及心律失常。 Dysfunction of potassium channels can lead to abnormal action potential duration and arrhythmias.
电压门控钾通道
I_to、I_KS、I_KR Voltage-gated potassium channels: I_to, I_KS, I_KR

一、瞬时外向钾电流 I_to

1
I_to是动作电位早期复极的关键瞬时外向钾电流。

I_to is the transient outward potassium current critical for the early phase of repolarization of the action potential.

2
人类以Kv4.3四聚体与调节亚基KChIP组成，啮齿类为Kv4.2/Kv4.3杂合四聚体。

In humans, it forms a homotetramer of Kv4.3 plus one regulatory subunit KChIP; in rodents, it forms a heterotetramer of Kv4.2/Kv4.3.

I_to有三种状态
关闭、开放、失活。

I_to channels exist in three states: closed, open, and inactivated.

4
该通道失活速度快，导致电流很快衰减。

This channel exhibits fast inactivation, causing the current to quickly decay.

5
I_to可被4-氨基吡啶特异性阻断。

I_to can be specifically blocked by 4-aminopyridine.

通道状态转换简图常见三态模型
初始关闭态C1→预开放关闭态CN→开放态O→失活态I。

The typical state transition model: initial closed state (C1) → closed state before open (CN) → open (O) → inactivated (I).

7
老师说明I_to激活迅速、峰值大、但极易失活，主要负责动作电位1期的早期复极（平台前下降）。

The instructor emphasized that I_to activates rapidly with a large peak but inactivates quickly, mainly responsible for the phase 1 early repolarization of the action potential.

8
I_to的电流-电压关系为随去极化而显著增加，平台期迅速失活，图中所示曲线反映这一特点。

The I_to current-voltage relationship shows a significant increase with depolarization and rapid inactivation during the plateau phase, as shown in the graph.

二、延迟整流钾电流 I_KS

1
I_KS（慢型外向钾电流）激活较慢，属于延迟整流类。

I_KS (slow delayed rectifier potassium current) activates slowly and belongs to the delayed rectifier class.

2
其主要亚基为Kv7.1（KvLQT1），由KCNQ1基因编码，并与调节亚基KCNE1结合。

Its main subunit is Kv7.1 (KvLQT1), encoded by the KCNQ1 gene and associated with the KCNE1 regulatory subunit.

3
I_KS主要参与动作电位的2期（平台期），调节平台期时程。

I_KS mainly participates in phase 2 (plateau) of the action potential, regulating the plateau duration.

4
I_KS可被吲达帕胺（indapamine）阻断。

I_KS can be blocked by indapamine.

5
电流随去极化缓慢激活，逐渐增大，见下方KCNQ1/KCNE1电流轨迹图。

The current gradually increases upon depolarization, as shown in the KCNQ1/KCNE1 current trace graph.

三、快速延迟整流钾电流 I_KR

1
I_KR（快型延迟整流钾电流）是一种比I_KS激活更快，且在更负电位下激活的电流。

I_KR (rapid delayed rectifier potassium current) activates faster and at more negative potentials than I_KS.

2
其主要亚基为Kv11.1（hERG），由KCNH2基因编码，可能与KCNE2调节亚基结合。

Its main subunit is Kv11.1 (hERG), encoded by the KCNH2 gene, likely associated with the KCNE2 regulatory subunit.

3
I_KR主要参与早期复极化。在心脏动作电位3期具有重要作用。

I_KR is mainly involved in the early phase of repolarization, being critical during phase 3 of the cardiac action potential.

4
I_KR可被多种药物阻断，阻断会带来长QT综合征相关的尖端扭转型室速致死风险。

I_KR is blocked by a large number of drugs; blockade increases the risk of torsades de pointes and sudden death associated with long QT syndrome.

5
老师强调I_KR对药物敏感，是心律失常药物安全性评价的关键靶点。

The instructor emphasized that I_KR is highly sensitive to drugs and a key target for cardiac safety evaluation of antiarrhythmic drugs.

电压门控钾通道
I_KR Voltage-gated potassium channels: I_KR
1
I_KR（慢型/快型延迟整流钾电流）是一种激活迅速且在更负电位下开启的电流，比I_KS起始电位更负。 I_KR (rapid delayed rectifier potassium current) is a current that activates rapidly and at more negative potentials than I_KS.
2
其主要亚基为K_v11.1（hERG），由KCNH2基因编码，通常与KCNE2调节亚基结合。 Its main subunit is K_v11.1 (hERG), encoded by the KCNH2 gene, and is probably associated with the KCNE2 regulatory subunit.
3
I_KR参与动作电位复极的早期阶段。 I_KR is involved in the early phase of repolarization.
4
I_KR可被大量药物阻断，阻断后会导致QT间期延长，增加尖端扭转型室速（torsades de pointes）和猝死的风险。 I_KR can be blocked by a large number of drugs, increasing the risk of death due to long QT syndrome-induced torsades de pointes.

电压门控钙通道与钠钙交换体 Voltage-gated Calcium Channels & Na⁺/Ca²⁺ Exchanger

一、电压门控钙通道（L型钙通道，I_CaL） Voltage-gated Calcium Channel (L-type, I_CaL)

α亚基结构
一个孔形成亚基α，由4个重复单位、每个具有6个跨膜区，S4为电压感受器。 The α subunit structure: one pore-forming α subunit, with 4 repeats each containing 6 transmembrane domains, S4 acts as the voltage sensor.
2
心室细胞的主要亚型为Ca_v1.2，属于L型钙通道。 In ventricular cells, the main subtype is Ca_v1.2, which is an L-type calcium channel.
通道有三种状态
关闭、开放、失活。去极化到-40 mV时通道打开。 There are three channel states: closed, open, and inactivated. The channel opens at a membrane potential of -40 mV.
4
通道的状态转换包括初始关闭C1、预开放关闭CN、开放O、失活I。 State transitions include initial closed (C1), closed before open (CN), open (O), and inactivated (I).
5
该通道是心肌细胞Ca²⁺主要进入通路。 This channel is the main pathway for Ca²⁺ entry into cardiomyocytes.
6
钙通道介导的钙流是激发-收缩耦联的核心。 Calcium influx via this channel is key to excitation-contraction coupling.
7
电压门控钙通道可被地氢吡啶类药物和维拉帕米阻断。 Voltage-gated calcium channels can be blocked by dihydropyridine drugs and verapamil.
电流－电压关系
钙电流在-10 mV左右达到最大，随后随着电位升高减少，极化时为内向电流，高正电位时反转。 Current-voltage relationship: the calcium current peaks around -10 mV, then decreases as the voltage increases; it is inward at negative potentials and reverses at high positive potentials.
9
老师强调，在心脏动作电位2期平台期，I_CaL与钾外流平衡决定平台维持和心肌收缩力。 The instructor emphasized that during phase 2 (plateau) of the cardiac action potential, I_CaL balances with outward potassium currents, determining the plateau and contractility.

二、钠/钙交换体（NCX） Sodium/Calcium Exchanger (NCX)

1
心肌细胞的主要亚型为NCX1，由SLC8A1基因编码。 The main isoform in cardiomyocytes is NCX1, encoded by the SLC8A1 gene.
2
正常模式下，NCX每输出1个Ca²⁺同时输入3个Na⁺。 In the normal mode, NCX extrudes 1 Ca²⁺ out of the cell and brings 3 Na⁺ in.
3
该过程带来正电荷内流，具有致去极化作用（电生成性）。 This process is electrogenic, producing an inward current and causing depolarization.
4
NCX既可外排Ca²⁺（正常/正向模式），也可在高Na⁺、高膜电位状态下反向工作，导入Ca²⁺并外排Na⁺（反向模式）。 NCX can extrude Ca²⁺ (normal/forward mode), or, under high Na⁺/high membrane potential, work in reverse to import Ca²⁺ and export Na⁺ (reverse mode).
交换体电流的I-V曲线
负电位下为Ca²⁺外排，正电位、高胞内Na⁺时为Ca²⁺内流。 The I-V curve for NCX: at negative potentials, Ca²⁺ is extruded; at positive potentials with high intracellular Na⁺, Ca²⁺ enters.
6
老师讲解交换体不仅参与细胞钙的外排，还能在特定病理状态下引起钙过载和致心律失常。 The instructor explained that NCX not only removes Ca²⁺ from cells but also, under certain pathological conditions, can cause calcium overload and arrhythmias.

三、总结 Summary

1
I_CaL在动作电位平台期占主导，是兴奋-收缩偶联的核心。 I_CaL dominates during the plateau phase of the action potential and is crucial for excitation-contraction coupling.
2
NCX负责维持收缩后钙的去除，并参与膜电位的调节。 NCX maintains calcium removal after contraction and participates in membrane potential regulation.
3
平台期I_CaL↑维持平台和收缩，NCX工作于后期，帮助Ca²⁺外排，调控舒张。 During the plateau phase, increased I_CaL maintains the plateau and contraction, while NCX works in the later phases to extrude Ca²⁺ and regulate relaxation.

心室复极的性别差异Gender differences in ventricular repolarization

心脏复极跨壁梯度与性别差异Transmural gradient of cardiac repolarization and gender differences

第一页
心室复极的性别差异Gender differences in ventricular repolarization
1
男女性心室肌复极存在差异，反映在动作电位平台期和QT间期等方面。 There are differences in ventricular repolarization between males and females, reflected in the action potential plateau and QT interval.
2
男性早期复极电流（如I_to）和复极相关钾电流（如I_KR, I_KS, I_K1）更大，女性则较小。 Males have larger early repolarization currents (e.g., I_to) and repolarization-related K+ currents (e.g., I_KR, I_KS, I_K1), while these are smaller in females.
3
动作电位时程和QT间期在女性更长，男性较短。 Action potential duration and QT interval are longer in females and shorter in males.
4
这种差异主要受性激素调节——睾酮（上调钾通道和I_to）、雌激素（下调多种K+通道）、孕激素（有上调和下调作用）。 These differences are mainly regulated by sex hormones—testosterone (upregulates K+ channels and I_to), estrogen (downregulates various K+ channels), and progesterone (both up- and downregulation effects).
颜色标注说明
紫色代表在男性电流更大，橙色为女性电流更大，黑色为无本质差异。 Color code: Purple means larger current in males, orange in females, black means no intrinsic difference.
老师强调
睾酮可上调I_K1、I_KR、I_KS、I_to通道蛋白表达；雌激素则下调，导致女性复极电流弱、QT间期长。 Instructor’s point: Testosterone upregulates I_K1, I_KR, I_KS, I_to channel protein expression; estrogen downregulates, leading to weaker repolarizing current and longer QT interval in females.
生理和药理意义
女性QT延长更明显，发生尖端扭转型室速（Torsades de pointes）等心律失常风险更高。 Physiological and pharmacological impact: Females have more pronounced QT prolongation and higher risk of arrhythmias such as torsades de pointes.
第二页
性别差异数据与离子通道Gender differences in ventricular repolarization (Data & Ion channels)
1
在人及多种动物，主要评价参数如动作电位时程（APD）、细胞长度、最大去极化速度（dV/dt）、静息膜电位（RMP）等均有性别差异。 Across humans and animals, key parameters such as action potential duration (APD), cell length, maximum depolarization rate (dV/dt), and resting membrane potential (RMP) show gender differences.
2
总体趋势为APD（动作电位时程）女性↑，男性↓；去极化电流（I_Na）性别差异不显著；外向瞬时钾电流（I_to）、L型钙电流（I_CaL）、延迟整流钾电流（I_KS、I_KR）等性别差异显著。 Overall, APD is ↑ in females and ↓ in males; depolarizing current (I_Na) shows no considerable gender difference; outward transient K+ current (I_to), L-type Ca2+ current (I_CaL), delayed rectifier K+ currents (I_KS, I_KR) show pronounced gender difference.
3
该表总结了不同动物各参数在不同性别间的变化趋势，便于认识性别对心脏电生理的普遍影响。 This table summarizes how various electrophysiological parameters change with gender in different animal species, aiding in understanding the universal impact of gender on cardiac electrophysiology.
第三页
心脏复极跨壁梯度与性别差异Transmural gradient of cardiac repolarization and gender differences
跨壁梯度
心室内心内膜（endocardium）和心外膜（epicardium）间存在复极差异，心外膜I_to等复极化电流更强。 Transmural gradient: A repolarization difference exists between endocardium and epicardium, epicardium has stronger repolarizing currents such as I_to.
2
男性因睾酮作用，心外膜I_to表达增强，跨壁电流梯度大，动作电位早期下降更明显，APD（动作电位时程）更短，QT间期短，J波显著。 Due to testosterone, males have stronger I_to expression in epicardium, a larger transmural gradient, more marked early AP decline, shorter APD and QT interval, and prominent J-waves.
3
女性因雌激素作用，I_to等复极化电流表达弱，跨壁梯度小，APD延长，QT间期延长。 Due to estrogen, females have weaker I_to and other repolarizing currents, a smaller transmural gradient, prolonged APD, and longer QT interval.
风险差异
女性更易发生Long QT综合征和尖端扭转型室速；男性则易发生Brugada综合征、早期复极综合征等。 Risk difference: Females are more prone to long QT syndrome and torsades de pointes; males are more likely to develop Brugada syndrome and early repolarization syndrome.
老师补充
复极跨壁梯度同步性对心脏防止心律失常至关重要——一旦梯度及时序丧失，易诱发室性心律失常。 Instructor’s comment: Synchronized transmural repolarization gradient is crucial in arrhythmia prevention—any gradient/timing loss can trigger ventricular arrhythmias.

公式回顾：

动作电位持续时间和QT间期主要受I_to、I_KR、I_KS、I_K1等复极相关离子通道电流调节，这些离子通道受性别激素调控上/下调：

APD↑​（女性，雌激素↓Ito​,IKR​,IKS​,IK1​）

APD↓ (男性，睾酮↑Ito​,IKR​,IKS​,IK1​）​

心室电活动的昼夜节律Circadian ventricular electrical activity

第一页听课笔记

1
心室电活动具有明显的昼夜节律调控。 Ventricular electrical activity is clearly regulated by the circadian rhythm.
2
课件展示QT间期在24小时内呈周期性波动，夜间QT间期延长，白天缩短。 The slide shows that the QT interval fluctuates periodically over 24 hours, with QT prolongation at night and shortening during the day.
3
这种节律性调控与Kv4.2钾通道（Kcnd2）密切相关。 This rhythmic regulation is closely related to the Kv4.2 potassium channel (Kcnd2).
4
钾通道表达的昼夜波动直接决定心室肌复极电流强弱，进而影响QT间期长短。 Circadian fluctuations in potassium channel expression directly determine the strength of ventricular repolarizing current, thus affecting the QT interval duration.
5
心律失常的发生同样具有明显的昼夜依赖性，不同类型心律失常多见于不同时间段。 The incidence of arrhythmias also shows clear circadian dependence; different types of arrhythmias are more prevalent at different times.
6
夜间QT间期延长，心律失常风险相应升高，尤其是长QT综合征和相关致死性事件。 Prolongation of the QT interval at night is associated with a higher risk of arrhythmias, especially long QT syndrome and related fatal events.
老师补充
昼夜节律调控涉及中枢昼夜节律系统，K+通道如Kcnd2表达受此系统影响，改变心脏复极性质。 Instructor’s addition: The circadian regulation involves central circadian systems, with K+ channels (like Kcnd2) expression modulated by these systems, altering cardiac repolarization properties.

第二页听课笔记

1
Kv4.2（Kcnd2）和KChIP2（Kcnip2）等钾通道基因的表达有显著昼夜波动。 The expression of potassium channel genes such as Kv4.2 (Kcnd2) and KChIP2 (Kcnip2) shows significant circadian fluctuations.
2
白天这些通道蛋白表达逐渐增多，夜间逐步减少。 The expression of these channel proteins increases during the day and decreases at night.
3
钾通道表达高→心室复极电流增强→QT间期缩短。 High potassium channel expression → stronger ventricular repolarizing current → shortened QT interval.
4
钾通道表达低→心室复极电流减弱→QT间期延长。 Low potassium channel expression → weaker ventricular repolarizing current → prolonged QT interval.
5
课件底部第三幅图直观显示QT间期随24小时节律性变化，与K+通道表达波动高度一致。 The third graph at the bottom of the slide directly shows the QT interval changing rhythmically over 24 hours, matching the fluctuation of K+ channel expression.
老师强调
这些分子水平变化是心室复极和QT间期昼夜节律的分子根基，是心电异常发生的基础之一。 Instructor’s emphasis: These molecular changes are the basis for the circadian rhythm of ventricular repolarization and QT interval, and form the molecular basis of cardiac electrical abnormalities.
7
动物实验和临床研究均发现，调控K+通道的昼夜周期会显著影响心律失常的易感性和心电图表现。 Both animal and clinical studies have found that regulating the circadian cycle of K+ channels significantly affects susceptibility to arrhythmias and ECG manifestations.

总结性公式 Summary formula：

心肌细胞的电偶联
缝隙连接Electrical Coupling of Myocytes: Gap Junctions
1
心肌细胞之间通过缝隙连接实现电信号的快速传播。 Electrical signals spread rapidly between myocytes via gap junctions.
2
缝隙连接是一种高电导孔道，对离子和小分子可渗透。 Gap junctions are large-conductance pores permeable to ions and small molecules.
3
每个缝隙连接由两个连接体（connexons）对接形成，每个连接体由6个亚基组成。 Each gap junction is formed by docking of two connexons, and each connexon consists of 6 subunits.
4
缝隙连接的作用是实现心肌细胞的电偶联，使心脏可以同步收缩。 Gap junctions enable electrical coupling of myocytes, allowing synchronized contraction of the heart.
5
在窦房结中心区，主要表达Cx45，传导能力较低，有利于窦房结本身的起搏功能。 In the center of the sinoatrial node (SAN), Cx45 is mainly expressed, which has low conductance and supports the pacemaking function of the SAN.
6
在窦房结外围，既有Cx45，也有Cx43，周边更容易与心房肌电偶联，使激动向心房肌传播。 In the periphery of the SAN, both Cx45 and Cx43 are expressed, facilitating electrical coupling from the SAN to atrial myocytes.
7
心房肌主要表达Cx40和Cx43，均为大电导亚型，有利于心房肌的快速电传导和同步收缩。 Atrial myocytes mainly express Cx40 and Cx43, which are large-conductance subtypes and favor rapid and synchronized conduction in the atria.
8
心室肌主要表达Cx43，保证心室肌细胞间有效的电信号传导与同步收缩。 Ventricular myocytes mainly express Cx43, ensuring effective conduction and synchronized contraction in the ventricles.
9
插图显示肌间盘是细胞间连接的关键区域，包括紧密连接（desmosome）和缝隙连接，后者专门负责电信号传递。 The illustration shows that the intercalated disk is a crucial region for cell connections, including desmosomes (anchoring) and gap junctions (for electrical transmission).
老师补充
窦房结中心的低电导连接有利于绝缘起搏点，外围的大电导连接则负责向心房快速传递起搏信号。 Instructor's addition: Low-conductance connections in the SAN center help insulate the pacemaker site, while large-conductance connections in the periphery enable rapid transmission of the pacemaker signal to the atria.

小结公式 Summary formula：

缝隙连接 = 2 × 6亚基（connexon）= 12亚基

Gap junction = 2 × 6 subunits (connexon) = 12 subunits

SAN中心
Cx45 ↓导通性
Center of SAN
Cx45, ↓ conductance
SAN外围 & 心房肌
Cx43/Cx40 ↑导通性

Peripheral SAN & atria: Cx43/Cx40, ↑ conductance

心室肌
Cx43 ↑导通性
Ventricles
Cx43, ↑ conductance

Electrical Coupling of Myocytes: Cx43与Nav1.5v​1.5在近连接区域共定位Electrical Coupling of Myocytes: colocalization of Cx43 and Nav1.5v​1.5 in perinexus

1
心肌细胞之间的电偶联主要通过间隙连接（Gap Junctions）实现，插图显示其位于插入盘（Intercalated Disk）区域。 Electrical coupling between cardiomyocytes mainly occurs via gap junctions, as illustrated at the intercalated disk area.
2
间隙连接由Cx43半通道组成，允许离子和小分子在细胞间快速流动，实现动作电位的同步传播。 Gap junctions are formed by Cx43 hemichannels, enabling rapid ion and small molecule flow between cells for synchronized action potential propagation.
3
在心肌细胞膜的特殊亚区——近连接区域（Perinexus），存在Cx43间隙连接和钠通道Nav1.5v​1.5的共定位。 In the perinexus, a specialized subdomain of the cardiomyocyte membrane, Cx43 gap junctions and Nav1.5v​1.5 sodium channels are colocalized.
4
插图中可见Nav1.5v​1.5电压门控钠通道（VGSC）紧邻Cx43分布，有助于动作电位的局部产生和传播。 The diagram shows Nav1.5v​1.5 voltage-gated sodium channels closely associated with Cx43, which supports local action potential initiation and propagation.
5
近连接区域（Perinexus）宽度小于30纳米，是电信号耦联的关键结构区域。 The perinexus (<30 nm wide) is a crucial structural region for electrical signal coupling.
课上补充
只有间隙连接还不足以产生新的动作电位，Nav1.5v​1.5必须与Cx43在perinexus共表达，才能有效地实现细胞间电信号的传递和放大。 Class addition: Gap junctions alone are insufficient for action potential initiation in neighboring cells; efficient coupling requires colocalization of Nav1.5v​1.5 with Cx43 in the perinexus for signal transfer and amplification.
7
该共定位结构不仅保证电偶联效率，也有助于防止异常传导、维持心脏正常节律。 This colocalization ensures efficient electrical coupling and helps prevent aberrant conduction, maintaining normal cardiac rhythm.

β肾上腺素能刺激与兴奋-收缩耦联β-adrenergic stimulation and excitation-contraction coupling

1.β-肾上腺素受体激活后，通过Gs蛋白活化腺苷酸环化酶　，提高cAMP水平，进而激活蛋白激酶A（PKA）。 β-adrenergic receptor activation stimulates adenylyl cyclase (AC) via Gs protein, increasing cAMP levels and activating protein kinase A (PKA).
答案 AC
2
PKA磷酸化L型钙通道，导致胞外Ca2+内流增加。 PKA phosphorylates L-type Ca2+2+ channels, increasing extracellular Ca2+ influx.
3
PKA同样促进肌浆网（SR）上的雷诺钙通道（RyR）磷酸化，增强Ca2+诱导的Ca2+释放（CICR），从而增强SR内Ca2+负荷。 PKA phosphorylates ryanodine receptors (RyR) on the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR), enhancing Ca2+-induced Ca2+ release (CICR), increasing SR Ca2+ load.
4
PKA还通过磷酸化磷蛋白（PLB），促进SR Ca2+-ATP酶（SERCA）对Ca2+的再摄取，提高舒张期Ca2+清除能力。 PKA also phosphorylates phospholamban (PLB), enhancing SR Ca\2+-ATPase (SERCA)-mediated Ca2+ reuptake, thereby improving diastolic Ca2+ clearance.
整体效果
Overall effects:

增加Ca2+内流、SR钙释放、CICR和SR Ca2+再摄取 Increased Ca2+ influx, SR Ca2+ release, CICR, and SR Ca2+ reuptake

增强心肌收缩（正性肌力作用） Enhanced contraction (positive inotropic effect)

加速心肌舒张（正性舒张作用） Accelerated relaxation (positive lusitropic effect)

老师补充
β肾上腺素能通路调控Ca2+动态，是心脏应对应激状态、增加心输出量的分子基础。 Class addition: The β-adrenergic signaling pathway regulates Ca2+dynamics and underlies the molecular basis for increased cardiac output during stress.

小结公式 Summary formulas：

电偶联关键复合物（Electrical coupling key complex）：

Cx43 + Nav1.5v​1.5 @ Perinexus → 高效的细胞间电信号传递

Cx43 + Nav1.5v​1.5 @ Perinexus → efficient intercellular electrical signal transfer

β肾上腺素作用下Ca2+途径（Ca2+ handling under β-adrenergic stimulation）：

β-AR↑ → AC↑ → cAMP↑ → PKA↑

→ L型Ca通道P↑（Ca2+入口↑）

→ RyR P↑（CICR↑, SR Ca负荷↑）

→ PLB P↑（SERCA介导的Ca回收↑）

→ 收缩力↑ 舒张加速↑

β-AR↑ → AC↑ → cAMP↑ → PKA↑

→ L-type Ca channel P↑ (Ca2+ influx↑)

→ RyR P↑ (CICR↑, SR Ca load↑)

→ PLB P↑ (SERCA-mediated Ca reuptake↑)

→ Contraction↑ Relaxation↑

心脏肥厚过程中的电生理重构 Electrophysiological remodelling during cardiac hypertrophy

1
心梗后老鼠心肌细胞发生肥大（心肌细胞体积变大）。 Cardiomyocytes develop hypertrophy (increased cell size) after myocardial infarction in rats.

1周及3周心梗后，心肌细胞容量（VCVC​）显著升高。 Both at 1 and 3 weeks post-infarction, myocyte volume (VCVC​) is increased.

3
