心肌细胞动作电位时程（APD）明显延长，延长幅度在1周及3周均有体现，APD20、APD50与APD90均升高，尤以APD90为著。 Action potential duration (APD) is significantly prolonged at both 1 and 3 weeks, for APD20, APD50 and especially APD90.
4
动作电位波形复极期出现了早后去极化（EADs），提示电压门控L型钙通道（ICaLICaL​）再激活。 Early afterdepolarizations (EADs) appear during the repolarization phase, indicating reactivation of L-type Ca2+2+ current (ICaLICaL​).
老师补充
动作电位延长和EADs会显著增加心律失常风险，心肌肥厚与心力衰竭患者常见该现象。 Instructor: APD prolongation and the appearance of EADs significantly raise arrhythmia risk, which is common in hypertrophy and heart failure.

电生理重构的离子机制 Ionic basis of electrophysiological remodelling

6
斜率-电压关系图显示，心梗后L型钙通道电流（ICaLICaL​）显著升高，导致Ca2+流入↑。 Current-voltage relationship plots demonstrate a significant increase in L-type calcium current (ICaLICaL​) post-infarction, resulting in elevated Ca2+ influx.
7
钾通道暂时外向电流（ItoIto​）显著降低，提示钾流减少。 Transient outward potassium current (ItoIto​) is markedly decreased, indicating reduced K++ efflux.
8
L型Ca2+流入↑和ItoIto​↓共同导致动作电位复极变慢，APD延长，为EADs提供基础。 The combination of increased L-type Ca2+ influx and decreased ItoIto​ slows repolarization, prolonging APD and predisposing to EADs.
老师强调
L型Ca2+通道再激活和K+通道表达下调（↓）是心脏肥厚电重构、心律失常易感性升高的分子基础。 Instructor: Reactivation of L-type Ca2+2+ channels and downregulation (↓) of K+ channels underlie electrical remodelling and increased arrhythmogenicity in hypertrophied hearts.

总结性公式 Summary formula：

L型Ca2+通道电流↑ → APD↑ → EADs↑

Increase in L-type Ca2+ current (ICaLICaL​) → increase in APD → increases EADs

Ito​↓ → APD↑

Decrease in ItoIto​ → increase in APD

Electrophysiological remodelling during heart failure 心力衰竭时的电生理重构

1
钠通道电流 I_Na 变化 Sodium channel current (I_Na) changes

总体I_Na无明显变化，晚钠电流（I_Na,late）↑。 Overall I_Na does not change significantly, late sodium current (I_Na,late) increases.

亚基
Nav1.5 Subunit: Nav1.5
讲解补充
老师强调晚钠电流上升会导致细胞内Na+堆积，易导致钙超载，增加心律失常风险。 Extra from lecture: The teacher emphasized that the increase of late sodium current leads to sodium accumulation, predisposes to calcium overload, and raises arrhythmia risk.
2
L型钙通道电流 I_Ca-L 变化 L-type Ca2+ channel current (I_Ca-L) changes

I_Ca-L变化不明显。 No significant change in I_Ca-L.

亚基
Cav1.2 Subunit: Cav1.2
讲解补充
L型钙通道调节心肌收缩，心衰时对此影响有限。 Extra from lecture: L-type Ca2+ channels regulate cardiac contraction; the effect is limited in heart failure.
3
钠钙交换电流 I_Na/Ca (NCX) ↑ Na+-Ca2+ exchanger current (I_Na/Ca, NCX) increases

NCX表达及电流↑。 NCX expression and current increase.

讲解补充
NCX升高能促进Ca2+外排，也会导致膜去极化更明显，增加DADs（延迟后去极化）和心律失常发生。 Extra from lecture: Upregulation of NCX enhances Ca2+ extrusion and causes more depolarization, increasing DADs (delayed afterdepolarizations) and arrhythmia risk.
4
If通道电流（hHCN2,4）变化不明，心力衰竭时去极化速度变慢 If current (hHCN2,4) - changes are uncertain; diastolic depolarization slows in heart failure
通道
hHCN2,4 Channel: hHCN2,4
讲解补充
If电流调控窦房结自律性，心衰时自发去极化变缓，心率降低。 Lecture addition: If current regulates SA node automaticity; in HF, depolarization rate slows, leading to reduced heart rate.
5
暂时外向钾流 I_to（Kv4.3）↓ Transient outward K+ current (I_to, Kv4.3) decreases

Kv4.3表达↓，I_to↓。 Kv4.3 expression decreases, I_to decreases.

讲解补充
I_to下降使动作电位复极变慢，APD延长，为EADs（早后去极化）发生奠定基础。 Extra from lecture: The reduction of I_to leads to slower repolarization, prolonged APD, and facilitates EADs (early afterdepolarizations).
6
延迟整流钾流 I_K（包括I_Kr, I_Ks）变化不确定 Delayed rectifier K+ currents (I_K including I_Kr, I_Ks) are of uncertain change
组成
I_Kr（HERG相关）、I_Ks（KvLQT1+minK）。 Components: I_Kr (HERG), I_Ks (KvLQT1+minK).
讲解补充
延迟整流钾流决定复极末期，变化与心律失常关系密切，但此处未见明确变化。 Lecture note: These currents determine late repolarization; changes closely relate to arrhythmia, but here no definitive change shown.
7
内向整流钾流 I_K1（Kir2.1,2,3）↓ Inward rectifier K+ current (I_K1, Kir2.1,2.2,2.3) decreases

Kir2.1/2.2/2.3表达↓，I_K1↓。 Kir2.1/2.2/2.3 expression decreases, I_K1 decreases.

讲解补充
I_K1减少导致静息电位去极化，细胞更易激发，促发心律失常。 Extra from lecture: Reduced I_K1 causes membrane depolarization, making cells more excitable and promoting arrhythmias.

总结性理解 Summary insight

晚钠电流↑、NCX↑、I_to↓、I_K1↓，共同导致心脏动作电位延长和膜稳定性下降，显著增加心律失常发生率。 Increased late sodium current, NCX, and decreased I_to and I_K1 together cause APD prolongation and reduced membrane stability, substantially raising arrhythmia risk.

重点公式 Key formulas（纯文本）

I_Na,late↑ + NCX↑ → 钙超载、DADs↑、心律失常风险↑ Increased I_Na,late plus increased NCX leads to calcium overload, more DADs, and higher arrhythmia risk

I_to↓ + I_K1↓ → APD延长、EADs↑、心律失常风险↑ Decreased I_to plus decreased I_K1 leads to APD prolongation, more EADs, and increased arrhythmia risk

Cx43侧向化在腹主动脉缩窄模型中的变化Lateralization of Cx43 in abdominal aortic constriction

1
Cx43在心肌细胞间隙连接处的分布 Distribution of Cx43 at gap junctions between cardiomyocytes

正常情况下（第0天），Cx43主要定位于细胞末端的插盘部位，表现为横向分布较少。 Under normal conditions (day 0), Cx43 is primarily located at intercalated disks at cell ends, and lateral (side) distribution is low.

腹主动脉缩窄后，随时间进展（7天、14天、28天），Cx43在心肌细胞侧面的分布逐渐增加。 After abdominal aortic constriction, the lateral distribution of Cx43 at the sides of cardiomyocytes gradually increases over time (days 7, 14, and 28).

图像和柱状统计显示侧向Cx43/总Cx43比例由约10%（0天）升高至近35%（28天）。 Both images and graph demonstrate the ratio of lateral/total Cx43 rises from about 10% (day 0) to nearly 35% (day 28).

2
侧向化机制及病理意义 Mechanism and pathological significance of lateralization

老师强调，心脏压力超负荷或损伤后，Cx43的侧向化是心肌电偶联重构的重要表现。 The teacher emphasized that after cardiac pressure overload or injury, Cx43 lateralization represents electrical remodeling of gap junctions.

Cx43侧向化导致心肌细胞间兴奋传导紊乱，增加心律失常发生风险。 Lateralized Cx43 impairs the orderly conduction of excitation between myocytes and heightens arrhythmia risk.

3
侧向化的定量变化 Quantitative changes of lateralization

柱状图显示，从第0天到第28天，Cx43侧向分布持续显著升高（P＜0.01）。 The bar chart shows the lateral proportion of Cx43 rises steadily and significantly from day 0 to day 28 (P < 0.01).

说明结构重构进展与病理过程紧密相关。 This indicates structural remodeling is tightly linked to disease progression.

心肌收缩-舒张耦联的离子机制Cardiac excitation-contraction coupling: ionic mechanisms

1
动作电位-钙信号-收缩关系 Relationship of action potential, calcium signal and contraction

心肌细胞动作电位到来时，L型钙通道开放，Ca2+流入细胞，引发肌浆网RyR受体释放Ca2+，迅速升高胞质Ca2+浓度，介导肌丝收缩。 Upon action potential arrival, L-type Ca2+ channels open, Ca2+ enters the cell, activating RyR on the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR) to release more Ca2+. This rapid rise in cytosolic Ca2+ mediates myofilament contraction.

老师补充，外源Ca2+输入虽有限，但其“触发”作用至关重要，主要Ca2+释放来自SR。 The teacher noted that although extracellular Ca2+ influx is small, it is crucial as a trigger; most Ca2+ release is from the SR.

2
钙移除机制 Calcium removal mechanisms

收缩终止后，Ca2+需被移出胞质，主要由SERCA泵将Ca2+泵回SR、NCX交换体将Ca2+移出细胞，以及线粒体摄取等完成。 After contraction, cytosolic Ca2+ must be removed mainly by SERCA pumps taking Ca2+ back into the SR, NCX exchangers extruding Ca2+ out of the cell and mitochondrial uptake.

3
动作电位、Ca2+变化和收缩的时序 Timing of action potential, Ca2+ transient and contraction

下方示意图显示，动作电位上升先于Ca2+瞬变，Ca2+浓度升高紧随其后，并引发收缩张力的上升；三者均于200ms内迅速完成。 The lower inset shows that the action potential precedes the Ca2+ transient, which in turn leads to contraction; all events occur rapidly within about 200 ms.

4
生理与病理意义 Physiological and pathological significance

老师讲解，任何影响动作电位时程、Ca2+通道或Ca2+转运的因素都会影响心脏收缩与舒张功能，并可能引发心律失常甚至心衰。 The teacher explained that any factor affecting action potential duration, Ca2+ channels, or Ca2+ cycling can alter contraction/relaxation and may contribute to arrhythmia or heart failure.

窦房结（SA结）解剖与组织结构Sinoatrial Node (SA Node): Description

1
窦房结的位置与发现 Location and Discovery of the SA Node

窦房结由Keith和Flack于1907年首次发现。 The sinoatrial node was first discovered by Keith and Flack in 1907.

1910年Wybau和Lewis证明这里是心脏激动的起始点。 In 1910, Wybau and Lewis confirmed this as the starting point of initial cardiac excitation.

窦房结位于右心房上腔静脉与右心耳交界处的心内膜下层，毗邻终末嵴。 The SA node is located subendocardially at the junction of the superior vena cava and right atrium, close to the terminal crest.

组织学可见其被心房肌、动脉、小静脉及结缔组织包围。 Histologically, it is surrounded by atrial muscle, arteries, small veins, and connective tissue.

详细解剖及组织见经典图谱（Keith & Flack, Chandler等）。 Detailed anatomy and histology are shown in classic atlases (Keith & Flack, Chandler et al.).

2
窦房结作为心脏起搏点的地位 Pacemaker Function of the SA Node

窦房结是心脏唯一的主起搏点，决定了正常心动周期的起始。 The SA node is the only main pacemaker of the heart, determining the onset of the normal heartbeat cycle.

右心房的其它部位（如终末嵴、心耳）无自发起搏能力。 Other parts of the right atrium (e.g., terminal crest, auricle) do not have intrinsic pacemaker activity.

课堂补充
老师强调，心脏自律性来源于窦房结特殊细胞的电生理特性，它是维持窦性心律的关键。 Lecture addition: The teacher emphasized that cardiac automaticity originates from the unique electrophysiological properties of SA node cells, which is essential for sinus rhythm maintenance.
3
窦房结起搏点分布及激动扩布 Position of Pacemaker Site and Activation Spread

人类窦房结起搏点主要位于SVC与RA上壁交界附近。 The human SA node pacemaker site is mainly located near the junction of the SVC and the upper wall of the RA.

着色电激活动地图可见激动从窦房结快速扩展至整个心房。 Color-coded activation maps show the rapid spread of excitation from the SA node to the entire atrium.

教师补充
起搏点可在自主神经调控或病理情况下发生迁移，这影响心率和心律。 Lecture addition: The teacher added that the leading pacemaker site can shift under autonomic stimulation or in pathological conditions, affecting heart rate and rhythm.
4
动物与新鲜组织观察 Observations in Animals and Fresh Tissue

新鲜分离的小鼠窦房结呈淡色，位于SVC与RA交界附近。 The freshly isolated mouse SA node is pale and located near the SVC-RA junction.

实验中常用新鲜组织定位起搏细胞进行功能研究。 In experiments, fresh tissue is used to precisely locate pacemaker cells for functional studies.

课堂强调
精确定位窦房结区域是后续电生理实验和细胞分离的基础。 In class, it was emphasized that precise localization of the SA node area is the basis for further electrophysiological experiments and cell isolation.
5
窦房结细胞的特殊组织学特点 Distinct Histological Features of SA Node Cells

小鼠用荧光标记（HCN4Cre-ERT2-tomato）可显示SA结细胞在组织中的特异分布。 In mice, fluorescent labeling (HCN4Cre-ERT2-tomato) reveals specific distribution of SA node cells within tissue.

共聚焦成像表明起搏细胞散在分布于窦房结区，与周围心房肌形成鲜明对比。 Confocal imaging shows pacemaker cells are scattered within the SA node region and contrast clearly with surrounding atrial myocytes.

染色区分
DAPI标记细胞核、GFP标记心肌纤维、Tomato标记特异细胞。 Staining: DAPI stains nuclei, GFP marks myofibrils, and Tomato stains specific cells.
教师补充
窦房结细胞具有小体积、稀疏肌丝、丰富间质，是其自动性基础。 Lecture addition: SA node cells have small size, sparse myofibrils, and abundant stroma, underlying their automaticity.

简明总结 Summary

窦房结结构解剖明确，起搏功能唯一，是心脏电活动的起源地。 The SA node has a well-defined anatomy and unique pacemaker function, serving as the origin of cardiac electrical activity.

激动可向心房快速扩布，且起搏点具可塑性。 Excitation rapidly spreads into the atrium, and the pacemaker site is plastic.

实验和临床均需准确识别窦房结，研究其特异细胞和功能。 Both research and clinic require precise localization and study of these unique cells and their function.

耦联钟系统在窦房结细胞中的作用 Coupled-Clock System in the SA Node Cell

1
窦房结细胞的自发节律性去极化由膜电压钟（Voltage Clock）和胞内钙钟（Calcium Clock）协同作用实现。 The spontaneous rhythmic depolarization in sinoatrial node cells is achieved by the coordinated action of the membrane voltage clock and the intracellular calcium clock.
2
膜电压钟指通过膜上的离子通道（如If、IK、INaCa、ICaL、ICaT、TREK-1等）产生的电活动调控起搏活动。 The voltage clock refers to pacemaking activity regulated by electrical activity generated through membrane ion channels (such as If, IK, INaCa, ICaL, ICaT, TREK-1, etc.).
3
钙钟主要指肌浆网内Ca2+的周期性释放与摄取（包括RyR介导的钙诱发钙释放[CICR]和SERCA介导的钙摄取），调控胞浆内钙浓度节律变化。 The calcium clock mainly refers to periodic Ca2+ release and uptake in the sarcoplasmic reticulum (including RyR-mediated Ca2+-induced Ca2+ release [CICR] and SERCA-mediated Ca2+ uptake), rhythmically regulating cytosolic Ca2+ concentration.
4
两者通过Na+-Ca2+交换电流（INaCa）、钙信号对膜电流的调节等机制密切耦联，共同产生自发起搏电活动。 These two clocks are tightly coupled via mechanisms such as the Na+-Ca2+ exchange current (INaCa) and regulation of membrane currents by calcium signals, together generating spontaneous pacemaker activity.
5
图中左侧展示了窦房结细胞动作电位的典型周期，包括第4期（静息期）、DD（慢去极化）、阈值点和去极化等阶段。 The left part of the figure shows a typical sinoatrial node cell action potential cycle, including phase 4 (resting), DD (diastolic depolarization), threshold point, and depolarization.
6
右侧图示详细展示了各种离子通道、钙信号通路在形成“电压钟”和“钙钟”中的作用及其耦联关系。 The right diagram details the roles of various ion channels and calcium signaling pathways in forming the "voltage clock" and "calcium clock" and their coupling relationship.
课上补充
老师强调，二者的协同是窦房结细胞稳定发放和调节心率的基础，是心脏起搏自动性的重要分子基础。 Instructor’s supplement: The instructor emphasized that this synergy is fundamental for stable pacemaking and heart rate regulation in sinoatrial node cells, and is a key molecular mechanism of cardiac pacemaker automaticity.

简要公式 Formula:

膜电压钟 + 钙钟 = 耦联钟系统

Voltage clock + calcium clock = coupled-clock system

兔窦房结的细胞类型Cell types in the Rabbit SA node

1
兔窦房结的主要细胞类型 Main cell types in the rabbit sinoatrial node
窦房结区域包含四类细胞
细长梭形细胞、梭形细胞、蛛网状细胞和心房肌细胞。 The SA node region contains four types of cells: elongated spindle cells, spindle cells, spider cells, and atrial myocytes.

细长梭形细胞形态狭长，细胞体较长。 Elongated spindle cells are narrow and long in shape.

梭形细胞呈纺锤状，体积略小。 Spindle cells have a spindle shape and are slightly smaller.

蛛网状细胞体积较大，具多条突起，呈蜘蛛状分布。 Spider cells are larger and have multiple extensions, presenting a spider-like appearance.

心房肌细胞呈长条状，横纹明显，属于典型心肌纤维。 Atrial myocytes are elongated, have clear striations, and are typical cardiac muscle fibers.

2
各类细胞在窦房结中的分布区域 Distribution of each cell type in the SA node

细长梭形细胞和梭形细胞主要分布于窦房结的中心区。 Elongated spindle cells and spindle cells are mainly located in the central area of the SA node.

蛛网状细胞多见于中间及外周区域。 Spider cells are commonly found in the intermediate and peripheral zones.

心房肌细胞主要分布在窦房结的边缘，与心房组织连接。 Atrial myocytes are primarily at the border, connecting with atrial tissue.

四种细胞类型在解剖结构上呈过渡分布，有助于信号从主起搏区顺利传向心房。 These four cell types are distributed in transition from the center to the periphery, facilitating smooth conduction of impulses from the primary pacemaker area to the atrium.

3
不同形态细胞的动作电位特征 Action potential characteristics of different cell types

细长梭形细胞和梭形细胞表现为慢起搏型动作电位，除极速度较慢，静息电位去极化水平较高。 Elongated spindle cells and spindle cells exhibit slow-type pacemaker APs, with slower depolarization rates and higher (less negative) maximum diastolic potentials.

蛛网状细胞自律性更强，动作电位斜率大，起搏活性高。 Spider cells show stronger automaticity, higher AP slope, and greater pacemaking activity.

心房肌细胞的动作电位更快，去极化和复极速度均高，静息电位更负，类似典型心房动作电位。 Atrial myocytes have faster APs, higher rates of depolarization and repolarization, and a more negative resting potential, characteristic of typical atrial APs.

4
课堂补充与功能意义 Lecture highlights and functional significance

老师强调，窦房结中心细胞（细长梭形、梭形）自律性最强，是主起搏源。 The instructor emphasized that central node cells (elongated spindle, spindle) have the highest automaticity and form the core pacemaker source.

外周区域细胞逐渐过渡为常规心房肌，利于信号有效传导。 Peripheral areas gradually transition into conventional atrial muscle, facilitating efficient impulse conduction.

这种多样性结构保证了窦房结在维持心率、自律性和信号传递上的独特作用。 This cellular diversity ensures the SA node’s unique function in maintaining heart rate, automaticity, and propagation of electrical activity.

窦房结过渡区的两种概念模型Two conceptual models for the sinoatrial transition

1
梯度模型 Gradient Model

梯度模型认为从典型窦房结细胞到心房细胞之间存在一个渐进变化的过渡区。 The gradient model proposes that there is a gradual transition zone from typical nodal cells to atrial cells.

细胞类型的变化是连续的，中间有过渡型细胞，这些细胞特征介于窦房结细胞和心房细胞之间。 The change in cell types is continuous, and there are transitional cells whose features are intermediate between SA nodal and atrial myocytes.

这种分布模式强调过渡区的存在，有助于心脏电信号顺畅传递。 This distribution highlights the existence of a transition zone, which facilitates the smooth propagation of cardiac electrical signals.

课堂补充
老师指出，梯度模型较好地解释了电信号在窦房结与心房之间的逐步衔接，有利于信号过渡、避免电学不连续引发阻滞。 Lecture addition: The teacher pointed out that the gradient model explains the stepwise connection of electrical signals between the SA node and atrium, helping to prevent conduction block due to electrical discontinuity.
2
镶嵌模型 Mosaic Model

镶嵌模型认为过渡区由两种不同类型细胞相间排列：典型窦房结细胞（白色）和心房细胞（黑色）。 The mosaic model proposes that the transition region comprises two intermixed cell types: typical nodal cells (white) and atrial cells (black).

这种模型中，窦房结细胞和心房细胞斑块状分布，过渡区实则是一种“镶嵌体”。 In this model, SA nodal and atrial cells are patchily distributed, and the transition region is essentially a "mosaic."

镶嵌区内随着远离窦房结中心，心房细胞比例逐渐增加。 Within the mosaic, the proportion of atrial cells gradually increases as one moves away from the nodal center.

课堂补充
老师强调，镶嵌模型反映了不同细胞岛屿状交错，对电活动的局部变化或非线性传播现象也有解释力。 Lecture addition: The teacher emphasized that the mosaic model reflects the patchy mixture of cell types, which can explain localized conduction or non-linear propagation seen in certain cases.
3
两种模型的对比与意义 Comparison and significance of the two models

梯度模型强调过渡的连续性，镶嵌模型强调异质性和结构“斑块性”。 The gradient model highlights continuity, while the mosaic model highlights heterogeneity and patchiness.

两种模型均有实验基础，不同物种或样本可呈现不同特征，有的更接近梯度，有的更偏向镶嵌。 Both models have experimental support; different species or samples may resemble one or the other.

课堂补充
老师补充实际中两者可能共存，但这两种模型思路有助于理解窦房结-心房过渡区在生理与病理下的电信号传导规律。 Lecture addition: The instructor noted that both models may coexist in reality, and these concepts help us understand physiological and pathological conduction at the SA node-atrial boundary.
4
小结 Summary

梯度模型和镶嵌模型分别描述了窦房结与心房细胞在空间上的连续过渡和异质“拼接”两种分布方式。 The gradient and mosaic models respectively describe spatially continuous transition and heterogeneous "patchwork" between SA nodal and atrial cells.

区分模型对研究窦房结功能及心律失常机制有重要意义。 Differentiating these models is important for studying SA node function and arrhythmia mechanisms.

心脏自律性的起源Genesis of cardiac automaticity

1
窦房结与心房肌动作电位的形态差异  Morphological differences between SA node and atrial muscle action potentials

窦房结动作电位（蓝线）和心房肌动作电位（绿线）在形态上明显不同。  The sinoatrial node action potential (blue line) differs markedly from the atrial muscle action potential (green line) in shape.

窦房结细胞没有真正的静息电位，而是有缓慢的、逐步去极化的起搏电位。  SA node cells lack a true resting potential, instead displaying a slow, gradual depolarizing pacemaker potential.

心房肌动作电位上升快且有稳定的静息电位，主要依赖钠通道形成快速去极化。  Atrial muscle action potentials have a rapid upstroke and a stable resting potential, primarily driven by fast Na⁺ channel-dependent depolarization.

2
窦房结起搏电位的离子机制  Ionic mechanisms underlying SA node pacemaker potential

窦房结细胞的自动性起源于起搏区的缓慢去极化过程。  The automaticity of SA node cells arises from the slow depolarization during the pacemaker potential phase.

关键离子电流包括
Key ionic currents involved include:  (a) IK​ 衰减——钾电流逐渐减弱，有助于膜去极化。   (a) IK​ decay – potassium current gradually declines, contributing to depolarization.  (b) If​（“钠通道”或称“funny current”）——在超极化时激活，促使自律去极化。   (b) If​ (“funny current”) – activated during hyperpolarization, promoting spontaneous depolarization.  (c) INaCa​——钠钙交换流、小量参与去极化过程。   (c) INaCa​ – sodium-calcium exchange, minor contribution to depolarization.  (d) ICa,T​ 与 ICa,L​——T型和L型钙通道，在阈电位附近开放并触发动作电位上升相。   (d)  ICa,T​ ​ and Ca,L​​ – T-type and L-type calcium channels, which open near threshold to trigger the upstroke of the action potential.
3
阈电位与自动去极化 Threshold and spontaneous depolarization

起搏细胞缓慢去极化，当膜电位到达阈值，即引发动作电位。  Pacemaker cells gradually depolarize, and when the membrane potential reaches threshold, an action potential is triggered.

没有外源刺激下即可周期性自动发放动作电位，体现了心脏的自律性。  This results in periodic, spontaneous generation of action potentials without external stimulation, which is the basis for cardiac automaticity.

1
课堂补充与老师讲解要点  Lecture highlights and explanation

老师指出，If​电流在窦房结自动性中的关键作用，该电流超极化时开放，导致膜电位自主向阈值推进。  The instructor emphasized the critical role of If​ current in SA node automaticity; it opens upon hyperpolarization and drives the membrane potential toward threshold.

ICa,T​ ​和 ICa,L​主导窦房结细胞的动作电位上升相，而不是经典心肌细胞依赖的INaINa​。  ICa,T​  and  ICa,L​​ dominate the upstroke in SA node cells, in contrast to typical myocytes that rely on INaINa​.

心房肌细胞动作电位较窦房结更快，上升更陡，反映出结构和通道表达的不同。  Atrial muscle action potentials are faster and steeper than those of the SA node, reflecting differences in cell structure and ion channel expression.

2
总结 Summary

窦房结细胞的自动节律性（自律性）是由多种离子通道协同造成的，特别是If​电流和钙通道的协同作用。  The automatic rhythmicity (automaticity) of SA node cells arises from the coordinated action of several ion channels, especially the collaboration of IfIf​ current and calcium channels.

这种自动性为心脏的基本搏动和节律提供了基础。  This automaticity underpins the basic heartbeat and rhythm of the heart

If通道及其在心脏起搏中的“funny”电流Focus: 'funny' current If—HCN channels

1
If/HCN通道结构与激活机制  Structure and activation mechanism of If/HCN channels

If电流又称为“funny current”，是由HCN（高极化激活环核苷酸门控）通道介导的。  If current, also called “funny current”, is mediated by HCN (Hyperpolarization-activated Cyclic Nucleotide-gated) channels.

HCN通道在膜电位超极化时开放，并受环磷酸腺苷（cAMP）正向调节。  HCN channels are activated by hyperpolarization of the membrane potential and are positively regulated by cAMP.

通道单体含有S1-S6六个跨膜区，S4带正电，C端含cAMP结合结构域（CNBD）。  Each channel subunit contains six transmembrane segments (S1–S6), S4 is positively charged, and the C-terminus has a cAMP binding domain (CNBD).

If通道允许Na⁺、K⁺通过，形成缓慢、去极化的离子流。  The If channel is permeable to both Na⁺ and K⁺, generating a slow, depolarizing ionic current40。

2
HCN家族亚型分布与表型  HCN family isoforms: expression and phenotypes

HCN通道分为4种亚型（HCN1-4），在神经系统和心脏分布不同。  There are four HCN isoforms (HCN1-4) with distinct distributions in the nervous system and heart.

HCN4主要分布于窦房结、房室结和浦肯野纤维，是窦房结自动性的关键通道。  HCN4 is mainly expressed in the sinoatrial node, atrioventricular node, and Purkinje fibers, playing a key role in SA node automaticity.

HCN2也在心脏表达，广泛分布于各组织。  HCN2 is also expressed in the heart and has widespread tissue distribution.

敲除模型显示，HCN4缺失导致胚胎致死、缓慢心律、主起搏细胞功能缺陷；HCN2敲除则导致窦性心律失常。  Knockout models show that lack of HCN4 leads to embryonic lethality, bradycardia, and pacemaker cell dysfunction; knockout of HCN2 causes sinus arrhythmia40。

3
If电流的生理作用与自动性机制  Physiological roles of If current and pacemaker mechanism

If电流主要存在于窦房结Pacemaker cells，主导舒张期缓慢去极化，是心脏自动节律的分子基础。  If current is primarily present in sinoatrial node pacemaker cells, dominating slow diastolic depolarization and forming the molecular basis for cardiac automaticity.

该电流使膜电位自发逐渐接近阈值，周期性触发动作电位。  The current gradually brings the membrane potential towards threshold, periodically triggering action potentials.

老师强调，HCN通道的开通是心脏起搏细胞自律性的关键；缺陷可引发心率减慢、传导阻滞或心律失常。  The lecturer emphasized that opening of HCN channels is essential for cardiac pacemaker automaticity; defects may cause bradycardia, conduction block, or arrhythmias1240。

4
药理学调节及临床意义  Pharmacological modulation and clinical significance

伊伐布雷定（Ivabradine）为经典If通道特异抑制剂，可选择性抑制If电流，减慢心率但无负性肌力性作用。  Ivabradine is a classic, specific If channel inhibitor, selectively suppressing If current, reducing heart rate with no negative inotropic effect.

临床用于心力衰竭、心绞痛等慢心率治疗。  Clinically, it is used for rate-control therapy in heart failure and angina.

药物作用机制
抑制If后舒张期去极化速度减慢，心率下降（见图中ΔHR）。  Mechanism: If inhibition slows diastolic depolarization, thus lowering the heart rate (see ΔHR in the figure)。
老师补充
If通道的调节对于窦房结自动性和心脏节律疾病治疗极为重要，是心脏节律药物研发的热点方向。  Teacher’s remark: Modulation of If channels is crucial for SA node automaticity and rhythm disorder therapy, making it a key target in cardiac drug development.

自主神经对心脏自律性的调控 Autonomic Regulation of Cardiac Automaticity

1
自主神经系统由交感神经和副交感神经组成，二者共同调节心脏起搏点（窦房结）的自律性。 The autonomic nervous system consists of sympathetic and parasympathetic divisions, both of which coordinate to regulate pacemaker (SA node) automaticity.
2
交感神经通过释放去甲肾上腺素（NEpi），作用于心脏，增强窦房结起搏细胞去极化的第4期斜率，从而加快心率。 The sympathetic nervous system releases norepinephrine (NEpi), which acts on the heart to increase the phase 4 slope of SA node pacemaker cell depolarization, thereby accelerating heart rate.
3
副交感神经通过迷走神经释放乙酰胆碱（ACh），降低窦房结细胞第4期斜率，减慢去极化，最终降低心率。 The parasympathetic nervous system, via the vagus nerve, releases acetylcholine (ACh), which decreases the phase 4 slope of SA node cells, slows depolarization, and ultimately reduces heart rate.
4
动脉感受器（如颈动脉窦、主动脉弓）感知血压变化，将信息通过感觉神经传入中枢，引发自主神经调节心率反应。 Baroreceptors (e.g., carotid sinus, aortic arch) sense blood pressure changes and send signals via sensory nerves to the brain, eliciting autonomic adjustment of heart rate.
右图展示三种情况
交感神经兴奋（红线）、正常（黑线）、副交感神经兴奋（绿线）下窦房结动作电位的变化。交感神经↑使Phase 4斜率↑，心率加快；副交感神经↑使Phase 4斜率↓ ，心率变慢。 The right panel shows changes in SA node action potential under three conditions: sympathetic activation (red), normal (black), and vagal (green). Sympathetic ↑ increases phase 4 slope and heart rate; parasympathetic ↑ decreases phase 4 slope and slows the heart rate.
课堂补充
老师强调交感/副交感神经系统能通过调控If通道、Ca²⁺通道和K⁺通道等多种机制改变窦房结细胞的起搏活动。交感作用上调cAMP，促进If等电流，副交感下调cAMP，并增强K⁺电流抑制细胞去极化。 Lecture addition: The instructor emphasized that the sympathetic/parasympathetic systems modulate pacemaker activity via mechanisms involving If channels, Ca²⁺ channels, and K⁺ channels. Sympathetic activity raises cAMP, enhancing If and other currents, while parasympathetic action lowers cAMP and increases K⁺ current to further suppress depolarization39.
总结
自主神经系统精确调节心脏自律性，实现对生理状态适应性心率调控，这对维持血压与循环稳定至关重要。 Summary: The autonomic nervous system tightly controls cardiac automaticity to adapt heart rate to physiological demands, which is crucial for stable blood pressure and circulation

交感和副交感神经对起搏细胞If电流的调控机制 Mechanisms of Sympathetic and Parasympathetic Regulation of If Current in Pacemaker Cells

1
交感神经对起搏细胞活动的调控 Sympathetic Regulation of Pacemaker Activity

交感神经主要通过释放去甲肾上腺素（NEpi）作用于β肾上腺素受体（βAR）。 The sympathetic nervous system releases norepinephrine (NEpi), which acts on β-adrenergic receptors (βAR).

激活βAR后，通过Gs蛋白促进腺苷酸环化酶　产生cAMP。 Upon βAR activation, the Gs protein stimulates adenylyl cyclase (AC) to produce cAMP.
答案 AC

cAMP可直接结合并促进HCN通道开放，增强If电流，加快舒张期去极化速率。 cAMP directly binds to HCN channels, promoting their opening, increasing If current and accelerating the rate of diastolic depolarization.

cAMP还激活PKA，促进L型钙通道（ICa,L）、RyR、SERCA等磷酸化，增强胞内Ca²⁺循环（即‘Ca²⁺ Clock’），间接促进Na⁺-Ca²⁺交换流（INCX）和膜去极化。 cAMP also activates PKA, phosphorylating L-type Ca²⁺ channels (ICa,L), RyR, and SERCA, enhancing intracellular Ca²⁺ cycling (‘Ca²⁺ Clock’), which indirectly boosts Na⁺-Ca²⁺ exchange (INCX) and membrane depolarization.

结果是起搏细胞自律去极化斜率↑，动作电位频率↑，心率↑。 The result is an increased slope of spontaneous depolarization, more frequent action potentials, and increased heart rate1112.

2
副交感神经对起搏细胞活动的调控 Parasympathetic Regulation of Pacemaker Activity

副交感神经通过迷走神经释放乙酰胆碱（ACh），作用于M₂型胆碱能受体（M₂R）。 The parasympathetic system, through the vagus nerve, releases acetylcholine (ACh), which acts on M₂ muscarinic receptors (M₂R).

M₂R通过Gi蛋白抑制AC，降低cAMP生成，减少HCN通道开放，If电流下调，舒张期去极化速率变慢。 M₂R activation via Gi protein inhibits AC, reducing cAMP production, decreasing HCN channel opening and If current, slowing diastolic depolarization.

乙酰胆碱激活GIRK通道（IKAch），产生钾外流，进一步过极化膜电位，降低细胞兴奋性。 ACh also activates GIRK channels (IKAch), causing K⁺ efflux, hyperpolarizing the membrane and reducing excitability.

还可抑制L型Ca²⁺通道与胞内Ca²⁺循环（‘Ca²⁺ Clock’），使自律性进一步下降。 It also inhibits L-type Ca²⁺ channels and intracellular Ca²⁺ cycling (‘Ca²⁺ Clock’), further suppressing automaticity.

结果是起搏细胞自律去极化斜率↓，动作电位频率↓，心率↓。 The net outcome is a decreased slope of spontaneous depolarization, fewer action potentials, and reduced heart rate12.

3
交感/副交感神经对If电流的具体影响 Summary: Sympathetic vs. Parasympathetic Effects on If Current

交感激动剂（如异丙肾上腺素，Iso）可显著增强If电流和起搏电位斜率，加快自发动作电位频率（见动作电位和If电流实验图左、中）。 Sympathetic agonists (e.g., isoproterenol, Iso) significantly enhance If current and the slope of pacemaker potential, accelerating spontaneous action potential frequency (see the experiment graphs, left/middle).

副交感激动剂（如乙酰胆碱，ACh）显著抑制If电流，降低斜率并减慢动作电位（见相应实验）。 Parasympathetic agonists (e.g., acetylcholine, ACh) markedly suppress If current, decrease the slope, and slow the action potentials (see corresponding experiments).

若绘制If电流激活曲线，交感上移（右移、曲线红色），副交感下移（左移、绿色），反映通道开放电位的变化。 On the If current activation curve, sympathetic shifts to the right (red), parasympathetic to the left (green), reflecting voltage-dependent channel gating alteration12.

4
课堂补充 Teacher's Key Points

老师强调，If电流是窦房结自律性的关键分子靶点，是交感和副交感神经调节心率的枢纽。 The instructor highlighted that If current is a key molecular target for SA node automaticity and is pivotal for both sympathetic and parasympathetic heart rate regulation.

交感活动可通过If、Ca²⁺流联合上调，加快窦房结放电；副交感则通过联合抑制If、Ca²⁺流、激活IKAch减慢心率。 Sympathetic tone upregulates both If and Ca²⁺ currents to speed up pacemaking; parasympathetic tone jointly downregulates If/Ca²⁺ currents and activates IKAch to slow the rate1112.

5
总结 Summary

交感和副交感神经通过对If电流及相关通道的精细调控，实现心率的快速升降和生理适应。 Sympathetic and parasympathetic nerves achieve rapid and adaptive heart rate modulation through precise control of If and related currents.

这一机制是临床药物（如β受体激动/拮抗剂和If通道抑制剂）干预心率的重要基础。 This mechanism underpins clinical interventions with β-agonists/antagonists and If channel inhibitors for heart rate control

磷酸二酯酶与起搏细胞活动 Phosphodiesterases and Pacemaker Activity

1
起搏细胞活动由多种离子通道和信号机制共同调控。 Pacemaker cell activity is regulated by multiple ion channels and signaling mechanisms.
2.去甲肾上腺素（NEpi）通过β肾上腺素受体（βAR）激活Gs蛋白，进而激活腺苷酸环化酶　生成cAMP。 Norepinephrine (NEpi) activates β-adrenergic receptors (βAR), which stimulate Gs protein, further activating adenylyl cyclase (AC) to produce cAMP.
答案 AC
3
cAMP直接激活HCN通道，增强If电流，加快舒张期去极化，提高心率。 cAMP directly activates HCN channels, increasing If current, accelerating diastolic depolarization, and raising the heart rate.
4
cAMP还激活蛋白激酶A（PKA），促进L型钙通道（I_Ca,L）、RyR、SERCA等的磷酸化，加强胞内Ca²⁺循环（Ca²⁺ Clock），进一步促进起搏活动。 cAMP also activates protein kinase A (PKA), which phosphorylates L-type Ca²⁺ channels (I_Ca,L), RyR, and SERCA, enhancing intracellular Ca²⁺ cycling (the Ca²⁺ Clock), further boosting pacemaker activity.
5
图中蓝色箭头显示了cAMP和PKA在调节If电流、钙通道和钠/钙交换流（I_NCX）等方面的作用。 The blue arrows in the diagram illustrate how cAMP and PKA regulate If current, calcium channels, and sodium/calcium exchange current (I_NCX), among others.
6
磷酸二酯酶（PDEs）水解cAMP，限制其水平，负向调控起搏细胞自律性和心率。 Phosphodiesterases (PDEs) hydrolyze cAMP, limiting its level and negatively regulating pacemaker automaticity and heart rate.
7
老师补充，PDE在窦房结功能调节中非常关键。PDE上调会↓cAMP，下调cAMP-依赖的If和Ca²⁺通道功能，减弱交感激活效应，心率下降；而PDE功能受损或抑制剂应用可导致cAMP积聚，增强If和Ca²⁺信号，增快心率，甚至诱发心律失常。 The instructor emphasized the key role of PDEs in SAN (sinoatrial node) function. Upregulation of PDE leads to ↓cAMP, downregulating cAMP-dependent If and Ca²⁺ channel function, weakening sympathetic effects, and slowing heart rate; PDE deficiency or PDE inhibitors cause cAMP accumulation, enhancing If and Ca²⁺ signaling, speeding up the heart rate, and potentially triggering arrhythmias.
图左侧曲线
黑线为正常自发起搏活动，蓝线为PDE受抑或交感活性提升后的频率增加。 The curve on the left: the black line represents normal spontaneous pacemaker activity, and the blue line shows increased frequency after PDE inhibition or sympathetic activation.
总结
磷酸二酯酶通过调控cAMP-依赖通道连接“电压钟”和“钙钟”机制，是心脏节律药物开发重要靶点。 Summary: Phosphodiesterases tune cAMP-dependent channels that link the “voltage clock” and “calcium clock” mechanisms and serve as an important drug target in rhythm control

心脏cAMP-磷酸二酯酶（PDEs）Cardiac cAMP-Phosphodiesterases (PDEs)

1
主要内容与结构 Main Content and Structure

本页展示了心脏中几类主要的cAMP-磷酸二酯酶（PDEs）家族及其相关基因。 This slide shows the main families of cardiac cAMP-phosphodiesterases (PDEs) and their associated genes.

所有PDE均可催化环磷酸腺苷（cAMP）→ 5’AMP的降解反应，终止cAMP信号通路。 All PDEs catalyze the degradation of cyclic AMP (cAMP) to 5'AMP, terminating the cAMP signaling pathway.

2
PDE家族与基因 Families and Genes

主要包括PDE1、PDE2、PDE3、PDE4、PDE8等5种家族，每种家族对应特定基因。 There are five main families: PDE1, PDE2, PDE3, PDE4, and PDE8, each associated with specific genes.

PDE1家族基因
pde1c PDE1 family gene: pde1c
PDE2家族基因
pde2a PDE2 family gene: pde2a
PDE3家族基因
pde3a、pde3b PDE3 family genes: pde3a, pde3b
PDE4家族基因
pde4a、pde4b、pde4d PDE4 family genes: pde4a, pde4b, pde4d
PDE8家族基因
pde8a PDE8 family gene: pde8a
3
结构模式图说明 Diagram Explanation

图示展示每种PDE的分子结构、调节位点（如CaM结合、磷酸化位点等）。 The diagram shows each PDE’s molecular structure and regulatory sites (such as CaM-binding, phosphorylation, etc).

不同颜色代表不同家族，PDE1为黄色，PDE2为蓝色，PDE3为绿色，PDE4为红色，PDE8为灰色。 Each family is color-coded: PDE1 (yellow), PDE2 (blue), PDE3 (green), PDE4 (red), PDE8 (gray).

关键调控元件如CaM（钙调蛋白）、PKA/CaMKII或磷酸化点也有标注。 Key regulatory elements, such as CaM (calmodulin), PKA/CaMKII or phosphorylation sites, are indicated.

4
作用机制 Mechanistic Summary

cAMP作为二级信使在心肌细胞中调控多种功能（如心率、收缩力），PDEs负责下调cAMP信号。 cAMP acts as a second messenger regulating various functions (like heart rate, contractility) in cardiomyocytes, while PDEs downregulate cAMP signaling.

PDE对心脏节律、收缩、兴奋—收缩耦联等生理过程具有重要调控作用。 PDEs play a pivotal role in regulating cardiac rhythm, contraction, and excitation–contraction coupling.

5
补充说明 Additional Notes

临床上，不同PDE亚型的抑制剂已被用于治疗心力衰竭等疾病。 Clinically, inhibitors of different PDE subtypes are used to treat diseases such as heart failure.

PDE的功能与其表达的器官、细胞亚型密切相关。 The function of specific PDEs is highly correlated with the organ and cell subtype where they are expressed.

公式(纯文本) Formula (Plain Text):

cAMP →(PDEs)→ 5’AMP

cAMP-PDEs在窦房结中的作用 cAMP-PDEs in Sinoatrial Node

1
cAMP被磷酸二酯酶（PDEs）水解为5'AMP，从而终止cAMP信号。 cAMP is hydrolyzed to 5'AMP by phosphodiesterases (PDEs), thereby terminating cAMP signaling.
2
IBMX是一种广谱PDE抑制剂，可以抑制PDEs，从而阻止cAMP的降解，导致细胞内cAMP水平上调↑。 IBMX is a broad-spectrum PDE inhibitor that blocks PDEs, preventing cAMP degradation and leading to increased intracellular cAMP levels ↑.
3
添加外源性cAMP类似物（CPT-cAMP），自发起搏速率显著上升↑。 Application of exogenous cAMP analog (CPT-cAMP) causes a significant increase ↑ in spontaneous pacemaker rate.
4
应用PDE抑制剂IBMX同样显著提高窦房结细胞的起搏频率↑，证明PDEs的活性可以负向调节起搏频率。 Applying IBMX also significantly increases ↑ the pacing frequency of sinoatrial node cells, indicating that PDE activity negatively regulates the pacemaker rate.
5
柱状图数据显示，不论是添加cAMP还是抑制PDEs都会使窦房结自律性增强，心率加快↑。 The bar graphs show that either adding cAMP or inhibiting PDEs increases sinoatrial node automaticity, accelerating heart rate ↑.
6
进一步分型实验显示，PDE1、PDE2、PDE3、PDE4抑制剂（分别为8MM-IBMX、EHNA、Cilostamide、Rolipram）均能不同程度地提高窦房结起搏率↑，其中PDE3和PDE4联合抑制的作用最强。 Further subtype experiments show that inhibitors of PDE1, PDE2, PDE3, and PDE4 (namely 8MM-IBMX, EHNA, Cilostamide, Rolipram) all increase pacemaker rate to various extents ↑, with combined PDE3 and PDE4 inhibition producing the strongest effect.
7
图中“ISO”为异丙肾上腺素（β受体激动剂），可作为阳性对照，说明cAMP信号增强均可促进心率↑。 In the graph, “ISO” stands for isoproterenol (a β-adrenergic agonist) and serves as a positive control, showing that enhanced cAMP signaling promotes heart rate ↑.
老师补充
实验采用兔窦房结细胞，用全细胞膜片钳记录动作电位，数据说明PDEs在调控cAMP和窦房结自律性中发挥关键负反馈作用。 Instructor's note: The experiments use rabbit sinoatrial node cells and record action potentials via whole-cell patch clamp, showing that PDEs play a key negative feedback role in regulating cAMP and SAN automaticity.
9
公式：cAMP →(PDEs)→ 5’AMP Formula: cAMP →(PDEs)→ 5’AMP

小结 Summary：

PDEs通过降解cAMP抑制心脏自律性，PDE抑制剂或cAMP类似物均可上调↑窦房结自发起搏率。这一机制对于理解心率调控和药物干预至关重要。

PDEs inhibit cardiac automaticity by degrading cAMP; both PDE inhibitors and cAMP analogs upregulate ↑ sinoatrial pacemaker rate. This mechanism is critical for understanding heart rate regulation and pharmacological interventions

cAMP-PDEs与整体心率调控cAMP-PDEs and Heart Rate in vivo

1
本页展示了PDE3A、PDE2、PDE4B在心脏过表达对小鼠整体心率和生存率的影响。 This slide shows the effects of cardiac overexpression of PDE3A, PDE2, and PDE4B on heart rate and survival in mice.
左图
PDE3A过表达小鼠静息和异丙肾上腺素（ISO）刺激下心率均显著低于野生型，提示PDE3A↑通过降解cAMP，导致心率下降↓。 Left panel: Heart rate in PDE3A-overexpressing mice is significantly lower than wild-type at rest and under isoproterenol (ISO) stimulation, indicating that increased PDE3A reduces cAMP levels, thereby decreasing heart rate↓.
中图
PDE2过表达小鼠在白天和夜间心率均低于野生型，且长期生存率下降，说明PDE2↑增强也抑制cAMP调控，降低窦房结自律性和心率，并影响寿命。 Middle panel: PDE2-overexpressing mice show reduced heart rate both day and night, and exhibit decreased long-term survival, suggesting that upregulation of PDE2 suppresses cAMP signaling, reduces SAN automaticity and heart rate, and impacts lifespan.
右图
PDE4B高表达（尤其是TG50组）在白天心率显著下降↓，且生存率显著下降，进一步表明PDE4B↑可增强cAMP降解，强烈抑制心率、自律性，且对机体存活不利。 Right panel: High PDE4B expression (especially in TG50) significantly reduces heart rate during the day and significantly lowers survival, confirming that increased PDE4B enhances cAMP degradation, suppressing heart rate and automaticity, with a negative effect on survival.
5
不同PDE家族上调均表现为心率下降、活性减弱，且强烈PDE抑制对心脏长期功能和生存可造成不良影响。 Upregulation of different PDE families consistently leads to decreased heart rate and weakened activity, and excessive PDE activity can have negative consequences on long-term cardiac function and survival.
课上补充
PDE是cAMP信号负向调控的核心，功能增强时会削弱交感兴奋对心率的加速作用，使心脏自律性下降。 Instructor’s addition: PDEs are key negative regulators of cAMP signaling; when their function is enhanced, sympathetic acceleration of heart rate is weakened, reducing cardiac automaticity.
7
公式：cAMP →（PDE）→ 5'AMP Formula: cAMP →(PDE)→ 5'AMP
总结
PDE的过度增强破坏了自律细胞的正常cAMP调控机制，导致心率显著下降，也提示临床上PDE调节需严格把控。 Summary: Excessive PDE activity disrupts normal cAMP regulation in pacemaker cells, resulting in marked reduction of heart rate, highlighting the need for careful clinical modulation of PDEs

042025-10-27

血管的组成与主要生理功能

Blood vessel composition and main physiological functions

1
血管的基本结构 Basic Structure of Blood Vessels

血管壁由内皮细胞、平滑肌细胞和基底膜等结构组成。 Vascular walls are composed of endothelial cells, smooth muscle cells, and basement membrane.

图示为一截血管横断面，内皮细胞铺在血管腔的最内层。 The diagram shows a cross-section of a blood vessel, with endothelial cells lining the innermost layer of the vessel lumen.

血管内皮（英文中为vascular endothelium）在免疫荧光图片中以绿色描绘，包围每个蓝色细胞核（DAPI染色）。 Vascular endothelium is labeled in green in the immunofluorescence image, surrounding each blue nucleus (DAPI staining).

2
血管中的主要细胞类型 Major Cell Types in Blood Vessels

内皮细胞（Endothelial Cells）：形成血管壁的内层，直接与血液接触。 Endothelial cells form the innermost layer of the vessel wall and are in direct contact with the blood.

红细胞（Red Blood Cells）：血管腔中的主要细胞，负责氧气和二氧化碳的运输。 Red blood cells are the main cells in the vessel lumen, responsible for transporting oxygen and carbon dioxide.

平滑肌细胞（Vascular Smooth Muscle Cells）：位于血管壁较外层，参与血管收缩和舒张，调节血管张力（Vascular Tone）。 Vascular smooth muscle cells are located in the outer layer of the vessel wall; they participate in vasoconstriction and vasodilation to regulate vascular tone.

3
血管内的其他成分及其功能 Other Components and Their Functions in Blood Vessels

白细胞（Leukocytes）：作为免疫细胞，在血管中被运输，参与炎症和免疫反应。 Leukocytes, as immune cells, are transported in the blood vessel and participate in inflammation and immune responses.

血小板（Platelets）：在血管损伤时启动止血机制，形成血栓，帮助止血。 Platelets participate in hemostasis by initiating clot formation upon vascular injury to stop bleeding.

4
血管的主要生理反应 Major Physiological Responses of Blood Vessels

止血（Hemostasis）：血小板与纤维蛋白共同形成血块，迅速封堵受损血管，防止失血。 Hemostasis: Platelets and fibrin form a clot to quickly seal damaged vessels and prevent blood loss.

炎症（Inflammation）：白细胞从血管中穿出，识别并清除细菌等病原体，启动修复过程。 Inflammation: Leukocytes exit the blood vessel to recognize and remove pathogens and initiate tissue repair.

血管张力（Vascular Tone）：平滑肌细胞通过收缩或舒张调控血管直径，调节血流和血压。 Vascular tone: Smooth muscle cells regulate vessel diameter by contraction or relaxation, adjusting blood flow and pressure.

5
血管内皮的核心作用 Core Role of the Vascular Endothelium

血管内皮是血液与组织之间的动态界面，维持血管通透性和功能。 The vascular endothelium is a dynamic interface between blood and tissue, maintaining vessel permeability and function.

内皮的完整性对于生理稳态至关重要。 The integrity of the endothelium is physiologically essential for homeostasis.

6
综合小结 Integrated Summary

血管的三大生理反应包括止血、炎症和血管张力调节，这些功能都是通过内皮细胞、血小板、白细胞和血管平滑肌细胞协调完成的。 The three main physiological responses of blood vessels—hemostasis, inflammation, and vascular tone regulation—are accomplished through the coordinated actions of endothelial cells, platelets, leukocytes, and vascular smooth muscle cells.

血管内皮细胞的完整和功能状态不仅仅影响血管通透性，还关系到机体的防御能力和快速修复。 The integrity and functional state of endothelial cells affects not only vessel permeability, but also the body's defense ability and rapid repair.

血管内皮是生理性屏障 The vascular endothelium is a physiological barrier

1
血管内皮细胞形成单层，构成血管壁的最内层。 Endothelial cells form a monolayer and constitute the innermost layer of the blood vessel wall.
2
内皮细胞之间通过多种连接结构牢固连接，称为内皮间连接。 Endothelial cells are tightly connected to each other by various junctional structures, called inter-endothelial junctions.
3
这些连接包括紧密连接、黏附连接和缝隙连接，分别有不同的分子基础和功能。 These junctions include tight junctions, adherens junctions, and gap junctions, each with distinct molecular basis and function.
4
紧密连接（Tight Junctions）由ZO-1、Occludin、Claudin、JAM等分子组成，主要限制分子的通过。 Tight junctions (composed of proteins such as ZO-1, Occludin, Claudin, JAM) mainly restrict passage of molecules.
5
黏附连接（Adherens Junctions）以VE-cadherin为代表，调控细胞间的黏着，并通过连接肌动蛋白骨架维持细胞结构稳定。 Adherens junctions, represented by VE-cadherin, regulate cell-cell adhesion and connect to the actin cytoskeleton to maintain structural integrity.
6
缝隙连接（Gap Junctions）由Connexin蛋白组成，允许细胞间小分子和信号的直接交换。 Gap junctions, formed by Connexin proteins, permit direct exchange of small molecules and signals between cells.
7
内皮间连接的完整性决定了血管的通透性。 The integrity of inter-endothelial junctions determines vascular permeability.
8
正常情况下，血管通透性表现为“尺寸依赖性”：小分子能够通过，大分子受到限制。 Under normal conditions, vascular permeability is "size-dependent": small molecules can pass, while macromolecules are restricted.

血管通透性的体内可视化 Visualization of vascular permeability in vivo

1
实验中常用活体显微镜检测小鼠耳部皮肤微血管通透性。 In experiments, intravital microscopy is frequently used to detect microvascular permeability in mouse ear skin.
2
通过向血管内注射荧光示踪物（如70kDa的FITC-Dextran），可以追踪分子的漏出情况。 By injecting fluorescent tracers (such as 70 kDa FITC-Dextran) into blood vessels, molecular leakage can be visualized.
3
在正常状态下，绝大部分大分子被血管屏障阻挡，仅有小分子能够漏出。 In a normal state, most macromolecules are blocked by the vascular barrier, with only small molecules able to leak through.

血管通透性的增强主要由内皮间连接的破坏引起

Increased vascular permeability is mainly due to disruption of inter-endothelial junctions

1
组胺等炎症介质可引起内皮细胞收缩或连接蛋白（如VE-cadherin）功能受损，导致连接断裂。 Inflammatory mediators such as histamine cause endothelial cell contraction or dysfunction of junctional proteins (like VE-cadherin), leading to junction disruption.
2
连接断裂后，血管屏障功能减弱，大分子和血浆液体漏到组织间隙，引发局部水肿。 After disruption, the vascular barrier weakens, allowing macromolecules and plasma fluid to leak into interstitial tissue, causing local edema.
3
这种通透性变化大多是短暂和可逆的，有利于炎症反应过程中免疫细胞和修复分子的到达。 This permeability change is mostly transient and reversible, facilitating the arrival of immune cells and repair molecules during inflammation.
4
如果屏障开放过久、过强，会导致大量液体和蛋白渗漏，出现严重水肿或低血压等病理状况。 If the barrier remains open too long or too intensely, massive leakage of fluid and proteins may occur, leading to severe edema or hypotension.

血管内皮在炎症中的核心作用 The vascular endothelium has a central role in inflammation

1
损伤或感染时，局部组织释放如组胺等信号分子，促使血管扩张、通透性增加。 Upon injury or infection, local tissue releases signaling molecules such as histamine, causing vasodilation and increased permeability.
2
白细胞和液体由血管进入损伤/感染区域，有助于细菌和细胞残骸的清除及组织修复。 Leukocytes and fluid leave the blood vessel and enter the injured/infected area, contributing to the clearance of bacteria/cell debris and tissue repair.
3
白细胞黏附及募集是炎症中生理性反应，但必须短暂且自限，避免持续损伤组织。 Leukocyte adhesion and recruitment are physiological responses in inflammation but must be transient and self-limited to avoid ongoing tissue damage.

激活的内皮介导白细胞募集 Leukocyte recruitment by an activated endothelium

1
白细胞募集依赖于激活内皮细胞表面黏附分子的表达。 Leukocyte recruitment depends on the expression of adhesion molecules on activated endothelial cells.
2
黏附分子主要包括选择素（P-和E-Selectin）和免疫球蛋白家族分子（ICAM-1和VCAM-1）。 The main adhesion molecules include selectins (P- and E-selectin) and immunoglobulin superfamily members (ICAM-1, VCAM-1).
白细胞招募步骤包括
滚动（P-和E-Selectin介导）、牢固黏附（ICAM-1、VCAM-1介导）、穿过血管壁（迁移）。 The leukocyte recruitment steps include: rolling (mediated by P- and E-selectin), firm adhesion (mediated by ICAM-1, VCAM-1), and transmigration through the vessel wall.
4
在静息（未激活）状态下，血管内皮表面基本不表达这些黏附分子，白细胞可自由流动。 In the quiescent (non-activated) state, endothelial cells scarcely express these adhesion molecules and leukocytes circulate freely.
5
激活状态下，内皮通过信号（如炎症）上调上述分子，才可“捕获”并黏附白细胞。 Upon activation (e.g., by inflammation), the endothelium upregulates these molecules to capture and adhere leukocytes.
6
滚动阶段的选择素-配体作用为低亲和力、易解离，有利于白细胞减速并“滚动”于内皮表面。 Selectin-ligand interactions during rolling are low-affinity and easily dissociate, helping leukocytes slow down and roll on the endothelial surface.
7
牢固黏附阶段的ICAM-1/VCAM-1-整合素作用为高亲和力，使白细胞牢牢附着，准备穿越血管壁。 ICAM-1/VCAM-1-integrin interactions during firm adhesion are high-affinity, ensuring the leukocyte sticks firm and prepares to transmigrate.

血管内皮在炎症中的核心作用The vascular endothelium has a central role in inflammation

1
局部组织损伤或感染时，会释放化学信号（如组胺）。 When local tissue is injured or infected, it releases chemical signals such as histamine.
2
这些化学信号引发血管扩张和通透性增加，使白细胞与液体离开血管到达损伤/感染区域。 These chemical signals cause vasodilation and increased vascular permeability, allowing leukocytes and fluid to exit the blood vessel and reach the injured/infected area.
3
白细胞与液体进入组织后，参与细菌和细胞残骸的清除，并促进组织修复。 Once leukocytes and fluid enter the tissue, they help clear bacteria and cell debris and facilitate tissue repair.
4
白细胞黏附和募集在炎症反应中属于生理性反应，但要求过程需短暂且自限，以避免对组织的持续损伤。 Leukocyte adhesion and recruitment are physiological responses during inflammation, but these processes must be transient and self-limited to avoid persistent tissue injury.

激活内皮介导的白细胞募集Leukocyte recruitment by an activated endothelium

5
激活后的内皮细胞表面表达粘附分子，介导白细胞的滚动、牢固黏附和穿越血管壁。 Activated endothelial cells express adhesion molecules on their surface, mediating leukocyte rolling, firm adhesion, and transmigration across the vessel wall.
白细胞招募包括三个步骤
Leukocyte recruitment involves three steps:

滚动（由P-和E-选择素介导） Rolling (mediated by P- and E-Selectin)

牢固黏附（由ICAM-1和VCAM-1介导） Firm adhesion (mediated by ICAM-1 and VCAM-1)

穿出（跨内皮迁移） Diapedesis (transmigration across the endothelium)

7
所有上述分子（P-选择素、E-选择素、ICAM-1、VCAM-1）统称为“粘附分子”。 All these molecules (P-Selectin, E-Selectin, ICAM-1, VCAM-1) are collectively known as “adhesion molecules”.

内皮粘附分子的表达状态Expression status of endothelial adhesion molecules

8
静息状态下的内皮细胞（未激活）表面几乎不表达粘附分子，白细胞在血管中自由流动。 Quiescent (non-activated) endothelial cells express little or no adhesion molecules on their surface, allowing leukocytes to circulate freely in the bloodstream.
9
当内皮被激活（例如炎症信号），会显著上调上述粘附分子，启动白细胞的“捕获”和募集过程。 Upon endothelial activation (e.g., by inflammatory signals), there is marked upregulation of these adhesion molecules, which triggers leukocyte “capture” and recruitment.
10
粘附分子的上调是白细胞能够牢固附着在血管壁并随后迁移到组织的关键。 The upregulation of adhesion molecules is key for leukocytes to firmly adhere to the vessel wall and subsequently transmigrate into the tissue.

老师补充与强调的知识点Teacher’s extra explanations and emphasis

11
滚动阶段依赖选择素和糖类配体相互作用，亲和力低，因此白细胞可反复结合和脱离，便于减速并沿内皮“滚动”。 The rolling stage depends on selectin–sugar ligand interactions, which are of low affinity, permitting repeated binding and detachment so that leukocytes slow down and “roll” along the endothelium.
12
牢固粘附阶段依赖ICAM-1/VCAM-1与白细胞上的整合素作用，亲和力高，使白细胞能够牢固停留。 The firm adhesion stage depends on high-affinity interactions between ICAM-1/VCAM-1 and integrins on leukocytes, enabling the leukocytes to firmly attach.
13
步骤顺序为先滚动、再牢固黏附，最后穿出血管壁迁移到病损区。 The sequential steps are first rolling, then firm adhesion, and finally transmigration out of the blood vessel into the affected site.
14
正常情况下只有在炎症激活时才表达大量粘附分子，防止无序招募白细胞导致组织损伤。 Normally, large numbers of adhesion molecules are only expressed upon inflammatory activation, which prevents unwanted leukocyte recruitment and tissue damage under homeostatic conditions.

来

白细胞募集由激活的血管内皮介导Leukocyte recruitment by an activated endothelium

1
用于观察白细胞募集的经典模型是小鼠提睾肌微血管。 The classic model for observing leukocyte recruitment is the mouse cremaster muscle microvessels.
2
通过活体显微镜技术可以实时追踪血管内白细胞的行为。 Intravital microscopy allows real-time tracking of leukocyte behavior within vessels.
3
动脉和小静脉的形态在显微镜下可以区分，图中已经标注。 Arterioles and venules can be distinguished morphologically under the microscope, as shown in the image.
4
白细胞的募集主要发生在毛细血管后的小静脉区段，而不是动脉。 Leukocyte recruitment mainly occurs in post-capillary venules, not in arterioles.
5
老师强调，这一过程之所以发生在小静脉，是因为这些血管段的血流速度较低，内皮易于激活表达各种黏附分子，有利于白细胞滚动、黏附和穿出。 The teacher emphasized that this process occurs in venules because the flow is slower and the endothelium is more easily activated to express adhesion molecules, which facilitates leukocyte rolling, adhesion, and transmigration.

血管内皮可发起血栓反应The vascular endothelium can mount thrombotic responses

6
正常情况下，血管内皮处于静息状态，内皮表面只允许红细胞和血小板流经，保持抗血栓性。 Under normal conditions, the endothelium is quiescent, allowing red blood cells and platelets to circulate while maintaining an antithrombotic surface.
7
当血管内皮被激活时（如炎症或损伤），可以诱导血小板聚集和激活，触发血栓形成。 Upon activation (such as by inflammation or damage), the endothelium can induce platelet aggregation and activation, triggering thrombus formation.
8
血栓主要由血小板聚集体和纤维蛋白交织形成。 Thrombi are mainly composed of aggregates of platelets and cross-linked fibrin.
9
老师补充，纤维蛋白的形成依赖于凝血瀑布级联反应，这与内皮表面表达促凝/促血小板因子密切相关。 The teacher added that fibrin formation depends on the coagulation cascade, which is closely linked to the expression of pro-coagulant/platelet factors on the endothelial surface.

血管芯片微流控模型Microfluidic model of a blood microvessel

10
人内皮细胞被培养在微流控芯片内，并赋予“生理性”流动条件，模拟真实血管环境。 Human endothelial cells are cultured in a microfluidic chip under "physiological" flow conditions to simulate the real vascular environment.
11
在持续流动下，内皮细胞维持静息表型，表现为血管屏障完整、抗血栓、低表达黏附分子。 Under continuous flow, endothelial cells maintain a quiescent phenotype, with intact barrier function, antithrombotic properties, and low expression of adhesion molecules.

芯片系统显示血管状态及血小板行为Chip system shows endothelial and platelet behavior

12
未处理（静息）条件下，芯片内只可见稀疏血小板，无明显聚集或血栓形成。 Under untreated (quiescent) conditions, only sparse platelets are observed in the chip, with no obvious aggregation or thrombus formation.
13
用TNF-α等炎症因子处理后，内皮被激活，大量血小板簇集、血栓形成。 After treatment with inflammatory factors such as TNF-α, the endothelium is activated, and massive platelet clusters and thrombi are formed.
14
芯片实验直接可视化了激活内皮在血小板粘附、聚集和血栓形成中的核心作用。 Chip experiments directly visualize the central role of activated endothelium in platelet adhesion, aggregation, and thrombus formation.

老师补充与强调的知识点Teacher’s extra points and emphasis

15
老师提到该系统模拟了血管内流动及细胞真实行为，有利于理解血管疾病尤其是血栓和炎症的机制。 The teacher mentioned that this system simulates real intravascular flow and cellular behavior, thus facilitating the understanding of mechanisms of vascular diseases, especially thrombosis and inflammation.
16
强调内皮的激活是血栓和白细胞招募的共同起点，而保持内皮静息状态对于血管健康极其重要。 It was emphasized that endothelial activation is the starting point for both thrombosis and leukocyte recruitment, while the maintenance of endothelial quiescence is essential for vascular health.

血管内皮表面血栓形成Thrombus formation on the endothelial surface

1
血管内皮激活时会表达多种血小板粘附分子，如vWF和P-选择素（P-sel）。 Upon activation, the vascular endothelium expresses various platelet adhesion molecules such as vWF and P-selectin (P-sel)。
2
在静息状态下，这些分子（vWF和P-sel）主要储存在内皮细胞内的Weibel-Palade小体中，内皮表面几乎检测不到。 In the quiescent state, these molecules (vWF and P-sel) are mainly stored in Weibel-Palade bodies inside endothelial cells, and are barely present on the cell surface。
3
只有当内皮受到炎症、损伤等激活刺激时，Weibel-Palade小体才发生外排作用，将vWF和P-选择素释放到细胞表面。 Only when the endothelium is activated by stimuli such as inflammation or injury do Weibel-Palade bodies undergo exocytosis, releasing vWF and P-selectin onto the cell surface。
4
vWF形成丝状结构（vWF strings），为血小板提供粘附平台。 vWF forms string-like structures (vWF strings) on the surface, providing a scaffold for platelet adhesion。
5
P-选择素也暴露于内皮表面，同样参与血小板的粘附和激活。 P-selectin is also exposed on the endothelial surface and participates in platelet adhesion and activation。
6
血小板先与vWF、P-选择素结合，继而被激活并进一步聚集，促成血栓的形成。 Platelets first bind to vWF and P-selectin, become activated, and then further aggregate, promoting thrombus formation。
7
实验可以通过流式芯片系统做人内皮细胞并模拟流动，荧光标记血小板后可直观看到这种vWF丝及血小板聚集。 Experiments using microfluidic chip systems with human endothelial cells under flow, and fluorescently labeled platelets, directly visualize these vWF strings and platelet aggregation。
8
激活内皮表面除了表达vWF和P-选择素，还外翻磷脂酰丝氨酸（PtdSer），并暴露组织因子（TF），启动凝血级联反应。 The activated endothelium, besides expressing vWF and P-selectin, also externalizes phosphatidylserine (PtdSer) and exposes tissue factor (TF), which initiates the coagulation cascade。
9
组织因子（TF）与外暴露的PtdSer协同，促进凝血酶原→凝血酶的转化，最终形成纤维蛋白网，完善血栓结构。 Tissue factor (TF) together with exposed PtdSer promotes the conversion of prothrombin to thrombin, ultimately forming a fibrin mesh to stabilize the thrombus。
10
老师强调，只有激活的内皮才具备完全的血栓形成能力，静息内皮对血小板和凝血反应表现出强烈的抑制性，保证血管内环境的稳定。 The teacher emphasized that only activated endothelium possesses the full capacity for thrombus formation, while quiescent endothelium strongly inhibits platelet and coagulation reactions, maintaining vascular homeostasis。
11
PtdSer的外翻和TF的暴露是经典标志，促进了外源性凝血途径的启动，对血栓形成具有决定性作用。 The externalization of PtdSer and exposure of TF are classic markers, decisively promoting the initiation of the extrinsic coagulation pathway and playing a key role in thrombus formation。
12
由此可见，血管内皮在血栓形成中的地位是“开关”——激活时促进血小板和凝血反应，静息时抑制所有相关机制。 Thus, the role of the endothelium in thrombosis is that of a “switch”—when activated, it promotes platelet and coagulation responses, while in the resting state it suppresses all related mechanisms。

磷脂酰丝氨酸（PtdSer）外翻及其促凝作用与血栓形成Phosphatidylserine (PtdSer) exposition, procoagulant effects, and thrombus formation

1
激活内皮细胞表面磷脂酰丝氨酸的外翻Exposition of phosphatidylserine (PtdSer) on activated endothelial cells

激活时，磷脂酰丝氨酸（PtdSer）从细胞膜内侧“翻转”到外侧膜表面，这一过程称为flip-flop。 Upon activation, phosphatidylserine (PtdSer) flips from the inner to the outer leaflet of the cell membrane, a process called flip-flop.

PtdCholine是一种中性的磷脂；PtdSer是一种带负电荷的磷脂。 PtdCholine is a neutral phospholipid; PtdSer is a negatively charged phospholipid.

这种PtdSer“外翻”与凋亡细胞表面的PtdSer暴露有一定相似性。 This PtdSer exposition is analogous to the exposure of PtdSer on the surface of apoptotic cells。

2
PtdSer为凝血提供促凝表面Procoagulant effects of phosphatidylserine exposition

PtdSer暴露可募集因子VIIa与组织因子（TF），激活外源性凝血途径。 PtdSer exposition recruits Factor VIIa and tissue factor (TF), activating the extrinsic coagulation pathway.

PtdSer与Ca2+协同，促进凝血酶的生成，从而加速凝血反应。 In combination with Ca2+, PtdSer promotes thrombin generation and accelerates coagulation reactions.

内皮细胞膜上PtdSer的外翻和组织因子的表达，使凝血反应大大增强。 PtdSer exposure and TF expression on the endothelial membrane markedly stimulate coagulation.

公式表达
促凝启动（TF+PtdSer+Ca2+）+ 凝血反应加速 = 强烈促进凝血 Formula: Coagulation initiation (TF + PtdSer + Ca2+) + Reaction acceleration = Strong stimulation of coagulation。
3
血管内皮表面血栓形成机制Mechanism of thrombus formation on the endothelial surface

激活后，内皮表面暴露PtdSer与TF，上调P-选择素（P-sel）、vWF等分子，促进血小板粘附和激活。 After activation, the endothelial surface exposes PtdSer and TF and upregulates P-selectin (P-sel), vWF, and other molecules to promote platelet adhesion and activation.

血小板聚集后，凝血级联反应加快，最终形成纤维蛋白网稳定血栓。 Platelet aggregation and accelerated coagulation cascade ultimately generate a fibrin mesh to stabilize the thrombus.

凝血酶不仅生成纤维蛋白，还能进一步活化血小板与内皮，放大血栓-炎症反应。 Thrombin not only generates fibrin but also strongly activates platelets and endothelial cells, amplifying thrombo-inflammation。

4
激活内皮的促血栓效应Thrombogenicity of the activated endothelium

激活内皮分泌促血小板黏附和激活分子，如TXA2、vWF、P选择素。 Activated endothelium releases molecules that promote platelet adhesion and activation, such as TXA2, vWF, and P-selectin.

同时表达组织因子和PtdSer，显著刺激凝血。 Endothelial cells also express TF and PtdSer, dramatically stimulating coagulation.

PAI-1等分子可抑制纤溶活性，进一步促进血栓形成。 Molecules such as PAI-1 inhibit fibrinolysis, further favoring thrombosis。

5
内皮激活的标志和结果Cardinal features and consequences of endothelial activation
激活的内皮有三大核心表现
促炎（炎症细胞招募）、促血栓（血小板/凝血）、增强通透性（易渗漏）。 The three cardinal features of activated endothelium are: pro-inflammatory (leukocyte recruitment), prothrombotic (platelet/coagulation), and increased permeability (leakiness).

各种粘附分子（E-selectin、ICAM-1、VCAM-1）促炎作用；vWF、P-选择素、TF和PtdSer促血栓；紧密连接损伤致屏障功能下降。 Adhesion molecules (E-selectin, ICAM-1, VCAM-1) mediate inflammation; vWF, P-selectin, TF, and PtdSer drive thrombosis; junction disruption increases permeability。

6
内皮激活既可生理也可致病Two sides of endothelial activation: physiological vs pathological

内皮激活是正常炎症和止血反应的基础，但必须短暂、自限。 Endothelial activation underlies physiological inflammation and hemostasis, but must be transient and self-limited.

若激活持续、范围广泛，则出现异常渗漏、大量白细胞和血栓形成，导致“血栓-炎症”病理状态（thrombo-inflammation）。 If activation is prolonged or widespread, it leads to abnormal leakage, massive leukocyte and thrombus formation, resulting in the pathological state called thrombo-inflammation。

血管内皮激活的反应性（上调）Strong reactivity of the vascular endothelium (↑)

1
内皮激活具有快速、强烈的反应性 Endothelial activation is characterized by rapid and strong reactivity
2
Weibel-Palade小体是内皮细胞特有的分泌颗粒，内含多种反应分子 Weibel-Palade bodies are secretion granules specific for endothelial cells, containing various reactive molecules
3
静息（未激活）状态下，Weibel-Palade小体存在于内皮细胞内，内容物不暴露于表面 In the quiescent state, Weibel-Palade bodies are found inside endothelial cells and their contents are not exposed on the cell surface
4
主要含有大量vWF（血管性血友病因子），同时还有P-选择素、Angiopoietin-2（Ang-2）、内皮素-1（Endothelin-1）、IL-8等 They mainly contain large amounts of vWF (von Willebrand Factor), as well as P-selectin, Angiopoietin-2 (Ang-2), Endothelin-1, IL-8 and more
5
这些分子平时储存在胞内囊泡，遇激活时，可通过胞吐作用（exocytosis）瞬间释放到细胞表面 These molecules are normally stored in intracellular vesicles, and upon activation, can be rapidly released to the cell surface via exocytosis
6
vWF在表面形成丝状结构，为血小板粘附、激活和血栓形成提供支架 vWF forms string-like structures on the surface, providing a scaffold for platelet adhesion, activation, and thrombus formation
7
P-选择素同样被转运至表面，促进白细胞和血小板的粘附 P-selectin is also transported to the surface, promoting adhesion of leukocytes and platelets
8
Ang-2等分子调控血管的稳定性和炎症反应 Molecules such as Ang-2 modulate vascular stability and inflammatory responses
9
活化内皮迅速分泌或暴露“血栓-炎症”反应分子，包括vWF、P选择素、Ang-2等 Activated endothelium rapidly secretes or exposes "thrombo-inflammatory" molecules, including vWF, P-selectin, Ang-2, and related factors
10
激活信号触发胞吐作用（exocytosis），这是一个几秒到几分钟内完成的快过程 Activation signals trigger exocytosis, a rapid process occurring within seconds to minutes
11
讲解中强调，Weibel-Palade小体只存在于内皮，不存在于血小板等其他细胞 The lecture emphasized that Weibel-Palade bodies are unique to endothelial cells and not found in platelets or other cell types
12
vWF的多聚结构能形成非常长的纤维，有时可达毫米级，驱动血栓纤维网的形成 The multimeric structure of vWF can form extremely long fibers, sometimes up to millimeters, driving the formation of a thrombotic fiber network
13
结论：强烈反应性是内皮细胞快速应对损伤与炎症的基础，但如果激活过度或持续则可能导致异常血栓和炎症反应 In summary: Strong reactivity allows endothelial cells to rapidly respond to injury and inflammation, but excessive or prolonged activation can lead to abnormal thrombosis and inflammatory responses

血管内皮激活的三大特征Cardinal features of endothelial activation

激活内皮具有三大核心表现
促炎、促血栓、增加通透性 The activated endothelium has three cardinal features: pro-inflammatory, prothrombotic, increased permeability
2
促炎表现为表达黏附分子（如E-selectin、ICAM-1、VCAM-1），招募白细胞滚动、牢固结合、进入组织 The pro-inflammatory property is the expression of adhesion molecules (such as E-selectin, ICAM-1, VCAM-1), recruiting leukocytes for rolling, firm adhesion, and transmigration
3
促血栓表现为表达血小板粘附激活分子如vWF、P-selectin，及促凝分子如TF、PtdSer The prothrombotic property manifests as expression of platelet adhesion/activation molecules like vWF, P-selectin, and procoagulant molecules like TF and PtdSer
4
增加通透性表现为细胞间连接松弛，血浆成分渗漏入组织 Increased permeability is characterized by loosened intercellular junctions, allowing plasma components to leak into tissues
5
内皮处于静息状态时对促炎和促血栓反应有强抑制力，仅激活状态才充分具备上述能力 The endothelium in the quiescent state provides strong suppression of these reactions; only in the activated state does it fully acquire these properties

激活内皮高度易血栓（上调）The activated endothelium is highly thrombogenic (↑)

1
激活内皮表面通过多机制促进血栓形成，三大方面如下 The activated endothelial surface promotes thrombosis through three main mechanisms:
促进血小板粘附和激活
Stimulation of platelet adhesion and activation:

分泌前列腺素TXA2，促进血小板激活 Secretes TXA2 to promote platelet activation

表达vWF和P-选择素，增强血小板在内皮表的粘附 Expresses vWF and P-selectin, enhancing platelet adhesion to the endothelial surface

促进凝血
Stimulation of coagulation:

表达组织因子（TF）启动外源性凝血 Expresses tissue factor (TF) to initiate the extrinsic coagulation pathway

外翻磷脂酰丝氨酸（PtdSer）为凝血级联反应提供促凝表面 Externalizes phosphatidylserine (PtdSer) to provide a procoagulant surface for the coagulation cascade

抑制纤溶
Inhibition of fibrinolysis:

表达PAI-1抑制纤溶酶原激活剂，减弱纤维蛋白的溶解 Expresses PAI-1 to inhibit plasminogen activators and reduce fibrin breakdown

5
公式： Formula: 内皮促血栓能力 = 血小板粘附/激活 + 凝血级联激活 + 抑制纤溶 Thrombogenicity of endothelium = stimulation of platelet adhesion/activation + activation of coagulation cascade + inhibition of fibrinolysis

内皮激活的生理与病理双面性Physiological and pathological sides of endothelial activation

1
内皮激活是生理应答（如止血、修复、抗感染）的基础，但需短暂自限 Endothelial activation underlies physiological responses (such as hemostasis, repair, anti-infection), but must be transient and self-limited
2
若激活持续、范围广泛则转为病理，表现为异常渗漏、白细胞和血栓大量形成，进入“血栓-炎症”状态 If activation is prolonged or widespread, it becomes pathological, manifesting as abnormal vascular leakage, excessive leukocyte influx, and clot formation, leading to "thrombo-inflammation"
3
典型例子包括重度感染、脓毒症等系统性炎症，既可有出血也可有血栓 Classic examples include severe infection, sepsis and other systemic inflammatory responses, with possible bleeding and thrombosis

内皮激活与内皮静息特性的要点总结Features of Endothelial Activation and Quiescence

1
内皮激活Endothelial activation

激活的内皮细胞会分为快速反应和慢速反应两种模式。 Activated endothelial cells display both rapid and slow response modes.

快速反应依赖于Weibel-Palade小体的胞吐，数秒至数分钟内将vWF、P-选择素、Ang-2等分子暴露到细胞表面。 The rapid response involves exocytosis of Weibel-Palade bodies, releasing vWF, P-selectin, Ang-2, and other molecules to the cell surface within seconds to minutes.

这些分子有助于血小板、白细胞的粘附和激活，为随后炎症和血栓反应作准备。 These molecules facilitate adhesion and activation of platelets and leukocytes, preparing for subsequent inflammation and thrombus responses.

慢速反应则涉及基因表达和蛋白合成，如E-selectin、ICAM-1、VCAM-1和组织因子（TF）的上调，通常需要几小时完成。 The slow response involves gene expression and protein synthesis, such as upregulation of E-selectin, ICAM-1, VCAM-1, and tissue factor (TF), which usually takes several hours.

激活状态下，内皮表面可以同时表达多种促炎、促粘附、促血栓分子。 Upon activation, the endothelial surface can simultaneously express multiple pro-inflammatory, pro-adhesive, and pro-thrombotic molecules.

2
静息内皮的屏障功能Barrier function of the quiescent endothelium

静息状态的内皮紧密连接，形成严密的屏障，用于限制血浆成分外渗。 In a quiescent state, the endothelium forms tight junctions that restrict leakage of plasma components.

内皮连接的表达和维持，是屏障功能的结构基础。 Expression and maintenance of inter-endothelial junctions is the structural basis for the barrier function.

显微成像可观察到血管壁的完整、致密结构。 Microscopy reveals the intact and dense structure of the vessel wall.

3
静息内皮天然抗炎特性Anti-inflammatory properties of quiescent endothelium

静息内皮很少表达或几乎不表达黏附分子。 The quiescent endothelium expresses very little or no adhesion molecules.

因此，循环的白细胞不会粘附或停留在内皮表面。 Thus, circulating leukocytes do not adhere to or linger on the endothelial surface.

这一特性使静息内皮抗炎、限制炎症反应的发生。 This property makes the quiescent endothelium anti-inflammatory and restricts inflammation.

4
静息内皮的抗血栓功能Antithrombotic function of quiescent endothelium

静息内皮通过多机制抑制血小板粘附/激活，抑制凝血，并促进纤溶。 The quiescent endothelium suppresses platelet adhesion/activation, inhibits coagulation, and promotes fibrinolysis through multiple mechanisms.

典型分子包括
NO、PGI2、CD39（抑制血小板），TM（血栓调节蛋白）、PS、TFPI（抑制凝血），肝素硫酸（激活抗凝蛋白），以及t-PA（促进纤溶）。 Representative molecules include NO, PGI2, CD39 (inhibiting platelets), TM (thrombomodulin), PS, TFPI (inhibiting coagulation), heparan sulfates (activating antithrombin), and t-PA (promoting fibrinolysis).

这些分子的作用，保证了血管内皮为“兼容血液的表面”，不易发生血栓。 The actions of these molecules ensure that the endothelium is a blood-compatible surface and resists thrombosis.

5
静息内皮“三重”功能模式The "triple" function profile of quiescent endothelium
静息内皮具备
不渗漏（屏障）、抗炎、抗血栓三重主要功能。 The quiescent endothelium is characterized by three main functions: impermeability (barrier), anti-inflammatory, and antithrombotic.

在显微镜下观察可见静息血管壁结构完整、细胞表面无血细胞或血小板粘附。 Under microscopy, the intact vessel wall and lack of cell/platelet adhesion can be observed on the surface of the quiescent endothelium.

6
内皮激活三大核心特征Three cardinal features of endothelial activation
内皮激活有三大核心表现
血管炎症（炎症细胞募集）、血管渗漏（液体、蛋白渗出）、血栓形成（血小板聚集、凝血活化）。 Endothelial activation presents three cardinal features: vascular inflammation (leukocyte recruitment), vascular leakage (fluid/protein extravasation), and thrombus formation (platelet aggregation, coagulation activation).

这一转变导致病理性炎症和血栓的发生，是多种疾病的核心驱动力。 This transition leads to pathological inflammation and thrombosis, which underlies many diseases.

7
静息内皮的维持机制Mechanism for maintaining quiescence in the endothelium

内皮Tie2受体持续受到由周细胞等产生的Ang-1的激活，维持内皮处于静息状态。 The endothelial Tie2 receptor is constitutively activated by Ang-1 produced by mural cells, keeping the endothelium quiescent.

Tie2受体激活能下调黏附分子和组织因子的表达，稳定细胞间连接，抑制内皮激活。 Tie2 receptor activation downregulates adhesion molecules and tissue factor expression, stabilizes intercellular junctions, and suppresses endothelial activation.

8
总结Summary

内皮静息状态通过屏障、抗炎、抗血栓三重机制维持血管健康，依赖于分子信号（如Tie2-Ang-1）持续调控。 The quiescent state of endothelium maintains vascular health through barrier, anti-inflammatory, and antithrombotic mechanisms, relying on persistent molecular signals such as Tie2-Ang-1.

激活状态下，内皮可迅速转变为促炎、促血栓、渗漏状态，为生理应答及病理过程提供基础。 Upon activation, the endothelium quickly shifts to pro-inflammatory, pro-thrombotic, and permeable states, providing the basis for physiological response and pathology.

Tie2信号通路与内皮静息的调控 Tie2 signaling and endothelial quiescence

1
Tie2是内皮细胞表面的受体酪氨酸激酶 Tie2 is a receptor tyrosine kinase expressed on the surface of endothelial cells
2
Tie2的激活依赖于配体Ang-1，Ang-1主要由血管周细胞分泌 Tie2 activation depends on its ligand Ang-1, which is mainly secreted by perivascular cells
3
Ang-1结合并激活Tie2后，引发受体自磷酸化，启动下游信号通路 Upon Ang-1 binding, Tie2 is activated and autophosphorylated, triggering downstream signaling pathways
4
Tie2- Ang-1信号维持内皮细胞的静息（“抗激活”） Tie2-Ang-1 signaling maintains endothelial cell quiescence (“anti-activation” state)
这一信号通路产生多重保护性生理效应
This pathway triggers multiple protective physiological effects:   a) 抗炎（下调黏附分子表达，抑制白细胞募集） a) Anti-inflammatory (downregulates adhesion molecules, inhibits leukocyte recruitment)   b) 抗血栓（增强抗凝因子，抑制促凝分子如TF等） b) Antithrombotic (upregulates anticoagulant factors, suppresses procoagulant molecules like TF)   c) 增强屏障功能（稳定细胞间连接，减少血管渗漏） c) Endothelial barrier protection (stabilizes intercellular junctions, reduces vascular leakage)   d) 促进细胞存活（诱导生存信号，防止内皮凋亡） d) Promotes cell survival (induces survival signaling and protects against apoptosis)
6
动画或图片中，Tie2激活的内皮细胞表现为结构紧密且“微笑”状态 In the illustration, Tie2-activated endothelium appears compact and “happy” (smiling)

Ang-2介导的Tie2信号失调及内皮激活 Dysregulation of Tie2 signaling by Ang-2

1
Ang-2是Ang-1的拮抗物，主要在应激、炎症、活化情况下表达和释放 Ang-2 is an antagonist of Ang-1, mainly expressed and released during stress, inflammation, or activation
2
Ang-2竞争性结合Tie2，阻断Ang-1的保护性激活信号 Ang-2 competitively binds Tie2, blocking the protective activation by Ang-1
3
Tie2失活后，内皮细胞转为易激活、促炎和促病理型 With Tie2 deactivation, endothelial cells shift toward activation, pro-inflammatory and pathological phenotype
结果表现为
As a result:   a) 上调炎症黏附分子，促进白细胞粘附和迁移 a) Increased adhesion molecule expression, promoting leukocyte adhesion and transmigration   b) 上调促凝分子（如TF、PtdSer），增强促血栓作用 b) Increased expression of procoagulant factors (TF, PtdSer), enhancing thrombogenicity   c) 细胞间连接变松，血管通透性上升，易渗漏 c) Loosened intercellular junctions, increased vascular permeability, prone to leakage   d) 激活促凋亡信号，易导致内皮细胞凋亡 d) Activates pro-apoptotic signals, leading to endothelial apoptosis   e) Exocytosis of Weibel-Palade小体，进一步促进炎症-血栓反应 e) Exocytosis of Weibel-Palade bodies, further promoting thrombo-inflammation

内皮功能障碍与药物开发 Endothelial dysfunction in development of drugs & health products

1
内皮功能障碍表现为促炎、促血栓、通透性升高等异常反应 Endothelial dysfunction is characterized by pro-inflammatory, prothrombotic, and hyperpermeable responses
2
临床上既是多种疾病的发病基础，也为药物筛选开发提供靶点 It is the pathological basis for various diseases and offers therapeutic targets for drug development
3
相关异常分子可作为生物标志物用于疾病诊断和疗效监测 Associated abnormal molecules can serve as biomarkers for disease diagnosis and monitoring

内皮功能障碍的病理生理作用 Pathophysiological role of endothelial dysfunction

1
内皮功能障碍是败血症、缺血性中风、镰状细胞病、动脉粥样硬化等多种疾病的共同驱动力 Endothelial dysfunction drives and aggravates many diseases such as sepsis, ischemic stroke, sickle cell disease, atherosclerosis, and others
2
不同疾病背景下，引发炎症、血栓、屏障紊乱等一系列连续反应 Under different disease backgrounds, it triggers a cascade of inflammation, thrombosis, and barrier disruption

药物激活Tie2有望恢复血管内皮健康 Pharmacological activation of endothelial Tie2 receptor

1
药物激活Tie2可恢复内皮静息与血管稳态 Pharmacological activation of Tie2 can restore endothelial quiescence and vascular homeostasis
2
表现为抗炎、抗血栓、增强屏障和促进存活等全方位保护效应 This results in anti-inflammatory, antithrombotic, barrier-enhancing, and survival-promoting protective effects

小结及知识点归纳 Summary and key points

1
Tie2-Ang-1通路是维持内皮静息和血管健康的核心信号轴 The Tie2-Ang-1 axis is a core signaling pathway for endothelial quiescence and vascular health
2
Ang-2是拮抗剂，参与炎症、疾病进展中的Tie2失活和内皮激活 Ang-2 acts as an antagonist, contributing to Tie2 deactivation and endothelial activation during inflammation and disease progression
3
针对Tie2的药物研发具有重要理论和临床意义 Drug development targeting Tie2 holds great theoretical and clinical promise

052025-10-27（第 2 讲）

器官灌注由动脉血压驱动 Organ perfusion is driven by arterial blood pressure

1
器官灌注依赖于动脉血压（ΔP_A） Organ perfusion depends on arterial blood pressure (ΔP_A)
2
ΔP_A = Q_C × PVR ΔP_A = Q_C × PVR
3
Q_C = HR × V_S Q_C = HR × V_S
4
Q_C为心输出量（每分钟升），PVR为外周血管阻力，HR为心率，V_S为每搏射血量 Q_C is cardiac output (L/min), PVR is peripheral vascular resistance, HR is heart rate, and V_S is systolic ejection volume
5
右心系统为低压系统 The right heart is a low-pressure system
6
左心系统为高压系统 The left heart is a high-pressure system
问7. 问题：如何治疗高血压（动脉高血压）？ Quiz: How to treat high blood pressure (arterial hypertension)?
答

神经激素调节与血压 Neurohormonal regulation and blood pressure

1
神经激素刺激主要包括交感神经系统和肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统 Neurohormonal stimulation mainly involves the sympathetic nervous system and the renin-angiotensin-aldosterone system
2
交感神经系统通过去甲肾上腺素（NA）作用于α₁肾上腺素能受体，导致血管收缩（血管平滑肌收缩） The sympathetic nervous system acts through noradrenaline (NA) on α₁ adrenergic receptors, causing vasoconstriction (vascular smooth muscle contraction)
3
结果是外周血管阻力(PVR)↑，进而血压升高 The result is increased peripheral vascular resistance (PVR↑), leading to increased blood pressure
4
心脏β₁肾上腺素能受体促进正性肌力、正性心率等作用，增强心输出量 Cardiac β₁ adrenergic receptors mediate positive inotropic and chronotropic effects, increasing cardiac output
5
结果是心输出量(Q_C)↑，血压升高 As a result, cardiac output (Q_C) increases, elevating blood pressure
6
肾脏β₁受体促使肾素分泌，启动肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统 Renal β₁ receptors stimulate renin secretion, activating the renin-angiotensin-aldosterone system
7
肾素促使血管紧张素I生成，继而被转化酶转为血管紧张素II Renin allows generation of angiotensin I, then converted by ACE into angiotensin II
8
血管紧张素II激活AT₁受体，导致血管收缩和醛固酮分泌 Angiotensin II activates AT₁ receptor, causing vasoconstriction and aldosterone secretion
9
醛固酮增加体液容量，导致血压进一步升高 Aldosterone increases fluid volume, further raising blood pressure
10
高血压调控的核心就是降低心输出量或降低外周阻力 The key to treating hypertension is to reduce cardiac output or peripheral vascular resistance

简要总结 Brief summary

动脉血压受心输出量和外周阻力共同决定 Arterial blood pressure is jointly determined by cardiac output and peripheral resistance

交感神经和肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统通过多靶点影响血压 The sympathetic nervous system and renin-angiotensin-aldosterone system regulate blood pressure through multiple targets

治疗高血压的核心策略基于这些机制 Critical strategies for managing hypertension are based on these mechanisms

心血管疾病药物作用靶点概述与肾上腺素能系统II. Some key pharmacological targets to treat cardiovascular diseases & Adrenergic system

1
主要药物作用靶点简介 Introduction to key pharmacological targets

心血管疾病药物主要作用三类分子：跨膜受体（如GPCR）、离子通道（如钙通道）、酶（如催化底物转为产物的酶）。 Most cardiovascular drugs target three types of molecules: transmembrane receptors (like GPCRs), ion channels (like calcium channels), and enzymes (which catalyze conversion of substrates into products)。

2
肾上腺素能系统相关疾病 Cardiovascular disorders related to the adrenergic system

肾上腺素能系统与心力衰竭、冠心病、高血压、心律失常、焦虑等疾病密切相关。 The adrenergic system is closely related to diseases such as heart failure, coronary artery disease, hypertension, arrhythmias, and anxiety。

3
肾上腺素能系统的介质和通路 Mediators and pathway of the adrenergic system

交感神经末梢释放去甲肾上腺素（NA），主要影响心脏和血管；肾上腺髓质释放肾上腺素（Ad），以激素形式循环全身。 Norepinephrine (NA) is released from sympathetic nerve endings, mainly acting on the heart and vessels; adrenaline (Ad) is secreted by the adrenal medulla and circulates as a hormone throughout the body。

4
靶受体与亲和力 Target receptors and affinity

α-肾上腺素能受体对去甲肾上腺素亲和力较高（NA > Ad）；β-肾上腺素能受体对NA和Ad亲和力相近（NA = Ad）。 α-adrenergic receptors have higher affinity for norepinephrine (NA > Ad); β-adrenergic receptors display similar affinity for NA and Ad (NA = Ad)。

5
肾上腺素受体结构及信号耦联 Structural features and signal coupling of adrenergic receptors

肾上腺素受体为典型七跨膜G蛋白偶联受体（GPCR）。 Adrenergic receptors are classical seven-transmembrane GPCRs。

α1受体偶联Gq蛋白，激活磷脂酶C（PLC），提高细胞内[Ca2+]和PKC活性。 α1 receptors couple to Gq protein, activating PLC, increasing intracellular [Ca2+] and PKC activity。

α2受体偶联Gi蛋白，抑制腺苷酸环化酶（AC），降低cAMP和PKA活性。 α2 receptors couple to Gi protein, inhibiting adenylate cyclase (AC), decreasing cAMP and PKA activity。

β受体（β1、β2、β3）主要偶联Gs蛋白，激活AC，升高cAMP和PKA活性，部分亚型可激活NOS生成NO。 β receptors (β1, β2, β3) mainly couple to Gs protein, activating AC, raising cAMP and PKA activity; some subtypes can activate NOS to produce NO。

6
主要心血管生理效应 Major cardiovascular effects

α1受体激活导致血管平滑肌收缩，升高血压。 α1 receptor activation causes vascular smooth muscle contraction, raising blood pressure。

α2受体中枢激活抑制交感神经，增强副交感，外周可致血管收缩。 Central α2 activation suppresses sympathetic, enhances parasympathetic tone; peripherally it induces vasoconstriction。

β1受体激活增强心脏收缩性（正性肌力）、加快心率（正性频率），并促进肾素分泌。 β1 activation increases heart contractility (positive inotropy), accelerates heart rate (positive chronotropy), and stimulates renin secretion。

β2受体激活引起平滑肌舒张，导致血管和支气管扩张。 β2 activation causes smooth muscle relaxation, leading to vasodilation and bronchodilation。

β3受体主要参与脂肪组织代谢，部分参与心血管舒张。 β3 receptors play a main role in adipose tissue metabolism and partly contribute to vasodilation。

7
激动剂与拮抗剂的作用 Agonists and antagonists

α1激动剂如去氧肾上腺素，升高血压；α1拮抗剂如哌唑嗪，降压。 α1 agonists such as phenylephrine raise blood pressure; α1 antagonists such as prazosin lower blood pressure。

α2激动剂如可乐定，有降压作用；α2拮抗剂如育亨宾，可提高血压。 α2 agonists like clonidine lower blood pressure; α2 antagonists such as yohimbine can increase blood pressure。

β1/β2激动剂如异丙肾上腺素可提高心输出，β1选择性激动剂如多巴酚丁胺主要用于心脏；β2激动剂如沙丁胺醇用于平喘/扩血管。 β1/β2 agonists like isoprenaline increase cardiac output; β1-selective agonists such as dobutamine act mainly on the heart; β2 agonists such as salbutamol are used for bronchodilation/vasodilation。

非选择性β受体拮抗剂（如普萘洛尔、卡维地洛），β1选择性拮抗剂（如美托洛尔、比索洛尔、奈比洛尔），广泛用于心血管疾病。 Non-selective β antagonists (e.g., propranolol, carvedilol) and β1-selective antagonists (e.g., atenolol, bisoprolol, nebivolol) are widely used in cardiovascular diseases。

8
拮抗剂的副作用与临床意义 Adverse effects and clinical significance of antagonists

β1拮抗剂作用于心脏和肾脏带来有益的降压和抗心脏重塑作用。 β1 antagonists act on the heart and kidney, providing beneficial antihypertensive and cardioprotective effects。

非选择性β拮抗剂抑制β2受体，可能引起支气管收缩、外周血管收缩、低血糖及胰高血糖素减少等不良反应，因此临床心血管首选β1选择性拮抗剂。 Non-selective β antagonists inhibit β2 receptors, which may cause bronchoconstriction, peripheral vasoconstriction, hypoglycemia, and reduced glucagon secretion; thus, β1-selective antagonists are preferred in cardiovascular therapy。

（复习要点：了解不同受体的心血管效应和药物作用机制，掌握代表药物及其临床适应证与副作用。Recap: Recognize the cardiovascular effects of each receptor and mechanisms of drug action, be familiar with key drugs, clinical indications, and side effects.）

肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统（RAAS）作用与调控 | The Renin-Angiotensin-Aldosterone System (RAAS): Function and Regulation

1
RAAS系统的激活与临床意义
1
Activation of the RAAS and Clinical Significance

RAAS系统在高血压、心力衰竭、急性冠脉综合征及心血管预防等疾病中发挥重要作用。 The RAAS is crucial in hypertension, heart failure, acute coronary syndrome, and cardiovascular prevention.

靶向RAAS系统可以有效控制上述疾病的进展与并发症。 Targeting the RAAS system is effective for controlling disease progression and complications in these conditions.

2
RAAS系统级联反应机制
2
RAAS Cascade Mechanism

肝脏合成释放血管紧张素原。 The liver synthesizes and releases angiotensinogen.

肾脏由于β-肾上腺素能受体刺激、入球小动脉灌注压下降或远曲小管NaCl浓度降低而分泌肾素。 The kidney secretes renin in response to β-adrenergic stimulation, decreased afferent arteriole pressure, or reduced NaCl at the macula densa.

肾素将血管紧张素原转化为血管紧张素I（十肽）。 Renin converts angiotensinogen into angiotensin I (a decapeptide).

血管紧张素I在肺等部位被ACE1（血管紧张素转换酶1）转化为血管紧张素II（八肽）。 Angiotensin I is converted by ACE1 (mainly in pulmonary endothelium) into angiotensin II (an octapeptide).

3
血管紧张素II的作用靶点与生理效应
3
Angiotensin II: Targets and Physiological Effects

血管紧张素II通过激活AT1受体介导其主要生理作用，同时还能激活AT2受体但功能相对弱。 Angiotensin II mediates its primary effects through the AT1 receptor; the AT2 receptor has weaker or adaptive functions.

在血管平滑肌细胞，AT1受体激活Ga_q-PLC通路导致收缩，升高血压。 In vascular smooth muscle, AT1 receptor activates Ga_q–PLC signaling, causing contraction and increasing blood pressure.

在心脏，通过AT1受体增强收缩力（正性肌力）、提高心率（正性频率），并促进心肌肥厚和重构。 In the heart, AT1 activation increases contractility (positive inotropy), heart rate (positive chronotropy), and promotes cardiac hypertrophy/remodeling.

在肾脏，AT1受体使近曲小管钠重吸收增加和远端集合管钾分泌增加。 In the kidney, AT1 receptor enhances proximal tubular sodium reabsorption and distal potassium secretion.

在肾上腺皮质，AT1受体促进醛固酮分泌，进一步通过矿物皮质激素受体（MR）作用增加钠重吸收、钾排泄。 In the adrenal cortex, AT1 receptor promotes aldosterone secretion, which acts via the mineralocorticoid receptor (MR) to increase sodium reabsorption and potassium excretion.

AT1受体刺激肾上腺素分泌和增强交感神经活性。 AT1 receptor also stimulates adrenaline secretion and increases sympathetic tone.

4
RAAS系统负反馈与适应/保护性机制
4
RAAS Feedback and Adaptive/Protective Actions

AT2受体激活主要调节适应性和保护功能，包括抗炎、抗氧化、抗纤维化、抗凋亡，增强心脏与肾脏功能。 AT2 receptor activation mediates adaptive/protective functions, including anti-inflammatory, antioxidant, antifibrotic, antiapoptotic, and improvement of cardiac and renal function.

5
RAAS的快慢加压反应
5
Rapid and Slow Pressor Responses of RAAS

快速加压反应：血管AT1受体致血管收缩；心脏AT1受体增强收缩和心率。 Rapid pressor response: AT1 receptor in vessels causes vasoconstriction; in the heart, increases contractility and heart rate.

慢速加压反应：肾脏及肾上腺AT1受体分别调节钠重吸收及醛固酮分泌，长期维持血容量和血压。 Slow pressor response: AT1 receptor in the kidney and adrenal gland regulates sodium reabsorption and aldosterone secretion, maintaining blood volume and pressure long-term.

6
关键酶与药物作用靶点
6
Key Enzymes and Drug Targets

治疗高血压和心衰等疾病的常用药物包括： Common drugs for hypertension and heart failure target:

肾素抑制剂（如阿利吉仑） Renin inhibitors (e.g., aliskiren)

ACE抑制剂（如贝那普利、卡托普利） ACE inhibitors (e.g., benazepril, captopril)
