AT1受体拮抗剂（如缬沙坦、洛沙坦） AT1 receptor antagonists (e.g., valsartan, losartan)

醛固酮受体拮抗剂（如螺内酯、依普利酮） Aldosterone receptor antagonists (e.g., spironolactone, eplerenone)

7
公式和信号传导简述
7
Formulas and Signal Overview

RAAS级联核心过程（简化）： Core RAAS cascade (simplified): angiotensinogen -(renin)-> angiotensin I -(ACE1)-> angiotensin II 血管紧张素原 -(肾素)-> 血管紧张素I -(ACE1)-> 血管紧张素II

下游受体信号： Downstream signaling: AT1受体（Ga_q-PLC-IP3/Ca2+）→ 收缩、重吸收、分泌等 AT1 receptor (Ga_q-PLC-IP3/Ca2+) → contraction, reabsorption, secretion, etc.

8
小结与考点提示
8
Summary and Exam Tips

需掌握RAAS各级反应、主要作用器官、靶点受体、药物靶点及其在常见心血管疾病管理中的作用。 You must understand each step of the RAAS, main acting organs, receptors, drug targets, and their roles in managing major cardiovascular diseases.

RAAS：调节剂的继发药效学 RAAS: secondary pharmacodynamics of modulators

1
RAAS系统药物靶点及级联反应
1
RAAS drug targets and cascade

肝脏合成血管紧张素原，肾脏分泌肾素催化生成血管紧张素I。 Liver produces angiotensinogen, kidney releases renin to produce angiotensin I.

肺等脏器的ACE1（血管紧张素转换酶）将血管紧张素I转化为血管紧张素II。 ACE1 (in the lungs/endothelium) converts angiotensin I to angiotensin II.

血管紧张素II作用于AT11​受体，促进血管收缩和醛固酮分泌。 Angiotensin II acts on AT11​ receptor, promotes vasoconstriction and aldosterone secretion.

2
主要抑制剂及其作用机制
2
Key inhibitors and mechanisms

肾素抑制剂可抑制血管紧张素原转化为血管紧张素I。 Renin inhibitors reduce conversion of angiotensinogen to angiotensin I.

ACE抑制剂抑制ACE1，减少血管紧张素II生成，也减少缓激肽降解。 ACE inhibitors block ACE1, decrease angiotensin II and reduce bradykinin breakdown.

AT11​受体拮抗剂直接阻断血管紧张素II对靶器官的效应。 AT11​ receptor antagonists block angiotensin II effects on target organs.

醛固酮受体（MR）拮抗剂阻断MR，减少醛固酮介导的肾脏作用。 MR antagonists block aldosterone receptor, reducing aldosterone actions in kidney.

3
缓激肽与副作用
3
Bradykinin and side effects

ACE抑制剂抑制缓激肽分解，增加缓激肽导致血管舒张。 ACE inhibitors inhibit bradykinin breakdown, increase bradykinin and cause vasodilation.

副作用包括：咳嗽、血管神经性水肿（少见）。 Side effects: cough, angioneurotic edema (rare).

4
主要药理效应及副作用
4
Main pharmacological effects and side effects

ACE抑制剂：

ACE inhibitors:

↑ 血管舒张 → ↓ 血压 ↑ Vasodilation → ↓ blood pressure

↗ 咳嗽、↗ 血管性水肿 ↗ Cough, ↗ angioneurotic edema

AT11​受体拮抗剂：

AT11​ receptor antagonists:

↓ 血管收缩 → 低血压（副作用） ↓ Vasoconstriction → hypotension (side effect)

适应证：高血压、心衰等 Indications: hypertension, heart failure etc.

MR拮抗剂：

MR antagonists:

↓ 醛固酮效应 → 高血钾，急性肾衰竭（副作用） ↓ Aldosterone effect → hyperkalemia, acute kidney failure (side effect)

5
其他重要机制提示
5
Other key mechanisms

血管紧张素II收缩肾小球入球小动脉可保持肾小球滤过率，若AT11​受体活性下降则GFR下降。 Angiotensin II constricts afferent arteriole to stabilize GFR; decreasing AT11​ activity decreases GFR.

若肾动脉狭窄患者使用ACEI或ARB，可能导致急性肾衰竭。 In renal artery stenosis, ACEI or ARB may cause acute kidney failure.

总结Summary

RAAS系统药物通过多级靶点调控血压及水盐代谢。 RAAS drugs control blood pressure and fluid balance via multiple targets.

临床使用时需警惕低血压、高血钾、肾损害以及ACEI的特有副作用（咳嗽、水肿）。 Be alert for hypotension, hyperkalemia, renal impairment, and ACEI-specific side effects (cough, angioedema).

L型钙通道在心血管系统中的作用The L-type Calcium channel in the cardiovascular system

1
L型钙通道的激活条件与跨膜电流
1
Activation conditions and transmembrane current of L-type Ca channel

L型钙通道在膜去极化（Em > -40mV）时激活，允许Ca²⁺跨膜内流。 L-type Ca channels are activated by membrane depolarization (Em > -40mV), permitting Ca²⁺ influx across the membrane.

细胞外Ca²⁺浓度高（约1.8 mM），细胞内浓度极低（10⁻⁷ M），导致强烈的驱动力使Ca²⁺内流。 Extracellular Ca²⁺ concentration is high (~1.8 mM), intracellular very low (10⁻⁷ M), creating a strong driving force for Ca²⁺ influx.

钙平衡电位E_Ca约为+116mV，激活时Em远小于E_Ca，所以Ca²⁺流入。 The reversal potential (E_Ca) is about +116mV; when activated, Em is much less than E_Ca, so Ca²⁺ enters the cell.

钙通道电流(I_Ca)导致细胞去极化，同时升高胞内Ca²⁺浓度。 The Ca channel current (I_Ca) causes cell depolarization and increases intracellular Ca²⁺ concentration.

公式： Formula: E_Ca = (RT/2F) × ln([Ca²⁺]_ext / [Ca²⁺]_int) E_Ca = (RT/2F) × ln([Ca²⁺]_ext / [Ca²⁺]_int)

2
L型钙通道的功能状态循环
2
Functional state cycle of L-type Ca channel

休息态时通道关闭；去极化后通道激活，Ca²⁺流入胞内。 At rest, the channel is closed; depolarization activates it, causing Ca²⁺ influx.

持续去极化和胞内Ca²⁺升高可使通道失活，通道进入失活态。 Sustained depolarization and increase in intracellular Ca²⁺ lead to channel inactivation.

通道具有慢失活特性，称为“长效电流（long lasting current, L型）”。 The channel shows slow inactivation, hence the name "long lasting current" (L-type).

状态循环包括：休息态→激活态→失活态，可逆转换。 The state cycle: rest → activated → inactivated, is reversible.

3
L型钙通道在心脏动作电位中的作用I
3
Role of L-type Ca channel in cardiac action potential (I)

窦房结起搏细胞的动作电位上升部分主要由L型钙通道介导的Ca²⁺内流产生。 In sinoatrial node (pacemaker) cells, the rising phase of the action potential is mainly mediated by Ca²⁺ influx through L-type Ca channels.

Ca_v1.3亚型参与起搏功能，Ca_v1.2亚型主导动作电位上升。 Ca_v1.3 subtype is involved in pacemaking, Ca_v1.2 dominates the upstroke phase.

其他去极化电流包括I_f（钠/钾混合电流）和I_Ca,T（T型钙通道）。 Other depolarizing currents include I_f (Na⁺/K⁺ mixed current) and I_Ca,T (T-type Ca channel).

动作电位上升期钙内流造成明显去极化，β肾上腺素能刺激可加快通道开放、加速起搏频率。 During the upstroke, Ca²⁺ influx causes marked depolarization; β-adrenergic stimulation increases channel opening and accelerates pacemaker rate.

4
L型钙通道在心脏动作电位中的作用II
4
Role of L-type Ca channel in cardiac action potential (II)

在窦房结和房室结，I_Ca,L介导动作电位上升期。 In SA and AV node, I_Ca,L mediates the upstroke (phase 0) of the action potential.

在心房、传导系统和心室肌细胞，I_Ca,L维持平台期（phase 2）。 In atria, conduction system, and ventricular myocytes, I_Ca,L sustains the plateau phase (phase 2).

平台期体现为钙内流（去极化）与钾外流（复极化）之间动态平衡。 Plateau phase reflects the balance between Ca²⁺ influx (depolarizing) and K⁺ efflux (repolarizing).

心脏各部位（窦房结、房肌、AV结、浦肯野纤维、心室肌）根据解剖和功能不同，L型钙通道在不同动作电位阶段发挥作用。 In different parts of the heart (SA node, atria, AV node, Purkinje fibers, ventricles), L-type Ca channels play roles in various AP phases according to anatomy and function.

只在特定阶段（例如起搏细胞上升期、心室肌平台期）L型钙通道最为活跃。 L-type Ca channels are most active at specific phases (e.g., pacemaker upstroke, ventricular plateau).

5
教师讲解要点补充
5
Key points from the lecture

L型钙通道是治疗高血压、心律失常、心绞痛等疾病的重要药物靶点。 L-type Ca channels are key drug targets for treating hypertension, arrhythmia, and angina.

钙通道阻滞剂通过阻断L型通道抑制心肌收缩、降低传导速度和心率，扩张血管以降低血压。 Calcium channel blockers suppress cardiac contraction, reduce conduction and heart rate, relax vessels, and lower blood pressure by blocking L-type channels.

比如地尔硫卓、维拉帕米主要用于心脏，二氢吡啶类（如硝苯地平）主要扩血管。 Verapamil and diltiazem work mainly on the heart; dihydropyridines (e.g., nifedipine) mainly act on vascular dilation.

在窦房结疾病或病态窦房结综合症患者，不应使用强效钙通道阻滞剂。 Potent Ca channel blockers are contraindicated in SA node disease/sick sinus syndrome.

β肾上腺素能激活能增强L型通道电流，解释心脏在应激、运动下心率和收缩力增强。 β-adrenergic activation increases L-type Ca current, explaining enhanced heart rate/contractility during stress or exercise.

小结Summary

L型钙通道在心脏动作电位生成和心肌收缩中起关键作用，是药物干预心血管疾病的重要分子靶点。 L-type Ca channels are essential for cardiac action potential generation and myocyte contraction, serving as crucial drug targets in cardiovascular disease intervention.

L型钙通道在兴奋-收缩耦联中的作用 The L-type Calcium Channel: Role in Excitation-Contraction Coupling

1
心肌细胞中的L型钙通道功能
1
Function of L-type Ca Channels in Cardiomyocytes

L型钙通道主要存在于心肌细胞膜上，介导Ca2+的内向电流（I(Ca,L)）。 L-type Ca channels are mainly present on the cardiomyocyte membrane and mediate the inward current of Ca2+ (I(Ca,L)).

Cav1.2是主导动作电位第二相（平台期）的L型钙通道亚型。 Cav1.2 is the L-type Ca channel subtype dominating phase 2 (plateau phase) of the action potential.

Ca2+进入细胞后，通过刺激RyR2（Ryanodine受体2）引发胞内钙释放（Ca2+-induced Ca2+ release），进一步增加胞内[Ca2+]，从而激发心肌收缩。 After Ca2+ enters the cell, it stimulates the RyR2 (Ryanodine Receptor 2) to induce intracellular Ca2+ release, further increasing [Ca2+] and triggering myocardial contraction.

Cav1.3亚型主要参与心房等组织的兴奋性调节。 The Cav1.3 subtype mainly contributes to atrial excitability.

2
兴奋-收缩耦联的分子和离子机制
2
Molecular and Ionic Mechanism of Excitation-Contraction Coupling

动作电位去极化导致L型钙通道开放，Ca2+流入心肌细胞，引起胞内钙瞬变上升。 Action potential depolarization opens the L-type Ca channel, leading to Ca2+ influx, which induces a rapid rise in intracellular Ca2+.

胞内Ca2+浓度升高促使肌动蛋白-肌球蛋白相互作用，引发心肌收缩；钙回收和浓度下降则导致舒张。 The elevated intracellular Ca2+ promotes actin-myosin interactions, causing contraction; Ca2+ reuptake and the fall in concentration lead to relaxation.

钙的清除依靠NCX（钠钙交换体）、SR（肌浆网）中的钙泵以及线粒体摄取。 Ca2+ removal depends on NCX (Na+/Ca2+ exchanger), SR (sarcoplasmic reticulum) Ca2+ pumps, and mitochondrial uptake.

3
L型钙通道在血管平滑肌中的作用
3
Role of L-type Ca Channel in Vascular Smooth Muscle

血管平滑肌细胞中的L型钙通道介导Ca2+的内流，主要通过Cav1.2亚型，引起血管收缩。 In vascular smooth muscle, L-type Ca channels (mainly Cav1.2) mediate Ca2+ influx, leading to vasoconstriction.

血管收缩过程：Ca2+内流→Ca2+-CaM（钙调蛋白）复合物形成→激活MLCK（轻链激酶）→磷酸化肌球蛋白轻链→收缩。 Vasoconstriction process: Ca2+ influx → formation of Ca2+-CaM (calmodulin) complex → activation of MLCK (myosin light chain kinase) → phosphorylation of myosin light chain → contraction.

MLCP（肌球蛋白轻链磷酸酶）去磷酸化则使血管平滑肌舒张，实现血管舒张。 MLCP (myosin light chain phosphatase) dephosphorylates the myosin light chain, enabling vascular smooth muscle relaxation and vasodilation.

4
Cav1.2通道抑制剂/阻断剂的药理机制
4
Pharmacology of Cav1.2 Channel Inhibitors/Blockers

Cav1.2通道可被分为两大类药物阻断：维拉帕米和地尔硫卓等非二氢吡啶类，以及二氢吡啶类（如硝苯地平）。 Cav1.2 channels can be blocked by two major classes: non-dihydropyridines such as verapamil and diltiazem, and dihydropyridines like nifedipine.

维拉帕米/地尔硫卓类药物偏好与通道处于开放态时结合，产生频率依赖性阻断（rate-dependent block）。 Verapamil/diltiazem prefer to bind to the channel in the open state, causing a rate-dependent block.

二氢吡啶类药物更高亲和于通道失活态，表现为电压依赖性阻断（voltage-dependent block）。 Dihydropyridines have a higher affinity for the channel in the inactivated state, resulting in a voltage-dependent block.

5
教师补充讲解重点
5
Key Points from Lecturer's Supplement

L型钙通道是心血管药物（降压、抗心律失常、抗心绞痛）的主要靶点。 L-type Ca channels are major targets for cardiovascular drugs (antihypertensive, antiarrhythmic, anti-angina agents).

阻断L型钙通道可降低心肌收缩力、减慢心率、扩张血管，从而降低心脏耗氧量和血压。 Blocking L-type Ca channels reduces myocardial contractility, slows heart rate, dilates blood vessels, and thereby decreases cardiac oxygen consumption and blood pressure.

钙通道阻滞剂在临床分为心脏型（维拉帕米、地尔硫卓）和血管型（二氢吡啶类），选择时需考虑患者实际情况。 Calcium channel blockers are classified as cardiac type (verapamil, diltiazem) or vascular type (dihydropyridines), and clinical choice depends on individual patient needs.

6
相关专业名词中英对照
6
Key Terms in Chinese and English

钙调蛋白 CaM：calmodulin

轻链激酶 MLCK：myosin light chain kinase

轻链磷酸酶 MLCP：myosin light chain phosphatase

钙通道阻滞剂 calcium channel blockers

去极化 depolarization

动作电位 action potential

平台期 plateau phase

血管收缩 vasoconstriction

血管舒张 vasodilation

7
总结
7
Summary

L型钙通道通过调节Ca2+内流在心肌兴奋-收缩耦联与血管平滑肌收缩中具有核心作用。 The L-type Ca channel is central in regulating Ca2+ influx, critical for excitation-contraction coupling in cardiomyocytes and for vascular smooth muscle contraction.

钙通道阻滞剂药理分型、结合状态依赖性及其靶向特性是临床应用和副作用出现的基础。 The pharmacological subtypes of calcium channel blockers, state-dependent binding properties, and selective targeting form the basis of their clinical use and side effects.

CaV1.2通道抑制剂/阻断剂 CaV1.2 Channel Inhibitors/Blockers

1
药理学分类与代表药物 Pharmacological classes and representative drugs

苯烷胺类：维拉帕米 Phenylalkylamines: verapamil

苯并噻嗪类：地尔硫卓 Benzothiazepines: diltiazem

1,4-二氢吡啶类：硝苯地平、非洛地平、氨氯地平等 1,4-dihydropyridines (DHP): nifedipine, felodipine, amlodipine, etc.

2
结合特性 Binding properties

苯烷胺类、苯并噻嗪类主要在通道开放态时易于结合，呈现频率依赖性阻断 Phenylalkylamines and benzothiazepines bind preferentially when the channel is open, showing rate-dependent block

1,4-二氢吡啶类对失活状态通道有极高亲和力，表现为电压依赖性阻断 1,4-dihydropyridines have very high affinity for the inactivated state, resulting in voltage-dependent block

3
组织选择性 Tissue selectivity of action

苯烷胺类、苯并噻嗪类优先作用于心脏组织（静息电位很负，去极化时间短） Phenylalkylamines and benzothiazepines act mainly on cardiac tissue (very negative resting Em, short depolarizations)

1,4-二氢吡啶类主要作用于血管平滑肌（静息电位较不负，去极化时间长），作用最强 1,4-dihydropyridines act mainly on vascular smooth muscle (less negative resting Em, long depolarizations), with greater effect

4
药效学作用 Pharmacodynamics

苯烷胺类、苯并噻嗪类表现为减少心脏传导（chronotropic-）、收缩性（inotropic-）、传导速度（dromotropic-）、兴奋性（bathmotropic-） Phenylalkylamines and benzothiazepines reduce heart rate (chronotropic-), contractility (inotropic-), conduction velocity (dromotropic-), and excitability (bathmotropic-)

1,4-二氢吡啶类引起血管扩张（vasodilation），有时可致反射性心动过速 1,4-dihydropyridines mainly cause vasodilation, sometimes leading to reflex tachycardia

5
课堂补充与要点 Lecturer’s supplements and key points

苯烷胺类与苯并噻嗪类适合心脏疾病如心律失常、高血压等病情需要减慢心率、减少心脏负荷的患者。 Phenylalkylamines and benzothiazepines are suitable for cardiac conditions where slowing the heart rate and reducing cardiac workload are desired, such as arrhythmias and hypertension.

1,4-二氢吡啶类适合以血管扩张为目标的高血压、缺血性心脏病等患者，慎防反射性加快心率。 1,4-dihydropyridines are preferred for conditions where vasodilation is needed, like hypertension and ischemic heart disease, with attention to possible reflex tachycardia.

两类药物作用部位、机制和适应证各有侧重，选择时需结合疾病特点与患者具体情况。 The two types differ in sites of action, mechanisms and indications, so selection should be tailored to the disease and patient profile.

6
总结 Summary

CaV1.2通道抑制剂/阻断剂分为心脏型与血管型，作用机制和临床选择具有针对性。 CaV1.2 channel inhibitors/blockers are divided into "cardiac-type" and "vascular-type", and have specific mechanisms and clinical indications.

开放态优先结合导致频率依赖性阻断，失活态优先结合导致电压依赖性阻断。 Open-state preferential binding results in rate-dependent block, while inactivated-state binding leads to voltage-dependent block.

药物选择时需关注主要作用部位、副反应及反射性反应。 Drug selection should consider primary site of action, side effects, and reflex reactions.

神经激素激活与心力衰竭的治疗思路 Deleterious neurohormonal activation and strategies in Heart Failure

1
有害的神经激素激活加剧不良机制

Deleterious neurohormonal activation fuels maladaptive mechanisms

高室内压力（前负荷）、高射血阻力（后负荷）、冠状动脉疾病缺血、先天性心肌病等可作为起始损伤，导致心肌O$_2$需求增加。 High loading pressure (preload), high afterload (ejection pressure), ischemia after coronary syndrome, and congenital cardiomyopathy initiate insults, resulting in increased cardiac O$_2$ needs.

损伤后，神经激素系统持续激活，主要包括交感神经系统（去甲肾上腺素、肾上腺素）和肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统。 Following injury, persistent activation of neurohormonal systems (mainly sympathetic system: noradrenaline/adrenaline, and renin-angiotensin-aldosterone system) occurs.

持续的神经激素激活导致心脏功能进一步减退，形成恶性循环。 Sustained neurohormonal stimulation further worsens cardiac function, creating a vicious cycle.

治疗要么减少O$_2$需求，要么增加O$_2$供给，从而中断病理循环。 Treatments aim to either reduce O$_2$ needs or increase O$_2$ supply, thus interrupting the pathological cycle.

增加室壁张力等于增加心肌O$_2$消耗，公式如下： Increased ventricular pressure increases wall tension (Laplace's law), raising myocardial O$_2$ demand; formula: ventricular pressure ↑ → wall tension ↑ (Laplace's law) → O$_2$ demand ↑

2
多种治疗心衰的策略示意

Rational for various treatment strategies

鞭策马（Whip the horse）： 强化心脏收缩但仅短效，长期有害。 Whip the horse: Increase cardiac inotropy, short-term effect, harmful long-term.

减轻马车负担（Unload the wagon）： 降低容量负荷（如利尿剂/去除过多液体）。 Unload the wagon: Reduce volume load (e.g., diuretics/volume removal).

放慢马速（Slow the horse）： 减慢心率（如β受体阻断剂）。 Slow the horse: Slow heart rate (e.g., β-blockers).

换新马（Get a new horse）： 心脏移植。 Get a new horse: Heart transplantation.

换成机械动力（Get a tractor）： 植入机械辅助装置。 Get a tractor: Use of mechanical assistance.

治愈马（Heal the horse）： 逆转病理过程（如抗纤维化、抗凋亡治疗等）。 Heal the horse: Reverse pathology (anti-fibrotic, anti-apoptotic treatment, etc.).

3
心功能改善的策略选择

Which strategy would you suggest to improve heart function

治疗目标有两大方向，降低心脏氧需求和增加供氧。 Two main therapeutic directions: reduce myocardial O$_2$ demand and increase O$_2$ supply.

最理想状态为O$_2$需求下降、O$_2$供给上升，达到供需动态平衡。 The optimal state is reduced O$_2$ needs and increased O$_2$ supply, achieving a balanced supply-demand match.

4
氧需求的决定因素
问Important concepts #1: determinants of O$_2$ demand?
答

心率增高、心肌收缩性增强、心壁张力增加都会提升O$_2$需求。 Increased heart rate, enhanced contractility, and heightened wall tension all raise O$_2$ demand.

壁张力受舒张压和收缩压左右——如心肌僵硬或舒张末期容积增大、外周收缩、动脉高压等情况。 Wall tension is influenced by diastolic and systolic pressures—e.g., stiffer myocardium, larger end-diastolic volume, peripheral vasoconstriction, arterial hypertension.

5
降低心脏氧需求的药物选择
问Which drug to decrease O$_2$ needs?
答

通过ACE抑制剂和血管紧张素II受体拮抗剂可降低AngII水平， ACE inhibitors and AngII receptor antagonists decrease AngII levels,

从而降低心率（↓heart rate）、降低收缩性（↓contractility）、降低壁张力（↓wall tension） thus lowering heart rate, contractility, and wall tension.

作用机制包括减少交感激活及降低醛固酮释放。 Mechanisms include decreased sympathetic stimulation and reduced aldosterone release.

6
通过抗醛固酮途径减低氧需求
问Which drug to decrease O$_2$ needs?
答

采用矿物皮质激素受体拮抗剂（如利尿剂）可抑制醛固酮信号， Use of mineralocorticoid receptor antagonists (e.g., diuretics) suppresses aldosterone signalling,

进而降低壁张力，降低O$_2$消耗。 thereby reducing wall tension and myocardial O$_2$ consumption.

其主要机制为减轻SRAA系统（肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统）对心脏的影响。 The main mechanism is alleviating the SRAA (renin-angiotensin-aldosterone system) impact on the heart.

7
慢心率带来的间接获益

Important concepts #2: indirect benefits from slowing the heart

降低心率可延长左心室舒张充盈时间，改善冠脉灌注，增加O$_2$供给。 Slowing heart rate lengthens left ventricular filling time (diastole), improves coronary perfusion, and increases O$_2$ supply.

约70-80%冠脉灌注在舒张期完成，慢心率对供氧有关键作用。 About 70-80% of coronary perfusion occurs during diastole, so slowing heart rate is critical for improving O$_2$ delivery.

糖皮质激素、β受体阻断剂等交感抑制药物可达此效果。 Sympatholytics, especially β-blockers, achieve this effect.

8
β受体阻断剂在改善心脏供氧的作用

Role of β-blockers in improving O$_2$ supply

β阻滞剂降低心率→延长舒张期→促进冠脉灌注→提升O$_2$供给。 β-blockers reduce heart rate→prolong diastole→promote coronary perfusion→increase O$_2$ supply.

因此β阻滞剂既能减少O$_2$需求，又能间接改善O$_2$供给，是慢性心衰、缺血性心脏病基础治疗药物。 Thus, β-blockers both reduce O$_2$ needs and indirectly improve O$_2$ supply, making them foundational drugs in chronic heart failure and ischemic heart disease.

总结 Summary

心脏药物治疗的核心目标在于动态平衡O$_2$的供与需，既可靶向需求端（降低心率、收缩性、壁张力），也可优化供给端（延长舒张、增强冠脉灌注）。 The core aim of cardiac drug therapy is balancing O$_2$ supply and demand: targeting the demand side (reducing heart rate, contractility, wall tension) and optimizing the supply side (prolonging diastole, improving coronary perfusion).

药物如ACEI、ARBs、β受体阻滞剂及抗醛固酮类均为临床一线选择。 Drugs such as ACE inhibitors, ARBs, β-blockers, and anti-aldosterone agents are all frontline clinical choices.

心血管系统药物干预实例Examples of pharmacological interventions to treat the CV system

1
药物干预目标与机制 Targets and Mechanisms of Pharmacological Interventions

β-肾上腺素受体拮抗剂（β-AR antagonists）、血管紧张素转换酶抑制剂（ACE inhibitor）、血管紧张素Ⅱ受体拮抗剂（ARA）、“心脏型”钙通道阻滞剂（如verapamil, diltiazem）被用于调控心血管系统。 β-AR antagonists, ACE inhibitors, angiotensin II receptor antagonists (ARA), and "cardiac" calcium channel blockers (such as verapamil, diltiazem) are used to regulate the cardiovascular system.

2
主要机制：降低心脏氧需求 Main Mechanisms: Decreasing Cardiac O2 Needs

降低心肌收缩性（↓inotropism）与降低心率（↓heart rate）可降低心脏对氧的需求（↓O2 needs）。 Reducing myocardial contractility (↓inotropism) and slowing the heart rate (↓heart rate) can decrease the oxygen demand (↓O2 needs) of the heart.

升高左心室壁张力（↑LV parietal tension）和血容量（↑blood volume）也会增加氧需求，因此给予抗醛固酮药物和利尿剂可帮助减轻壁张力和血容量。 Increased left ventricular wall tension (↑LV parietal tension) and blood volume (↑blood volume) increase oxygen demands. Mineralocorticoid receptor (MR) antagonists and diuretics help reduce wall tension and blood volume.

常用的药物有 ACE 抑制剂、ARA、β-AR 抗药物和 MR 拮抗剂。 Common drugs include ACE inhibitors, ARA, β-AR antagonists, and MR antagonists.

3
主要机制：增加心脏供氧 Main Mechanisms: Increasing Cardiac O2 Supply

增加冠脉灌注（↑coronary perfusion）是提升心肌供氧（↑O2 supply）的重要途径。 Improving coronary perfusion (↑coronary perfusion) directly increases the oxygen supply to the myocardium (↑O2 supply).

冠脉灌注增加可以通过延长舒张期（减慢心率）或扩张血管（vasodilation）实现。 Increased coronary perfusion can be achieved either by prolonging diastole (via lower heart rate) or by vasodilation.

血管扩张剂，如二氢吡啶类钙通道阻滞剂（dihydropyridines）、verapamil和diltiazem，也常用于此目的。 Vasodilators such as dihydropyridines, verapamil, and diltiazem are commonly used for this purpose.

4
平衡心脏氧供需 Balancing Myocardial Oxygen Supply and Demand

有效的心血管治疗需要在降低氧需求和提升供氧之间取得平衡。 Effective cardiovascular therapy requires balancing the reduction of O2 needs with the improvement of O2 supply.

各类药物组合协同作用，最终目的是既减少心脏负担，又增加组织灌注和氧的供应。 Different drug combinations act synergistically, with the ultimate goal to both reduce cardiac workload and increase tissue perfusion and oxygen delivery.

5
辅助药物类别 Additional Drug Classes

其他常用药物：MR拮抗剂/利尿剂（MR antagonists/diuretics）、ACE抑制剂、ARA和β-AR拮抗剂。 Other commonly used drugs include: MR antagonists/diuretics, ACE inhibitors, ARA, and β-AR antagonists.

强效钙通道阻滞剂如二氢吡啶（dihydropyridines +++）、verapamil（++）、diltiazem（+）对血管扩张和减压有不同影响。 Potent calcium channel blockers such as dihydropyridines (+++), verapamil (++), and diltiazem (+) have varying effects on vasodilation and blood pressure reduction.

问结论：你需要掌握什么？Conclusion: what do I need to know?

干预肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统（RAAS） Acting on the renin-angiotensin-aldosterone system (RAA system)

干预心脏和血管的钙通道 Acting on calcium channels in the heart and vessels

补充讲解要点：老师课堂录音强调

Additional Key Points from Class Recording

老师重点提到药物对心脏耗氧和供氧的影响都可通过调控心率、收缩性、壁张力、血容量和血管阻力来实现。 The teacher emphasized that drugs impact myocardial O2 demand and supply by regulating heart rate, contractility, wall tension, blood volume, and vascular resistance.

在药物选择及临床应用时，需综合考量降低心脏负荷和增强组织灌注，优先选用可逆转不良心肌重构及抑制不利神经激素激活的药物。 In clinical management, drug selection should target both reducing cardiac load and enhancing tissue perfusion, prioritizing those that reverse adverse remodeling and inhibit harmful neurohormonal activation.

血管扩张剂及β-受体阻断剂等机制尤为关键，应用时需理解其对血压、心率及冠脉灌注时程的精细调控。 The mechanism of vasodilators and beta-blockers is especially important; one must appreciate their fine regulation of blood pressure, heart rate, and duration of coronary perfusion.

小结 Summary

以上药物通过多环节调节心脏氧供需动态，直接影响心血管疾病的治疗效果。 All these drug categories regulate the dynamic balance of myocardial O2 supply and demand, directly impacting cardiovascular disease therapy effectiveness.

掌握上述药物的分子靶点和临床应用逻辑，是心血管药理学的重要基础。 Understanding the molecular targets and clinical logic of these drugs is the foundation of cardiovascular pharmacology.

062025-10-27（第 3 讲）

血管平滑肌细胞（VSMC）：血管中的一种关键细胞类型

Vascular smooth muscle cells (VSMC): a cell type among others in blood vessels

一、血管的灌注取决于动脉血压（ΔP_A）

I. Organ perfusion is driven by arterial blood pressure (ΔP_A)

1
血液循环包括体循环和肺循环；动脉血压驱动血液灌注各组织。 Blood circulation includes systemic and pulmonary circuits; arterial blood pressure drives perfusion to all organs.
2
心脏将富含氧气的血液通过主动脉泵送到全身；血液在毛细血管床交换O2和CO2。 The heart pumps oxygenated blood via the aorta to the whole body; blood exchanges O2 and CO2 within capillary beds.
3
静脉将脱氧血带回心脏，再循环至肺部进行气体交换。 Veins return deoxygenated blood to the heart, which then circulates to the lungs for gas exchange.

二、血管平滑肌细胞在血管结构中的定位

II. VSMCs in the blood vessel

1
血管平滑肌细胞（绿色）位于血管壁的中层（tunica media），环绕血管呈环状排列。 Vascular smooth muscle cells (green) are located in the tunica media, forming circumferential rings around the vessel wall.
2
血管外侧为结缔组织（tunica adventitia），含有大量细纤维，内层为内皮细胞（tunica intima）。 The adventitia on the outside contains connective tissue with many fine fibers; the inner layer is endothelium (tunica intima).
3
激光共聚焦图像显示VSMC和细胞外基质紧密排列，有助于维持血管功能和稳定性。 Confocal images demonstrate tightly arranged VSMCs and extracellular matrix, supporting vessel function and stability.
4
VSMC的排布与内皮细胞不同，后者在血管内呈纵向排列。 VSMCs are arranged differently from endothelial cells, which are longitudinally oriented inside the vessel.

三、血管壁的组织结构及平滑肌细胞

III. Vascular tissue structure and VSMCs

动脉的壁由三个层次组成
外膜、中膜和内膜。VSMC主要分布在中膜。 Arterial walls consist of three layers: tunica adventitia, tunica media, and tunica intima. VSMCs are mostly in the media.
2
合同装置和细胞骨架使VSMCs具备收缩能力，调节血管直径。 The contractile apparatus and cytoskeleton in VSMCs enable contraction, allowing regulation of vessel diameter.
3
内弹性层和外弹性层加强血管的弹性与稳定。 Internal and external elastic lamina reinforce elasticity and stability of the vessel.
4
不同血管层次的结构差异决定其功能，如血流输导与局部血流控制。 Structural differences among vessel layers determine functions such as blood conduction or local flow control.

四、多样的血管大小及其功能适应

IV. Various sizes of blood vessels for various functions

1
大血管（导管系统，直径1-25 mm）内膜含结缔组织，中膜带丰富弹性纤维，主要功能为血流输导和压力储备。 Large vessels (conducting system, diameter 1-25 mm) have endothelium and connective tissue in intima, thick media with abundant elastic fibers, functioning in blood conduction and pressure reserve.
2
小动脉（阻力系统，直径5-500 μm）中膜平滑肌丰富，决定外周阻力和血流灌注。 Small arteries (resistance system, diameter 5-500 μm) have media enriched with smooth muscle, controlling peripheral resistance and perfusion.
3
毛细血管（直径5 μm）仅有内皮层，主要负责局部流量调节和物质交换。 Capillaries (diameter 5 μm) have only endothelium, for local flow control and exchange of gases/nutrients.

五、血管细胞的主要功能

V. Functions of vascular cells

内皮细胞
调控血管通透性、血液凝固、增殖及血管生成。 Endothelial cells: regulate vascular permeability, hemostasis/coagulation, proliferation, and angiogenesis.
血管平滑肌细胞
调节血管张力, 参与平滑肌细胞的增殖和迁移。 VSMCs: regulate vascular tone and participate in proliferation and migration.
3
外周血管脂肪组织和血小板也对血管功能有调控作用。 Perivascular adipose tissue and platelets also modulate vascular functions.

六、血管阻力与灌注的物理基础：泊肃叶定律

VI. Physical basis of vascular resistance and perfusion: Poiseuille’s Law

1
器官灌注由动脉血压差推动。ΔP = Qc × PVR Organ perfusion is driven by arterial pressure gradient. ΔP = Qc × PVR
2
Qc（心输出量）= HR × Vs（心率 × 每搏射血量） Qc (cardiac output) = HR × Vs (heart rate × stroke volume)
3
外周血管阻力（PVR）= 8ηL / (πr^4)。阻力随血管半径变化极为敏感，半径微小变化可导致阻力巨大改变。 Peripheral vascular resistance (PVR) = 8ηL / (πr^4). Resistance is highly sensitive to vessel radius; small changes yield big variations in resistance.
4
η为血液黏度，L为血管长度，r为血管半径。 η is blood viscosity, L is vessel length, and r is vessel radius.

七、课堂补充与教师讲解要点

VII. Additional key points from lecture

1
老师强调VSMC通过调节血管半径在控制血流分配、组织灌注及维持血压中发挥核心作用。 The instructor emphasized that VSMCs control vessel radius, playing a key role in blood flow distribution, perfusion, and maintenance of blood pressure.
2
小动脉和微动脉（阻力系统）对全身血压调控和器官血流分配作用最为显著。 Arterioles and resistance arteries are most important for systemic blood pressure and organ-specific flow control.
3
动脉三个层次的不同细胞与基质组分，各自在血管功能和疾病中的作用不同。 Different vessel wall layers' cells and matrix have distinct roles in vascular function and pathology.
4
结构与功能的对应关系是理解心血管药理学干预的基础。 The structural-function relationship lays a foundation for pharmacological interventions in cardiovascular diseases.

八、小结

VIII. Summary

1
血管平滑肌细胞是血管壁的重要组成部分，调控血流和血压。 VSMCs are key components of the vessel wall, regulating blood flow and pressure.
2
动脉壁多层结构决定其不同生理功能，内皮与平滑肌协同支持血管的动态调节。 The multilayered arterial wall determines distinctive physiological roles; endothelium and smooth muscle cooperate in dynamic vascular regulation.
3
控制血管半径可显著影响阻力和灌注，理解其基础对于后续学习血管疾病与药物治疗至关重要。 Control of vessel radius greatly impacts resistance and perfusion; understanding this is crucial for further study of vascular pathology and therapy.

血管张力Vascular tone

1
定义及显微图像 Definition & Microscopic Image

血管张力指血管平滑肌细胞（SMC）环绕管腔、调控血管半径和血流的紧张状态。 Vascular tone refers to the tension state of vascular smooth muscle cells (SMCs) arranged circumferentially around the vessel, regulating vessel radius and blood flow.

共聚焦显微镜图像显示SMC（绿色）及细胞外基质（红色），SMC以环状结构分布，外层为细纤维（外膜）。 Confocal microscopy images show SMCs (green) and extracellular matrix (red); SMCs form ring-like structures, and the outer layer is fine fibers (adventitia).

旁边的图像为血管收缩时Ca2+信号（细胞内和细胞间Ca2+震荡），表明收缩由去甲肾上腺素能激动剂（如苯肾上腺素）引起。 The side image shows Ca2+ oscillations (intracellular and intercellular) during vasoconstriction, indicating recruitment by adrenergic agonists (like phenylephrine).

小肠系膜动脉加10 μM苯肾上腺素诱导收缩，表现为Ca2+信号的异步和同步波动。 Mesenteric artery was stimulated by 10 μM phenylephrine, displaying asynchronous and synchronized Ca2+ oscillations.

2
平滑肌细胞收缩装置的结构 Organisation of the contractile apparatus in SMC

收缩装置包含粗、细两类纤维，粗丝为平滑肌肌球蛋白II二聚体，细丝主要为α-肌动蛋白。 The contractile apparatus consists of thick and thin filaments: thick filaments are myosin II dimers, and thin filaments are mainly α-smooth muscle actin.

细丝网络包括
肌动蛋白（α-SM actin）、原肌球蛋白、caldesmon和calponin，SMC中无TnI/TnC（三联蛋白）。 The thin filament network comprises actin (α-SM actin), tropomyosin, caldesmon, and calponin; SMCs lack troponin.

动物实验中显示，收缩时细胞纤维重新组织，实现血管变细。 Experimental results show reorganization of cellular fibers during contraction, which leads to vessel narrowing.

显微照片为新鲜分离的大鼠小肠系膜动脉平滑肌细胞。 The microscopy photo shows freshly isolated rat mesenteric artery smooth muscle cells.

3
平滑肌肌球蛋白II（smooth muscle myosin II） Contractile apparatus in SMC: smooth muscle myosin II

一个功能性肌球蛋白II分子由2条（200kDa）重链和2对轻链组成。 A functional myosin II molecule contains two heavy chains (200kDa) and two pairs of light chains.

一对17kDa（基本）轻链和一对20kDa（调节/肌球蛋白）轻链（LC20），其中LC20在Ser-19位磷酸化后启动桥联循环，导致收缩。 The light chains include one essential (17kDa) and one regulatory (20kDa, LC20), and phosphorylation of LC20 at Ser-19 initiates cross-bridge cycling and contraction.

肌球蛋白头部具有ATP酶活性，实现能量转换。 The myosin head exhibits ATPase activity, converting chemical energy into mechanical force.

RLC20（调节轻链）磷酸化（RLC20 phosphorylation）是平滑肌收缩的关键步骤。 RLC20 phosphorylation is the key regulatory step for smooth muscle contraction.

4
肌动蛋白-肌球蛋白桥联循环机制 Actin-myosin cross bridge cycling

跨桥循环包括收缩（contraction）和舒张（relaxation）两种状态。 The cross-bridge cycle consists of contraction and relaxation phases.

收缩时，ATP首先结合、解离肌球蛋白与肌动蛋白，随后水解ATP并驱动“动力冲程”，使肌球蛋白头拉动肌动蛋白丝。 During contraction, ATP binding dissociates myosin from actin, followed by ATP hydrolysis, driving the power stroke and pulling actin filaments.

舒张时，MLCP（肌球蛋白轻链磷酸酶）去磷酸化LC20，肌球蛋白失活，桥联断开。 Relaxation occurs when MLCP (myosin light chain phosphatase) dephosphorylates LC20, inactivating myosin and breaking the cross-bridge.

MLCK（肌球蛋白轻链激酶）负责LC20磷酸化，促进收缩；MLCP负责去磷酸化，促进舒张。 MLCK (myosin light chain kinase) phosphorylates LC20 to enhance contraction, while MLCP dephosphorylates it to promote relaxation.

主要过程为
The main steps are:

ATP结合，肌球蛋白与肌动蛋白分离 ATP binds, causing myosin to dissociate from actin

ATP水解，头部复位 ATP hydrolysis resets the myosin head

无机磷(Pi)和ADP释放，桥联形成与动力冲程 Release of phosphate (Pi) and ADP, cross-bridge formation and power stroke

5
机制特点简介与课上补充 Special Mechanistic Features & Lecture Highlights

机制类似骨骼肌，但平滑肌收缩慢、可持续。 Mechanism is similar to skeletal muscle, but smooth muscle contraction is slower and more sustained.

ATP在肌动蛋白和肌球蛋白分离时被水解。 ATP is hydrolyzed while actin and myosin are detached.

能量转化为机械张力依赖磷酸根（Pi）与ADP的释放。 The conversion of energy to mechanical tension depends on the release of phosphate (Pi) and ADP.

老师特别强调，血管平滑肌收缩调节直接影响全身外周阻力和血压。 The instructor stressed that regulation of SMC contraction directly affects systemic peripheral resistance and blood pressure.

临床中，血管紧张调节失衡可导致高血压、缺血等多种血管病理状态。 In clinical settings, dysregulation of vascular tone leads to hypertension, ischemia, and other vascular pathologies.

6
小结 Summary

血管平滑肌张力变动源于细胞内Ca2+信号，最终通过RLC20磷酸化调控肌丝滑动，实现血管收缩或舒张。 Vascular smooth muscle tone is modulated by intracellular Ca2+ signaling, ultimately controlling filament sliding via RLC20 phosphorylation to induce vessel contraction or relaxation.

收缩装置分为粗细丝，核心分子为α-肌动蛋白和肌球蛋白II。 The apparatus comprises thick and thin filaments, with α-actin and myosin II as core molecules.

桥联循环与ATP酶活性为细胞转化能量的基础。 Cross-bridge cycling and ATPase activity underpin cellular energy conversion.

MLCK和MLCP的作用及其调控机制Role and Regulation of MLCK and MLCP

一、MLCK和MLCP在血管平滑肌中的功能

I. The function of MLCK and MLCP in vascular smooth muscle

MLCK是平滑肌肌球蛋白轻链激酶，通过磷酸化肌球蛋白轻链LC20（Ser-19位点），促进血管平滑肌细胞收缩，引发血管收缩（vasoconstriction）。 MLCK is myosin light chain kinase, which phosphorylates myosin light chain LC20 (at Ser-19), promoting vascular smooth muscle contraction and causing vasoconstriction.

MLCP是平滑肌肌球蛋白轻链磷酸酶，通过去磷酸化LC20（Ser-19），促进平滑肌松弛，引发血管舒张（vasodilatation）。 MLCP is myosin light chain phosphatase, which dephosphorylates LC20 (at Ser-19), promoting smooth muscle relaxation and causing vasodilatation.

两者在细胞内形成一个可逆过程，决定了血管的张力状态。 These two processes form a reversible cycle within the cell, determining vascular tone.

二、MLCK与MLCP的结构组成

II. Structure of MLCK and MLCP

MLCK具有肌动蛋白结合区、催化区和调控区，还含有一个Ca2+-钙调蛋白（CaM）结合结构域，需Ca2+-CaM结合激活。 MLCK has an actin binding domain, a catalytic domain, and a regulatory domain, including a Ca2+-calmodulin (CaM) binding site that requires Ca2+-CaM binding for activation.

MLCP由PP1c-δ（催化亚基）、MYPT1（靶向亚基）和M20（非催化亚基）组成。 MLCP is composed of PP1c-δ (catalytic subunit), MYPT1 (targeting subunit), and M20 (non-catalytic subunit).

三、L型钙通道与血管张力的调节

III. Regulation of vascular tone by L-type calcium channel

血管平滑肌细胞膜上的L型钙通道（Ca_v1.2）介导Ca2+内流，升高胞内Ca2+浓度。 L-type calcium channels (Ca_v1.2) on the vascular smooth muscle cell membrane mediate Ca2+ influx, increasing intracellular Ca2+ concentration.

Ca2+与钙调蛋白（CaM）结合，形成Ca2+-CaM复合物。 Ca2+ binds to calmodulin (CaM) to form a Ca2+-CaM complex.

Ca2+-CaM激活MLCK，促进LC20磷酸化，引起平滑肌收缩。 The Ca2+-CaM complex activates MLCK, promoting LC20 phosphorylation and initiating smooth muscle contraction.

四、MLCK和MLCP活性的调控

IV. Regulation of MLCK and MLCP activities

蛋白激酶A（PKA）可激活MLCP，强化脱磷酸化作用，产生“Ca2+去敏化”效应，促进血管舒张。 Protein kinase A (PKA) can activate MLCP, enhancing dephosphorylation and causing "Ca2+ desensitization," leading to vasodilation.

蛋白激酶C（PKC）、Rho激酶通过磷酸化MYPT1抑制MLCP，产生“Ca2+敏化”效应，促进血管收缩。 Protein kinase C (PKC) and Rho kinase inhibit MLCP by phosphorylating MYPT1, resulting in "Ca2+ sensitization" and promoting vasoconstriction.

CPI17等蛋白可被PKC磷酸化，进一步抑制MLCP活性。 CPI17 can be phosphorylated by PKC and further inhibits MLCP activity.

蛋白激酶G（PKG）可促进MLCP活性，增强血管舒张。 Protein kinase G (PKG) enhances MLCP activity, facilitating vasodilation.

五、机制概括与教师补充讲解

V. Mechanistic summary and lecture highlights

MLCK和MLCP共同调节LC20的磷酸化状态，是血管平滑肌张力调控的核心分子。 MLCK and MLCP together regulate the phosphorylation status of LC20, which is the core control point for vascular smooth muscle tone.

教师强调，L型钙通道的Ca2+内流和下游MLCK的活化是血管收缩的核心步骤；MLCP的被抑制可在无进一步Ca2+升高时仍维持高张力（即“Ca2+敏化”）。 The instructor emphasized that Ca2+ influx via L-type calcium channels and subsequent MLCK activation are central to contraction; sustained inhibition of MLCP can maintain high tone even without additional Ca2+ elevation ("Ca2+ sensitization").

课上补充，临床药物如L型钙通道阻滞剂、NO/GC/cGMP通路激活剂（PKG激动剂）等可通过调节这些通路发挥抗高血压、抗痉挛作用。 Additionally, clinically used drugs such as L-type calcium channel blockers and NO/GC/cGMP pathway activators (PKG agonists) can modulate these pathways for antihypertensive or antispasmodic effects.

六、小结

VI. Summary

MLCK通过Ca2+依赖性激活，促进LC20磷酸化和血管收缩；MLCP脱磷酸化LC20，促使血管舒张。 MLCK is activated by Ca2+, promotes LC20 phosphorylation and vasoconstriction; MLCP dephosphorylates LC20 and causes vasodilation.

上述通路的统合调控决定了血管平滑肌的功能及血压动态变化，是药物干预的重要靶点。 The integrated regulation of these pathways controls vascular smooth muscle function and blood pressure, constituting a key target for pharmacological intervention.

血管反应性的测定方法——肌条张力测量法Myography: a method for studying vascular reactivity

一、原理与基本流程

Principle and basic procedure

肌条张力测量法是体外研究小鼠或大鼠等动物血管反应性的经典实验方法。 Myography is a classical ex vivo method for studying vascular reactivity in animals such as mice or rats.

实验通常选取直径0.2~2 mm的小动脉（如冠状动脉），切成环状血管段。 Typically, small arteries (diameter 0.2–2 mm, e.g., coronary artery) are isolated and cut into ring segments.

血管段固定在特殊的肌条测量仪（myograph）上，两根金属丝穿过管腔并夹持于仪器夹具之间。 The vessel rings are mounted on a myograph, with two metal wires inserted through the lumen and clamped between the jaws of the device.

仪器可一次测量多条血管，便于高通量实验。 The system allows simultaneous measurement of several vessels for high-throughput experiments.

肌条与力传感器（force transducer）连接，记录血管对不同刺激的收缩或舒张张力变化。 The myograph system is connected to a force transducer, which records tension changes (contraction/relaxation) in response to stimuli.

二、实验条件与张力记录

Experimental settings and tension recording

血管环浸泡于含生理盐水的恒温氧合室，维持37°C、pH 7.4等近生理条件。 The vessel rings are bathed in physiological solution at 37°C and pH 7.4, mimicking in vivo conditions.

本方法为离体记录（ex vivo recording），能在可控环境下检测血管功能。 This is an ex vivo recording method, enabling functional testing under controlled conditions.

实验记录为等长张力（isometric tension recording）： The recording is isometric:

固定张力下将血管条牵拉至一定初张l（预张力、basal tension）。 The tissue is stretched to a baseline resting tension.

血管平滑肌收缩时，产生的拉力被力传感器检测，无长度变化。 Contraction generates force (no length change), which is detected by the force transducer.

三、信号转化与数据处理

Signal transformation and data processing

力传感器将机械张力信号转换为模拟电信号（电压）。 The force transducer converts mechanical force into an analog (voltage) signal.

数据采集系统（Data Acquisition device）将模拟信号转为数字信号，输入电脑。 The analog signal is digitized by a data acquisition device and sent to a computer.

专用软件用于后续张力-时间曲线的分析，如药理反应动力学、剂量-反应关系等。 Computer software is used to analyze tension–time curves, dose–response relationships, and pharmacological effects.

四、血管反应性实验流程与类型

Vascular reactivity assay protocol and types

血管可通过加入不同药物/溶液进行功能检测： The vessels are challenged with various compounds, for functional assessment:

收缩剂（vasoconstrictors）：如去甲肾上腺素、苯肾上腺素、血栓素A2、PGF2α、5-HT等，检测收缩反应。 Vasoconstrictors (e.g., phenylephrine, thromboxane A2, PGF2α, 5-HT) to detect contraction.

去极化溶液
高K+，用于直接诱导收缩。 Depolarizing (high K+) solution, to directly trigger contraction.

舒张剂（vasorelaxants）：异丙肾上腺素、乙酰胆碱、NO供体等，检测舒张反应。 Vasorelaxants (isoprenaline, acetylcholine, NO donors) to test relaxation capacity.

典型实验流程为
A typical protocol involves:

设定基础张力，血管静息、被牵拉； Set the basal tension; muscle is at rest, tissue is stretched.

加入收缩剂诱发持续收缩，张力迅速上升至平台； Add vasoconstrictor, which induces a sustained increase in tension.

顺次加入舒张剂，张力降低，评估舒张能力。 Add vasodilators stepwise; the tension falls, allowing assessment of relaxation.

五、数据解析与药理分析

Data interpretation and pharmacological analysis

通过时间-张力曲线可以量化药物效应大小与动力学。 Tension–time curves provide quantitative and kinetic information of drug responses.

药效分析常用剂量-反应关系曲线（dose-response curve）： Pharmacological evaluation includes plotting dose–response curves:

横轴为药物对数浓度（log[药物]），纵轴为收缩或舒张百分比。 X-axis: log[dose], Y-axis: % contraction or relaxation.

可计算最大效应（Emax）和半最大效应浓度（EC50）。 Calculate maximal effect (Emax) and half-maximal effective concentration (EC50).

例如，异丙肾上腺素（β受体激动剂）诱导大鼠冠脉等浓度依赖性舒张，最大舒张效应约90%，EC50约10^-8 M。 For example, isoprenaline (β-AR agonist) induces concentration-dependent relaxation in rat coronary artery, with Emax ≈ 90% and EC50 ≈ 10^-8 M.

六、老师课堂强调与补充

Key teacher’s notes and additions

老师重点指出，肌条张力测量是药理、血管生理功能研究和药物筛选的基础方法。 The instructor stressed that myography is a fundamental method for pharmacological, vascular function research, and drug screening.

同一动物个体、同一实验条件下可以直接比较不同药物或不同干预对血管功能的影响，可用于疾病模型（如高血压、糖尿病等）与对照组比较。 It allows comparison of interventions or drugs in identical animals/background, e.g., disease versus control.

老师补充，剂量-反应曲线不仅评估Emax和EC50，也反映血管对药物敏感性变化，可区分药物选择性和信号通路差异。 In addition to Emax and EC50, the dose–response relationship reveals sensitivity changes, drug selectivity, and signaling differences.

该技术是小动脉功能性研究的“金标准”，常用于评估内皮功能、血管平滑肌功能的独立贡献。 This technique is a gold standard for small artery functional studies, allowing separate assessment of endothelium and smooth muscle cell contribution.

七、小结

Summary

肌条张力测量法通过“体外-等长”方法动态、定量评估小血管对多种刺激的反应，是研究血管药理及疾病的重要实验工具。 Myography enables dynamic, quantitative, ex vivo isometric assessment of small vessel reactivity to various stimuli, making it a vital tool in vascular pharmacology and pathophysiology research.
