肾脏的功能与解剖结构 The Functions and Anatomy of Kidneys

一、肾脏的主要功能 Main Functions of Kidneys

1
排除体内废物（内源性、外源性） Elimination of body waste (endogenous, exogenous)

老师讲解时补充，内源性包括尿素、酸（如氢离子、铵离子）等；外源性包括药物和代谢物。

The teacher added
Endogenous wastes include urea, acids (such as H⁺, NH₄⁺); exogenous wastes include drugs and their metabolites.

肾脏通过生成尿液来清除体内的废物，这也是我们从日常生活中可以直接观察到的肾脏作用。

Kidneys eliminate waste through urine production, which is a function easily observed in daily life.

2
维持内环境的稳定 Maintaining the composition of the internal environment

保持水分平衡

Maintaining water balance

保持电解质（如Na⁺、K⁺、HCO₃⁻、Cl⁻、H⁺、Ca²⁺、Mg²⁺等）浓度稳定

Maintaining stable concentrations of electrolytes (such as Na⁺, K⁺, HCO₃⁻, Cl⁻, H⁺, Ca²⁺, Mg²⁺, etc.)

保持有机物（如葡萄糖、氨基酸、维生素等）的适当水平

Maintaining appropriate levels of organic substances (glucose, amino acids, vitamins, etc.)

老师强调维持内环境稳定对于心脏、神经等生命活动至关重要，举例如钾离子的浓度变化会影响心脏节律。

The teacher emphasized that maintaining internal environment homeostasis is crucial for vital activities such as heart and nerve function, e.g., potassium level disturbances can lead to cardiac arrhythmia.

肾脏也有平衡体内水分丢失的能力，比如通过呼吸、出汗等丢失的水分需要肾脏通过调节保留。

Kidneys also compensate for water loss from breathing, sweating, etc., ensuring water is retained as necessary.

3
调节动脉血压 Regulation of arterial blood pressure

肾脏通过调节体液容量和分泌激素（如肾素）来调控血压。

Kidneys regulate blood pressure by controlling body fluid volume and secreting hormones (such as renin).

4
内分泌功能 Endocrine function

分泌维生素D3、红细胞生成素（EPO）和肾素

Secretion of vitamin D3, erythropoietin (EPO), and renin

EPO促进骨髓生成红细胞；维生素D3有助于钙的吸收。肾功能丧失会导致这些功能减退，进而影响血液和骨骼等。

EPO stimulates the bone marrow to produce red blood cells; vitamin D3 promotes calcium absorption. Loss of kidney function leads to deficits in these aspects, affecting blood and bone health.

5
糖异生功能 Neoglucogenesis

肾脏可在必要时通过糖异生帮助维持血糖水平。

Kidneys can perform gluconeogenesis to help maintain blood glucose levels when necessary.

二、肾脏及尿路解剖结构（结合PPT图片和老师讲述） Anatomy of the Urinary Tract

1
尿路系统包括肾脏、输尿管、膀胱、尿道 The urinary tract includes kidneys, ureters, bladder, and urethra

图片展示了女性和男性尿路解剖结构的差异（男性有前列腺）。

The diagram shows the anatomical differences between female and male urinary tracts (prostate is present in males).

老师补充
肾脏位于腰部两侧，并非所有腰痛都是肾脏问题。肾痛通常会放射到腹部或其他部位。
The teacher added
Kidneys are located on both sides of the lower back, but not all back pain is due to kidney problems. Kidney pain often radiates to the abdomen or other areas.
2
肾脏主要结构包括肾柱、肾锥体、肾乳头、肾盂、肾门、输尿管 Main structures of the kidney include renal column, renal pyramid, papilla, calyx, hilum, and ureter
老师讲述
肾脏切面可以看到外层的皮质（深红色颗粒状）和内部的髓质（肾锥体，纤维状）。

The teacher explained: On cross-section, the kidney shows a granular, dark red cortex outside and fibrous medulla (pyramids) inside.

三、肾单位（肾的功能单位） Renal Functional Unit: Nephron

1
每个肾脏含有约100万至120万个肾单位 Each kidney contains about 1 to 1.2 million nephrons

老师特别强调肾单位结构非常复杂、细腻，是肾功能的基本实现载体。

The teacher particularly emphasized the nephron’s subtle and complex structure as the basic functional entity of the kidney.

2
肾单位组成 Structure of the nephron

肾小球（肾小体）：负责血液的过滤

Glomerulus (renal corpuscle): responsible for blood filtration

肾小管（包含近曲小管、亨利袢、远曲小管）：负责对原尿的重吸收和分泌

Renal tubule (including proximal convoluted tubule, loop of Henle, distal convoluted tubule): responsible for reabsorption and secretion

3
肾小管的不同部分 Different sections of the nephron
近曲小管（PCT），亨利氏袢（LH），远曲小管（DCT），集合管　
答案 CD
Proximal convoluted tubule (PCT), loop of Henle (LH), distal convoluted tubule (DCT), collecting duct 　
答案 CD

每部分功能各异，保证肾单位对物质的精确调控。

Each part has distinct functions, ensuring precise regulation of substances by the nephron.

肾单位的真实解剖结构与血管化 Anatomic Reality and Vascularization of the Nephron

一、肾单位的真实解剖结构 Anatomic Reality of the Nephron

肾单位由多部分组成
肾小球、近曲小管（PCT）、亨利袢、远曲小管（DCT）和集合管。 The nephron consists of several parts: glomerule, proximal convoluted tubule (PCT), loop of Henle, distal convoluted tubule (DCT), and collecting duct.
2
肾小球为球状结构，是肾单位的起始部分，负责血液的初步过滤。 The glomerule is a spherical structure and the starting part of the nephron, responsible for primary blood filtration.
3
近曲小管紧接肾小球，进行大量的重吸收作用。 The PCT is connected to the glomerule and is responsible for extensive reabsorption.
4
亨利袢深入髓质，有降支和升支，参与尿液浓缩。 The loop of Henle extends into the medulla with descending and ascending limbs, involved in urine concentration.
5
远曲小管连接于亨利袢后段，继续调节离子和水的重吸收。 The DCT is attached to the end of the loop of Henle and further regulates ion and water reabsorption.
6
集合管汇集多个肾单位的尿液，最终通向肾盂。 The collecting duct collects urine from multiple nephrons and leads to the renal pelvis.
老师补充
现实中，肾小球和肾小管的排列远比课本示意图更为复杂，空间结构紧密细腻，功能分区明确。 Teacher's addition: In reality, the arrangement of glomerule and tubules is much more complex and delicate than textbook diagrams, with clear functional zoning.

二、肾单位的血管化 NEPHRON Vascularization

1
肾单位的血供极为丰富，是全身血流量最大的器官之一。 The nephron receives extremely rich blood supply and the kidney is one of the most highly perfused organs in the body.
2
血液首先通过入球小动脉进入肾小球毛细血管丛，发生原尿的过滤。 Blood first enters the glomerular capillary tuft through the afferent arteriole, where primary urine filtration occurs.
3
滤过后的血液经由出球小动脉流出，并进入第二套毛细血管系统。 The filtered blood leaves via the efferent arteriole and enters the second capillary network.
该毛细血管网分为两种
This secondary capillary network consists of two types:

肾小管周围毛细血管，主要负责重吸收（ABSORPTION）； Peritubular capillaries, mainly responsible for absorption.

直小血管（Vasa recta），深入髓质，参与浓缩尿液（CONCENTRATION）。 Vasa recta, which penetrates into the medulla and is involved in urine concentration.

5
出球小动脉→肾小管周围毛细血管→集合管最后经过微静脉回流静脉系统。 Efferent arteriole → peritubular capillaries → collecting duct → finally via venules return to the venous system.
6
现实组织结构镜下可见大量肾小球密集分布，通过入球、出球小动脉与毛细血管错综相连。 Under the microscope, numerous glomeruli are densely distributed and intricately connected with afferent and efferent arterioles as well as capillaries.
老师强调
肾脏血管网络的特殊性在于连续存在两套串联毛细血管床，有别于体内大多数器官的一套微循环。 Teacher's emphasis: The unique feature of renal vasculature is the presence of two capillary beds in series, unlike the usual single microcirculation in most organs.

三、分区功能概述 Functional Zoning Overview

1
入球小动脉—肾小球毛细血管负责原尿过滤（FILTRATION）； Afferent arteriole and glomerular capillaries are responsible for primary urine filtration (FILTRATION).
2
出球小动脉—肾小管周围毛细血管主要进行物质重吸收（ABSORPTION）； Efferent arteriole and peritubular capillaries mainly handle reabsorption (ABSORPTION).
3
直小血管深入肾髓质，有助于建立和维持髓质的高渗透梯度，实现尿液的高效浓缩功能（CONCENTRATION）。 The vasa recta penetrates deep into the medulla, helping establish and maintain the high osmotic gradient necessary for efficient urine concentration (CONCENTRATION).

肾小球与近球小体的结构 Structure of the GLOMERULE and JUXTAGLOMERULAR Apparatus

一、肾小球及近球小体的组成 Compositions of Glomerulus and Juxtaglomerular Apparatus

1
入球小动脉将血液带入肾小球。 The afferent arteriole brings blood into the glomerulus.
2
肾小球毛细血管由大量内皮细胞组成，承担原尿的初步生成。 The glomerular capillaries consist of many endothelial cells, responsible for the initial formation of primary urine.
3
肾小球由特殊细胞——足细胞（podocyte）包覆，其细长突起称为足突，形成过滤裂隙。 The glomerulus is covered by specialized cells called podocytes, whose elongated processes (pedicels) form filtration slits.
4
足细胞覆盖在基底膜（basal membrane/lamina）外侧，三者共同组成过滤屏障。 Podocytes cover the basal membrane (lamina), and together with endothelial cells, form the filtration barrier.
5
鲍曼囊包绕肾小球，包内腔收集通过过滤屏障的原尿。 Bowman’s capsule surrounds the glomerulus and its cavity collects the filtrate that passes through the barrier.
6
近端曲小管负责原尿的后续重吸收和分泌。 The proximal convoluted tubule further processes the filtrate for reabsorption and secretion.
7
远端小管、致密斑（Macula densa）与入球、出球小动脉相邻，参与检测与调节。 The distal tubule and the Macula densa are positioned near the afferent and efferent arterioles, involved in sensing and regulation.
8
近球小体含有致密斑细胞及近球小动脉壁上的近球小细胞，分泌肾素，调控血压。 The juxtaglomerular apparatus contains the macula densa and juxtaglomerular cells in the arteriole wall, which secrete renin to regulate blood pressure.

二、肾小球毛细血管超微结构和过滤屏障 The Ultrastructure of Glomerular Capillaries and Filtration Barrier

1
肾小球毛细血管内皮为有窗孔的类型，允许小分子和水通过。 Glomerular endothelial cells are fenestrated (with tiny windows), allowing water and small molecules to pass.
2
内皮与足细胞之间为基底膜，阻挡大分子如蛋白质通过。 The basal membrane lies between the endothelium and the podocytes, preventing large molecules such as proteins from passing through.
3
足细胞的足突相互交错，形成过滤裂隙，是第三重过滤屏障。 Interdigitating pedicels of podocytes create filtration slits, acting as the third layer of the filtration barrier.
4
三重屏障依次阻挡血细胞、大分子和绝大多数蛋白；水、电解质、葡萄糖、氨基酸等小分子可自由通过。 These three layers sequentially block blood cells, macromolecules, and most proteins; water, electrolytes, glucose, and amino acids can pass freely.
5
如果过滤屏障受损（如肾炎），蛋白质会漏出，导致蛋白尿。 If the barrier is damaged (as in nephritis), proteins may leak out, resulting in proteinuria.
6
电子显微镜下可见足细胞足突紧密包裹在毛细血管表面，网状结构明显。 Under electron microscopy, podocyte processes tightly cover the capillary surfaces, showing a clear meshwork structure.
7
多个肾小球在皮质内密集排列，能够高效完成大体积血浆的过滤。 Multiple glomeruli are densely distributed in the cortex, enabling efficient plasma filtration at scale.

三、课上强调要点 Key Highlights from the Lecture

1
过滤屏障的三重结构（内皮有窗孔、基底膜、足细胞足突）决定了选择性通透性。 The three-layer structure (fenestrated endothelium, basal membrane, podocyte pedicels) determines selective permeability.
2
红细胞无法通过过滤屏障，小分子能够通过，蛋白质在健康情况下很难进入原尿。 Red blood cells cannot cross the barrier; small molecules can pass; proteins rarely appear in primary urine in health.
3
打比方为“有窗子的墙→防蚊网→百叶窗”，小分子如“空气、气味”能通过，大分子如“蚊子”被阻挡。 By analogy: “a wall with windows → a mosquito net → shutters”; small molecules like “air, odors” pass through, large ones like “mosquitoes” do not.
4
肾小体结构对于后续的血压调节和肾素分泌至关重要。 The structure of the renal corpuscle is crucial for downstream blood pressure regulation and renin secretion.
5
任何对毛细血管-基底膜-足细胞结构的损伤都会引起临床问题，如蛋白尿、血尿等。 Any injury to the capillary-basal membrane-podocyte complex leads to clinical issues such as proteinuria or hematuria.

四、电子显微镜下真实结构 Real Structure Under Electron Microscopy

1
足细胞足突的三维网络紧密、复杂，是保证肾脏高效过滤的关键。 The three-dimensional, intricate meshwork of podocyte pedicels is key to efficient renal filtration.
2
多个肾小球在皮质内密密排列，可见丰富的结构和功能分区。 Numerous closely packed glomeruli are visible in the cortex, each with rich structure and functional zoning.
II-肾脏生理学 Renal Physiology
  • A肾脏功能的一般过程 General Processes of Renal Function

一、肾脏血流与结构 Renal Blood Flow and Structure

1
肾脏接收心输出量的四分之一，血流极为丰富，每分钟约1.2升血液流经肾脏（每天约1756升）。 The kidneys receive one quarter of cardiac output and are highly vascularized, with about 1.2 liters of blood flowing through every minute (about 1756 liters per day).
2
入球小动脉将血液送入肾小球毛细血管，之后流出至出球小动脉，再分布到肾小管周围毛细血管及直小血管，最后汇入小静脉。 The afferent arteriole delivers blood to glomerular capillaries, then blood flows out via the efferent arteriole, is distributed to peritubular capillaries and vasa recta around tubules, finally merging into venules.
3
尿路侧从鲍曼囊开始，依次进入近曲小管、亨利袢、远曲小管、集合管，最终生成尿液。 On the urine side, filtrate enters Bowman’s capsule, then flows through the proximal convoluted tubule, loop of Henle, distal convoluted tubule, and collecting duct, finally forming urine.

二、肾单位的基本生理过程 Fundamental Processes of Nephron Function

1
滤过 Filtration 肾小球毛细血管产生肾小球超滤液，肾小球滤过率(GFR)为120毫升/分钟（每天约180升原尿）。 Glomerular capillaries produce glomerular ultrafiltrate, with glomerular filtration rate (GFR) of 120 mL/min (about 180 liters primary urine per day).
2
重吸收 Reabsorption 超过99%的滤过物将在肾小管和集合管被重吸收，每天重吸收约179升。 Over 99% of the filtrate is reabsorbed in tubules and collecting ducts, about 179 liters daily.
3
分泌 Secretion 某些物质（如药物、部分离子）可主动从血液分泌到肾小管腔内，以便排出。 Some substances (e.g., drugs, certain ions) are actively secreted from blood into the tubule lumen for excretion.
4
排泄 Excretion 最终形成的终尿约为每天1升（受饮水和环境影响可变）。 The final urine output is about 1 liter per day (variation depending on fluid intake and environment)14.

三、肾脏清除率及相关公式 Renal Clearance and Key Equation

清除率定义
单位时间内，某种物质被肾脏完全清除的血浆体积。 Definition: Clearance is the volume of plasma completely cleared of a substance by the kidney per unit time.
2
计算公式 CL=([U]×V)/[P] Calculation: CL = ([U] × V)/[P] CL为清除率，[U]为尿中物质浓度，V为单位时间内尿量，[P]为血浆中该物质浓度。 Where CL is clearance, [U] is urinary concentration, V is urine volume per unit time, [P] is plasma concentration.
解读
Interpretation:
CL=0-120 mL/min
物质被滤过并重吸收（如葡萄糖）。 CL = 0–120 mL/min: Filtered and reabsorbed (e.g., glucose).
CL=120 mL/min
仅被滤过，未重吸收也未分泌（如伊ヌ林）。 CL = 120 mL/min: Only filtered, neither reabsorbed nor secreted (e.g., inulin).
120<CL≤625 mL/min
被滤过且分泌（如对氨基马尿酸PAH）。 120 < CL ≤ 625 mL/min: Filtered and secreted (e.g., para-aminohippuric acid, PAH)15.

四、经典物质的肾脏清除率举例 Examples of Classical Renal Clearance

1
内源性物质 Endogenous Substances
葡萄糖
CL=0，全过滤后完全重吸收。 Glucose: CL = 0; completely filtered and reabsorbed.
肌酐
CL=125-130 mL/min，主要被滤过，略有分泌。 Creatinine: CL = 125–130 mL/min; mainly filtered, slight secretion.

肌酐清除率常用于估算肾小球滤过功能。 Creatinine clearance is commonly used to estimate glomerular filtration.

2
外源性物质 Exogenous Substances
菊粉
CL=120 mL/min，仅被滤过，不被重吸收或分泌，是评估GFR的金标准。 Inulin: CL = 120 mL/min; only filtered, not reabsorbed or secreted, the gold standard for GFR measurement.

对氨基马尿酸（PAH）：CL=600 mL/min，近似全部被滤过和分泌，用于估算肾血浆流量。 Para-aminohippuric acid (PAH): CL = 600 mL/min; nearly all filtered and secreted, used for renal plasma flow estimation15.

五、水与电解质的重吸收 Reabsorption of Water and Electrolytes（部分详表）

1
近100%葡萄糖和HCO3-、近99%钠和氯、近99%水被重吸收，钾约重吸收93%。 Nearly 100% of glucose and HCO3-, about 99% of Na+ and Cl-, and about 99% of water are reabsorbed; K+ is about 93% reabsorbed.
2
尿素约有50%被重吸收，其余随尿排出。 Around 50% of urea is reabsorbed, the remainder is excreted16.

六、课堂讲解重点与补充 Key Lecture Points and Additions

1
大量原尿被滤过并几乎全部重吸收，保证精准调节体内环境。 A large amount of primary urine is filtered and almost entirely reabsorbed, guaranteeing precise adjustment of the internal environment.
2
清除率是药物动力学和肾功能监测的重要参数。 Clearance is a key parameter in pharmacokinetics and renal function assessment.
3
学会区分滤过、重吸收、分泌、排泄这四个核心过程及其作用点。 It is important to distinguish filtration, reabsorption, secretion, and excretion, and know their sites of action.
4
近端小管、亨利袢、远端小管、集合管各部分功能不同，导致不同物质重吸收和分泌的差异。 The proximal tubule, loop of Henle, distal tubule, and collecting duct have different functions, resulting in differences in reabsorption and secretion of substances.
5
肾小球滤过依赖血压、毛细血管通透性及分子大小，只有水和小分子能自由通过滤过屏障。 Glomerular filtration depends on blood pressure, capillary permeability, and molecular size; only water and small molecules can freely pass through the filtration barrier.

肾小球滤过的被动机制B. Mechanisms of Filtration: PASSIVE Mechanisms

滤过转运的决定因素Factors Determining Filtration Transfers

肾小管的功能Renal Tubule Functions

肾单位各段主要转运与调控Major Transport and Regulation in Nephron Segments

一、滤过的被动机制 Mechanisms of Filtration: PASSIVE Mechanisms

1
肾小球滤过发生在由内皮细胞、基底膜（基膜）和足细胞（含足突）组成的三层屏障上。 Glomerular filtration occurs across a three-layer barrier: endothelial cells, basal membrane (lamina), and podocytes (with pedicels).
2
滤过主要由血液和原尿（初级尿）两侧的压力差驱动，无需能量，属于被动过程。 Filtration is mainly driven by the pressure gradient between blood and primitive urine, requiring no ATP, hence a passive process.
3
毛细血管内皮有窗孔，有助于水和小分子的通过。 Capillary endothelium is fenestrated, facilitating water and small molecule passage.

二、压力参数与筛选限制 Pressure Parameters and Filtration Limits

1
毛细血管血液侧静水压为45 mmHg，鲍曼囊侧为10 mmHg，驱动滤过的有效静水压为35 mmHg。 Hydrostatic pressure is 45 mmHg in capillary blood and 10 mmHg in Bowman’s capsule, resulting in an effective hydrostatic pressure of 35 mmHg driving filtration.
2
胶体渗透压由蛋白质造成，血液侧为20 mmHg，囊内为0 mmHg，对滤过起阻碍作用。 Oncotic (colloid osmotic) pressure is 20 mmHg in blood and 0 mmHg in the capsule, opposing filtration.
3
有效滤过压力=静水压力差-胶体渗透压差=35-20=15 mmHg。 Net filtration pressure = hydrostatic gradient – oncotic gradient = 35 – 20 = 15 mmHg.
4
只有分子质量小于10000、直径小于7-9 nm的分子可自由通过滤过屏障（如水、电解质、葡萄糖、氨基酸），大分子和血细胞则被阻挡。 Only molecules with molecular mass less than 10,000 and diameter below 7-9 nm (e.g., water, electrolytes, glucose, amino acids) cross freely, while large proteins and cells are blocked.
5
这个三层屏障可比作“带窗的墙－防蚊网－百叶窗”，小分子如“空气”可通过，蛋白相当于“蚊子”被阻隔。 This three-layer barrier is analogous to “a windowed wall-mosquito net-shutter”—small molecules like “air” pass, while proteins (like “mosquitoes”) are stopped.

三、滤过转运的决定因素 Factors Determining Filtration Transfers

1
静水压随毛细血管长度逐渐下降，胶体渗透压逐步上升，两者差值形成超滤压力，使滤过持续进行。 Hydrostatic pressure gradually decreases, and oncotic pressure gradually increases along capillary length; their difference creates ultrafiltration pressure, maintaining filtration throughout.
2
超滤压力面积代表了单位时间内滤出液体体积，即肾小球滤过率（GFR），正常为120-125 mL/min。 The area between these curves represents the liquid volume filtered per unit time, i.e., glomerular filtration rate (GFR), normally 120-125 mL/min。

四、肾小管的基本功能 Renal Tubule Functions

1
重吸收水和重要元素（如葡萄糖、电解质、离子等），保证体液组成恒定。 Reabsorption of water and essential elements (e.g., glucose, electrolytes, ions), maintaining homeostasis.
2
分泌代谢废物和外源性物质（代谢产物、异生物等）。 Secretion of protons and metabolic/xenobiotic wastes.
3
按激素信号灵活调节重吸收和分泌。 Adjustment according to hormonal messages.
4
形成皮质-髓质浓度梯度，实现尿液浓缩调节。 Creation of cortico-papillary (cortico-medullary) concentration gradient for urine concentration regulation.
5
肾小管功能既有被动现象（不需能量），也有主动现象（需ATP）。 Tubular functions involve both passive phenomena (no energy requirement) and active phenomena (requiring ATP).

五、肾单位不同部位主要转运和激素调节 Major Transport and Regulatory Hormones in Nephron Segments

1
近端小管（PCT）：重吸收葡萄糖、氨基酸、碳酸氢根、钾、钠、磷酸根、氯、镁、钙和等渗水。分泌H⁺、NH₄⁺。 Proximal Convoluted Tubule (PCT): Reabsorbs glucose, amino acids, HCO3−HCO3−​, K⁺, Na⁺, PO₄²⁻, Cl⁻, Mg²⁺, Ca²⁺, and isotonic water. Secretes H⁺, NH₄⁺.
2
髓袢（亨利袢）：下行支重吸收水，上行支重吸收溶质，尿液逐步高渗。 Loop of Henle: Descending limb reabsorbs water; ascending limb reabsorbs solutes, progressively concentrating the urine.
3
远端小管（DCT）：重吸收Na⁺，在副甲状腺激素（PTH）调控下重吸收Ca²⁺。 Distal Convoluted Tubule (DCT): Reabsorbs Na⁺ and, under PTH control, Ca²⁺.
4.集合管　：在抗利尿激素（ADH）调控下重吸收水，酸碱平衡时分泌H⁺和HCO3−HCO3−​。 Collecting Duct (CD): Reabsorbs water under ADH control; secretes H⁺ and HCO3−HCO3−​ for acid-base balance.
答案 CD
5
醛固酮促进远端小管和集合管对Na⁺、水的重吸收以及K⁺的分泌。 Aldosterone enhances Na⁺ and water reabsorption, and K⁺ secretion, in DCT and CD.
6
所有段的精细调节共同实现电解质平衡、酸碱平衡和尿液浓缩/稀释功能。 The precise regulation in all segments of the nephron ensures electrolyte and acid-base balance as well as urine concentration/dilution.

六、补充及讲解重点 Lecture Supplement and Key Points

1
肾小球滤过过程无能量消耗，完全由物理压力驱动。 Glomerular filtration is ATP-independent and driven entirely by physical forces.
2
静水压增加、血浆蛋白减少均会增强滤过，反之则降低滤过。 Increased hydrostatic pressure or decreased plasma protein boosts filtration; the reverse reduces it.
3
滤过膜受损或血液成分异常会导致蛋白尿或多尿等临床表现。 Filtration barrier damage or abnormal blood composition results in proteinuria or polyuria.
4
不同区域分子的“通行”决定肾脏的排毒和内环境调节能力。 Molecular passage in different areas dictates kidney’s detoxification and homeostatic roles.
II-肾脏疾病Renal pathologies
  • A肾小球滤过功能改变Alterations of glomerular filtration

一、肾小球功能障碍机制 Mechanisms of Glomerular Dysfunction

1
肾小球疾病（如肾小球肾炎）可导致肾小球滤过屏障损伤，蛋白质通过滤过膜进入尿液，出现蛋白尿，有时伴有血尿。 Glomerular diseases (such as glomerulonephritis) can damage the glomerular filtration barrier, allowing proteins to pass into urine, causing proteinuria and sometimes hematuria.
2
损伤常见于肾小球基底膜与足细胞等部位，导致对大分子的选择性减弱。 The injury often affects the glomerular basement membrane and podocytes, reducing selectivity for macromolecules.
3
滤过屏障正常情况下阻止分子量＞10000、直径＞7-9纳米的大分子（如血浆蛋白等）通过。 Under normal conditions, the filtration barrier blocks macromolecules with molecular weight over 10,000 and diameter over 7-9 nm (such as plasma proteins).

二、压力变化与滤过的关系 Pressure Changes and Effects on Filtration

1
肾小球毛细血管静水压通常从45 mmHg降至10 mmHg，超滤压力为35 mmHg推动滤过过程。 Glomerular capillary hydrostatic pressure typically drops from 45 mmHg to 10 mmHg; ultrafiltration pressure of 35 mmHg drives filtration.
2
胶体渗透压血液侧为20 mmHg，囊内接近0 mmHg。 Blood-side oncotic pressure is 20 mmHg, near zero in Bowman’s capsule.
3
滤过障碍损伤时，蛋白漏出使胶体渗透压下降，进一步影响滤过、导致水肿。 When the barrier is damaged with protein leakage, oncotic pressure drops, further affecting filtration and causing edema.

三、滤过障碍的图示与指标 Interpretation of Diagrams and Indicators

1
静水压沿毛细血管长度逐步下降，而胶体渗透压逐渐升高（因水分滤出，蛋白浓缩增加），二者差值构成超滤压力。 Hydrostatic pressure gradually decreases along capillary length, while oncotic pressure rises (due to water loss and protein concentration); their difference generates net ultrafiltration pressure.
2
如果滤过屏障受损，GFR（肾小球滤过率）上升，蛋白尿明显，有时伴随血尿。 If the filtration barrier is damaged, GFR (glomerular filtration rate) rises and proteinuria becomes obvious, sometimes with hematuria.

四、肾病综合征的发病机制 Mechanisms of Nephrotic Syndrome

1
感染性和自身免疫性因素均可引发肾小球滤过屏障损伤，导致白蛋白和红细胞漏入尿液（蛋白尿/血尿）。 Both infectious and autoimmune factors can damage the glomerular filter, resulting in albumin and/or red blood cell leakage (proteinuria/hematuria).
2
白蛋白尿→低蛋白血症→胶体渗透压下降→组织间隙液体潴留形成水肿。 Albuminuria leads to hypoalbuminemia, then decreased oncotic pressure, and fluid retention in the interstitial space causing edema.
3
血浆容量减少→肾脏感知低血容量，启动钠/水潴留→进一步加重水肿。 Reduced plasma volume prompts renal sensing of hypovolemia, triggering sodium/water retention and aggravating edema.
4
利尿/多尿时可致脱水、继发性烦渴。 Diuresis/polyuria can cause dehydration and secondary polydipsia (increased thirst).
5
肾素-血管紧张素-醛固酮系统（RAAS）和抗利尿激素（ADH）均参与调控体液，维持血容量和渗透压。 The renin-angiotensin-aldosterone system (RAAS) and anti-diuretic hormone (ADH) both help control body fluids, maintaining blood volume and osmolarity.

五、重点总结 Key Points Summary

1
肾小球滤过功能障碍常表现为蛋白尿、水肿、低蛋白血症及相关并发症。 Glomerular dysfunction often manifests as proteinuria, edema, hypoalbuminemia, and related complications.
2
压力变化（静水压↑/胶体渗透压↓）均可影响GFR和尿蛋白水平。 Changes in pressure (increased hydrostatic/decreased oncotic) both affect GFR and urinary protein levels.
3
相关调控机制（RAAS、ADH、抗利尿）和体液调节引起临床症状如多尿、烦渴、低血容量等。 Regulatory mechanisms (RAAS, ADH, anti-diuresis) and body fluid adjustment lead to clinical signs like polyuria, polydipsia, and hypovolemia.

肾病综合征的典型水肿与治疗方式Typical oedema (keeping the cup) and Therapies of nephrotic syndrome

一、肾病综合征的典型水肿 Typical oedema in nephrotic syndrome

1
在肾病综合征中，常见的水肿是“凹陷性水肿”，即用手指按压后，会留下明显的凹坑，称为“保持杯痕现象”。 In nephrotic syndrome, the common edema is "pitting edema," which means if you press the skin with your finger, a noticeable pit remains, called "keeping the cup."
2
此类水肿多为苍白、柔软且易移动，通常在早晨出现在眼睑和面部，白天则逐渐转移到下肢。 This edema is often pale, soft, and easily displaced. It usually appears around the eyelids and face in the morning and gradually shifts to the lower limbs during the day.
3
形成机制主要为低白蛋白血症导致血浆胶体渗透压下降，组织液回流减少，血管内液体逸出至组织间隙。 The mechanism is mainly hypoalbuminemia, resulting in decreased plasma oncotic pressure, less fluid returns to blood vessels, and fluid leaks into interstitial tissue.
4
若站立较久，下肢水肿尤为明显，且按压后可见指痕持续存在。 After standing for a long time, lower limb edema becomes more obvious, and finger marks persist after pressing.
5
这是肾脏丧失大量蛋白质、引发体液转移和钠水潴留等病理结果的直接表现。 This is a direct result of the kidneys losing large amounts of proteins, causing fluid shift and sodium/water retention.

二、肾病综合征的经典治疗方式 Therapies of nephrotic syndrome

1
传统治疗 Classical Treatments
生活方式调整
低盐、低蛋白饮食，限制饮水。 Lifestyle modifications: Low sodium, low protein diet, and fluid restriction.
定期随访
监测血压、肾功能、蛋白尿。 Follow-up: Monitor blood pressure, renal function, and proteinuria.
主要药物治疗
Main Medications:

糖皮质激素（如泼尼松）：一线用药，尤其是儿童，可减少炎症和蛋白尿。 Corticosteroids (such as prednisone): First-line therapy, especially in children, to reduce inflammation and proteinuria.

免疫抑制剂（如环孢素、他克莫司、利妥昔单抗）：适用于糖皮质激素无效患者。 Immunosuppressants (such as cyclosporine, tacrolimus, rituximab): For patients unresponsive to corticosteroids.

血管紧张素转换酶抑制剂（ACEI）或血管紧张素II受体阻断剂（ARB）（如拉普利、贝那普利、洛萨坦等）：有助于降低蛋白尿和血压。 Angiotensin-Converting Enzyme 　 inhibitors or Angiotensin II Receptor Blockers (ARBs) (such as lisinopril, benazepril, losartan, etc.): Help reduce proteinuria and blood pressure.
答案 ACE

利尿剂（如呋塞米、螺内酯）：用于缓解水肿。 Diuretics (such as furosemide and spironolactone): Used to alleviate edema.

治疗潜在病因
如糖尿病、红斑狼疮、感染等。 Treatment of Underlying Causes: Such as diabetes, lupus, and infections.
2
新兴和临床试验中的潜在疗法 New potential therapies / emerging therapies in clinical trials
新药及靶向治疗
Novel and Targeted Therapies:

阿曲生坦（Atrasentan，内皮素受体拮抗剂），针对IgA肾病成人。 Atrasentan (endothelin receptor antagonist) for adults with IgA nephropathy.

Sibeprelimab 和 Atacicept：调节B细胞反应。 Sibeprelimab and Atacicept: Regulate B-cell response.

Inaxaplin（靶向APOL1蛋白，遗传型肾病）。 Inaxaplin (targets APOL1 protein, for genetic nephropathies).

Belimumab（贝利木单抗，用于狼疮性肾炎和其他自身免疫病）。 Belimumab (Benlysta, for nephritic lupus and autoimmune diseases).

Iptacopan
补体系统抑制剂，IgA肾病。 Iptacopan: Inhibits complement system, for IgA nephropathy.
单克隆抗体
Monoclonal Antibodies:

如利妥昔单抗和贝利木单抗，靶向免疫系统部分组分，可诱导缓解。 Such as rituximab and belimumab; targeting specific immune system components to induce remission.

HDAC抑制剂
HDAC inhibitors:

靶向组蛋白脱乙酰酶，调控基因表达，改善足细胞损伤（研究尚有限）。 Target histone deacetylase enzymes to regulate gene expression and address podocyte injury (limited studies).

药物再利用
Drug Repurposing:

全反式维甲酸、丙戊酸（减少蛋白尿，临床数据有限）；还有钠-葡萄糖共转运蛋白2抑制剂(SGLT2)的探索。 All-Trans Retinoic Acid, Valproic Acid (reduce proteinuria, limited data); and investigating SGLT2 inhibitors.

三、课堂讲解重点补充 Key Points from Lecture

1
老师强调水肿的特征为按压后“留痕”，称“保持杯痕”，这种水肿在肾病综合征尤其典型。 The teacher emphasized that the hallmark of this edema is “keeping the cup” (persistent pit after pressing), especially typical in nephrotic syndrome.
2
老师还提到白蛋白丢失和水钠潴留形成恶性循环，促发更多水肿。 The teacher mentioned that albumin loss and sodium/water retention form a vicious cycle, promoting even more edema.
3
使用糖皮质激素需注意副作用，包括血糖升高、生长受抑（儿童）、高血压等，需权衡风险。 Corticosteroid use requires monitoring for side effects, including hyperglycemia, growth retardation (children), hypertension, etc., and balancing risks.
4
治疗过程中常依据病因进行个体化药物调整，肾病综合征的新治疗方法需持续关注临床进展。 Treatment should be individualized based on etiology, and new therapies for nephrotic syndrome require ongoing attention to clinical progress.
5
维持低盐、优质蛋白和适度控制饮水有助于管理水肿及肾脏负担。 Maintaining low salt, quality protein intake, and moderate fluid restriction helps manage edema and kidney burden.

B.间质性肾炎 B. Interstitial nephritis

一、全肾性炎症 Global renal inflammation

间质性肾炎指肾脏间质广泛发生炎症，病因多样。 Interstitial nephritis refers to widespread inflammation of the renal interstitium, with various causes.

可能病因包括细菌感染（上行性肾盂肾炎）、结晶/矿物质沉积（尿酸、钙）、以及中毒或过敏反应（锂、NSAID、PPI、抗生素如β-内酰胺类、磺胺类、氟喹诺酮、利福平，铅等）。 Possible causes include bacterial infection (ascendant pyelonephritis), crystal/mineral deposits (uric acid, calcium), and toxic or allergic reactions (lithium, NSAID, PPI, antibiotics such as beta-lactams, sulfonamides, fluoroquinolones, rifampicin, lead, etc.)

感染性因素通常伴有发热。 Infectious causes are usually accompanied by fever.

二、早期肾小管损伤 Early tubular lesions

首先受累的是亨利袢和集合管。 The initial damage affects the Loop of Henle and collecting duct.

损伤表现为尿液浓缩能力下降、钠的重吸收减少、钾和氢的分泌减少。 Manifestations include decreased urine concentrating ability, reduced sodium reabsorption, and decreased secretion of potassium and hydrogen ions.

临床结果为多尿、夜尿、钠尿增加、尿液偏碱性。 Clinically results in polyuria, nocturia, increased natriuresis, and alkaline urine.

可出现高钾血症、代谢性酸中毒、低血容量和脱水。 May result in hyperkalemia, metabolic acidosis, hypovolemia, and dehydration.

三、晚期肾小管损伤 Late tubular lesions

损伤逐步累及肾小球、近曲小管和远曲小管。 As the disease progresses, it involves the glomerulus, proximal and distal tubules.

表现为葡萄糖、氨基酸和小分子的重吸收能力下降。 Leads to reduced reabsorption of glucose, amino acids, and small proteins.

可见糖尿、蛋白尿（中度）、如有感染还可见白细胞尿。 Glycosuria, moderate proteinuria, and leucocyturia (if infection present) may be detected.

四、疾病进展及后果 Disease progression and outcome

若损伤持续，可逐步发展为肾功能衰竭（KF）。 If damage continues, the disease may evolve towards kidney failure (KF).

典型表现为
尿浓缩、离子平衡、酸碱平衡等功能障碍，逐步丧失肾单位的选择性重吸收与排泄功能。 Typical features include disturbances in urine concentration, electrolyte balance, acid-base balance, and a gradual loss of selective reabsorption and excretion by the nephron.

五、临床和药物学补充补充 Clinical and pharmacological highlights

常见药物诱发因素包括β-内酰胺类、磺胺类、氟喹诺酮、利福平等。 Common drug-related causes include beta-lactams, sulfonamides, fluoroquinolones, and rifampicin.

老师特别强调有些药物和毒性反应需要及时识别，以避免不可逆肾损伤。 The lecturer emphasized that some drug- and toxin-induced cases need prompt recognition to avoid irreversible kidney damage.

酸碱及电解质失衡常导致全身其他系统受累。 Acid-base and electrolyte disorders often lead to complications in other organ systems.

六、要点总结 Key points summary

间质性肾炎是一种全肾性、渐进性损伤过程，早期主要影响尿浓缩和离子调节，晚期波及肾单位各段。 Interstitial nephritis is a global, progressive renal injury with early effects on urine concentration and ion regulation, later involving all nephron segments.

及时发现和阻断致病因十分关键，否则可进展至终末期肾功能衰竭。 Early detection and removal of the causative factor is crucial; otherwise, the disease may progress to end-stage renal failure.

急性肾功能衰竭（ARF）：急性肾损伤（AKF） C. Acute renal failure (ARF): acute kidney failure (AKF)

一、定义及基本概念 Definition and Basic Concept

急性肾损伤是指肾功能的突然中断，通常是可逆的。 Acute kidney failure refers to a sudden interruption of renal function, which is usually potentially reversible.

其核心表现为肾小球滤过率（GFR）急剧下降，导致代谢产物潴留和液体、电解质紊乱。 The main feature is a rapid decline in glomerular filtration rate (GFR), leading to retention of metabolic wastes and disturbances of fluid and electrolytes.

二、三大主要病因 Three Main Etiological Categories

1
肾前性原因（功能性AKF） Pre-renal causes (Functional AKF):
常见原因包括
低血压、低血容量（休克、大出血、心力衰竭）、脱水和利尿药中毒。 Common causes include: hypotension, hypovolemia (shock, hemorrhage, heart failure), dehydration, intoxication with diuretics.

其机制是肾脏灌注不足，导致肾血流减少。 Mechanism: decreased renal perfusion causes reduced renal blood flow.

2
肾实质性原因（器质性AKF） Renal causes (Organic AKF):

主要由于肾小球（如自身免疫、肾炎）、肾小管（如血红蛋白、肌红蛋白、造影剂、氨基糖苷类、两性霉素等）、血管/缺血性病变。 Mainly due to glomerular (autoimmune, nephritis), tubular (hemoglobin, myoglobin, iodine, aminoglycoside antibiotics, amphotericin, etc.), and vascular/ischemic (hypoperfusion) lesions.

导致肾单位结构和功能受损，表现为肾实质功能障碍甚至坏死。 These result in structural and functional impairment of nephrons, manifesting as renal parenchymal dysfunction or necrosis.

3
肾后性原因（梗阻性AKF） Post-renal causes (Obstructive AKF):

包括结石（伴或不伴膀胱球）、前列腺增生、肿瘤（前列腺、膀胱）、尿路硬化等机械性梗阻。 Causes include obstructions, lithiasis (+/- bladder globe), prostate hypertrophy, tumors (prostate, bladder), sclerosis.

阻碍尿液排出，引发肾盂和肾盏扩张，长期可致肾组织损害。 Obstruct urinary outflow, causing dilatation of renal pelvis and calyces, which can lead to tissue damage if persistent.

三、功能障碍表现 Manifestations of Dysfunction

肾前性
肾小球滤过下降，肾小管功能基本维持，表现为少尿。 Pre-renal: Decreased glomerular filtration, tubular functions maintained, oliguria (reduced urine output).
肾实质性
肾小球及肾小管均受损，表现为滤过和小管功能皆下降，可见少尿甚至多尿。 Renal (parenchymal): Both glomerular and tubular dysfunction, reduced filtration and tubular reabsorption, may show oliguria or polyuria.
肾后性
滤过多无本质性损害，小管功能维持，但因梗阻导致少尿甚至无尿。 Post-renal: No intrinsic glomerular filtration alteration, tubular function maintained, but due to outflow block, oliguria or anuria (no urine) occurs.

四、典型病理图片 Typical Pathological Image

患有严重急性肾损伤患者的肾脏实体切面常见苍白与多发暗红色淤血区，提示肾皮质和髓质区域发生广泛损伤和血流障碍。 In the gross section of the kidney from a patient who died of severe AKF, one sees pale areas and multiple dark red congested regions, reflecting widespread injury and blood flow disturbance in both cortex and medulla.

这种改变反映了灌注障碍、缺血及肾小管坏死等病理过程。 These changes indicate perfusion disorder, ischemia, and tubular necrosis.

五、补充
急性肾损伤的可逆性及救治关键 Supplement: Reversibility and Management of AKF

急性肾衰竭在早期通常可逆，关键在于迅速去除病因（如补液、纠正休克/脱水、解除梗阻、停用毒性药物等）。 AKF is often reversible in early stages. Rapid removal of the cause (such as fluid resuscitation, correcting shock/dehydration, relieving obstruction, stopping nephrotoxic drugs) is crucial.

若抢救及时，肾功能可完全恢复；若延误，肾损害可能不可逆并发展为慢性肾衰。 If intervention is prompt, renal function can be fully restored; if delayed, damage may be irreversible, progressing to chronic renal failure.

六、要点总结 Key Summary

急性肾衰竭可分为肾前、肾实质、肾后性三大类，所有类型最终都表现为肾小球滤过下降和少尿或无尿。 Acute renal failure is divided into pre-renal, renal (parenchymal), and post-renal types; all ultimately feature reduced glomerular filtration and oliguria or anuria.

病理本质是灌注不足、炎症/坏死或机械性梗阻，及时干预是预后改善的关键。 The essence lies in underperfusion, inflammation/necrosis, or mechanical obstruction; timely management is the key to prognosis improvement.

慢性肾衰竭
慢性肾病（CKD）D. Chronic kidney failure: chronic kidney disease (CKD)

一、定义与主要特征 Definition and Main Features

慢性肾病（CKD）指肾功能逐渐不可逆下降，影响泌尿和内分泌功能（RAS系统、EPO、降钙素等）。 Chronic kidney disease (CKD) is a progressive and often irreversible decline in kidney functions, affecting both urinary and endocrine (RAS, EPO, calcitriol) functions.

这种进展多为缓慢，最终常导致终末期肾功能衰竭。 This decline is usually slow and eventually leads to end-stage renal failure.

二、发病机制与主要病理 Mechanism and Key Pathologies

主要慢性肾脏病因包括糖尿病、高血压、以及其他肾脏病变（如肾小球肾炎、多囊肾等）。 Main causes are diabetes, hypertension, and other renal diseases (e.g., glomerulonephritis, polycystic kidney disease).

随着肾单位（肾小球及肾小管）持续受损，肾小球滤过率（GFR）下降，浓缩与稀释功能也逐渐丧失。 As nephron (glomeruli and tubules) damage accumulates, GFR decreases and ability to concentrate or dilute urine is lost.

损伤初期，存活的肾单位代偿性增加过滤和重吸收负担，称为代偿期CKD。 In early stages, surviving nephrons compensate via hyperfiltration and increased reabsorption (compensated CKD).

随后，肾单位大量消失，进入失代偿期，出现顽固水肿、贫血、钠水潴留等。 Later, when many nephrons disappear (decompensated KD), severe edema, anemia, sodium and water retention develop.

生化表现为血清肌酐、尿素氮上升，肌酐清除率下降。 Biochemically, creatininemia and uremia increase, creatinine clearance decreases.

肾病早期患者可因高滤过和蛋白尿出现多尿，进而因失水导致脱水。 Early CKD may have polyuria due to hyperfiltration and proteinuria, sometimes leading to dehydration.

三、多囊肾的病理影像 Pathology Image of Polycystic Kidney Disease

多囊肾表现为肾脏表面及内部充满多个大小不等的囊泡，正常肾实质被大面积替代破坏。 Polycystic kidney is characterized by multiple cysts of various sizes on the surface and inside, with normal tissue largely replaced.

多囊肾属于遗传性疾病，是CKD的常见原因之一。 Polycystic kidney is a genetic disease and a common cause of CKD.

四、CKD的临床风险因素 Clinical Risk Factors for CKD

临床因素包括高血压、糖尿病、血脂异常、肥胖、心血管病、自身免疫病、泌尿系统疾病、家族史、既往急性肾损伤等。 Clinical factors: hypertension, diabetes, dyslipidemia, obesity, cardiovascular diseases, autoimmune diseases, urologic diseases, family history, previous acute kidney injury (AKF).

常见药物风险包括NSAIDs、锂剂、氨基糖苷类抗生素。 Drug risks: NSAIDs, lithium, aminoglycoside antibiotics (ABQ).

社会人口学因素
吸烟、年龄大于60岁、环境暴露（重金属）、种族（非洲裔、西班牙裔）、低文化/收入水平等。 Socio-demographic risk: tobacco use, age over 60, environmental exposure to heavy metals, ethnicity (African, Hispanic), low education and income.

五、补充讲解 Additional Key Lecture Points

老师特别强调，CKD的发病机制是肾单位持续损伤与代偿失衡，终致进行性丧失肾功能。 The lecturer emphasized that CKD progression is driven by ongoing nephron injury and failed compensation, leading to inevitable functional loss.

血清肌酐和尿素氮的上升，以及肌酐清除率的下降，是临床评估CKD严重程度的重要指标。 Rising serum creatinine and urea, along with declining creatinine clearance, are key markers of CKD severity.

早期肾病常有高滤过性多尿，晚期则以水盐潴留、少尿或无尿为主。 Early CKD presents as hyperfiltration-induced polyuria; late CKD features fluid retention, oliguria, or anuria.

多囊肾为遗传性导致的常见慢性肾脏疾病之一，发病机制为无数囊泡代替正常肾组织，最终导致肾功能衰竭。 Polycystic kidney is a classic hereditary CKD cause; its mechanism is the replacement of normal tissue by cysts, ending in renal failure.

CKD的管理需要综合控制危险因素（如血压、血糖）、谨慎用药、避免肾毒性暴露，并关注患者生活方式干预。 CKD management includes multifactorial risk control (BP, glucose), careful drug use, avoiding nephrotoxins, and lifestyle interventions.

六、听课小结 Key Takeaway Notes

慢性肾病常因糖尿病、高血压或遗传病等发展，临床表现复杂，进展缓慢而危险。 CKD frequently arises from diabetes, hypertension, or genetic conditions, with insidious and dangerous progression.

改善预后需早发现、早干预，并防范可控危险因素。 Early detection, prompt intervention, and modifiable risk prevention are crucial for improving prognosis.

肌酐清除率与慢性肾脏病的评估与治疗 Creatinine clearance and CKD assessment and therapies

一、肌酐清除率的标准与分级 Biological standard and classification of creatinine clearance

生理学标准值
约为125毫升/分钟。 Physiologist standard: about 125 ml/min.
生物学标准值
95-135毫升/分钟/1.73平方米（生物化学标准120毫升/分钟）。 Biologist standard: 95-135 ml/min/1.73 m² (biochemist: 120 ml/min).
肾功能衰竭诊断标准
肌酐清除率<90毫升/分钟/1.73平方米。 Renal failure threshold: creatinine clearance <90 ml/min/1.73 m².

轻度CKD（慢性肾病）：清除率90-60毫升/分钟，无临床表现。 Minor CKD: clearance 90-60 ml/min, no clinical signs.

中度CKD
清除率60-30毫升/分钟，中度临床或生物标志物异常。 Moderate CKD: clearance 60-30 ml/min, moderate clinical/biological signs.
重度CKD
清除率30-15毫升/分钟，出现明显临床及生物异常。 Advanced CKD: clearance 30-15 ml/min, notable clinical/biological signs.
终末期CKD
清除率<15毫升/分钟，严重并发症。 Terminal CKD: clearance <15 ml/min, major complications.

二、肌酐清除率的血肌酐评估及计算方法 Creatinine clearance: assessment by blood creatinine and calculation formulas

血肌酐水平可用于近似肾小球滤过率（GFR）和肾功能。 Blood creatinine levels approximate GFR and renal function.

CKD-EPI公式
考虑肌酐、年龄、种族和性别。 CKD-EPI formula: uses creatinine, age, ethnicity, and sex.

Cockcroft & Gault公式：考虑肌酐、年龄、体重和性别。 Cockcroft & Gault formula: uses creatinine, age, weight, and sex.

GFR下降，血肌酐升高，表现为肾功能下降趋势。 As GFR decreases, blood creatinine rises, indicating reduced renal function.

三、慢性肾病失代偿期的后果 Consequences of decompensated CKD

内分泌功能下降
EPO↓、活性维生素D（骨化三醇）↓，诱发贫血和低钙血症。 Endocrine dysfunction: ↓EPO, ↓calcitriol leading to anemia and hypocalcemia.

钙磷代谢紊乱及继发甲状旁腺激素（PTH）升高，导致骨脱矿。 Disturbed Ca/P metabolism, increased PTH, causes bone demineralization.

肾小管功能减退
血磷、血钾、血氢离子升高，酸中毒。 Tubular dysfunction: ↑plasma phosphate, potassium, H+, causing acidosis.
钠水潴留
高容量血症（高水肿），易并发高血压和心脏病。 Sodium/water retention: hypervolemia (edema), leading to hypertension, cardiac disease.
代谢产物潴留
尿素、肌酐、尿酸、毒素清除减少。 Reduced clearance of urea, creatinine, uric acid, toxins.
脂代谢异常
高脂血症，促进动脉粥样硬化。 Dyslipidemia: hyperlipidemia contributes to atherosclerosis.

四、慢性肾病分期及症状 Stages and symptoms of CKD

第一阶段（>90）：最轻微损伤，功能正常或增高，无症状。 Stage 1 (>90): minimal deterioration, normal/increased function, no symptoms.

第二阶段（60-89）：渐进性下降，轻度肌酐和尿素升高，尚无症状。 Stage 2 (60-89): progressive decline, slight increase in creatinine/urea, no symptoms.

第三阶段（30-59）：显著损伤，首次出现乏力、食欲不振、瘙痒和贫血。 Stage 3 (30-59): advanced damage, first symptoms: fatigue, anorexia, itching, anemia.

第四阶段（15-29）：严重损伤，常伴持久疲劳、食欲差、瘙痒、贫血、高血压。 Stage 4 (15-29): severe damage, persistent fatigue/appetite loss/itching/anemia/hypertension.

第五阶段（<15）：终末期CKD，危及生命，失眠、呼吸困难、瘙痒、频繁呕吐。 Stage 5 (<15): terminal CKD, life-threatening, insomnia, dyspnea, itching, frequent vomiting.

五、晚期/终末期CKD治疗 Advanced/terminal CKD treatment

血液透析
通过机器将血液中废物和多余液体滤除，维持体内电解质平衡。 Hemodialysis: filters blood through a machine to remove waste and excess fluid, maintains electrolyte balance.
腹膜透析
利用腹膜作半透膜，引入透析液清除毒素，再排出体外。 Peritoneal dialysis: uses peritoneum as semi-permeable membrane, dialysate introduced and toxins drained out.

六、CKD的经典与新型治疗方法 Classical and new therapies for CKD

经典治疗
Classical treatments:
饮食调整
低盐、低钾、低磷、低蛋白，严格控制水分。 Dietary changes: low Na⁺, K⁺, PO₄³⁻, protein, controlled fluid.

停烟、运动、体重管理等生活方式优化。 Lifestyle: smoking cessation, physical activity, weight control.

药物治疗
Medications:

ACEI/ARB控制血压，保护肾脏。 ACEI/ARBs control BP, nephroprotection.

利尿剂管理水肿。 Diuretics for edema management.

他汀类降脂，磷结合剂减少高磷血症。 Statins for cholesterol, phosphate binders for hyperphosphatemia.

并发症管理
Management of complications:

贫血用铁剂或EPO。 Anemia: iron and/or EPO.

骨健康用活性维生素D。 Bone health: vitamin D₃ (calcitriol).

晚期采用透析或肾移植。 Advanced options: dialysis or kidney transplant.

新型治疗
New therapies:

SGLT2抑制剂，延缓肾损伤进展，保护心肾。 SGLT2 inhibitors slow CKD progression, cardiorenal protection.

GLP1激动剂，尤其对糖尿病肾病患者有益。 GLP1 agonists, especially for diabetic nephropathy.

醛固酮受体拮抗剂，减缓蛋白尿和CKD发展。 Aldosterone receptor antagonists slow progression/proteinuria.

补体抑制剂、细胞治疗及异种移植等创新方式。 Complement inhibitors, autologous cell therapy, xenotransplantation.

七、老师补充讲解要点 Teacher's key explained points

CKD常需结合临床、化验及估算公式综合评估。 CKD generally requires combined clinical assessment, lab results, and formula calculations.

常用的血清肌酐、GFR估算公式为CKD分期的基础。 Serum creatinine and GFR formulas are fundamental for staging CKD.

终末期CKD治疗以透析和肾移植为主，但需提前管理并发症和改善生活方式。 Terminal CKD treatment is mainly dialysis and transplantation, but complication management and lifestyle optimization are essential in advance.

创新治疗方案和药物不断涌现，有望改善患者预后与生活质量。 New therapies and drugs are emerging to improve patient outcomes and quality of life.

八、小结 Summary

肌酐清除率是CKD评估核心指标，严重下降预示疾病进展及并发症风险。 Creatinine clearance is a core CKD assessment index; severe decrease indicates disease progression and complication risk.

慢性肾病需分期管理，治疗目标是延缓进展、预防并发症并维持患者整体健康。 CKD requires stage-based management; aims are to slow progression, prevent complications, and maintain overall health.

I–胃疾病的发病机制（呕吐与恶心） I–Pathophysiology of gastric disorders (Nausea and Vomiting)

A. 呕吐与恶心 A. Nausea and vomiting

呕吐和恶心是机体的防御性反射机制。 Nausea and vomiting are protective reflex mechanisms of the body.

1
呕吐反射的主要中枢（定位与结构） Main Centers of the Vomiting Reflex (Localization and Structure)
区后叶（Area postrema，延髓）位于血脑屏障之外，是化学感受激活区（CTZ） The area postrema (medulla oblongata) is located outside the blood-brain barrier 　, functioning as a chemoreceptor trigger zone (CTZ).
答案 B

呕吐中枢（Vomiting Center，延髓）位于血脑屏障之内 The vomiting center (medulla oblongata) is located behind the BBB.

2
激活区后叶的因素 Activation of the Area Postrema

由致吐物质激活，包括： Activated by emetic substances, including:

多巴胺激动剂（如阿朴吗啡、抗帕金森病药物） Dopamine agonists (such as apomorphine, anti-parkinsonian agents)

药物（如抗癌药物、阿片类、地高辛、尼古丁） Drugs (anticancer drugs, opioids, digitalis, nicotine)

毒素（肠毒素、毒物等） Toxins (enterotoxins, poisons, etc.)

3
呕吐中枢的激活途径 Activation Routes of the Vomiting Center
可被下列途径激活
The vomiting center can be activated by:

区后叶（Area postrema） Area postrema

平衡器官直接刺激（如前庭，涉及到晕船、晕车等） Directly by organs of balance (vestibule, related to motion sickness)

来自胃肠道的迷走神经传入信号 By vagal afferents from the GI tract

其他因素 Other factors

4
呕吐的诱因与调控 Vomiting Triggers and Regulation

毒素、药物、缺氧、尿毒症可直接作用于区后叶激活呕吐中枢 Toxins, drugs, hypoxia, and uremia can activate the area postrema to stimulate the vomiting center.

平衡系统疾病、前庭疾病及运动病可直接激活呕吐中枢 Disorders of balance, vestibular diseases, and motion sickness can directly activate the vomiting center.

脑损伤、脑水肿、头部外伤等可激活呕吐中枢（损伤后若见恶心呕吐需警惕） Brain injuries, brain edema, and head trauma can activate the vomiting center (vomiting after trauma indicates urgency).

精神应激、巨大压力及妊娠等因素也能通过高级中枢激活呕吐中枢 Psychic stress, high pressure, and pregnancy can activate the vomiting center via higher centers.

来自胃部与消化道的信号如炎症、膨胀、排空延迟等同样可经迷走神经通路激活呕吐中枢 Signals from the stomach and GI tract (inflammation, distension, delayed emptying) can activate the vomiting center via the vagal pathway.

5
呕吐启动前的预警信号 Warning Signs Before Vomiting

呕吐前通常会有明显的预警症状，如出汗、流涎、恶心等 There are often warning signs before vomiting, such as sweating, salivation, and nausea.

若刺激超过阈值则完全启动呕吐反射 When stimulus exceeds the threshold, the full vomiting reflex is triggered.

补充讲解 Extra Teacher’s Points

呕吐是保护性反射，防止体内有毒物质进入血液循环或加重中毒 Vomiting is a protective reflex to prevent toxic substances from entering the bloodstream or exacerbating poisoning.

区后叶因缺乏血脑屏障可感受血液中多种有害物质，是中枢致吐药物的作用关键位置 The area postrema, lacking a blood-brain barrier, senses various harmful substances in the blood and is a key site for central emetic drug action.

有些动物不能呕吐（如马），而人类、狗、猫等均具备此反射 Some animals cannot vomit (e.g., horses), while humans, dogs, and cats possess this reflex.

妊娠呕吐与hCG（人绒毛促性腺激素）有关，双胎妊娠呕吐更重 Pregnancy-related vomiting is associated with beta-hCG; vomiting is more severe in twin pregnancies.

脑部损伤后伴随呕吐为危险信号，提示需紧急处理 Vomiting with brain injury is a red flag indicating the need for emergency care.

（本部分内容如上，若老师接下来继续讲解，请及时补充整理，避免重复） (If the teacher continues with further explanation on the topic, please update notes accordingly and avoid repetition.)

胃疾病的发病机制I—恶心与呕吐 Pathophysiology of Gastric Disorders I—Nausea and Vomiting

一、防御性反射机制 Defense Reflex Mechanism

恶心和呕吐是一种防御性反射，用于清除体内有害物质。 Nausea and vomiting are defense reflexes to expel harmful substances from the body.

二、发病机制 Pathophysiology

机体通过复杂的神经通路协调启动呕吐过程，包括消化道、大脑和周围神经。 The body coordinates the vomiting process via complex neural pathways involving the digestive tract, brain, and peripheral nerves.

三、并发症 Complications

营养不良 Malnutrition

离子丢失
低钠血症、低钾血症、碱中毒 Ion losses: hyponatremia, hypokalemia, alkalosis

脱水 Dehydration

四、呕吐机制 Mechanism of Vomiting

1
预警信号 Warning Signs

面色苍白 Pallor

出汗 Sweats

流涎 Salivation

血压降低 Hypotension

咽部紧缩感 Strangulation

恶心 Nausea

2
呕吐反射 Vomiting Reflex

出现逆蠕动 Antiperistaltic movement

声门关闭（有意识时）导致上呼吸道阻塞 Glottis closure (if conscious) causing upper airway blockage

腹壁肌肉剧烈收缩，使腹内压升高 Powerful contraction of abdominal wall muscles, increasing abdominal pressure

贲门开放和食管松弛 Cardia opening and esophageal relaxation

胃内容物排出 Evacuation of gastric contents

五、其他老师补充讲解 Extra Teacher’s Explanation

呕吐前常见的预警表现包括面色发白、出汗、流口水等生理反应。 Common warning signs before vomiting include pallor, sweating, and salivation.

呕吐反射依赖于大脑干中特定部位的协调，包括化学感受区和呕吐中枢。 The vomiting reflex depends on coordination of specific brainstem areas, including the chemoreceptor trigger zone and vomiting center.

在呕吐前的反应如恶心、出汗、流口水、低血压等，一旦神经阈值被超过，即会进入呕吐阶段。 Pre-vomiting reactions such as nausea, sweating, salivation, hypotension occur; once the neural threshold is exceeded, vomiting is triggered.

呕吐的主要步骤为逆蠕动、声门关闭、腹肌剧烈收缩（腹压升高）、贲门开启和食管松弛，最终导致胃内容物被排出。 The main steps are antiperistalsis, glottis closure, strong contraction of abdominal muscles (increasing pressure), cardia opening and esophageal relaxation, resulting in evacuation of gastric contents.

六、呕吐的治疗 Therapies of Vomiting

1
经典止吐治疗 Classical Treatments: Antiemetics

抗组胺药（如地晕明、美克洛嗪等）：用于晕动病、轻度恶心 Antihistamines (e.g., dimenhydrinate, meclizine): for motion sickness, mild nausea

D2多巴胺受体拮抗剂（如多潘立酮、甲氧氯普胺）：用于多种恶心/呕吐 D2 dopaminergic antagonists (e.g., domperidone, metoclopramide): for various nausea/vomiting

抗胆碱药（东莨菪碱）：用于晕动病等 Anticholinergics (scopolamine): for motion sickness and other nausea

5-HT3拮抗剂（如昂丹司琼）：用于放化疗相关恶心/呕吐 5HT-3 antagonists (ondansetron): for radio-/chemotherapy-induced nausea/vomiting

糖皮质激素（地塞米松）：联合用药，控制化疗相关呕吐 Corticosteroids (dexamethasone): in combination, for chemotherapy-induced nausea/vomiting

神经激肽-1（NK-1）受体拮抗剂（艾瑞匹坦）：防治化疗相关呕吐 NK-1 receptor antagonists (aprepitant): prevent/treat chemotherapy-induced nausea/vomiting

2
新潜力治疗 New Potential Therapies
联合疗法
多药联用提高疗效 Combination therapies: combining different classes to improve efficacy
奥氮平
多受体拮抗的非典型抗精神病药 Olanzapine: atypical antipsychotic blocking multiple receptors
大麻素类
探索中，具有止吐特性 Cannabinoids: being explored for antiemetic properties
胃肠电刺激
用于顽固性恶心呕吐 Gastric electrical stimulation: for chronic/resistant vomiting
生姜
作为经典治疗的补充 Ginger spice: as a complement to classical treatments

胃疾病的发病机制I—胃炎与消化性溃疡 Pathophysiology of Gastric Disorders I—Gastritis and Peptic Ulcer

A- 胃炎 Gastritis

定义
急性/慢性胃黏膜炎症性损害 Definition: Acute/chronic inflammatory involvement of the gastric mucosa

多数无症状 Most cases are asymptomatic

病理生理
90%与细菌感染有关，尤其是幽门螺杆菌 Pathophysiology: 90% are due to bacteria, especially Helicobacter pylori

胃炎的类型 Types of Gastritis

1
NSAIDs、酒精、缺血性损伤导致的急性糜烂和出血性胃炎 Acute erosive and hemorrhagic gastritis caused by NSAIDs, alcohol, ischemic lesions, injury

可进一步发展为急性溃疡，甚至出血或穿孔 May progress to acute ulcers, hemorrhage or perforation

2
幽门螺杆菌感染导致的慢性胃窦炎 Chronic gastritis of the antrum caused by Helicobacter pylori infection

10%的患者发展为慢性溃疡和反应性胃炎，其中1%可能癌变 10% develop chronic ulcer and reactive gastritis, among which 1% may progress to cancerous transformation

3
自身免疫性（如Biermer病）导致的慢性萎缩性胃炎 Chronic atrophic gastritis of the fundus due to autoantibodies (e.g., Biermer's disease)

伴随着胃分泌减少（胃蛋白酶、盐酸和内因子减少） Characterized by decreased gastric secretions (pepsin, HCl, intrinsic factor)

可导致恶性贫血 Can result in pernicious anemia

B- 消化性溃疡 Peptic (Gastroduodenal) Ulcer

定义
胃或十二指肠黏膜壁层显著损伤，累及肌层 Definition: Significant lesion of the wall of the gastric and/or duodenal mucosa affecting the muscular layer
典型症状为
上腹或脐上区疼痛，类似绞痛或扭转样，中度到剧烈，通常进食后约1小时发作，可被饮食或抗酸药缓解 Typical pain: epigastric or supraumbilical, cramp- or torsion-like, moderate to intense, postprandial (approx. 1 hour), relieved by food or antacids

法国每年新增约九万病例 About 90,000 new cases/year in France

消化性溃疡的发病机制 Pathophysiology of Peptic Ulcer

溃疡的形成由胃酸/胃蛋白酶与保护系统（黏液/碳酸氢盐）之间的失衡及壁的愈合能力减弱共同导致 Ulcer formation is due to imbalance between HCl/pepsin production and protective systems (mucus/bicarbonates) plus reduced healing capacity of the wall

消化性溃疡的常见原因 Common Causes of Peptic Ulcer

1
幽门螺杆菌感染 Helicobacter pylori infection

尿素酶将尿素分解为氨和二氧化碳，从而缓冲酸性环境，提高溃疡风险10倍 Urease breaks down urea into NH3 and CO2, buffering acidity and increasing ulcer risk by 10-fold

2
NSAIDs及其他药物 NSAIDs and other drugs (aspirin, cortisol, other steroids)

破坏黏膜保护，增加损伤及溃疡形成 Disrupt mucosal protection, lead to injury and ulcer formation

其他
胃泌素瘤（如Zollinger-Ellison综合征）、克罗恩病等 Others: Gastrinomas (e.g., Zollinger-Ellison), Crohn's disease, etc.

消化性溃疡的病因和后果 Causes and Consequences

慢性胃炎→屏障功能受损→化学性攻击或促氢离子、胃蛋白酶分泌↑ Chronic gastritis → Barrier dysfunction → Chemical aggression or ↑H+ and pepsin secretion

上皮损伤如果愈合不良形成溃疡，表现为疼痛、可有穿孔和出血 Epithelial lesion fails to heal and develops into an ulcer, causing pain, with risk of perforation and hemorrhage

常见疼痛部位 Common Sites of Pain

胃炎、溃疡多表现为上腹部或左上腹疼痛 Gastritis and ulcers often present with pain in the epigastric or left hypochondriac region

总结 Summary

胃炎多数无症状，但可能因感染或损伤发展为溃疡，严重时并发癌变、贫血等 Gastritis is often asymptomatic but may progress to ulcer, cancer, anemia due to infection or injury

消化性溃疡的本质是破坏与修复的不平衡，最常见诱因为幽门螺杆菌和NSAIDs The essence of peptic ulcer is the imbalance between injury and repair, most commonly due to Helicobacter pylori and NSAIDs

临床表现主要为上腹部疼痛，如未及时治疗可并发出血、穿孔等严重后果 Main clinical feature is epigastric pain; without prompt treatment, complications like bleeding and perforation may occur

胃疾病的发病机制I—胃部疾病的发病机制 I—Pathophysiology of Gastric Disorders

A- 恶心与呕吐 A- Nausea and vomiting

恶心与呕吐是一种防御性反射，有助于清除体内有害物质。 Nausea and vomiting are protective reflexes helping to expel harmful substances from the body.

机体通过中枢（包括延髓的化学感受激活区和呕吐中枢）、胃肠道、前庭系统等多途径协调启动呕吐。 The body coordinates vomiting via central centers (chemoreceptor trigger zone and vomiting center in the medulla), GI tract, vestibular system, among other pathways.

常见诱因包括毒素、药物（如化疗药、阿片类）、前庭刺激（晕车、晕船）、胃肠道刺激、脑损伤、妊娠、精神应激等。 Common triggers include toxins, drugs (e.g. chemotherapy agents, opioids), vestibular stimulation (motion sickness), GI irritation, brain injury, pregnancy, and psychological stress.

主要并发症有脱水、电解质紊乱（低钾、低钠、碱中毒）、营养不良和食管损伤。 Main complications include dehydration, electrolyte disturbances (hypokalemia, hyponatremia, alkalosis), malnutrition, and esophageal injury.

B- 胃炎 B- Gastritis

胃炎为胃黏膜急性或慢性炎症。 Gastritis is inflammation of the gastric mucosa, acute or chronic.

多数人无明显症状，部分可进展为溃疡，常见病因为幽门螺杆菌感染或药物损伤。 Most cases are asymptomatic, though some progress to ulcer; common causes are Helicobacter pylori infection or drug injury.

幽门螺杆菌感染常导致胃窦部慢性胃炎，可在10%病例发展为溃疡、1%癌变；自身免疫性胃炎累及胃底部，导致胃分泌减少甚至恶性贫血。 H. pylori commonly causes chronic antral gastritis, 10% progress to ulcers, 1% to cancer; autoimmune gastritis affects the gastric fundus, causing decreased gastric secretion and even pernicious anemia.

C- 消化性溃疡 C- Peptic Ulcer

定义 Definition 是指胃或十二指肠黏膜壁的层次性损伤，累及肌层。 Defined as deep injury through the mucosal wall of the stomach or duodenum involving the muscle layer.

病理生理 Pathophysiology 由胃酸、胃蛋白酶攻击与黏膜防御（黏液、碳酸氢盐）之间失衡及愈合能力下降共同导致。 Results from imbalance between gastric acid/pepsin aggression and mucosal defense (mucus, bicarbonate), plus reduced healing capacity.

溃疡常见原因 Causes of Ulcers

1
幽门螺杆菌感染。 Helicobacter pylori infection.
2
非甾体类抗炎药（NSAIDs）和其他药物（阿司匹林、皮质激素等）损伤黏膜。 NSAIDs and other drugs (aspirin, corticosteroids) damage mucosa.
3
罕见原因如胃泌素瘤（Zollinger-Ellison综合征）、克罗恩病等。 Rare causes: gastrinomas, Crohn’s disease, etc.

临床表现与并发症 Clinical Signs and Complications

典型表现为上腹痛或脐上痛，多在餐后约1小时发作，类似绞痛，中度至剧烈，进食或服用抗酸药可缓解。 Typical pain is epigastric or supraumbilical, cramp-like, moderate to severe, appears about 1 hour after meals, relieved by food or antacids.

亦可见非典型胸腹痛，有时与心脏病相混淆。 Atypical pain may also occur and can sometimes be mistaken for cardiac pain.

并发症包括出血（黑便、呕血）、穿孔、腹膜炎。 Complications: hemorrhages (melena, hematemesis), perforation, peritonitis.

溃疡治疗 Therapies of Ulcers

1
经典治疗方案 Classical Treatments of Ulcers

预防（戒烟、戒酒，避免NSAIDs和高危患者应用PPI） Prevention: stop smoking/alcohol, avoid NSAIDs, PPIs for at-risk patients

抗生素（四环素+铋剂+甲硝唑）根除幽门螺杆菌 Antibiotics (tetracycline, bismuth, metronidazole) for H. pylori eradication

质子泵抑制剂（PPI）：奥美拉唑、兰索拉唑等，抑制壁细胞H+/K+泵 Proton Pump Inhibitors (PPIs): omeprazole, lansoprazole, inhibit H+/K+ ATPase in parietal cells

抗酸剂（如氢氧化铝、氢氧化镁）中和胃酸 Anti-acids: aluminum hydroxide, magnesium hydroxide, neutralize gastric acid

H2受体阻滞剂（雷尼替丁、法莫替丁）通过阻断壁细胞组胺受体减少酸分泌 H2 blockers (ranitidine, famotidine, cimetidine): reduce acid by blocking histamine receptors on parietal cells

2
新型/潜力疗法 New Potential Therapies
新型PPI/钾竞争性酸阻断剂
沃诺拉赞、扎斯普拉赞，直接抑制H+/K+ ATP酶，起效快、耐受性好 New PPIs/Potassium-Competitive Acid Blockers (P-CAB): vonoprazan, zastaprazan, reversible H+/K+ ATPase inhibition, rapid onset, improved tolerance
组织工程与再生医学
利用支架、生长因子、干细胞促进黏膜再生和愈合 Tissue engineering and regenerative medicine: scaffolds, growth factors, stem cells for mucosal regeneration and healing

连接蛋白43（Cx43）调节：ACT1模拟肽可加速慢性溃疡愈合 Connexin 43 (Cx43) modulation: ACT1 mimetic peptide accelerates healing in chronic ulcers

肠道疾病的发病机制II—肠道疾病的发病机制 II – Pathophysiology of bowel disorders

A-肠道转运障碍A-Intestinal transit disorders

1
腹泻 Diarrhea  - 腹泻是指每天排便次数增加，粪便变稀，通常超过三次/天。   Diarrhea is defined as increased stool frequency, with loose or watery stools, usually more than three times/day.  - 常见原因有感染（细菌、病毒、寄生虫）、药物（抗生素、化疗药）、食物不耐受、高渗性物质（如甜味剂、镁盐）、肠道炎症与功能障碍等。   Common causes include infections (bacteria, viruses, parasites), medications (antibiotics, chemotherapy), food intolerance, osmotic agents (sweeteners, magnesium salts), intestinal inflammation, and functional disorders.  - 持续腹泻会导致脱水、电解质丢失（尤其是儿童和老年人危险性大）、营养不良和体重下降。   Prolonged diarrhea can cause dehydration, electrolyte loss (especially dangerous in children and elderly), malnutrition, and weight loss.  - 有些腹泻伴有粘液或血液（如细菌感染、炎症性疾病），需及时就医。   Some diarrhea involves mucus or blood (as in bacterial infections, inflammatory diseases) and requires prompt medical attention.  - 治疗以预防和对因处理为主，如纠正脱水、慎重选择抗生素，部分情况可用益生菌调节肠道菌群。   Treatment focuses on prevention and addressing the cause, including rehydration, careful antibiotic use, and probiotics in some cases to restore microbiota【@ref_3】。
2
便秘 Constipation  - 便秘指排便次数少于每周三次，伴硬便和排便困难；若排便完全停止则考虑肠梗阻。   Constipation is defined as stool frequency less than three times per week, with hard stools and difficult evacuation; total stoppage suggests bowel obstruction.  - 便秘常因肠蠕动变慢，包括：   Constipation most often results from slowed gut transit, including:   • 神经源性（如帕金森病、多发性硬化、神经损伤）    Neurogenic (e.g., Parkinson’s, multiple sclerosis, nerve damage)   • 药物（如阿片类、抗抑郁药、部分降压药）    Medication-induced (e.g., opioids, antidepressants, some antihypertensives)   • 功能性（如孕期、长期卧床）    Functional (e.g., pregnancy, prolonged bed rest)   • 排便困难（肛周疾病、动力障碍）    Mechanical or functional evacuation difficulties (anorectal disorders, motility impairment)  - 治疗以改善生活方式和饮食、增加活动、必要时使用泻药。   Treatment includes lifestyle and dietary modifications, increased activity, and use of laxatives when needed【@ref_3】。
功能性结肠病
肠易激综合征（IBS）Functional colopathy: irritable bowel syndrome (IBS)  - IBS表现为腹泻、便秘交替，伴腹痛和肠痉挛，症状时轻时重，无结构性器质病变。   IBS presents as alternating diarrhea and constipation with abdominal pain and spasms, symptoms are variable and there is no structural lesion.  - 病因多因素，包括肠动力和感觉异常、肠道菌群失调、应激等。   Etiology is multifactorial, involving altered motility and sensation, gut dysbiosis, and psychological stress.
4
肠气排放 Flatulence  - 肠气多由肠道菌发酵、吞咽空气产生，常因饮食成分或消化吸收障碍加重。   Flatulence is mainly caused by fermentation of food by gut microbiota and swallowed air, aggravated by certain foods or malabsorption syndromes.
肠道刺激症
结肠炎 Irritations: colitis  - 结肠炎表现为腹痛、腹泻、便血等，可急性或慢性，多由感染、炎症或缺血引起。   Colitis presents as abdominal pain, diarrhea, bloody stools and can be acute or chronic, commonly due to infection, inflammation, or ischemia.

B-慢性炎症性肠病B-Chronic inflammatory bowel diseases (IBD)

1.炎症性肠病IBD (Inflammatory bowel disease)  - 主要包括克罗恩病(Crohn's disease, CD) 和溃疡性结肠炎(ulcerative colitis, UC)。   Key types are Crohn’s disease 　 and ulcerative colitis (UC).  - IBD特征：肠黏膜脆弱，易形成溃疡、瘘管、狭窄（梗阻风险），甚至穿孔、脓肿、腹膜炎。   IBD is characterized by mucosal fragility, leading to ulceration, fistulae, strictures (risk of obstruction), and possible perforation, abscess, and peritonitis.  - 临床病程多有急性发作（危象）、缓解与复发交替。   The clinical course often alternates between acute flare-ups (crises), remissions, and relapses.  - CD多累及回肠和结肠，但可从口腔到肛门任何部位受累，常见区域性病变。   CD commonly affects the ileum and colon, but any part of the GI tract from mouth to anus can be involved, with typical patchy lesions.  - UC主要累及结肠和直肠，典型为连续炎症。   UC mainly involves colon and rectum with continuous mucosal inflammation.  - 症状包括长期腹泻、便血（直肠出血）、腹痛、体重下降、发热（脓肿时），以及继发性贫血。   Symptoms include prolonged diarrhea, rectal bleeding, abdominal pain, weight loss, fever (if abscess), and secondary anemia.  - 危险因素包括环境（如吸烟）、遗传背景。   Risk factors include environmental factors (such as smoking) and genetic predisposition.  - 治疗主要为糖皮质激素、免疫抑制剂和生物制剂。   Management includes corticosteroids, immunosuppressants, and biotherapies【@ref_3】。
答案 CD

C-克罗恩病的内镜表现 Endoscopic appearance of Crohn's disease

克罗恩病内镜下可见正常肠黏膜与表浅溃疡交替，显示肠壁病变区域多样、斑片状。  Endoscopically, Crohn’s disease shows alternating areas of normal mucosa and superficial ulcers, illustrating the patchy and varied nature of intestinal involvement.

斑片状炎症和溃疡是其典型特征，有助于与溃疡性结肠炎区分。  This patchy inflammation and ulceration is a hallmark of CD and helps distinguish it from ulcerative colitis.

克罗恩病肠道表现及慢性炎症性肠病的治疗Appearance of the Intestine in Crohn's Disease & Therapies of Chronic IBD

一、克罗恩病肠道表现 Appearance of the Intestine in Crohn's Disease

正常结肠的肠壁光滑、结构完整。 The wall of a healthy colon is smooth and structurally intact.

克罗恩病的肠壁呈节段性受累，表现为炎症、肿胀、溃疡区与正常区间隔分布。 In Crohn's disease, the bowel wall shows segmental involvement with alternating inflamed/swollen/ulcerated and normal areas.

常见并发症包括腹泻、腹痛、脓肿、瘘管。 Common complications include diarrhea, abdominal pain, abscess, and fistula.

克罗恩病患者存在癌变风险，需定期监测。 There is a risk of malignant transformation in Crohn’s disease patients, requiring regular monitoring【@ref_1】。

二、克罗恩病腹部CT表现 Crohn's Disease Abdominal CT Scan

CT上可见肠黏膜局部增厚，与正常肠黏膜对比明显。 On CT scan, focal thickening of the bowel mucosa is seen, in contrast to normal mucosal segments.

增厚和炎症的区域提示活动性病变，常为疾病诊断与严重程度评估的重要线索。 These areas of thickened and inflamed mucosa indicate active lesions and are essential for disease diagnosis and severity assessment【@ref_1】。

三、慢性炎症性肠病的治疗 Therapies of Chronic IBD

1
经典治疗 Classical Treatments

糖皮质激素（如泼尼松、布地奈德）用于中重度IBD诱导缓解，但不宜长期使用以避免副作用。 Corticosteroids (e.g., prednisone, budesonide) are used to induce remission in moderate/severe IBD, but not suitable for long-term use due to side effects.

免疫调节剂（如硫唑嘌呤、6-巯基嘌呤、甲氨蝶呤）通过抑制免疫反应，减少炎症，与其他药物合用可维持缓解和减少激素依赖。 Immunomodulators (e.g., azathioprine, 6-mercaptopurine, methotrexate) suppress immune response, reduce inflammation, and in combination help maintain remission and reduce steroid dependency.

氨基水杨酸类（如柳氮磺吡啶、美沙拉嗪）主要用于轻中度IBD，通过减少黏膜炎症。 Aminosalicylates (e.g., sulfasalazine, mesalamine) are mainly for mild/moderate IBD, reducing inflammation in the intestinal lining.

抗生素（如甲硝唑、环丙沙星）用于处理并发感染，如脓肿、瘘管。 Antibiotics (e.g., metronidazole, ciprofloxacin) are for treating complications such as abscesses or fistulas【@ref_1】。

2
新型生物治疗 New Biotherapies

抗TNFα单抗（如英夫利昔单抗、阿达木单抗等）为一线生物制剂，能有效诱导和维持CD及UC缓解。 Anti-TNF agents (e.g., infliximab, adalimumab, etc.) are first-line biotherapeutics, effective for inducing and maintaining remission in both Crohn's disease and ulcerative colitis.

抗整合素疗法（如维多珠单抗）靶向α4β7整合素，阻断白细胞进入肠道，有效于CD与UC，对抗TNF无效时使用。 Anti-integrin therapies (e.g., vedolizumab) target α4β7 integrin to prevent leukocyte infiltration, used for both CD and UC when anti-TNF is ineffective.

抗白介素制剂（如乌司替单抗、risankizumab）针对特定IL信号通路，主要为第二线选择。 Anti-interleukin agents (e.g., ustekinumab for IL-12/23, risankizumab for IL-23) are second-line options targeting specific interleukin pathways.

JAK抑制剂（如托法替尼、乌帕达替尼、非戈替尼）为第三线口服药，针对多种细胞因子信号，增加感染风险。 JAK inhibitors (e.g., tofacitinib, upadacitinib, filgotinib) are third-line oral medications affecting multiple cytokine pathways, with increased infection risk.

迷走神经刺激等新方法用于缓解疼痛和痉挛。 Vagal stimulation and similar approaches are being explored to relieve pain and spasms.

新型口服免疫调节药物（如Obefazimob、etrazimob）正处于开发阶段，改善炎症。 New oral modulators of inflammation (e.g., obefazimob, etrazimob) are under development to improve inflammation【@ref_1】。

四、知识点补充 Additional Key Points

克罗恩病可累及整个消化道，最常见于末端回肠和结肠。 Crohn's disease can affect the entire GI tract, most commonly the terminal ileum and colon.

典型内镜下见健康与病变肠黏膜交替，肠壁增厚、狭窄、甚至梗阻。 Endoscopically, alternating healthy and diseased mucosa is seen with wall thickening, stenosis, and potential obstruction.

病程呈慢性反复发作，急性发作（危象）期间症状最重，严重可致穿孔、脓肿、腹膜炎。 The course is chronic and relapsing, with acute flare-ups causing severe symptoms and potentially leading to perforation, abscess, or peritonitis.

五、总结 Summary

克罗恩病的肠壁炎症与结构损伤导致多种并发症并增加癌变风险。 Structural and inflammatory damage in Crohn's disease leads to complications and raises carcinoma risk.

治疗强调免疫调控和生物药物联合，个体化用药以降低复发和并发症。 Therapy focuses on immunoregulation and biotherapies in combination, personalizing regimens to reduce relapse and complications【@ref_1】。

肝脏疾病的发病机制III–Pathophysiology of liver disorder

A-胆结石（胆石症）A-Gallstones (cholelithiasis)

定义
胆囊内晶体和结石的形成。
Definition
Formation of crystals and stones in the gallbladder.

约80%的胆结石由胆固醇组成，原因是胆固醇溶解度下降

About 80% of cases are cholesterol stones due to loss of cholesterol solubility.

约20%的胆结石由胆红素组成，多见于慢性溶血。

About 20% of cases are bilirubin stones, often associated with chronic hemolysis.

病因学 Etiology

胆固醇结石源于胆固醇与胆汁酸比例失衡，危险因素包括超重和女性。

Cholesterol stones result from an imbalance between cholesterol levels and bile acids, with risk factors including overweight and female sex.

胆红素结石多因溶血造成胆红素增多。

Bilirubin stones are mainly caused by hemolysis leading to elevated bilirubin.

并发症 Complications

胆囊炎
当石头卡在胆囊或胆管，可引发胆囊炎或（若在胆管）胆管炎。 Gallbladder inflammation: cholecystitis (if in ducts: cholangitis/colitis).

剧烈阵发性绞痛（肝绞痛）：结石阻塞会引起右上腹至右侧腰部剧烈疼痛。 Painful spasms (hepatic colic): blockage leads to severe pain from the right upper abdominal region to the right lumbar area.

结石刺激导致胆囊或胆道溃疡，严重可穿孔。 Ulceration: Stones may cause mucosal ulceration, which can progress to perforation.

胆汁淤积（胆汁性黄疸）：结石阻塞胆道导致结合胆红素升高，引发黄疸。 Cholestasis (obstructive jaundice): blockage increases conjugated bilirubin, causing jaundice.

脂肪泻
胆汁排出障碍导致脂肪无法消化吸收，粪便呈淡色油腻。 Steatorrhea: impaired bile flow prevents fat digestion/absorption, resulting in pale, greasy stool.
胰腺炎
大结石阻塞胆总管末端时，既影响胆道又影响胰管，可致急性胰腺炎。 Pancreatitis: large stones at common bile duct end can block both bile and pancreatic duct, triggering pancreatitis.

临床表现补充及解剖定位 Additional clinical features and localization

胆结石症状多表现为右上腹和右侧腰部剧烈阵发性绞痛，常放射至背部。 Gallstone attacks typically present as severe, intermittent colicky pain in the right upper abdomen extending to the right lumbar and back.

慢性阻塞时患者可见皮肤和巩膜黄染（黄疸），尿色加深，粪色变浅。 Chronic obstruction leads to jaundice (yellow skin and sclera), dark urine, and pale stools.

腹部分区的右上腹（右季肋区）、右侧腰部为典型疼痛部位。 Anatomically, pain localizes to the right hypochondrium and right lumbar region (see diagram).

影像和肉眼表现 Imaging and gross appearance

胆结石的大小与数量不一，小结石易移动，大结石可引起胆囊腔变形甚至胆囊炎。 Gallstones vary in size and number; small stones may migrate, large stones may deform the gallbladder and cause cholecystitis.

急性胆囊炎时，胆囊壁红肿，腔内容物混浊，伴大量结石。 In acute cholecystitis, the gallbladder wall is inflamed/red, the cavity turbid, and filled with stones.

黄疸的体征和机制 Jaundice
signs and mechanism

黄疸为全身皮肤、巩膜黄染，通常为结合胆红素升高所致。 Jaundice presents as yellowing of skin and sclera, typically due to increased conjugated bilirubin.

结石阻塞导致胆汁不能进入肠道，致使胆红素回流进入血液。 Stones blocking ducts prevent bile from entering the intestine, causing bilirubin to reflux into blood.

胆汁无法参与脂肪消化导致脂肪泻，粪色变浅。 Bile absence prevents fat digestion, leading to steatorrhea and pale stool.

黄疸患者常伴皮肤瘙痒，严重时可出现全身症状。 Jaundice may cause pruritus (itching) and, in severe cases, systemic symptoms.

总结 Summary

胆结石主要由胆固醇或胆红素沉积形成，多在胆固醇溶解度下降或溶血背景下。 Gallstones form mainly from cholesterol or bilirubin deposition, often due to reduced cholesterol solubility or hemolysis.

典型并发症为胆囊炎、肝绞痛、溃疡穿孔、黄疸、脂肪泻及胰腺炎。 Typical complications include cholecystitis, hepatic colic, ulceration/perforation, jaundice, steatorrhea, and pancreatitis.

注重右上腹/右腰部定位及黄疸表现，对于疾病早期识别和处理至关重要。 Recognizing localization to the right upper abdomen/lumbar region and signs of jaundice is vital for early diagnosis and management【@ref_6】。

肝脏疾病的发病机制III–Pathophysiology of liver disorders

A-胆结石（胆石症）A-Gallstones (cholelithiasis)

本部分已在前面整理，此处不再重复。

This part was summarized previously and will not be repeated here.

B-肝功能不全与肝硬化B-Hepatocellular insufficiency and cirrhosis

1
定义 Definition

肝硬化是肝组织逐步坏死、炎症，继而被纤维化组织所替代，同时形成异常的肝细胞结节，破坏了正常肝小叶结构。

Cirrhosis is the progressive destruction and necrosis of liver tissue with inflammation, replaced by fibrotic tissue, and formation of abnormal hepatocyte nodules that disrupt the normal hepatic lobule structure.

2
主要病因 Causes
慢性中毒性
酒精（占肝硬化50%）、铁沉积（血色病）、脂肪（脂肪肝，早期可逆，但持续则不可逆纤维化）。 Chronic toxic causes: alcohol (50% of cirrhosis), iron overload (hemochromatosis), fat accumulation (steatosis: initially reversible, can progress to irreversible fibrosis).
慢性感染性
乙型或丙型病毒性肝炎。 Chronic infection: chronic viral hepatitis B or C.

自身免疫作用。 Autoimmune causes.

肝脏循环要点 Reminders
Hepatic Circulation

肝血供主要来自肝动脉（25-35%）和门静脉（65-75%），门静脉汇聚脾、胰、胃肠等器官的血液至肝脏进行代谢解毒，然后经肝静脉回体循环。

The liver is supplied by the hepatic artery (25-35%) and portal vein (65-75%), which collects blood from the spleen, pancreas, stomach, and intestines for metabolism and detoxification, and returns it to the circulation via the hepatic vein.

肝脏主要功能 Reminders
Liver Functions
代谢
Metabolism:
碳水化合物
储存与再分配葡萄糖（糖异生、糖原合成与分解）。 Carbohydrates: storage and redistribution of glucose (gluconeogenesis, glycogenesis, glycogenolysis).
蛋白质
合成白蛋白、凝血因子、运输蛋白、炎症蛋白、生长因子。 Protein: synthesis of albumin, coagulation factors, transport, inflammatory proteins, growth factors.
脂质
胆固醇、甘油三酯、磷脂的合成、储存、转运、分解与清除。 Lipids: synthesis, storage, transport, hydrolysis, and clearance of cholesterol, triglycerides (TG), and phospholipids (PL).

脂溶性维生素（A、D、E、K）的储存和加工。 Storage and processing of liposoluble vitamins (A, D, E, K).

废物处理
Waste Treatment:

内源性废物（蛋白代谢产物氨NH4+）：合成尿素、谷氨酰胺排出。 Endogenous waste (ammonia, NH4+ from protein catabolism): converted to urea or glutamine for excretion.

胆固醇转化为胆汁酸。 Cholesterol is converted to bile acids.

胆红素（血红素分解产物）在肝结合后排泄。 Bilirubin (a hemoglobin breakdown product) is conjugated and excreted.

解毒
药物、污染物等进行I、II相代谢。 Xenobiotic detoxification: Phase I and II metabolism of drugs/toxins.
胆汁生成
Bile Production:

合成分泌胆汁酸/盐、胆红素、胆固醇、TG、PL及电解质。 Synthesis and secretion of bile acids/salts, bilirubin, cholesterol, TG, PL, and electrolytes.

5
常见致病与转归 Causes and Outcomes

肝细胞损伤与毒物（如对乙酰氨基酚、某些溶剂、真菌等）可急性导致肝衰竭。 Hepatotoxic agents (e.g., paracetamol, solvents, toxic mushrooms) can acutely cause liver failure.

慢性病因包括病毒肝炎、酒精、铁过载、脂肪肝、静脉回流障碍、心功能不全等。 Chronic causes include viral hepatitis, alcohol, iron overload, fatty liver, impaired venous return, heart failure.

所有慢性损伤可导致肝硬化，最终引发肝功能不全与门脉高压。 All chronic liver injuries may progress to cirrhosis, ultimately causing hepatic insufficiency and portal hypertension.

6
肝功能不全的主要后果 Consequences of Hepatocellular Insufficiency

胆汁淤积（cholestasis）：导致黄疸。 Cholestasis: leads to jaundice.

尿素合成减少（氨代谢障碍）：血氨升高，可致肝性脑病、代谢性碱中毒。 Decreased urea synthesis (ammonia metabolism disorder): blood ammonia rises, leading to hepatic encephalopathy and alkalosis.

凝血因子合成减少
出血倾向。 Reduced synthesis of coagulation factors: tendency to bleed.
白蛋白合成下降
低蛋白血症、水肿、腹水。 Decreased albumin synthesis: hypoalbuminemia, edema, ascites.
门脉高压
消化道毛细血管压力升高、脾肿大、静脉曲张、消化道大出血。 Portal hypertension: increased digestive capillary pressure, splenomegaly, varicose veins, GI hemorrhage.
7
典型体征和并发症 Typical Signs and Complications
黄疸
皮肤和巩膜黄染。 Jaundice: yellowing of skin and sclera.
腹水
腹腔积液。 Ascites: accumulation of fluid in the abdomen.
静脉曲张、Medusa头征
腹壁浅表静脉扩张。 Varicose veins, "Medusa head": dilation of superficial abdominal veins.
食管胃底静脉曲张
消化道大出血风险。 Esophageal/gastric varices: risk of major digestive hemorrhage.
其他
杵状指、肝掌、指甲白斑等。 Others: digital clubbing (Hippocratism), palmar erythema, leukonychia (white nails).
8
治疗与预防 Treatment and Prevention

无根本治疗，主要为预防和并发症处理。 No curative treatment; key focus is prevention and management of complications.

预防措施包括
戒酒、乙肝疫苗或抗病毒、肥胖和代谢综合征管理。 Preventive measures: alcohol abstinence, hepatitis vaccination/antivirals, control of obesity/metabolic syndrome.

对症治疗如腹水管理（利尿、穿刺）、曲张静脉处理、肝性脑病用乳果糖等。 Symptomatic treatments: manage ascites (diuretics, drainage), treat varices, use lactulose for encephalopathy.

肝移植为终末期肝硬化的最终治疗选择。 Liver transplantation is the definitive treatment for end-stage cirrhosis.

9
潜在新疗法 Potential New Treatments

部分新药（如resmetirom）及细胞治疗、免疫细胞工程等方法在试验阶段，目标是逆转肝纤维化和修复肝功能。 Several new therapies (such as resmetirom) and cell-based or engineered immune cell therapies are in trials, aiming to reverse fibrosis and restore liver function.

10
小结 Summary

肝硬化本质上是慢性进行性、结构和功能双重破坏的疾病，最终导致多系统受累。 Cirrhosis is essentially a chronic progressive disorder causing both architectural and functional liver failure, affecting multiple organ systems.

提前预防、控制危险因素极为重要，临床处理以综合对症为主。 Prevention and risk factor management are critical; clinical care mainly targets complications and symptom relief.
