Ca²⁺调控的心肌细胞功能机制Mechanisms of Cardiomyocyte Processes Regulated by Ca²⁺

一、Ca²⁺调控的心肌细胞生理过程 Cardiomyocyte processes regulated by Ca²⁺

1
钙离子在心肌细胞内调控多种关键生理过程，包括电活动、心肌收缩和基因转录。 Ca²⁺ regulates several key physiological processes in cardiomyocytes, including electrical activity, muscle contraction, and gene transcription.
2
电活动通过调节动作电位导致心律失常等疾病发生。 Electrical activity influences action potential and can contribute to the development of arrhythmias.
3
钙离子促进收缩蛋白丝滑动，直接驱动心肌细胞收缩。 Ca²⁺ promotes myofilament sliding, directly driving contraction of cardiomyocytes.
4
钙信号还能进入细胞核，调控转录因子，促进心肌细胞重塑和基因表达变化。 Ca²⁺ signaling can enter the nucleus, regulate transcription factors, and promote cardiac remodeling and changes in gene expression.
5
上述过程统称为“兴奋-收缩耦联”。 The above processes collectively form “excitation-contraction coupling”.

二、心肌细胞核结构 Cardiac Nuclear Structure

1
心肌细胞核包膜由内、外两层膜（INM与ONM）组成，两层膜之间为核周空间。 The nuclear envelope in cardiomyocytes consists of two layers, the inner (INM) and outer (ONM) nuclear membranes, with a perinuclear space between them.
2
该结构通过核孔复合体（NPC）与胞质相连，同时与内质网/肌浆网（ER/SR）互相连接。 This structure is linked to the cytoplasm via nuclear pore complexes (NPCs) and interconnected with the endoplasmic/sarcoplasmic reticulum (ER/SR).
3
核周空间有助于Ca²⁺信号的局部控制和转导。 The perinuclear space helps in local regulation and transduction of Ca²⁺ signaling.

三、核孔复合体功能与钙离子转运 Nuclear Pore Complex (NPC) and Ca²⁺ Transport

1
核孔复合体（NPC）为大分子蛋白复合物，分子量约125 MDa。 The nuclear pore complex (NPC) is a large multiprotein complex with a molecular weight of about 125 MDa.
2
NPC具选择性过滤功能，可以选择性通过离子和40 kD以下分子。 The NPC acts as a selective filter, allowing passage of ions and molecules below 40 kD.
3
钙离子可通过NPC自由扩散，实现胞核与胞质之间的Ca²⁺交换。 Ca²⁺ can freely diffuse through NPCs, mediating exchange between the nucleoplasm and cytoplasm.
4
但钙离子浓度在平衡时有动力学延迟，导致[Ca²⁺]_核与[Ca²⁺]_胞质的变化不同步。 However, there is a kinetic delay in the equilibration of Ca²⁺ concentration between nucleus and cytoplasm, so changes in [Ca²⁺]_nuc and [Ca²⁺]_cyto are not perfectly synchronized.

四、心肌细胞核Ca²⁺来源与调控 Nuclear Sources of Ca²⁺ in Cardiomyocytes

核内Ca²⁺有两大来源
一是动作电位和兴奋-收缩耦联过程中经LTCC及RyR进入胞质后通过NPC传入胞核，形成核Ca²⁺瞬变。 There are two major sources of nuclear Ca²⁺: (1) Ca²⁺ influx during action potential and excitation-contraction coupling, entering the cytosol via LTCC and RyR, then diffusing into the nucleus through NPCs to form nuclear Ca²⁺ transients.
2
另一类是受体介导型路径，GPCRs激活Gq-PLC通路产生IP₃，激活核/周核膜上的IP₃受体，直接释Ca²⁺入核。 The other pathway is receptor-mediated; GPCRs activate the Gq-PLC pathway, generating IP₃ which stimulates IP₃ receptors on the nuclear/perinuclear membrane, directly releasing Ca²⁺ into the nucleus.
3
SERCA泵和SR可调控局部核周Ca²⁺浓度，实现核内Ca²⁺信号的精细调节。 SERCA pumps and the SR/ER regulate perinuclear Ca²⁺ concentration, allowing precise control of nuclear Ca²⁺ signals.

五、老师课上补充要点 Teacher’s Supplement Key Points

1
钙信号调控心肌细胞电活动、收缩和基因表达，三者互相关联，Ca²⁺作为核心信号分子。 Calcium signaling co-regulates electrical activity, contraction, and gene expression in cardiomyocytes, acting as a central signal.
2
动作电位时，钙离子首先通过膜表L型Ca²⁺通道流入，然后引发SR上的RyR开放，大量Ca²⁺释放。 During action potentials, Ca²⁺ enters through the L-type Ca²⁺ channel on the membrane, then triggers opening of RyRs on the SR, releasing large amounts of Ca²⁺.
3
部分Ca²⁺可以通过NPC进入细胞核，或通过受体激活的IP₃R使核膜释放Ca²⁺，影响基因表达。 A fraction of Ca²⁺ diffuses into the nucleus via NPCs, or is released from the nuclear envelope through receptor-activated IP₃Rs, affecting gene expression.
4
核内Ca²⁺对心肌细胞重塑如肥大、转录程序变化有重要作用。 Nuclear Ca²⁺ is critical for remodeling processes in cardiomyocytes, such as hypertrophy and changes in transcriptional programs.
5
调控与平衡机制精细，失衡可导致心律失常、收缩异常或病理重塑等疾病。 These regulatory mechanisms are precisely balanced; disruption can cause arrhythmias, contractile dysfunction, or pathological remodeling.
核Ca²⁺信号研究方法
共聚焦显微镜Method of study of nuclear Ca²⁺ signalling: Confocal microscopy

胞质与核Ca²⁺信号Cytosolic vs. nuclear Ca²⁺ signals

核内膜内褶与核Ca²⁺信号Nuclear invagination and nuclear Ca²⁺ signaling

核[Ca²⁺]依赖于搏动频率Nuclear [Ca²⁺] depends on beating frequency

一、核Ca²⁺信号的检测方法 Method for detecting nuclear Ca²⁺ signaling

1
采用分离的心室肌细胞以及Ca²⁺荧光染料，利用共聚焦显微镜进行分析。 Isolated ventricular cardiomyocytes are loaded with a Ca²⁺-sensitive fluorescent dye and imaged with confocal microscopy.
2
通过拉取一条穿过细胞核与胞质的扫描线，实现对不同亚细胞区Ca²⁺动态的同步监测。 A scan line is drawn through the nucleus and cytoplasm to simultaneously record Ca²⁺ dynamics in different subcellular regions.
3
由于Ca²⁺通过核孔自由扩散，但在动力学上有延迟，因此荧光信号能反映胞质与核Ca²⁺瞬变的不同步。 Although Ca²⁺ can freely diffuse through nuclear pores, there is a kinetic delay, and the fluorescence signals capture the temporal lag between cytosolic and nuclear Ca²⁺ transients.
4
实验结果显示，核Ca²⁺上升速度更慢、峰值更低且恢复时间更长。 Results show that the nuclear Ca²⁺ rises more slowly, has a lower peak, and returns to baseline more slowly than cytosolic Ca²⁺.

二、胞质与核Ca²⁺瞬变差异 Differences between cytosolic and nuclear Ca²⁺ transients

1
在高Ca²⁺外液（1.5 mM）和低Ca²⁺外液（0.3 mM）条件下，均显示核区Ca²⁺信号上升坡度更缓、峰值更低、回落更慢。 Under both high Ca²⁺ (1.5 mM) and low Ca²⁺ (0.3 mM) extracellular conditions, the nuclear Ca²⁺ signal has a slower upstroke, lower peak, and prolonged decay compared to the cytosol.
2
这种差异说明Ca²⁺信号在细胞核经历传递阻滞，即信号进入核内的速度受限。 This indicates that transmission of Ca²⁺ signals into the nucleus is restricted, leading to a temporal and amplitude lag.
老师强调
实验时要选择代表性的扫描线，才能真正反映胞质-核间Ca²⁺信号传导的物理基础。 The teacher emphasized that a properly drawn scan line is needed for accurately capturing the physical basis of cytosolic-to-nuclear Ca²⁺ communication.

三、核内膜内褶对Ca²⁺信号的影响 Effect of nuclear envelope invagination on Ca²⁺ signaling

1
核内膜内褶（invagination）可将肌浆网（SR）紧贴拉入核内，促进Ca²⁺信号与基因调控的空间耦联。 Nuclear envelope invaginations bring the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR) into close proximity with the nucleoplasm, facilitating spatial coupling of Ca²⁺ signaling and gene regulation.
2
内褶数量越多，核Ca²⁺信号的上升更快、峰值更高，下降也更迅速；相反，内褶越少，核Ca²⁺信号变化越缓慢且幅度更低。 A higher density of invaginations results in faster upstroke, higher peak, and quicker fall of nuclear Ca²⁺, whereas lower density leads to slower and smaller transients.
3
说明核内膜结构塑性显著影响核内Ca²⁺信号动力学，进而调控基因转录速度及细胞适应性反应。 This demonstrates that the structural plasticity of the nuclear envelope strongly impacts nuclear Ca²⁺ kinetics, potentially affecting gene transcription and cellular adaptation.

四、核[Ca²⁺]受搏动频率影响 Nuclear [Ca²⁺] is affected by beating frequency

1
实验监测不同刺激频率下（0.5、1、2、5 Hz）胞质与核Ca²⁺瞬变，发现频率升高时核Ca²⁺呈现明显积累而基线升高。 Experiments show that as stimulation frequency increases (from 0.5 Hz to 5 Hz), nuclear Ca²⁺ exhibits progressive accumulation and higher resting baseline.
2
由于核Ca²⁺恢复（衰减）速度慢，高频刺激时舒张期缩短，Ca²⁺来不及完全清除，导致核内Ca²⁺信号持续增强。 Because nuclear Ca²⁺ decays more slowly, high-frequency stimulation shortens diastole, preventing complete clearance of Ca²⁺ and causing sustained elevation.
此现象暗示
高强度工作状态下，心肌细胞核内Ca²⁺信号调控基因表达和重塑能力随之变化。 This implies that under higher workload, nuclear Ca²⁺ signaling adjusts gene expression and remodeling capacity in cardiomyocytes.
老师补充
频率相关的Ca²⁺积累，可能是心脏适应长期应激和功能重塑的分子基础之一。 Teacher’s note: Frequency-dependent Ca²⁺ accumulation in the nucleus may underlie long-term cardiac adaptation and remodeling.

五、总结 Summary

1
共聚焦显微镜是研究心肌细胞核Ca²⁺信号动力学的重要工具，能进行区分胞质/核的亚细胞分辨。 Confocal microscopy is a key tool for investigating nuclear Ca²⁺ kinetics in cardiomyocytes, allowing subcellular resolution between cytoplasm and nucleus.
2
核Ca²⁺信号变化慢于胞质，易受细胞结构和搏动频率调控。 Nuclear Ca²⁺ signals are slower, lower in amplitude, and more susceptible to regulation by structure and beating frequency than those in the cytosol.
3
这些差异和动力学特性对心脏基因表达调节和病理生理反应至关重要。 These differences and kinetic properties are critical in controlling cardiac gene expression and pathophysiological responses.

Ca²⁺依赖性的转录通路Ca²⁺-dependent transcriptional pathways

一、两条主要的Ca²⁺依赖性转录通路 Two main transcriptional pathways depending on Ca²⁺

第一条
钙调磷酸酶/核因子NFAT通路 First: Calcineurin/NFAT pathway
第二条
钙/钙调蛋白依赖性激酶II/组蛋白去乙酰化酶/MEF2通路 Second: CaMKII/HDAC/MEF2 pathway

二、Ca²⁺依赖性转录通路在心脏发育中的作用 Participation in normal heart development

1
这两条通路参与正常心脏的发育和功能调节。 These two pathways are involved in normal heart development and functional regulation.

三、病理状态下的异常调控 Alteration in pathologies

1
在心脏肥大和心力衰竭等疾病中，这些通路会被异常激活或抑制，导致病理性改变。 In pathological conditions like hypertrophy and heart failure, these pathways become abnormally activated or suppressed, resulting in pathological changes.

钙调磷酸酶/NFAT通路Calcineurin/NFAT pathway

一、通路基本机制 Basic mechanism of the pathway

1
有激动剂作用时，Ca²⁺与钙调蛋白（CaM）结合，激活钙调磷酸酶（Calcineurin）。 When agonists act, Ca²⁺ binds with calmodulin (CaM) to activate calcineurin.
2
钙调磷酸酶受CsA和FK506抑制，其下游作用是去磷酸化NFATc4。 Calcineurin is inhibited by CsA and FK506, and its downstream effect is to dephosphorylate NFATc4.
3
被去磷酸化的NFAT进入细胞核，激活GATA-4，促进肥大相关基因表达。 Dephosphorylated NFAT translocates into the nucleus, activates GATA-4, and promotes hypertrophic gene expression.
4
若通路异常激活，可引起肌节功能障碍和心肌肥大。 If this pathway is abnormally activated, it can cause sarcomeric dysfunction and cardiac hypertrophy.

二、NFAT的去磷酸化及核转位 Dephosphorylation and nuclear translocation of NFAT

1
NFAT是活化T细胞核因子，在心脏有两种亚型。 NFAT is the nuclear factor of activated T cells, with two cardiac isoforms.
2
钙调磷酸酶去磷酸化NFAT后，NFAT从胞质转位到胞核。 Calcineurin dephosphorylates NFAT, which then translocates from cytoplasm to nucleus.
3
实验表明，使用钙调磷酸酶抑制剂（如CsA），NFAT核/胞质比值下降，NFAT无法高效进入细胞核。 Experiments show that with calcineurin inhibitor (like CsA), the nuclear/cytoplasmic ratio of NFAT decreases and NFAT cannot efficiently enter the nucleus.
4
通常以GFP标记NFAT亚型并进行细胞成像，统计不同处理组的NFAT核定位情况。 Normally, GFP-labeled NFAT isoforms are imaged in cells, and nuclear localization is quantified in different treatment groups.
5
畸形转位与病理心肌肥大密切相关。 Abnormal translocation is closely associated with pathological cardiac hypertrophy.

三、Epac调控Ca²⁺/钙调磷酸酶/NFAT转录通路 Regulation of Ca²⁺/Calcineurin/NFAT transcription pathway by Epac

1
刺激Epac通路可增高胞质Ca²⁺释放，进而激活钙调磷酸酶和NFAT。 Activation of the Epac pathway increases cytosolic Ca²⁺ release, subsequently activating calcineurin and NFAT.
2
NFAT活性常以萤光素酶等报告基因检测，结果显示Epac激活组有更高的NFAT活性。 NFAT activity is often detected using luciferase reporter assays, showing higher activity in Epac-activated groups.
3
使用钙调磷酸酶抑制肽（如VIVIT），可显著降低NFAT活化和相关基因表达。 Using calcineurin inhibitory peptides (such as VIVIT) significantly reduces NFAT activation and related gene expression.
4
该通路的激活促进心肌细胞肥大，为心脏病理性重塑的重要机制。 Activation of this pathway promotes cardiomyocyte hypertrophy, which is a key mechanism in pathological cardiac remodeling.
5
实验应用原代新生鼠心肌细胞，监测Ca²⁺动态及心肌结构变化，结果与上述通路一致。 Experiments in neonatal cardiomyocytes monitor Ca²⁺ dynamics and structural changes, which match the above pathway effects.

四、课堂重点与补充 Key points and teacher's supplement

1
钙调磷酸酶/NFAT通路是心肌肥大和重塑的核心信号枢纽。 Calcineurin/NFAT pathway is a central signaling hub in cardiac hypertrophy and remodeling.
2
其活性既受Ca²⁺调控，也可被药物（CsA, FK506, VIVIT等）有效抑制。 Its activity is regulated by Ca²⁺ and can be effectively suppressed by drugs such as CsA, FK506, and VIVIT.
3
在疾病和应激状态下，该通路过度激活导致心室结构和功能异常。 Under disease or stress, overactivation of this pathway leads to ventricular structural and functional abnormalities.
4
通路终末靶点包括GATA-4等转录因子，调控肥厚相关基因。 Downstream targets include transcription factors like GATA-4, regulating hypertrophy-associated genes.
教师补充
NFAT核转位实验需严格对照、定量，结果解读应结合心脏功能指标。 Teacher's supplement: NFAT nuclear import assays require strict controls and quantification, with careful results interpretation in the context of cardiac function.

T-1通过IP3R和CaMKII通路调控HDAC核输出及MEF2转录因子ET-1 Controls HDAC Nuclear Export via IP3R and CaMKII Pathways, and MEF2 Transcription Factor Activation

一、ET-1激活相关Ca²⁺依赖信号通路和分子ET-1 Activates Key Ca²⁺-Dependent Signaling Pathways and Molecules

1
神经激素（包括ET-1等）通过受体-效应器（GPCR-Gq/PLC-IP3）激活胞内钙信号，调控心肌细胞功能和基因表达。 Neurohormones, including ET-1, activate intracellular calcium signaling via receptor-effector systems (GPCR-Gq/PLC-IP3) to regulate cardiomyocyte function and gene expression.
2
ET-1作用可升高胞质和核内Ca²⁺，上调重要关键信号分子（如CaMKII、CaN、HDAC、MEF2）并参与转录调节。 ET-1 elevates cytosolic and nuclear Ca²⁺, upregulates key molecules (such as CaMKII, CaN, HDAC, MEF2), and participates in transcriptional regulation.
3
典型下游通路包括CaMKII/HDAC/MEF2通路和Calcineurin/NFAT通路。 Classic downstream pathways include CaMKII/HDAC/MEF2 and Calcineurin/NFAT pathways.

二、ET-1诱导的核钙瞬变及功能 ET-1-Induced Nuclear Ca²⁺ Transient and Functional Consequences

1
实验结果显示，ET-1可增强心肌细胞核内Ca²⁺信号幅度，同时延迟达峰时间。 Experimental results show that ET-1 increases nuclear Ca²⁺ signal amplitude in cardiomyocytes and delays the time to peak.
2
数据对比表明，10 nM ET-1能显著升高核[Ca²⁺]，而对胞质[Ca²⁺]影响相对较小。 Data comparison reveals that 10 nM ET-1 significantly increases nuclear [Ca²⁺], while having less effect on cytosolic [Ca²⁺].
3
这种核Ca²⁺信号的增强为分子转录调控提供动力，尤其影响HDAC、MEF2等关键蛋白。 This enhancement of nuclear Ca²⁺ provides the driving force for molecular transcriptional regulation, especially affecting key proteins like HDAC and MEF2.

三、ET-1通过IP3R激活HDAC核输出 ET-1 Triggers HDAC Nuclear Export via IP3R

1
ET-1刺激下，HDAC由核内转位至胞质，核内HDAC含量逐步降低。 Upon ET-1 stimulation, HDAC translocates from the nucleus to the cytoplasm, with nuclear HDAC levels gradually decreasing.
2
该过程依赖于核膜IP3受体（IP3R），去除或敲除IP3R会显著阻断这一核输出过程。 This process depends on the nuclear membrane IP3 receptor (IP3R); removal or knockout of IP3R severely blocks this nuclear export.
3
实验采用HDAC-GFP标记，以及核/胞质（F_Nuc/F_Cyto）比值变化定量输出。 Experiments use HDAC-GFP tagging and quantify the nuclear/cytoplasmic (F_Nuc/F_Cyto) ratio changes.
4
关键实验表明，野生型IP3R存在下，ET-1能促进HDAC持续性从核输出。对照组或IP3R敲除组效应消失。 Key experiments show that in wild-type IP3R, ET-1 induces sustained HDAC export from the nucleus. This effect disappears in controls or IP3R knockout groups.

四、CaMKII依赖性HDAC核输出 CaMKII-Dependent HDAC Nuclear Export

1
ET-1刺激增加CaMKII磷酸化水平（P-CaMKII），促进HDAC由核内转移至胞质。 ET-1 stimulation increases CaMKII phosphorylation (P-CaMKII), promoting translocation of HDAC from the nucleus to the cytoplasm.
2
阻断Ca²⁺信号（如用2-APB或Adenophosin处理），上述CaMKII活性及HDAC输出均被抑制。 Inhibition of Ca²⁺ signaling (e.g., using 2-APB or Adenophosin) suppresses CaMKII activity and HDAC export.
3
CaMKII-GFP实验验证ET-1特异性促进CaMKII在核外定位增强。 CaMKII-GFP experiments confirm that ET-1 specifically increases extranuclear localization of CaMKII.
4
总之，CaMKII为HDAC核输出的关键分子枢纽，提升下游转录活性。 In summary, CaMKII is a key molecular hub for HDAC nuclear export, enhancing downstream transcriptional activity.

五、MEF2转录因子及其调控 MEF2 Transcription Factor and Its Regulation

1
MEF2为肌细胞特异性基因转录因子，存在4种亚型。 MEF2 is a transcription factor for muscle-specific genes, with four isoforms.
2
MEF2对Ca²⁺调节信号（CaMKII、CaN等）高度敏感。 MEF2 is highly responsive to Ca²⁺-regulated signaling (CaMKII, CaN, etc.).
3
实验表明，激活型CaMKIIδ可以显著增强MEF2介导的下游基因表达（萤光素酶报告基因检测）。 Experiments show that activated CaMKIIδ can significantly enhance MEF2-mediated downstream gene expression (luciferase-reporter assay).
4
使用显性负突变型CaMKII或共转染HDAC4则可显著削弱MEF2激活效应，说明HDAC/MEF2调控完全依赖其核内定位。 Using dominant-negative CaMKII or co-transfecting HDAC4 markedly attenuates MEF2 activation, indicating that HDAC/MEF2 regulation fully depends on their nuclear localization.

六、总结与课堂重点 Summary and Key Lecture Points

1
ET-1参与调控心肌细胞核Ca²⁺及下游转录调控，驱动心脏肥大等适应或病理性反应。 ET-1 participates in regulating nuclear Ca²⁺ and downstream transcription in cardiomyocytes, driving adaptation or pathological responses such as cardiac hypertrophy.
2
HDAC的核-胞质转运是Ca²⁺信号主导的可逆调控环节，与MEF2转录因子的活性紧密相关。 Nuclear-cytoplasmic shuttling of HDAC is a Ca²⁺-dependent, reversible regulatory step, closely linked to the activity of MEF2 transcription factors.
3
CaMKII和IP3R为通路中的分子枢纽，其活性决定信号转导效率与终末基因调控，涉及肥厚与心衰的表型塑造。 CaMKII and IP3R are molecular hubs in these pathways; their activity determines signal transduction efficiency and downstream gene regulation, which shape hypertrophy and heart failure phenotypes.

神经激素对ETC的调控Neurohormonal regulation of ETC & Epac对核Ca²⁺信号及转录因子的影响Epac effects on nuclear Ca²⁺ signaling and transcription factors

一、神经激素对ETC的调控机制 Mechanisms of Neurohormonal Regulation on ETC

1
神经激素（如ET-1等）通过激活GPCR-Gq/PLC-IP₃信号级联，增加胞内Ca²⁺释放，影响心肌细胞功能和基因表达。 Neurohormones (e.g., ET-1) activate the GPCR-Gq/PLC-IP₃ signaling cascade, increasing intracellular Ca²⁺ release and affecting cardiomyocyte function and gene expression.
2
上游信号通过IP₃R、CaMKII、CaN（钙调神经磷酸酶）等效应蛋白调节核内Ca²⁺水平和转录。 Upstream signals regulate nuclear Ca²⁺ and transcription via effectors such as IP₃R, CaMKII, and calcineurin.
3
关键转录因子MEF2、GATA和NFAT在Ca²⁺依赖信号调节下被活化，驱动心肌肥大和重塑相关基因表达。 Key transcription factors MEF2, GATA, and NFAT are activated under Ca²⁺-dependent signaling, driving gene expression associated with hypertrophy and remodeling.
4
HDAC（组蛋白去乙酰化酶）可被磷酸化并发生核外转位，解除对转录因子的抑制。 HDAC (histone deacetylase) can be phosphorylated and exported out of the nucleus, releasing inhibition on transcription factors.

二、Epac激活后调节核内Ca²⁺释放 Epac Activation and Nuclear Ca²⁺ Release

1
Epac激活剂（如8-pCPT）可显著增强心肌细胞核内Ca²⁺释放（包括基线、峰值及平均水平升高）。 Epac activators (e.g., 8-pCPT) significantly increase nuclear Ca²⁺ release in cardiomyocytes (raising baseline, peak, and mean Ca²⁺ levels).
2
这种增强依赖于CaMKII和IP₃信号通路，分别可用KN93（CaMKII抑制剂）和2-APB（IP₃R抑制剂）阻断。 This enhancement depends on CaMKII and IP₃ signaling pathways, which can be blocked by KN93 (a CaMKII inhibitor) and 2-APB (an IP₃R inhibitor).
3
实验数据显示，无论是CaMKII抑制还是IP₃R抑制，Epac对核Ca²⁺的促进效应均消失，说明两者均为必要分子。 Experimental data show that with CaMKII or IP₃R inhibition, the Epac-induced nuclear Ca²⁺ increase disappears, indicating both are essential for this process.

三、Epac促进HDAC转位及MEF2激活 Epac Promotes HDAC Translocation and MEF2 Activation

1
Epac激活下，HDAC由核内转位到胞质，核/胞比降低，从而解除对MEF2等转录因子的抑制。 Upon Epac activation, HDAC translocates from the nucleus to the cytoplasm, reducing the nuclear/cytoplasmic ratio, thus lifting repression on transcription factors like MEF2.
2
HDAC外转导致MEF2转录活性显著上升，实验中采用荧光素酶报告基因检测，Epac组MEF2活性为对照组300%以上。 HDAC export results in a marked increase of MEF2 activity (as shown by luciferase reporter assays, Epac group >300% of control).
3
HDAC转位与CaMKII和IP₃信号密切相关，用KN93或2-APB处理均可阻断HDAC外转。 HDAC translocation is dependent on CaMKII and IP₃ signaling; treatment with KN93 or 2-APB blocks HDAC export.

四、ETC核心意义及Ca²⁺信号功能 Key Points of ETC and Functional Role of Ca²⁺ Signaling

1
Ca²⁺是病理性ECC和ETC过程中的关键信号分子。 Ca²⁺ is a key signal molecule in both pathological ECC and ETC.
2
ETC过程调控促心肌肥厚的信号转导及相关基因表达。 ETC regulates pro-hypertrophic signal transduction pathways and associated gene expression.
教师强调
这类信号调节不仅影响心肌收缩，还能通过Ca²⁺依赖性转录决定细胞的长期基因表达和适应性反应。 Teacher emphasized: This signal regulation not only affects cardiac contraction, but also determines long-term gene expression and adaptation via Ca²⁺-dependent transcription.

兴奋-收缩偶联的定义 Definition of Excitation-Contraction Coupling

1
兴奋-收缩偶联的概念 Excitation-Contraction Coupling Concept

兴奋-收缩偶联是指电信号（去极化）通过细胞内 Ca2+ 信号的方式转化为机械收缩力的过程。

Excitation-contraction coupling is the process allowing the conversion of an electrical signal (depolarization) into a mechanical force through intracellular Ca2+ signaling.

2
电信号和机械收缩 Electrical Signal and Mechanical Contraction

电信号（通常为去极化）首先在心肌细胞传导。

The electrical signal (usually depolarization) is first conducted in cardiomyocytes.

随后，细胞内 Ca2+ 浓度上升，触发心肌细胞的机械收缩。

Subsequently, intracellular Ca2+ concentration increases, triggering mechanical contraction of cardiomyocytes.

心肌细胞的特性 Cardiomyocytes Properties

1
心肌细胞的尺寸 Dimensions of Cardiomyocytes

长度约为25微米，最长可达100微米，宽度小于20微米。

Length is around 25 μm, can be up to 100 μm, width less than 20 μm.

2
有横纹结构 Striated Structure

心肌细胞中可见明显的横纹。

Cardiomyocytes show distinct striations.

3
单个中央细胞核 Mono/Central Nucleus

多为单核细胞，最多可有两个细胞核，且位于细胞中央。

Mainly mononuclear, up to 2 nuclei, located central.

4
可兴奋性 Excitable

能对电刺激产生响应。

Capable of responding to electrical stimulation.

5
可收缩性 Contractile

具有收缩功能。

Have contractile function.

6
可传导性 Conductive

能传导电信号。

Able to conduct electrical signals.

心肌细胞结构 Cardiomyocytes Structure

1
主要结构组成 Main Structural Components

肌膜（Sarcolemma）包裹整个细胞，厚约1.8微米。

Sarcolemma surrounds the whole cell, around 1.8 μm thick.

横管系统（T-tubule）协助电信号向细胞内部传递。

T-tubule system helps transmit electrical signals inside the cell.

肌浆网（Sarcoplasmic reticulum）储存并释放 Ca2+。

Sarcoplasmic reticulum stores and releases Ca2+.

细胞核（Nucleus）多数位于中央。

Nucleus is mostly central.

线粒体（Mitochondria）为细胞提供能量，细胞能量的80%由此提供。

Mitochondria provide energy for the cell, contributing 80% of cellular energy.

肌原纤维（Myofibrils/myofilaments）负责收缩。

Myofibrils (myofilaments) are responsible for contraction.

2
剖面观察 Transverse Section

用特定染色或电压加载可观察横切面的细胞结构。

Loading with specific dyes or voltage highlights the transverse section for structure visualization.

可看到肌节（sarcomere）的排列与组织生长的深度。

Arrangement of sarcomeres and depth of tissue growth can be visualized.

肌原纤维和亚结构 The Myofilaments and Substructures

1
基本结构 Basic Structure

肌节（Sarcomere）是心肌收缩的基本单位。

Sarcomere is the basic contractile unit of the heart.

I带（I Band）：为未与肌球蛋白重叠的肌动蛋白区域。

I Band
Actin region not overlapping with myosin.

A带（A Band）：为完整的肌球蛋白区域。

A Band
Full myosin filament region.

H区（H Zone）：为未与肌动蛋白重叠的肌球蛋白区域。

H Zone
Myosin region not overlapping with actin.

M线（M Line）：为连接细胞骨架的区域。

M Line
Region for cytoskeleton connection.
2
肌动蛋白与肌球蛋白的相互作用 Interaction between Actin and Myosin

肌节收缩的核心是肌动蛋白与肌球蛋白的滑动。

Central to sarcomere contraction is the sliding of actin over myosin.

在静止状态下，结合位点被阻断，肌动蛋白与肌球蛋白不能结合。

At rest, binding sites are blocked; actin and myosin cannot bind.

Ca2+ 进入细胞后与调节蛋白结合，使结合位点暴露，引发肌动蛋白与肌球蛋白结合，产生收缩。

After Ca2+ enters the cell and binds to regulatory proteins, binding sites are exposed, allowing actin-myosin interaction and resulting in contraction.

老师补充内容补充 Additional Points from Lecture

1
能量供应与细胞器 Energy Supply and Organelles

线粒体为心肌细胞提供约80%的能量，是能量代谢的关键细胞器。

Mitochondria provide about 80% of cardiomyocyte energy and are key organelles for metabolism.

2
横管和细胞内结构 The Role of T-tubule and Intracellular Structures

横管不仅参与信号传导，还便于观察肌节变化和组织生长。

T-tubules participate in signal conduction and also allow observation of sarcomere changes and tissue growth.

3
收缩调控中的带区与线区 Zones and Lines in Contraction Regulation

肌节的不同区域（I带、A带、H区、M线）通过不同蛋白的分布与重叠，协同完成收缩调控。

Different regions of the sarcomere (I band, A band, H zone, M line) achieve contraction regulation through distribution and overlap of specific proteins.

4
钙信号的放大与释放 Amplification and Release of Calcium Signals

初步去极化带来的 Ca2+ 浓度不足以引起广泛收缩，细胞通过钙信号放大机制（如钙诱发钙释放）增强信号。

Initial depolarization-induced Ca2+ concentration is insufficient for extensive contraction. The cell amplifies it via calcium-induced calcium release mechanisms.

钙离子进入细胞后与雷诺定受体结合，引起胞内钙库（如肌浆网）的释放，使细胞内 Ca2+ 浓度骤升，实现实际收缩。

Calcium enters the cell and binds ryanodine receptors, triggering sarcoplasmic reticulum calcium release, increasing intracellular Ca2+ concentration rapidlу and leading to contraction.

5
收缩结束后钙的去除 Removal of Calcium Following Contraction

大部分 Ca2+ 被再次摄回肌浆网，细胞内只保留少量残余钙。

Most intracellular Ca2+ is re-sequestered into the sarcoplasmic reticulum, leaving only a small amount of residual calcium in the cell.

总结 Summary

心肌细胞结构与功能密切相关，电信号通过 Ca2+ 信号被高效放大并转化为机械收缩，完成心脏正常的泵血活动。

The structure and function of cardiomyocytes are closely related. Electrical signals are efficiently amplified via Ca2+ signaling and converted to mechanical contraction to achieve normal cardiac pumping.

心脏兴奋-收缩偶联Cardiac Excitation Contraction Coupling

1
膜去极化 Depolarisation (PA) Sarcolemma

首先，心肌细胞膜发生去极化，形成动作电位。

First, the cardiomyocyte membrane undergoes depolarisation, generating an action potential.

2
钙离子内流 Ca2+ influx TTubules ICa,L

动作电位沿横管（T-tubule）传导，激活L型钙通道，导致少量Ca2+进入细胞。

The action potential travels along T-tubules, activates L-type Ca2+ channels, and causes a small influx of Ca2+ into the cell.

3
钙诱导钙释放Ca2+-induced Ca2+ release: SR RyR

外来Ca2+触发肌浆网（SR）上的雷诺受体（RyR）开放，引起大量Ca2+从SR释放到胞质。

The incoming Ca2+ triggers the opening of ryanodine receptors (RyR) on the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR), releasing large amounts of Ca2+ into the cytosol.

收缩
肌丝Contraction: Myofilaments

增加的Ca2+与肌丝上的调节蛋白结合，启动收缩作用——肌动蛋白与肌球蛋白滑动。

The increased Ca2+ binds with regulatory proteins on myofilaments, initiating contraction as actin and myosin slide past each other.

舒张
SERCA和NCX Relaxation: SERCA NCX

收缩结束后，绝大部分Ca2+被SERCA泵回收到SR，小部分被NCX（钠钙交换体）排出胞外，实现心肌舒张。

After contraction, most Ca2+ is taken back into the SR by the SERCA pump, and a smaller portion is extruded from the cell by the sodium-calcium exchanger (NCX), resulting in relaxation.

补充说明 Additional Explanation

进入心肌细胞的初始Ca2+量不足以引发充分收缩，细胞通过“钙诱导钙释放”机制放大信号。

The initial Ca2+ entering cardiomyocytes is insufficient for full contraction, so the cell amplifies the signal via the "calcium-induced calcium release" mechanism.

大部分Ca2+在收缩结束后被SERCA泵回到肌浆网，只有少量剩余Ca2+被NCX排出细胞外。

Most Ca2+ returns to the sarcoplasmic reticulum via the SERCA pump after contraction, with only a small amount removed by NCX to the extracellular space.

能量供应主要依赖线粒体，约80%的能量消耗用于心肌收缩。

The energy supply relies mainly on mitochondria, and about 80% of energy is consumed for cardiomyocyte contraction.

心室心肌细胞钙通量的物种差异 Species differences in ventricular cardiomyocytes Ca2+ fluxes

1
兔与鼠心室肌细胞对比 Comparison between Rabbit and Rodent Ventricular Cardiomyocytes

在兔心肌细胞中，SR回收Ca2+比例为70%，NCX为28%，其他机制2%。

In rabbits, 70% of Ca2+ is reuptaken by SR, 28% by NCX, and 2% by other mechanisms.

在鼠心肌细胞中，SR回收Ca2+比例高达92%，NCX只有7%，其他机制1%。

In rodents, up to 92% of Ca2+ is reuptaken by SR, 7% by NCX, and only 1% by other mechanisms.

2
SERCA泵在小鼠中的作用 The Role of SERCA in Rodents

鼠类SERCA泵表达更高，因此舒张期大多依赖SERCA完成Ca2+回收，极大提高了心肌舒张效率。

Higher SERCA expression in rodents means relaxation mainly depends on SERCA-mediated Ca2+ uptake, greatly enhancing cardiomyocyte relaxation efficiency.

3
实验结论 Experimental Conclusion

在鼠类，SERCA为主导的机制导致NCX贡献较低。

In rodents, the SERCA-dominated mechanism significantly reduces the contribution of NCX.

ECC的研究方法
共聚焦显微镜 Method of Study of ECC: Confocal Microscopy
1
钙荧光染料 Ca2+ Dye (Fluo-4)

应用Ca2+荧光染料Fluo-4可检测胞内Ca2+动态。

The Ca2+ dye Fluo-4 is used to detect intracellular Ca2+ dynamics.

2
收缩期与舒张期Ca2+信号 Systole and Diastole Ca2+ Signals

舒张期，局部RyR簇释放的Ca2+信号表现为Ca2+火花。

In diastole, local RyR cluster releases appear as Ca2+ sparks.

收缩期，所有RyR同步开放，形成全细胞的Ca2+瞬变。

During systole, all RyRs open synchronously, producing a cell-wide Ca2+ transient.

3
图像采集与分析 Imaging and Analysis

通过激光扫描细胞，并记录时间-空间分布，观测Ca2+动态变化。

A laser scans the cell, recording spatial and temporal Ca2+ signal changes.

可在信号谱线中分析Ca2+信号幅值和衰减时间，反映SERCA/NCX的功能。

The amplitude and decay time of Ca2+ signals can be analyzed, reflecting the function of SERCA/NCX.

总结 Summary

心脏兴奋-收缩偶联依赖于Ca2+信号的放大与精确回收，不同动物回收机制比例存在显著差异。

Cardiac excitation-contraction coupling depends on the amplification and precise reuptake of Ca2+ signals, with significant differences in reuptake mechanisms across species.

ECC的研究方法
膜片钳-共聚焦显微镜Method of study of ECC: Patch-clamp coupled to confocal microscopy
1
实验原理及步骤 Experimental Principle and Steps

实验中，通过膜片钳记录心肌细胞的电流并同步加加载Ca2+荧光染料。

In the experiment, patch-clamp is used to record the current of cardiomyocytes while simultaneously loading Ca2+ fluorescent dye.

利用共聚焦显微镜，可以追踪细胞内Ca2+浓度变化和空间分布。

Confocal microscopy is used to track the changes and spatial distribution of intracellular Ca2+ concentration.

2
兴奋-收缩偶联信号的三个主要记录信号 Three Key Signals in Excitation-Contraction Coupling
可同步获得三种信号
L型钙通道电流（IcaL）、Ca2+瞬变（Ca2+ transient）、以及细胞短缩（shortening）。

Three signals can be recorded simultaneously: L-type Ca2+ current (IcaL), Ca2+ transient, and cell shortening.

通过设定膜电位（如-45mV到0mV，再到-80mV），激活L型钙通道，记录IcaL。

By setting membrane potentials (e.g. from -45mV to 0mV, then to -80mV), L-type Ca2+ channels are activated and IcaL is recorded.

Ca2+荧光信号显示Ca2+瞬变的空间-时间分布，体现整个细胞的Ca2+释放情况。

The Ca2+ fluorescence signal displays the spatiotemporal distribution of Ca2+ transients, reflecting the cell-wide Ca2+ release.

细胞短缩反映心肌细胞收缩幅度，与Ca2+信号峰值对应。

Cell shortening reflects the amplitude of cardiomyocyte contraction and corresponds with the peak of the Ca2+ signal.

3
信号分析的关键要素 Key Factors for Signal Analysis

IcaL反映跨膜钙离子流入，是ECC启动的直接指标。

IcaL reflects transmembrane calcium influx and is the direct indicator for ECC initiation.

Ca2+瞬变的幅度（F/F0）表示细胞内Ca2+浓度的最高上升幅度。

The amplitude of Ca2+ transient (F/F0) indicates the maximal increase in intracellular Ca2+ concentration.

Ca2+信号的衰减时间反映细胞除钙效率，可用于评估SERCA及NCX功能。

The decay time of the Ca2+ signal indicates the cellular Ca2+ removal efficiency and can be used to evaluate SERCA and NCX function.

细胞短缩的同步性和幅度结合Ca2+信号共同揭示收缩与Ca2+动态之间的耦合延迟。

The synchrony and amplitude of cell shortening, together with Ca2+ signals, reveal the lag between contraction and Ca2+ dynamics.

4
教师补充与要点回顾 Teacher’s Supplements and Key Points

Ca2+瞬变是指细胞内Ca2+浓度的暂时升高（上升-峰值-下降），这是肌丝收缩前的信号。

Ca2+ transient refers to the temporary increase in intracellular Ca2+ concentration (rise-peak-fall), which precedes myofilament contraction.

Ca2+扩散需一定时间，因此Ca2+峰值早于细胞实际短缩峰值。

Diffusion of Ca2+ takes time, so the Ca2+ peak occurs before the actual cell shortening peak.

信号幅值可以用来估算进入细胞的Ca2+总量，衰减时间反映清除机制的效率。

Signal amplitude estimates the total Ca2+ entry into the cell, and decay time reflects the efficiency of removal mechanisms.

若SERCA或NCX功能下降，Ca2+信号消退缓慢，提示心肌舒张障碍。

If SERCA or NCX function decreases, the Ca2+ signal decays more slowly, indicating impaired cardiac relaxation.

心脏ECC的主要调控因子Key regulators of the cardiac ECC

1
细胞结构 Cellular Structure

横管（T-Tubules）是细胞膜内陷，有助于信号传递与Ca2+均匀分布。

T-tubules are invaginations of the cell membrane, aiding in signal transmission and even Ca2+ distribution.

二联体（Dyads, TT-SR）是横管和肌浆网终池的接触区，是Ca2+信号放大的核心部位。

Dyads (TT-SR) are the contact zones between T-tubules and the sarcoplasmic reticulum terminal cisternae, central for Ca2+ signal amplification.

肌丝（Myofilaments）是力量产生的基础结构。

Myofilaments are the basic structure for force generation.

2
Ca2+诱导的Ca2+释放（CICR）Ca2+-induced Ca2+ release (CICR)

L型Ca2+通道（IcaL）负责去极化后外源性Ca2+流入，是Ca2+信号的启动器。

L-type Ca2+ channels (IcaL) are responsible for Ca2+ influx after depolarization, acting as initiators of the Ca2+ signal.

雷诺受体（RyR）位于肌浆网，通过Ca2+诱导开放，使胞内Ca2+急剧增加，实现信号放大。

Ryanodine receptors (RyR) are located in the sarcoplasmic reticulum and open in response to Ca2+, causing a dramatic increase in intracellular Ca2+ to amplify the signal.

3
肌浆/内质网Ca2+-ATP酶（SERCA）SR/ER Ca2+-ATPase (SERCA)

SERCA泵将胞内Ca2+主动转运回收进SR，主导舒张期除钙，是心肌舒张的关键环节。

SERCA pumps actively reuptake cytosolic Ca2+ back into the sarcoplasmic reticulum, dominating Ca2+ removal during relaxation and being crucial for cardiac relaxation.

4
钠钙交换体（NCX）Na+/Ca2+ Exchanger (NCX)

NCX主要将胞内部分Ca2+排出胞外，辅助维持细胞内Ca2+动态平衡。

NCX primarily extrudes a portion of intracellular Ca2+ to the extracellular space, helping maintain cellular Ca2+ homeostasis.

总结 Summary

膜片钳-共聚焦显微镜方法可系统同步测量电流、Ca2+动力学及力学收缩，是研究心脏ECC机制的核心技术。

Patch-clamp coupled with confocal microscopy allows systematic and synchronous measurement of electrical current, Ca2+ dynamics, and mechanical contraction, making it a core technology in studying cardiac ECC mechanisms.

ECC的正常可靠依赖细胞结构特化、钙信号放大与高效清除机制，每一环节异常都可能导致心脏功能障碍。

Proper ECC depends on specialized cellular structures, Ca2+ signal amplification, and efficient clearance mechanisms; any abnormalities can lead to cardiac dysfunction.

横管与二联体结构及心脏兴奋-收缩耦联的主要调控因子与机制Transverse Tubules, Dyadic Cleft Structure and Key Regulators/Mechanisms of Cardiac ECC

1
横管结构及网络 Transverse Tubule (TT) Structure and Network

横管（T-Tubules）是心肌细胞膜向胞质内的深度内陷，形成有规律的三维网状结构。

T-tubules are deep invaginations of the cardiomyocyte membrane forming a regular 3D network inside the cytoplasm.

通过膜标记染料（如di-8 ANEPPS），可显示TT沿细胞轴呈周期性分布。

Using membrane dyes (like di-8 ANEPPS), TT appear periodically along the cell longitudinal axis.

横截面观察类似切面面包，TT网络封装整个细胞，有助于信号同步传导。

In cross-section (like a sliced bread analogy), the TT network surrounds the cell, aiding synchronous signal conduction.

TT网络保证胞外信号快速深度到达细胞内部，维持Ca2+均匀分布。

The TT network ensures that external signals reach deep inside rapidly, maintaining uniform Ca2+ distribution.

2
二联体裂隙结构 Dyadic Cleft Structure

二联体（Dyads）指一个横管与一个肌浆网终池的紧密接触区域。

Dyads refer to the close-contact zone between a T-tubule and a terminal cistern of the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR).

二联体内含有多个关键蛋白：

Dyads contain several key proteins:

L型Ca2+通道（LTCC）位于T管膜上，负责触发外源性Ca2+内流。

L-type Ca2+ channels (LTCC) on the T-tubule membrane trigger external Ca2+ influx.

雷诺受体（RyR）位于SR膜上，对面排列，感受Ca2+信号后大规模释放SR内Ca2+。

Ryanodine receptors (RyR) on the SR membrane face LTCC, sensing Ca2+ and releasing large amounts of SR Ca2+.

还存在Na+通道、钠钙交换体（NCX）、钠钾泵（NKA）等功能性蛋白。

Additional proteins like Na+ channels, Na+/Ca2+ exchangers (NCX), and Na+/K+ ATPase (NKA) are present.

二联体结构保证Ca2+信号跨膜放大和高效传递。

The dyadic arrangement ensures signal amplification and effective Ca2+ transduction between membranes.

3
心脏ECC的主要调控因子 Key Regulators of Cardiac ECC
细胞结构
TT网、肌浆网（SR）、肌丝（Myofilaments）为力学基础与信号传递关键框架。

Cellular structures: TT network, sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR), and myofilaments provide structural and signaling framework.

Ca2+诱导的Ca2+释放（CICR）：

Ca2+-induced Ca2+ release (CICR):

L型Ca2+通道（ICa,L）激活RyR，产生放大效应。

L-type Ca2+ channel (ICa,L) activates RyR, resulting in signal amplification.

SERCA（SR/ER Ca2+-ATP酶）：主动回收胞内Ca2+，调控舒张过程。

SERCA (SR/ER Ca2+-ATPase): actively reuptakes Ca2+ into SR, regulating relaxation.

Na+/Ca2+交换体（NCX）：细胞膜上交换Na+和Ca2+，辅助胞内Ca2+平衡。

Na+/Ca2+ exchanger (NCX): exchanges Na+ and Ca2+ across the cell membrane, helping maintain Ca2+ balance.

4
Ca2+诱导的Ca2+释放机制 CICR Mechanism

外源性Ca2+通过L型Ca2+通道流入，引发SR内RyR大量释放Ca2+。

External Ca2+ influx via L-type Ca2+ channels triggers massive Ca2+ release from SR RyR.

此机制导致Ca2+信号迅速放大，产生收缩所需的胞内Ca2+峰。

This mechanism rapidly amplifies the Ca2+ signal, generating the cytosolic Ca2+ peak needed for contraction.

主要触发因子为L型Ca2+通道，释放关键为RyR和SR Ca2+储备。

Main triggers are the L-type Ca2+ channels, and release depends on RyR and SR Ca2+ stores.

CICR的调控
L型Ca2+通道 Regulating CICR: L-type Ca2+ Channel

L型Ca2+通道具有电压依赖特性，对膜电位变化高度敏感。

L-type Ca2+ channels are voltage-dependent, highly sensitive to changes in membrane potential.

通道结构由α1c亚基构成孔道，另有β、γ等调节亚基。

The pore is formed by the α1c subunit, with β and γ as modulatory subunits.

Cav1.2是心肌特有的同工型。

Cav1.2 is the cardiac-specific isoform.

通道可被二氢吡啶类药物（DHPR）特异性结合和调制。

The channel is specifically bound and modulated by dihydropyridine drugs (DHPR).

活化开口时Ca2+内流引发CICR，随后失活。

Upon activation, Ca2+ influx initiates CICR followed by inactivation.

6
重要讲课补充 Essential Lecture Additions

TT网络和二联体结构的完整性直接影响Ca2+信号同步，缺陷会导致心脏收缩异常。

The integrity of the TT network and dyads directly impacts Ca2+ signal synchrony; defects cause contractile dysfunction.

ECC核心步骤包括
信号传递、信号放大、收缩、除钙和舒张各环节。

Key ECC steps include signal transmission, amplification, contraction, calcium removal, and relaxation.

某些疾病或遗传变异可影响L型Ca2+通道、RyR、SERCA等蛋白功能，最终导致心脏疾病。

Certain diseases or mutations affect L-type Ca2+ channels, RyR, and SERCA, leading to heart diseases.

总结 Summary

心肌细胞TT与二联体结构及相关转运/通道蛋白，是兴奋-收缩偶联与Ca2+信号高效同步的基础。

Cardiomyocyte TT and dyadic structures, together with associated transporters/channels, underpin efficient and synchronous ECC and Ca2+ signaling.

CICR调控
L型Ca2+通道的激活与失活机制Regulation of CICR: Activation and Inactivation of L-Type Ca2+ Channel
1
L型Ca2+通道的激活 Activation of L-type Ca2+ Channel
L型Ca2+通道是心脏兴奋-收缩偶联　的关键触发器。
答案 CE
The L-type Ca2+ channel is the key trigger in cardiac excitation-contraction coupling 　.
答案 CE

当心肌细胞去极化至约-40 mV及以上时，L型Ca2+通道被激活。

When the cardiomyocyte is depolarized to around -40 mV or above, the L-type Ca2+ channel is activated.

电流-电压关系（Ica-V关系）显示，Ica在0到+10 mV附近达到峰值。

The current-voltage (Ica-V) relationship shows that Ica peaks around 0 to +10 mV.

Ca2+通道激活使Ca2+进入细胞，诱导肌浆网（SR）上的RyR2受体开放，放大胞内Ca2+信号，实现Ca2+诱导Ca2+释放（CICR）。

Activation of the Ca2+ channel leads to Ca2+ influx, which triggers opening of RyR2 receptors on the SR, amplifying intracellular Ca2+ and achieving calcium-induced calcium release (CICR).

2
膜片钳实验与荧光成像 Patch-clamp and Fluorescent Imaging

通过膜片钳与Ca2+指示剂联合，可以同时测量Ica和Ca2+瞬变。

Combined patch-clamp and Ca2+ indicator allows simultaneous measurement of Ica and Ca2+ transient.

当膜电位逐步增加时，Ca2+瞬变F/F0随之增强，在去极化至0到+10 mV附近最大，与Ica峰值一致。

As membrane potential increases, Ca2+ transient (F/F0) also increases, peaking near 0 to +10 mV, consistent with Ica.

右图显示不同电压下的电流和F/F0曲线：负电位下Ca2+流入与Ca2+信号较弱，电位升高后电流和Ca2+瞬变均增强。

The right panel shows current and F/F0 traces at different voltages: low at negative potentials, increasing as voltage rises.

3
L型Ca2+通道的失活机制 Inactivation of L-type Ca2+ Channel
L型Ca2+通道有双相失活
钙依赖性失活和电压依赖性失活。

The L-type Ca2+ channel shows biphasic inactivation: Ca2+-dependent and voltage-dependent inactivation.

钙依赖性失活主要由Ca2+内流以及RyR2介导的Ca2+释放所致，反应迅速，部分由钙调蛋白调控。

Ca2+-dependent inactivation is mainly due to Ca2+ influx and RyR2-mediated Ca2+ release; it is fast and partly regulated by calmodulin.

电压依赖性失活较慢，与细胞膜保持高电位有关。

Voltage-dependent inactivation is slower and related to sustained high membrane potential.

实验中，使用EGTA可以缓冲胞内Ca2+，分别获得钙流和钡流，进一步区分两种失活机制。

In experiments, EGTA buffers intracellular Ca2+, allowing distinction between Ca2+ current and Ba2+ current to differentiate the two inactivation mechanisms.

4
NCX在CICR调控中的作用 NCX in CICR Regulation

Na+/Ca2+交换体（NCX）参与Ca2+的清除和流入，调节胞内Ca2+稳态。

The Na+/Ca2+ exchanger (NCX) participates in Ca2+ extrusion and influx, regulating intracellular Ca2+ homeostasis.

正常情况下，NCX以3 Na+交换1 Ca2+，将Ca2+排出胞外。

Normally, NCX exchanges 3 Na+ for 1 Ca2+ to extrude Ca2+.

当膜电位为正或胞内Na+增高时，NCX可逆向工作，将Ca2+导入细胞，这在某些病理情况下显著。

When membrane potential is positive or intracellular Na+ is high, the NCX can operate in reverse mode, bringing Ca2+ into the cell, which is notable in some pathological states.

NCX的进出向判断取决于膜电位、胞内外Na+、Ca2+浓度。

The direction of NCX transport depends on membrane potential, intra/extracellular Na+ and Ca2+ concentrations.

高浓度胞内Ca2+促进Ca2+外流（I_Na/Ca内向）；高膜电位和高胞内Na+促进Ca2+外流（I_Na/Ca外向）。

High intracellular Ca2+ favors Ca2+ extrusion (inward I_Na/Ca); high membrane potential and high intracellular Na+ favor Ca2+ influx (outward I_Na/Ca).

5
CICR机制总结 Summary of CICR Mechanism
CICR核心机制
L型Ca2+通道/NCX介导触发，RyR2介导放大，SERCA调控SR Ca2+载量。

Core CICR mechanism: L-type Ca2+ channel/NCX trigger, RyR2 amplify, SERCA regulates SR Ca2+ load.

触发阶段电流和Ca2+信号幅度同步，失活机制确保信号不过度延长，维持细胞功能安全。

Trigger phase has synchronized current and Ca2+ amplitude, inactivation ensures signal does not prolong excessively, securing cellular function.

NCX既可促进Ca2+清除也可作为反向触发源，心脏病理条件下尤为重要。

NCX facilitates both Ca2+ clearance and, as a reverse trigger, is particularly important under cardiac pathological conditions.

6
教师课堂补充讲解 Teacher’s Key Additions

L型Ca2+通道电流是电压依赖的，通道主要由α1c亚基组成，β、γ亚基调节功能。

L-type Ca2+ channel current is voltage-dependent, mainly composed of the α1c subunit with β and γ modulating subunits.

不同通道的开关门阈值不同，在心肌细胞中Cav1.2为主。

Different channels have different thresholds; Cav1.2 is predominant in cardiomyocytes.

CICR需要L型通道带来的“触发型”Ca2+，这一量虽小，但至关重要，否则无法启动SR内RyR2的大量Ca2+释放。

CICR requires “trigger-type” Ca2+ from L-type channels, which although small in quantity is essential to activate RyR2-mediated Ca2+ release from the SR.

失活如异常，容易导致Ca2+信号失控，进而造成心律失常等病理后果。

Abnormal inactivation can cause Ca2+ signal misregulation, potentially resulting in arrhythmias and other cardiac pathologies.

总结 Summary

L型Ca2+通道的激活和钙依赖/电压依赖性失活共同保障CICR过程的精确与安全。

Activation and Ca2+-/voltage-dependent inactivation of L-type Ca2+ channels jointly secure the accuracy and safety of CICR.

NCX不仅是钙清除的主要动力，也是触发源之一，心脏调控灵活但易受损。

NCX is not only the main force for Ca2+ removal but also a trigger; cardiac regulation is flexible but susceptible to injury.

RyRs，大分子复合体RyRs, a macromolecular complex

心脏中的RyR类型 Heart
Type 2 RyRs

心脏中主要存在2型雷诺受体（RyR2）。

Type 2 ryanodine receptors (RyR2) are the primary type in the heart.

2
分子结构 Molecular Structure

RyR2是同源四聚体，由四个565 kDa的单体组成。

RyR2 is a homo-tetramer, made up of four monomers of 565 kDa each.

3
通道特性 Channel Properties

RyR2是跨膜通道，对生物碱雷诺定有敏感性。

RyR2 is a trans-membrane channel sensitive to the alkaloid ryanodine.

4
胞质侧的调节因子 Cytosolic Side Regulators
Ca2+
高浓度胞质钙可激活或调节RyR2开放。
Ca2+
High cytosolic calcium concentration can activate or modulate RyR2 opening.

FKBP12.6（Calstabin 2）：结合并稳定RyR2的关闭状态，预防异常Ca2+泄漏。

FKBP12.6 (Calstabin 2): Binds and stabilizes the closed state of RyR2, preventing abnormal Ca2+ leak.

钙调蛋白（Calmodulin）：感受Ca2+浓度变化，调节RyR2的活性。

Calmodulin
Senses Ca2+ concentration changes and modulates RyR2 activity.

磷酸化修饰，包括CaMKII、PKA：通过在特定位点添加磷酸，增强或抑制RyR2活性。

Phosphorylation, including CaMKII and PKA: Adds phosphate groups at specific sites to enhance or inhibit RyR2 activity.

磷酸酶（如PP1，PP2A）：去除磷酸基，恢复或逆转磷酸化状态。

Phosphatases (e.g., PP1, PP2A): Remove phosphate groups, restoring or reversing phosphorylation.

5
腔面侧的调节因子 Luminal Side Regulators
Ca2+
SR腔面Ca2+浓度也是RyR2敏感的信号。
Ca2+
SR luminal Ca2+ concentration also serves as a signal for RyR2 sensitivity.
Triadin
帮助RyR2感知腔面Ca2+，参与信号调节。
Triadin
Assists RyR2 in sensing luminal Ca2+ and participating in signaling.
Junctin
与RyR2和腔面蛋白协作，调节Ca2+释放。
Junctin
Works with RyR2 and luminal proteins to regulate Ca2+ release.

Calsequestrin（腔蛋白）：SR Ca2+缓冲蛋白，间接影响RyR2开放门槛。

Calsequestrin
SR Ca2+ buffer protein, indirectly affects RyR2 opening threshold.
6
教师强调和扩充要点 Teacher’s Key Additions and Elaboration

RyR2功能失调会导致心肌细胞异常钙泄漏，引发心律失常和心力衰竭等疾病。

Dysfunction of RyR2 can cause abnormal Ca2+ leak in cardiomyocytes, leading to arrhythmias and heart failure.

堆积在SR腔内的Ca2+以及调节蛋白决定了每次心肌收缩时的钙释放量。

The accumulation of Ca2+ in the SR lumen and the regulatory proteins determine the amount of Ca2+ released during each cardiac contraction.

通道调控高度依赖磷酸化状态变化，不同激酶或磷酸酶的参与形成动态调节网络。

Channel regulation is highly dependent on phosphorylation state, with various kinases and phosphatases forming a dynamic regulatory network.

FKBP12.6缺失会使RyR2更容易异常开放，成为许多心脏疾病的发病基础之一。

FKBP12.6 deletion makes RyR2 prone to abnormal opening, underlying mechanism in many cardiac diseases.

总结 Summary

RyR2是心脏中介导Ca2+释放的核心巨型跨膜复合体，其功能高度依赖多种胞质侧和腔面侧调节因子，包括Ca2+浓度、调控蛋白和磷酸化等状态。

RyR2 is the core macromolecular transmembrane complex mediating Ca2+ release in the heart, and its function highly depends on various cytosolic and luminal regulators such as Ca2+ concentration, regulatory proteins, and phosphorylation state.

**当然可以！以下是结合你课堂录音内容，整理的RyR与心肌兴奋-收缩偶联流程相关常考题型，中英逐句对照版：**

---

### **一、流程与机制类 Process & Mechanism**

1.**请简述心肌兴奋-收缩偶联　的基本流程。**
答案 CE
Please briefly describe the general process of cardiac excitation-contraction coupling 　.
答案 CE

- **去极化激活L型Ca²⁺通道，微量Ca²⁺流入胞内，诱发RyR开放，SR大量Ca²⁺释放，Ca²⁺与肌动蛋白结合引发收缩，SERCA与NCX清除胞质Ca²⁺导致舒张。**

Depolarization activates L-type Ca²⁺ channels, a small amount of Ca²⁺ enters the cell, triggering RyR to open and massive release of Ca²⁺ from the SR, Ca²⁺ binds to myofilaments to induce contraction, SERCA and NCX remove cytosolic Ca²⁺ to cause relaxation .

---

### **二、调控与反馈 Regulation & Feedback**

2
**请举例说明L型Ca²⁺通道与RyR之间的正反馈机制。**

Please give an example of the positive feedback between L-type Ca²⁺ channels and RyR.

- **L型Ca²⁺通道开放时，少量Ca²⁺流入触发RyR开放，导致SR内大量Ca²⁺释放，产生信号放大作用。**

When L-type Ca²⁺ channels open, a small Ca²⁺ influx triggers RyR opening, leading to massive SR Ca²⁺ release and signal amplification .

3
**简述调控RyR2开放/关闭的主要胞质侧与腔面调控因子，并描述其对Ca²⁺瞬变与收缩的影响。**

Briefly describe the main cytosolic and luminal regulators of RyR2 opening/closure and their impact on Ca²⁺ transients and contraction.

- **主要包括Ca²⁺本身、FKBP12.6、磷酸化、氧化还原修饰等，这些因子影响RyR2活性及心肌细胞的Ca²⁺释放效率与节律。**

These include Ca²⁺ itself, FKBP12.6, phosphorylation, and redox modifications, all affecting RyR2 activity and the efficiency/rhythm of cardiac Ca²⁺ release .

---

### **三、功能异常与推理 Dysfunction & Mechanistic Reasoning**

4
**如果FKBP12.6表达下调，对钙瞬变与心肌收缩力有何影响？并解释原因。**

If FKBP12.6 expression is downregulated, what happens to Ca²⁺ transients and cardiac contractility? Explain why.

- **FKBP12.6减少会导致RyR2稳定性下降，舒张期钙漏增加，容易诱发自发性瞬变和心律失常，并导致收缩力降低。**

Reduced FKBP12.6 destabilizes RyR2, increases diastolic Ca²⁺ leak, promotes spontaneous Ca²⁺ transients and arrhythmia, and decreases contractile strength .

5
**实验中若SERCA或NCX功能异常，Ca²⁺瞬变衰减曲线会有何变化？分子机制是什么？**
问If SERCA or NCX function is abnormal in experiments, how does the Ca²⁺ transient decay curve change? What is the molecular mechanism?
答

- **SERCA功能异常会使Ca²⁺清除变慢，瞬变衰减时间延长，反映SR钙泵效率降低；NCX异常时Ca²⁺外排减少，也影响钙瞬变衰减。**

SERCA dysfunction slows Ca²⁺ clearance, prolongs transient decay, indicating reduced SR Ca²⁺ pump efficiency; NCX abnormality reduces Ca²⁺ extrusion, also affecting Ca²⁺ transient decay .

---

### **四、比对分析 Analytical Comparison**

6
**请比较不同物种（如小鼠与兔）心肌细胞中SR和NCX在Ca²⁺清除中的作用比例，并分析其机制意义。**

Compare the proportions of SR and NCX involvement in Ca²⁺ removal in cardiomyocytes of different species (e.g., mouse vs. rabbit) and analyze their mechanistic significance.

- **小鼠约90%Ca²⁺由SR（SERCA）清除，兔约70%由SR，其余主要靠NCX，说明不同物种细胞对Ca²⁺代谢和节律调节的依赖性不同。**

In mice, ~90% of Ca²⁺ is removed by SR (SERCA), in rabbits about 70% by SR, with the rest mainly by NCX, revealing species differences in Ca²⁺ metabolism and rhythm regulation dependence .

---

### **五、实验数据与图谱解读 Experimental Data & Figure Interpretation**

7
**根据Fluo-4等荧光钙信号检测，怎么判断RyR2功能异常？要关注哪些指标？**
问Using Fluo-4 or other Ca²⁺ fluorescent signals, how do you judge RyR2 dysfunction? What indicators should you focus on?
答

- **可关注Ca²⁺瞬变幅度、衰减时间、diastolic spot数量和频率等指标来判断RyR2活性与异常释放。**

Look at Ca²⁺ transient amplitude, decay time, diastolic spot number and frequency to assess RyR2 activity and abnormal release .

### **六、典型互动例题 Typical Interactive Questions**

- **“如果FKBP12.6过表达，心肌收缩过程中的钙瞬变和收缩力有哪些变化？”**

"If FKBP12.6 is overexpressed, what changes occur in cardiac Ca²⁺ transients and contractility?"

- **“过表达会使RyR2更稳定，SR钙泄漏减少，舒张期SR Ca²⁺储存增加，Ca²⁺瞬变幅度增大，收缩力增强。”**

"Overexpression makes RyR2 more stable, reduces SR Ca²⁺ leak, increases diastolic SR Ca²⁺ storage, enhances Ca²⁺ transient amplitude, and increases contractility."

RyRs调控：钙离子和相关蛋白的作用机制RyRs regulation: Mechanisms of Ca2+ and Regulatory Proteins

— 一、钙离子对RyR的双模调控作用Bi-modal regulation of RyRs by Ca2+

1
RyR受钙离子的双模调控，有高亲和力和低亲和力两个结合位点。 RyRs are regulated by Ca2+ in a bi-modal manner, with both high-affinity and low-affinity binding sites.
2
高亲和力位点结合Ca2+后激活RyR开放，促进Ca2+释放。 The high-affinity site activates RyRs when bound by Ca2+, promoting Ca2+ release.
3
低亲和力位点在Ca2+浓度过高时介导抑制作用，防止过量Ca2+释放。 The low-affinity site mediates inhibition at high Ca2+ concentrations, preventing excessive Ca2+ release.
4
在活细胞实验中，随着Ca2+浓度从0.8微摩尔上升到11微摩尔，RyR开放概率显著增加，进一步升高到1毫摩尔时开放几率反而下降，表现为“钟形”调控曲线。 Experimental data show that as Ca2+ concentration increases from 0.8 μM to 11 μM, the open probability of RyRs increases significantly; further elevation to 1 mM causes a decrease in open probability, resulting in a bell-shaped regulation curve.

— 二、FKBP12.6对RyR的调控作用Regulation of RyRs by FKBP12.6 (calstabin)

1
FKBP12.6属于免疫亲和蛋白，可结合FK506和雷帕霉素等药物，调节RyRs功能。 FKBP12.6 is an immunophilin that binds drugs such as FK506 and rapamycin and modulates RyRs function.
2
FKBP12.6有助于舒张期将RyRs稳定在关闭状态，防止不适当的Ca2+泄漏。 FKBP12.6 helps stabilize RyRs in the closed state during diastole, preventing inappropriate Ca2+ leakage.
3
FKBP12.6还能同步RyRs的开放，提高心肌细胞内Ca2+释放的精准性。 FKBP12.6 also synchronizes RyRs opening, enhancing the precision of Ca2+ release in cardiomyocytes.
4
当FKBP12.6从RyR解离（如用FK506处理）时，Ca2+火花频率升高，提示RyR在舒张期易于过度开放，钙漏增加。 When FKBP12.6 dissociates from RyR (e.g., after FK506 treatment), the frequency of Ca2+ sparks increases, indicating diastolic RyR instability and increased calcium leak.
5
相反，过表达FKBP12.6会使Ca2+火花频率下降，使RyR更加稳定，减少非生理性Ca2+释放。 Conversely, overexpression of FKBP12.6 reduces Ca2+ spark frequency, making RyRs more stable and reducing pathological Ca2+ release.

— 三、FKBP12.6过表达对兴奋-收缩偶联的影响Impact of FKBP12.6 overexpression on ECC

1
过表达FKBP12.6可显著增加肌浆网SR的Ca2+储量，表现为SR Ca2+负荷明显上升。 Overexpression of FKBP12.6 significantly increases the SR Ca2+ load, as shown by a marked rise in SR Ca2+ content.
2
同时，Ca2+瞬变幅度上升，表明心肌细胞对兴奋刺激的钙释放增强。 Simultaneously, the Ca2+ transient amplitude increases, indicating enhanced Ca2+ release upon excitation in cardiomyocytes.
3
实验显示，心肌细胞收缩幅度（shortening）也随之明显增强，反映心肌收缩力上调。 Experimental data show that cell shortening (contraction) is greatly enhanced, reflecting increased cardiac contractility.

— 四、钙调蛋白（CaM）对RyR的抑制作用Regulation of RyRs by Calmodulin (CaM)

1
钙调蛋白可依赖Ca2+直接结合并抑制RyRs。 Calmodulin (CaM) can directly bind and inhibit RyRs in a Ca2+-dependent manner.
2
CaM抑制RyRs开放、降低其开放概率，使RyR稳定于关闭状态。 CaM inhibition reduces RyR open probability, stabilizing the channel in a closed state.
3
随着CaM浓度增加，RyR开放概率逐步降低，表现为剂量-效应关系。 As CaM concentration rises, the open probability of RyRs decreases in a dose-dependent manner.

— 补充：老师课堂讲解中的要点补充Supplement: Key Points from Classroom Explanation

1
RyRs通道活性的调控依赖于胞质侧和腔面多种因子，包括钙离子、FKBP12.6、磷酸化、氧化还原修饰以及CaM等。 The activity of RyRs is regulated by multiple factors on both cytosolic and luminal sides, including Ca2+, FKBP12.6, phosphorylation, redox modification, and CaM.
2
钙离子的调节作用呈双相效应——既可激活也可抑制RyR通道，维持细胞内稳态。 Calcium exerts a biphasic effect—both activating and inhibiting RyR channels—to maintain intracellular homeostasis.
3
Ca2+或其它调节因子的异常，可能导致舒张期钙漏增加和心律失常等病理状态。 Abnormalities in Ca2+ or other regulators may cause increased diastolic Ca2+ leak and arrhythmias.

RyRs的磷酸化Phosphorylation of RyRs

一、β-肾上腺素能刺激及相关信号通路 β-adrenergic stimulation and related cascades

1
β-肾上腺素能刺激能够促进cAMP生成，进而激活多种激酶对RyR进行磷酸化修饰。 β-adrenergic stimulation promotes cAMP production, which activates various kinases to phosphorylate RyRs.
2
蛋白激酶A (PKA) 主要作用于S2808和S2030位点，调节RyR的功能。 Protein kinase A (PKA) primarily acts at S2808 and S2030 sites to regulate RyR function.
3
钙调蛋白激酶II (CaMKII) 作用于S2814位点，对RyR开放概率有重要调控作用。 Ca2+/calmodulin-dependent kinase II (CaMKII) acts at the S2814 site, which is important for RyR open probability.
4
磷酸酶通过去磷酸化抑制RyR的磷酸化水平，限制其活性。 Phosphatases inhibit RyR phosphorylation by dephosphorylation, limiting its activity.

二、磷酸化修饰的结果与分子机制 Consequences and molecular mechanisms of phosphorylation

1
RyR上的关键磷酸化位点包括S2030、S2808和S2814，分别受PKA和CaMKII调控。 Key phosphorylation sites on RyRs include S2030, S2808, and S2814, which are regulated by PKA and CaMKII.
2
不同位点的磷酸化协同影响RyR的开放概率，通道可进入亚导电状态（sub-conductance state），这在心力衰竭等病理状态下尤为突出。 Phosphorylation at different sites cooperatively increases RyR open probability and may promote sub-conductance states, especially in pathological conditions like heart failure.
3
FKBP12.6的解离是磷酸化后RyR活性升高的重要机制之一。 Dissociation of FKBP12.6 is a key mechanism for increased RyR activity after phosphorylation.
4
磷酸化热点（phosphorylation "hot spot"）集中分布，调节通道局部功能动态。 Phosphorylation "hot spots" are clustered to modulate the local dynamic channel function.

三、RyRs的巨分子复合体组成 RyRs as a macromolecular complex

1
RyR作为跨膜蛋白，与多种调节蛋白在胞质侧和腔面侧形成巨分子复合体。 As a transmembrane protein, RyR forms a macromolecular complex with various regulators at both cytosolic and luminal sides.
2
胞质侧主要调节因子包括：游离Ca2+、FKBP12.6（又称Calstabin2）、钙调蛋白（CaM）、CaMKII、PKA、磷酸酶等。 Major cytosolic regulators include free Ca2+, FKBP12.6 (also called Calstabin2), calmodulin (CaM), CaMKII, PKA, and phosphatases.
3
腔面侧主要调控因子有：游离Ca2+、Triadin、Junctin、Calsequestrin（CSQ）。 Main luminal-side modulators are free Ca2+, triadin, junctin, and calsequestrin (CSQ).

四、腔面Ca2+ 调控及电压依赖性 Luminal Ca2+ regulation and voltage dependence

1
腔面Ca2+浓度升高（例如5 mM vs 20 μM）显著提升RyR的开放概率。 Increased luminal Ca2+ concentration (e.g., 5 mM vs. 20 μM) significantly increases the RyR open probability.
2
这一调控与跨膜电位关系密切，低和高电位下开放概率均随腔面Ca2+上升而增加。 This regulation is voltage-dependent, with open probability increasing with luminal Ca2+ regardless of the holding potential.
3
相关实验证明，开放概率呈随腔面Ca2+上升而上升的曲线。 Experimental data show a rising curve of open probability with increasing luminal Ca2+.

五、Junctin/Calsequestrin复合物调控 Junctin/Calsequestrin complex regulation

1
Calsequestrin（CSQ）、Triadin（Tr）、Junctin（Jn）复合物作为腔面Ca2+传感器调控RyR。 The CSQ/Triadin/Junctin complex acts as a luminal Ca2+ sensor to regulate RyRs.
2
当腔面Ca2+低时，CSQ通过Triadin和Junctin与RyR强烈结合，抑制RyR开放。 At low luminal Ca2+, CSQ interacts strongly with RyR via triadin and junctin to inhibit channel opening.
3
随着腔面Ca2+升高，CSQ与Triadin/Junctin和RyR的结合减弱，使通道易被激活。 As luminal Ca2+ increases, CSQ-triadin/junctin-RyR interactions weaken, allowing RyR activation.
4
这一精细调控有助于心肌细胞在不同Ca2+负荷状态下实现功能稳态，防止异常钙释放与心律失常。 Such fine regulation enables cardiomyocytes to maintain homeostasis at varying Ca2+ loads and prevents abnormal Ca2+ release and arrhythmias.

Ca²⁺诱导的Ca²⁺释放机制（CICR）The Ca²⁺-induced Ca²⁺ release (CICR) mechanism

一、CICR原理、耦联与放大 Basic Principle, Coupling and Amplification

1
心肌细胞内出现Ca²⁺诱导的Ca²⁺释放（CICR），指少量Ca²⁺流入触发大量Ca²⁺释放。 In cardiomyocytes, Ca²⁺-induced Ca²⁺ release (CICR) means a small influx of Ca²⁺ triggers a large Ca²⁺ release.
2
主要“Trigger”包括L型Ca²⁺通道开放和高[Na⁺]_i时NCX反转。 The main "Trigger" includes opening of L-type Ca²⁺ channels and NCX reversal when [Na⁺]_i is high.
3
这些信号通过细胞膜进入，促使肌浆网(SR)上的RyR₂开放，释放储存的Ca²⁺。 These signals enter through the plasma membrane and stimulate the ryanodine receptor (RyR₂) on the sarcoplasmic reticulum (SR) to release stored Ca²⁺.
4
CICR实现细胞内信号耦联和放大，使心肌收缩反应更迅速高效。 CICR couples and amplifies cellular signals, resulting in a rapid and efficient cardiac contraction.
5
老师补充：CICR机制依赖电生理事件与信号同步——L型Ca²⁺通道和RyR需空间紧密排列。 Teacher’s note: Effective CICR depends on synchronization of electrical and biochemical signals—L-type Ca²⁺ channels and RyR must be closely positioned.

二、SR Ca²⁺负荷调控 Regulation of the SR Ca²⁺ Load

1
SR中Ca²⁺含量主要由三因素调节： SR Ca²⁺ content is mainly regulated by three factors:   1）SERCA将Ca²⁺主动泵回SR储存（依赖ATP）。 SERCA actively pumps Ca²⁺ back into the SR for storage (ATP-dependent).   2）NCX将胞质Ca²⁺与胞外Na⁺交换，起到清除作用。 NCX exchanges cytosolic Ca²⁺ for extracellular Na⁺, helping to extrude Ca²⁺.   3）L型Ca²⁺通道介导Ca²⁺流入胞内，参与SR负荷补充。 L-type Ca²⁺ channels mediate Ca²⁺ influx into the cell, replenishing SR Ca²⁺ load.
2
SR Ca²⁺负荷直接决定收缩时Ca²⁺释放量，进而影响心肌收缩强度。 The SR Ca²⁺ load directly determines the amount of Ca²⁺ released during contraction, thus affecting contractile strength.
3
老师强调：哺乳动物如鼠类SR对Ca²⁺清除贡献约90%，而兔/人这一比例约70%，物种间差异明显。 Teacher’s note: SR contributes about 90% to Ca²⁺ removal in rodents, about 70% in rabbits/humans, showing clear species differences.

三、β肾上腺素能调控机制 Activation by β-adrenergic Signaling: PKA and EPAC

1
应激状态下，去甲肾上腺素/肾上腺素激活β受体，升高cAMP，激活PKA和EPAC通路。 Under stress, norepinephrine/epinephrine activates β-adrenergic receptors, increasing cAMP and activating PKA and EPAC pathways.
2
PKA作用位点包括L型Ca²⁺通道、磷脂酶抑制蛋白PLB（实现SERCA去抑制）、RyR等，整体提升Ca²⁺内流与SR回收速率。 PKA targets L-type Ca²⁺ channels, phospholamban (PLB, relieving SERCA inhibition), RyRs, overall enhancing Ca²⁺ influx and SR reuptake.
3
EPAC可通过激活CaMKII进一步磷酸化RyR，增强SR Ca²⁺释放。 EPAC can activate CaMKII, further phosphorylating RyR to enhance SR Ca²⁺ release.
4
急性激活产生“战斗/逃跑反应”，慢性持续则易诱发心力衰竭、肥大和心律失常等病理过程。 Acute activation produces the “fight or flight” response; chronic stimulation contributes to heart failure, hypertrophy and arrhythmia.

四、信号通路具体分子效应 Molecular Effects of Signaling Pathways

1
I_Ca,L的PKA磷酸化提升胞外Ca²⁺流入，增强SR Ca²⁺储备。 PKA phosphorylation of I_Ca,L increases Ca²⁺ influx, boosting SR Ca²⁺ loading.
2
PLB被PKA磷酸化后，SERCA活性增强，提高Ca²⁺回收速率和SR负荷。 When PLB is phosphorylated by PKA, SERCA activity is accelerated, increasing Ca²⁺ reuptake and SR load.
3
结果是心肌收缩幅度明显增强。 The result is a marked increase in cardiomyocyte contractility.

五、EPAC对ECC的调控 Regulation of ECC by EPAC

1
实验数据显示8-CPT处理后SR Ca²⁺火花频率显著升高，表明RyR易被激活。 Experimental data show that after 8-CPT treatment, SR Ca²⁺ spark frequency markedly increases, indicating RyR facilitation.

8-CPT诱导RyR2特定位点Ser^2815磷酸化增强，表现为斑点加深。 8-CPT increases phosphorylation at RyR2 Ser^2815, as shown by darker spots on the blot.

3
这些效应依赖于EPAC存在，双敲除小鼠则响应减弱。 These effects depend on the presence of EPAC; the response is blunted in EPAC double knockout mice.

六、老师补充 & 录音要点 Teacher's Supplement & Key Lecture Points

1
触发Ca²⁺电流（I_Ca,L）增大，NCX反转时亦可协助Ca²⁺进入，放大SR内Ca²⁺释放效应。 An increase in triggering Ca²⁺ current (I_Ca,L) and NCX reversal both help Ca²⁺ entry, amplifying SR Ca²⁺ release.
2
细胞实验通过荧光染料测量Ca²⁺瞬变，可反映收缩-舒张周期中的启动与回收动态。 Experiments use fluorescence dyes to measure Ca²⁺ transients, capturing the dynamics of excitation-contraction cycles.
3
评估SR负荷靠钙瞬变幅度和衰减速率，功能异常时推断相关泵、通道或调控蛋白改变。 SR load is assessed using the amplitude and decay of Ca²⁺ transients; dysfunction suggests changes in pumps, channels or regulatory proteins.
